# Constitution of Zimbabwe (2013) — Ndebele translation

This is a translation of the original 2013 text of theConstitution of Zimbabwe. It does not include amendments made by Acts of Parliament in 2017 and 2021; the validity of those Acts is still the subject of litigation in the Constitutional Court.

IsisekamthethoseZimbabwe Utshintsho (No. 20)

2013

ZIMBABWE

UMTHETHO Osusa njalo othatha isikhundla seSisekamthetho seZimbabwe. Lumthetho umiswe liDale lePhalamende loMongameli waseZimbabwe.



Isihloko esifitshane


Ukuchasiswa

Lumthetho ungabizwa ngokuthi nguMthetho weSisekamthetho seZimbabwe Utshintsho (No. 20), 2013.

Kulo Umthetho— “iSisekamthetho esikhona” kutshiwo ukuthi iSisekamthetho seZimbabwe esaqala ukusebenza mhlaka 18 Mabasa 1980, esatshintshwa ngemva kwalokho;


“ISisekamthetho esitsha” kutshiwo iSisekamthetho seZimbabwe esethulwe kulolu uHlelo. 15


Ukususwa kweSisekamthetho esikhona yiSisekamthetho esitsha

(1) Kulandelwa isahlukwana (2), iSisekamthetho esikhona siyasuswa indawo yaso ithathwe yiSisekamthetho esitsha.

(2) Ukuze kungabi lokuthandabuza, kuyabikwa ukuthi—

(a) isiSekamthetho esitsha siyamiswa ngelanga “lokwethulelwa uzulu” njengokuchasiswe kuhlelo lwesithupha kulesoSisekamthetho, lokhu Printed by the Government Printer, Harare. 1

DISTRIBUTED BY VERITAS e-mail: veritas@mango.zw; website: www.veritaszim.net Veritas makes every effort to ensure the provision of reliable information, but cannot take legal responsibility for information supplied.

kutsho ukuthi, ngelanga lapho lo uMthetho usethulelwa uzulu encwadini yeGazette kulandelwa isigaba 51(1) yeSisekamthetho esikhona; njalo

(b) Kusukela ngelanga lokumiswa kwesiSekamthetho esitsha njengokucazwe kundima engaphezulu u (a), iSisekamthetho esikhona sizakwala silamandla njengalokho okunqunywe kuHlelo lweSithupha lweSisekamthetho esitsha.

UHLELO (ISIGABA 2) ISISEKAMTHETHO SEZIMBABWE _______________ Isendlalelo.

UKUHLELWA KWEZIGABA ISAHLUKO 1

Izimiso Eziyinsika Isigaba

IRiphabliki. Amandla eSisekamthetho. Insika lezimiso eziligugu zeSizwe. Ifulegi yelizwe, Ingoma yeSizwe, Isidindo sikaHulumende loPhawu lukaHulumende. Amazinga kaHulumende. Izindimi. Ukusakaza iSisekamthetho kuzulu. ISAHLUKO 2

## Section 31

Izinhloso ezikhokhela iSizwe lazo zonke izingatsha kanye labamela uHulumende. Ukubusa kuhle. Ukumanyana, ukuthula lokuhlaliseka elizweni. Ukunanzwa kwamalungelo lezinkululeko eziyinsika. Ukudlelana lamanye amazwe. Ingqubelaphambili yelizwe. Ukuphakanyiswa kukazulu lokudalwa kwemisebenzi. Ukutholakala kokudla. Amasiko. Ukulinganiswa kobulili. Ukumelwa kwezindawo okufaneleyo. Abantwana. Abatsha. Impunga. Abakhubazekileyo. Abalweli bempi yenkululeko. Imisebenzi kanye lezokuqhatshwa. Ukuvikeleka kwemuli. Umtshado. Imfundo. Indawo yokuhlala. Ezempilakahle. Inhlalakahle kazulu. Usizo kwezemithetho.

Isigaba

Imidlalo lezindawo zokuzilibazisa. Ukulondolozwa kolwazi lomdabu. Ukunanzwa kwemithetho yomhlaba. ISAHLUKO 3

## Section 43 — Ubuzalwane

Ukuba yisizalwane seZimbabwe. Ukuba yisizalwane ngokuzalwa. Ukuba yisizalwane ngosendo. Ukuba yisizalwane ngokubhalisa. Ukwemukwa ubuzalwane. Ukugcina ubuzalwane lanxa umuntu setshadile kumbe eseqamule umtshado. Ibhodi ebona ngezobuzalwane kanye lezokungena elizweni. Amandla edale lePhalamende mayelana lezobuzalwane. Ukuqhubeka lokuvuselelwa kobuzalwane osebake babakhona.

## Section 66

Ilungelo lokuphila. Ilungelo lenkululeko yomuntu. Amalungelo abantu ababotshiweyo labavalelweyo. Ilungelo lokuba lesizotha njengomuntu. Ilungelo lokuvikeleka komuntu. Inkululeko ekuhlukunyezweni, ekujezisweni, ekuphathweni ngochuku kumbe ngendlela eyehlisa isithunzi. Inkululeko ekugqilazweni kumbe ekwenziweni isichaka. Inkululeko ekubanjweni ngamandla ukuthi usebenze. Ukulingana lokungabandlululwa. Ilungelo lokubawedwa. Inkululeko yokubuthana lokuhlanganyela. Inkululeko yokutshengisela lokunika izikhalazo. Inkululeko yomzwangedwa. Inkululeko yokuveza imibono lenkululeko yemisakazo. Ukuthola imibiko. Ulimi lamasiko. Inkululeko yokudinga umsebenzi lokuqhatshwa. Amalungelo abaqhatsheneyo. Inkululeko yokuhamba leyokuhlala. 4

Amalungelo aphathelane lomhlabathi wezolimo. Amalungelo aphathelane lemvelo. Inkululeko yokungathuthiswa mahlayana endaweni. Ilungelo lokufunda. Ilungelo lokuthola impilakahle. UMongameli. Imisebenzi kaMongameli. Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu anelise ukuvotelwa njengoMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli. Ukuvotelwa kukaMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli.

bakaMongameli.

Ukungathonisiswa kukaMongameli.

Imisebenzi yabaSekeli bakaMongameli.

## Section 100

Osaphethe isikhundla sikaMongameli.

## Section 101

Ukungenwa kwesikhundla sikaMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli nxa angafa, angatshiya umsebenzi kumbe engasenelisi.

## Section 102

Iholo likaMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli.

## Section 103

UMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli kanyelasebake baphatha lezozikhundla abazukuphatha ezinye izihlalo loba ukuqhatshwa kweminye imisebenzi. ISAHLUKWANA 3

Omphathintambo, Abasekeli BabophathIntambo Lekhabhinethi

## Section 109

Ukuqhatshwa kwaboMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo. IKhabhinethi. Ukuziphatha kwabaSekeli bakaMongameli, oMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo. Umlandu wabaSekeli bakaMongameli, oMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo. Isikhathi esibekiweyo sokuba sesikhundleni kwaboMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo. Ivoti yokugumula uHulumende ongathembekanga. ISAHLUKWANA 4

## Section 112

## Section 113 — Imisebenzi Yeziphathambuso

Imisebenzi ebhekane loMongameli kanye leKhabhinethi. Impi lokuthula. Amandla okuxolela. Izikhathi uzulu ebhekane lomumo omanzonzo. ISAHLUKWANA 5

## Section 115 — Ugqwethanhloko

UGqwethanhloko. Ukususwa esikhundleni kukaGqwethanhloko. ISAHLUKO 6

Idale lePhalamende. Umlandu wedale lePhalamende ISAHLUKWANA 3 Idale Lesenethi

## Section 123

Amalunga abumba idale leSenethi. Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu enelise kumbe engenelisi ukuvotelwa njengelunga ledale leSenethi. UMongameli wedale leSenethi. UMsekeli kaMongameli wedale leSenethi. ISAHLUKWANA 4

## Section 127 — Indlu Yabameli

Amalunga abumba iNdlu YabaMeli. Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu enelise kumbe engenelisi ukuvotelwa eNdlini yabaMeli. USomlomo weNdlu yabaMeli. UMsekeli kaSomlomo weNdlu yabaMeli. ISAHLUKWANA 5

Isikhathi Esibekiweyo Sokuhlala Esikhundleni Kwamalunga Edale Lephalamende

## Section 128

## Section 129 — Isifungo seLunga ledale lePhalamende

Isikhathi esibekiweyo sokuhlala esikhundleni kweLunga ledale lePhalamende. ISAHLUKWANA 6

## Section 130

## Section 131

## Section 132

## Section 133

## Section 134 — Amandla Ezibumbamthetho Lamanye Athile

Amandla lemisebenzi yedale leSenethi kanye leNdlu yabaMeli. IMithetho emiswe yiPhalamende lokuqhutshwa kwayo ukuze igcwaliseke. Ukuqalisa ukusebenza kweMithetho emiswe yiPhalamende. Ukubhalwa kweMithetho emiswe yiPhalamende. UMthetho Osekelayo. ISAHLUKWANA 7

## Section 136

## Section 137

## Section 138

## Section 139

## Section 140

## Section 141

## Section 142 — Ukuqhutshwa Komsebenzi Edale Lephalamende

Inhloko yedale lePhalamende. Abantu abakhokhela umsebenzi edale lePhalamende. Inani lamalunga okumele abe khona ukuze umsebenzi uqhutshwe edale lePhalamende. Ukuvota kanye lelungelo lokuzwakala edale lePhalamende. IziLawulo ezimiyo. Izethulo lemibiko kaMongameli kudale lePhalamende. Ukufinyelela lokuphatheka kukazulu emisebenzini yedale lePhalamende. Ukuba semthethweni kwengqubo zedale lePhalamende.

ISAHLUKWANA 8

Isikhathi Esibekiweyo, Ukudilizwa Lokuhlalwa Kwedale Lephalamende Isigaba

## Section 143

## Section 144

## Section 145

## Section 146

## Section 147

Isikhathi esibekiweyo lokudilizwa kwedale Phalamende. UKhethojikelele oluqhutshwa ngemva kokudilizwa kwedale lePhalamende. Ukuhlalwa kwakuqala kwedale lePhalamende ngemva koKhethojikelele. Izikhathi zokuhlala lezokuthatha ikhefu. Ukugoqwa kweMithetho ebisakhandwa, iziphakamiso, izikhalazo lezinye izingqubo ekudilizweni kwedale lePhalamende. ISAHLUKWANA 9

## Section 154 — Okunye Okuphathelane Ledale lePhalamende

Izimfanelo lokuvikeleka emthethweni kwedale lePhalamende. Ilungelo lokukhalaza kuPhalamende. Lapho okungenelwa khona idale lePhalamende. IKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo eziMiyo. IKomithi yezoMthetho edale lePhalamende. Iholo likaMongameli wedale leSenethi, uSomlomo kanye lamaLunga edale lePhalamende. UNobhala wedale lePhalamende lezinye izisebenzi. ISAHLUKO 7 Ukhetho

## Section 157 — Izindlela Zokuqhutshwa Kokhetho

Izimiso zendlela zokuqhuba uKhetho. Ukuqhutshwa koKhetho lamavoti okudinga umbono kazulu. UMthetho woKhetho. ISAHLUKWANA 2

## Section 158

## Section 159 — Ukubekwa Kwesikhathi Sokhetho

Ukubekwa kwesikhathi soKhetho. Ukuvotelwa ezikhundleni ezisele zingelabantu. ISAHLUKWANA 3

## Section 160

## Section 161 — Ukuqunywa Kwemingcele Yokhetho

Inani lezifunda lamawadi. Ukuqunywa kwemingcele yoKhetho.

## Section 162

## Section 163 — Imithethwandaba

Igunya leziLawulamthetho. IBumba leziLawulamthetho.

Isigaba

## Section 176

Ukuzimela kweBumba leziLawulamthetho. Izimiso ezihola iziLawulamthetho. UMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho. Umkhawulo woMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho. UMthethwandaba oMkhulu. Umkhawulo woMthethwandaba oMkhulu. UMthethwandaba oPhezulu. Umkhawulo woMthethwandaba oPhezulu. UMthethwandaba wezeMisebenzi. UMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa Kwamandla. Eminye iMithethwandaba lamaDale. Amandla eMithethwandaba endabeni eziphathelane leSisekamthetho. Amandla aqukethwe nguMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho, UMthethwandaba oMkhulu loMthetwandaba oPhezulu. ISAHLUKWANA 2

Ukuqhatshwa Kwezilawulamthetho Lesikhathi Esibekiweyo Sokuhlala Kwazo Esikhundleni

## Section 188 — Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu anelise ukuqhatshwa njengoMahluleli

woMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho. Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu anelise ukuqhatshwa njengoMahluleli woMthethwandaba oMkhulu. Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu anelise ukuqhatshwa njengoMahluleli woMthethwandaba oPhezulu, uMthethwandaba wezeMisebenzi loMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa Kwamandla. Ukuqhatshwa kwaBehluleli. Ukuqhatshwa kwabasamele amalunga eBumba leziLawulamthetho. Ukuqhatshwa kwaboMantshi lamanye amalunga eBumba leziLawulamthetho. IziLawulamthetho kazizukuqhatshelwa emithethwandaba eyedlula owodwa. Ukuqhatshwa kweziLawulamthetho kumele kukhombise isimo somphakathi. Isifungo sokuqhatshwa. Isikhathi esibekiweyo sokuhlala esikhundleni kwaBehluli. Ukususwa kwaBehluli esikhundleni. Izimiselo zomsebenzi lesikhathi esibekiweyo sokuhlala esikhundleni kwaBehluli. ISAHLUKWANA 3

## Section 189

## Section 190

## Section 191 — Ikhomishini Yemisebenzi Yezilawulamthetho

Ukumiswa kanye lokubunjwa kweKomishini yemiSebenzi yeziLawulamthetho. Imisebenzi yeKomishini yemiSebenzi yeziLawulamthetho. Ukufanela lokubamgceke kwezingqubo zemisebenzi yeKomishini yemiSebenzi yeziLawulamthetho. ISAHLUKWANA 4

## Section 192

## Section 193 — Okunye

UMthetho ozalawulwa. Umkhawulo wamandla emithethwandaba emacaleni okwephula imithetho.

## Section 198 — Okuqakathekiswayo kanye lezimiso ezihola ukuphathwa kwemisebenzi

ephathelane lozulu. Amabhizimusi angaphansi kweSizwe. Imilandu yezisebenzi zikazulu lezimiso zobukhokeli. Izikhathi ezibekiweyo zokuphatha izikhundla kwezinkokheli zezinhlanganiso ezingaphansi kukaHulumende. Imithetho yokuphoqelela iSahluko 9. ISAHLUKO 10

## Section 205 — Izisebenzi Zikahulumende

Ukusebenzela uHulumende. Ukuziphatha kwezisebenzi zikaHulumende. UMphathintambo okhangelane lezisebenzi zikaHulumende. Ukumiswa kweKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende. Umsebenzi weKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende. Amanxusa eZwe labanye abakhulu abamela iZimbabwe. OmaBhalane Abakhulu. ISAHLUKO 11

## Section 210 — Izindingeko

Ukuvikelwa kwelizwe. Abavikela ilizwe. Ukuziphatha kwabavikela ilizwe. Inhlanganiso yokuvikelwa kwelizwe. Indlela ezimeleyo yezikhalazo.

## Section 218 — Ibutho Lomvikela

IButho loMvikela. Imisebenzi yeButho loMvikela. Ukuthunyelwa kwamaButho oMvikela. Umlandu wezombusazwe ngokuthunyelwa kwamaButho oMvikela. UMphathintambo okhangelane lamabutho okuvikela ilizwe. Ukulawulwa kwamabutho omvikela. Ukumiswa lokubunjwa kweKomishini yengatsha zamabutho omvikela. Umsebenzi weKomishini yengatsha zamabutho omvikela. ISAHLUKWANA 3

## Section 219

## Section 220

## Section 221 — Ugatsha Lwamapholisa

Ugatsha lwamapholisa lomsebenzi walo. UMphathintambo wengatsha zamapholisa. INdunankulu yamaPholisa.

## Section 222

## Section 223

Ukumiswa kweKomishini yemiSebenzi yamaPholisa. Umsebenzi weKomishini yemiSebenzi yamaPholisa. ISAHLUKWANA 4

## Section 225

## Section 226 — Abacuphi

Ukumiswa kwengatsha zabacuphi, UMphathintambo okhangelane lengatsha zokucupha elizweni, Ukulawulwa lokuqondiswa kwengatsha zabacuphi elizweni, ISAHLUKWANA 5

## Section 231 — Abezintolongo Lokuqondiswa Kwezibotshwa

Ugatsha lwezintolongo lokuqondiswa kwezibotshwa lomsebenzi walo. UMphathintambo okhangelane lezinkantolo lokuqondiswa kwezibotshwa. UNdunankulu wogatsha lwezintolongo lokuqondisa izibotshwa. Ukumiswa kweKomishini yogatsha lwezintolongo lokuqondiswa kwezibotshwa. Umsebenzi weKomishini yogatsha lwezintolongo lokuqondiswa kwezibotshwa. ISAHLUKO 12

## Section 237 — Izimiso-Jikelele

AmaKomishini azimele wodwa. Izinhloso zamaKomishini azimele wodwa. Izisebenzi zamaKomishini azimele wodwa. Inkululeko yamaKomishini. Amalunga amaKomishini azimele wodwa kumele angabi lebandla lombusazwe alisekelayo. Ukuqhatshwa lokususwa esikhundleni kwamalunga amaKomishini azimele wodwa ISAHLUKWANA 2

## Section 239

## Section 240

## Section 241 — Ikomishini Ebona Ngezokhetho Ezimbabwe

Ukubunjwa lokuqoqwa kweKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe. Imisebenzi yeKomithi ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe. Ukugunyulwa kuKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe. IKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele yazise mayelana loKhetho, kugoqela olokudinga umbono kazulu. ISAHLUKWANA 3

## Section 242

## Section 244 — Ikomishini Ebona Ngezamalungelo Oluntu Ezimbabwe

Ukubunjwa lokuqoqwa kweKomishini ebona ngezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe. Imisebenzi yeKomishini ebona ngezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe. Imibiko eya kumbe evela kuKomishini yezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe.

ISAHLUKWANA 4

Ikomishini Ebona Ngezobulili Ezimbabwe Isigaba

## Section 247

## Section 329 — Okunye Nje

Izivumelwano lamazwe omhlaba, izivumelwano ezenziwe ngokusemthethweni Kanye lezivumelwano ezingumthetho. Inguquko kuSisekamthetho. ## Section 345 — Ukuchasiswa

Ukusetshenziswa kweSahlukwana 3. Izimiso zokuchasisa iSisekamthetho. Izingcazelo. Lapho okukhulunywa ngeZahluko, Izigaba, lokunye. Amagama asebunyeni agoqela ubunengi kanjalo lasebunengini agoqela asebunyeni. Uluhlu lezihloko. Lapho okukhulunywa ngezikhathi. Lapho okukhulunywa ngeziphathamahofisi. Lapho okukhulunywa ngePhalamende. Inkuthazo lokuxoxisana ngosizo. Ukuqhatshwa. Ukutshiya isikhundla. Ukwenza umsebenzi, lokunye. Nxa umuntu ethathwa njengokuthi kaphathanga isikhundla sikaHulumende. Inani lamalunga adingeka ukuze izinqumo zibe semthethweni Kanye lempumela yezikhexe kunhlanganiso ezibunjwe ngaphansi kweSisekamthetho. Ukungavumelani phakathi kwemibhalo ehlukeneyo yeSisekamthetho.

UHLELO LWAKUQALA: iFulegi yelizwe, iNgoma yesizwe, uPhawu lukahulumende lesidindo sikahulumende. UHLELO LWESIBILI: umkhawulo wamalungelo ngesikhathi kulesimo esimanzonzo. UHLELO LWESITHATHU: Izifungo lokuvuma. UHLELO LWESINE: Indingeko zabavoti.

UHLELO LWESIHLANU: iziqondiso eziphathelane lemithetho engakethulwa kanye lezinye izinto edale lePhalamende. UHLELO LWESITHUPHA: ukuqala ukusebenza kwalesiSisekamthetho, imithetho yaphakathi kwesikhathi lemithetho engasasebenziyo.

ISENDLALELO _________

Thina abantu beZimbabwe, Simanyaniswe ekwahlukeni kwethu yikulangazelela kwethu inkululeko, ukulinganiswa emthethweni lempeleni, kanye lobuqhawe bethu ekulwiseni umbuso wabacindezeli, inkethabetshabi layo yonke imihlobo yokubusa ngamandla langocindezelo, Sibabaza njalo siziqhenya ngesibindi samadoda labafazi abanikela impilo yabo ngesikhathi sempi yeMfazo kanye lempi yenkululeko, Sihlonipha okhokho bethu lezinye izizalwane ezasebenza gadalala ukuze ilizwe lethu liphumelele, Sinanza ukuqakatheka kokuba siqinise umbuso kazulu, umbuso omuhle, obalulekileyo, omgceke, lokubusa ngomthetho, Siqinisa njalo ukuzimisela kwethu ekunanzeni lekuvikeleni amalungelo abantu lezinkululeko eziyinsika, Sisamukela inotho equkethwe yimvelo yethu, Sijabulela ubugagu bemikhuba lamasiko ethu, Sijongise ukuqeda zonke izinhlupho lemigoqo ephazamisa impumelelo yethu, Sithakazelela inkululeko, ukulingana, ukuthula, ukulinganiswa emthethweni, ukubekezela, ukuphumelela kanye lokuziqhenya ngelizwe lethu lapho sidinga ukuzelula sisenaba njengesizwe ngambono munye, Sisamukela ubukhulu bukaMlimu onguSomandla, yena onguye ophethe ikusasa lethu ngesakhe isandla, Siyabopha ngokuqukethwe kulesi Sisekamthetho ukuthi sizinikele ekwakheni isizwe esimanyeneyo, esidalelwe eziqwini zokukhanya, ukulingana, inkululeko, ukwahlulela okulungileyo, ukuthembeka kanye lesizotha sokusebenza nzima, Njalo, ekuceleni ukukhokhelwa langokuphathiswa nguMlimu uSomandla, sibumba lesi Sisekamthetho sibuye sizimisele ngaphansi kwaso njengoMthetho oyinsika welizwe lethu esilithandayo.

ISAHLUKO 1 Insika Yelizwe


IRiphabliki


Ubukhulu beSisekamthetho

IZimbabwe yiriphabliki eyimbumba, elombuso kazulu njalo ezibusayo.

(1) Lesi Sisekamthetho yiso Umthetho ophezu kwayo yonke kweleZimbabwe njalo kungaba yiwuphi omunye umthetho, ingqubo, umkhuba loba ukwenza okungahambelani laso akukho emthethweni nxa kukhangelwe lokho okungahambelani leSisekamthetho.

(2) Zonke izibopho ezibekwe yilesi Sisekamthetho zibopha wonke umuntu, ophilayo kumbe inhlanganiso ephathwa ngumthetho njengomuntu, kugoqela uHulumende leziPhathambuso, iziBumbamthetho kanye lezinhlanganiso zonke zeziLawulamthetho labameli bakaHulumende kumabanga wonke, njalo kumele zigcwaliswe yibo. 3

Insika lezimiso eziligugu okwakhelwe khona iSizwe

(1) IZimbabwe yakhelwe phezu kwenhlonipho yensika lezimiso eziligugu ezilandelayo—

(a)

ubukhulu beSisekamthetho;

(c)

amalungelo lenkululeko eziyinsika;

(e)

ukunanzwa kwesizotha lokuqakatheka okuqukethwe ngumuntu munye ngamunye;

(b) ukubusa ngomthetho;

(d) imikhuba lamasiko, ubuntu kanye lezinkolo zelizwe ezitshiyatshiyeneyo;

(f)

ukunanzwa kokulingana kwabantu bonke;

(g) ukulinganiswa kobulili;

(h) umbuso omuhle; njalo

(i)

Ukunanzwa kanye lokuhlonitshwa kwempi yenkululeko.

(2) Izimiso zombuso omuhle, eziyizo ezibopha iSizwe kanye lezinhlanganiso zonke labameli bakaHulumende kumabanga wonke, zigoqela—

(a)

ezombusazwe ezivumela amabandla ehlukeneyo ukuphatheka ekubunjweni kombuso oqotho;

(b) ezokhetho ezimiselwe phezu—

(i) kokunika wonke umuntu omdala amandla okuvota lokulinganiswa ekuvoteni jikelele;

(ii) kokuqhutshwa kokhetho olukhululekileyo, olumsulwa njalo olubuya ngemva kwesikhathi esihleliweyo;

(iii) kokumelwa kukazulu ovotayo okufaneleyo;

(c)

kokutshiyelwana kwamandla okuhlelekileyo ngemva kokhetho;

(e)

kokunanzwa kwesimiso sezokwahlukaniselwa kwamandla;

(d) kokuhlonitshwa kwamalungelo amabandla wonke ezombusazwe;

(f)

kwenhlonipho okumele iphiwe abantu beZimbabwe, abayibona sisusa samandla okubusa;

(g) kokukhanya, ukulinganiswa emthethweni lokunika izimpendulo ezisuthisayo;

(h) kokudalwa komanyano, ukuthula lokuhlaliseka elizweni, kunanzwa ukutshiyatshiyana kwezindimi, imikhuba lamasiko.

(i)

kokunanzwa kwamalungelo—

(i) amaqembu atshiyeneyo awosendo, umbala, amasiko, indimi lawokholo;

(ii) abantu abagogekileyo;

(iii) awabantu besifazana, asebegugile, intsha labantwana;

(iv) awamaqhawe empi yenkululeko;

(j)

kokwabelana izingcebo zesizwe ngendlela efaneleyo, kubaliselwa lomhlabathi;

(k) kokuhlonitshwa kwamalungelo wonke aphathelane lomuntu;

(l) 4

kokwahlukaniselana kwamandla ombuso eziqintini ezitshiyeneyo lokusabalaliswa kwamawofisi aphatha imisebenzi kaHulumende.

Ifulegi yelizwe, Ingoma yeSizwe, Isidindo sikaHulumende loPhawu lukaHulumende

IZimbabwe ilefulegi yelizwe, ingoma yeSizwe, Isidindo sikaHulumende loPhawu lukaHulumende, okubalulwe konke kuHlelo lwakuqala. 5

Amazinga kaHulumende

Amazinga kahulumende eZimbabwe agoqela la—

(a)

uHulumende welizwe;

(c)

lamaKhansili amancane, okutsho ukuthi—

(i) amaKhansili emadolobheni, angabizwa yiloba yiliphi ibizo, amela kanye lokuhola inhlalo yabantu emadolobheni; ndawonye

(ii) lamaKhansili emaphandleni, angabizwa yiloba yiliphi ibizo, amela kanye lokuhola inhlalo yabantu emaphandleni asezifundeni ezibumba izabelo.

(b) amaKhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho amakhulu;


Izindimi

(1) Izindimi ezilandelayo, ezithi Chewa, ChiBarwe, English, Kalanga, Koisan, Nambya, Ndau, Ndebele, Tshangani, Shona, Sign Language, Sotho, Tonga, Tswana, Venda lesiXhosa, yizo ezinanzwa ngokusemthethweni njengendimi zeZimbabwe.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungaba lakho ukwethula ezinye izindimi njengendimi ezinanzwa okusemthethweni njalo ungethula izindimi okumele zisetshenziswe ukubhala okwenzakeleyo ezindaweni ezibekiweyo.

(3) UHulumende lezingatsha zonke zikaHulumende kanye labameli bakaHulumende emabangeni wonke kumele—

(a)

Babone ukuthi zonke izindimi ezinanzwa okusemthethweni ziyalinganiswa; lokuthi

(b) Bananzelele izifiso zabantu abathintwa ngamanyathelo kumbe imibiko ekhutshwa nguHulumende mayelana lezindimi abafuna ukuzisebenzisa.

(4) uHulumende kumele akhuthaze njalo aqinise ukusetshenziswa kwezindimi zonke ezikhulunywa eZimbabwe, sihlanganisela iSayini, njalo kumele adale umumo ovumela ukuthuthuka kwalezo ndimi. 7

Ukusakazwa kolwazi ngeSisekamthetho kuzulu

UHulumende kumele anelisele ukusabalaliswa kolwazi ngalesi Sisekamthetho kuzulu, ethatha ikakhulu amanyathelo alandelayo—

(a)

Ukusitolikela sifakwa ezindimini zonke ezinanzwa yiSizwe okusemthethweni lokusifinyelelisa ezindaweni ezisabalale ilizwe lonke nxa kusaneliseka;

(b) Ukubophela ukuthi lesi Sisekamthetho sifundiswe ezikolo njengengxenye yezifundo zabantwana lezamalunga ezomvikela, izisebenzi zikaHulumende lamalunga kanye lezisebenzi zezingatsha ezisebenzela uzulu; kanye

(c)

Lokukhuthaza wonke umuntu kanye lezinhlanganiso, ezibalisela lezo ezisebenzelana lozulu, ukuthi zisabalalise ulwazi mayelana lalesi Sisekamthetho kuzulu wonke jikelele. ISAHLUKO 2 Izinhloso Zelizwe


Izinhloso zokukhokhela isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha kanye labameli bakaHulumende

(1) Izinhloso eziphawulwe kulesisahluko zikhokhela isizwe lezingatsha zikaHulumende zonke labameli bakahulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye ekubumbeni lekusebenziseni imithetho lezinqumo zezimiso ezizakhokhelela ekumisweni, lekuphakamisweni komphakathi osekelekayo, olokulunga, okhululekileyo lapho abantu abaphila khona impilo elenjabulo lesuthisayo.

(2) Kumele kube lokunanzwa kwezinhloso eziphawulwe kulesisahluko nxa kuhunyutshwa imilandu yesizwe ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho ingqe lakuwuphi umthetho. 9

Ukubusa kuhle

(1) Isizwe kumele sithathe njalo sisebenzise izimiso lokubekwa kwemithetho yokuthuthukisa ukusebenza kuhle, ukwenelisa, ukwenelisa ukuchaza ngengqubo, ukubamgceke, ukuthembeka komuntu lokulunga kwezezimali—

(a)

ukuqhatshelwa esikhundleni esiphathelane lozulu kumele kwenziwe kugxilwe onhlozini lolwazi umuntu alalo kuqala;

(b) kumele kuthathwe amanyathela awokuveza, awokulwisana kanye lawokususa yonke imihlobo yenkohlakalo lokungaphathwa kuhle kwamandla yilabo abaphethe izikhundla zombusazwe kanye lezikhundla eziphathelane lozulu.

(2) Isizwe kumele sibone ukuthi zonke izingatsha zikahulumende labameli bakahulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye, ikakhulu amaKomishini lamanye amaqembu amiswe yilesi Sisekamthetho loba ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho, ayaphiwa izimpahla ezaneleyo lezinto zokusebenzisa ezizawenza anelise ukwenza imisebenzi yawo ngokunanzelela, ngokumgceke, ngokwethembeka njalo kakuhle.

Ukumanyana kwelizwe, ukuthula lokuhlaliseka


Ukunanzwa kwamalungelo ayinsika lezinkululeko eziyinsika


Isiqondiso somthetho wangaphandle kwelizwe

2013

Ilizwe kanye lomuntu munye ngamunye, kugoqela inhlanganiso eziphathwa ngumthetho njengabantu logatsha lunye ngalunye labameli bakahulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye, kumele baphakamise ukumanyana kwesizwe, ukuthula kanye lokuhlaliseka.

Ilizwe kumele lithathe wonke amanyathela ekuvikeleni amalungelo ayinsika lezinkululeko eziyinsika ezikusahluko sesine kanye lokuphakamisa ukunanzwa ngokupheleyo lokugcwaliseka kwazo.

(1) Isiqondiso somthetho wangaphandle kwelizwe eseZimbabwe kumele sigxile ekulandeleni izimiso ezilandelayo—

(a)

ukuphakanyiswa lokuvikelwa kwezindingeko zesizwe seZimbabwe;

(c)

ukuphilisana ngokuthula lamanye amazwe; kanye

(b) ukuhlonitshwa komthetho wamazwe omhlaba;

(d) lokulungisisa ingxabano zamazwe omhlaba ngezindlela ezilokuthula.

(2) Isizwe kumele siphakamise ukuhlangana lokuxubana kwezamasiko, kwezezimali lakwezombusazwe okwakulelilizwe kanye lalokho okusekela iAfrica. 13

Ingqubelaphambili yelizwe

(1) Ilizwe lazo zonke izingatsha zikahulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele lizame ukuphathisa ukuthi kube lentuthuko ngokuphangisa njalo elungileyo, njalo khona kanye sibili kumele lithathe amanyathela oku—

(a) sekela ukuzenzela lokuzimela;

(b) ukukhulisa intuthuko kwezokulima lokufuya,kwezokuthengiselana, kwezemisebenzi,kwezolwazi lwezemitshina kanye lesayensi;

(c)

Ukukhulisa intuthuko yezinqgubo kwezemisebenzi lokuthengiselana ngenjongo yokupha amandla kuzizalwane zeZimbabwe; kanye

(d) lokuletha intuthuko elokulingana yezindawo ezitshiyeneyo zeZimbabwe, khona kanye sibili ukulingana okufaneleyo ekuthuthukisweni kwezindawo zemaphandleni lezemadolobheni.

(2) Amanyathela okukhulunywa ngawo kulesisigaba kumele abelokuphatheka kwabantu abayibo abathinthekayo ekubunjweni lasekufakweni kwamacebo entuthuko lezinhlelo ezibantithayo bona.

(3) Amanyathela okukhulunywa ngawo kulesisigaba kumele avikele njalo akhuphule ilungelo labantu, ikakhulu abesifazana, ekutholeni amathuba alinganayo entuthukweni.

(4) Isizwe kumele sibeleqiniso lokuthi izakhamuzi zakuleyondawo ziyathola inzuzo evela ezimpahleni ezitholakala endaweni yazo. 14

Ukuphiwa amandla lokudalwa kwemisebenzi

(1) Isizwe lezingatsha zikahulumende zonke labameli bakahulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele bazame ukuphathisa lokuthatha amanyathela okupha amandla,

ngezindlela zokuvumelana ezifaneleyo, ezimgceke, ezingelakubandlulula ezilungileyo bonke abantu ababandluliliweyo, amaqembu kanye labantu bezindawo zeZimbabwe.

(2) Ngazo zonke izikhathi Isizwe kanye lezingatsha zikahulumende zonke labameli bakahulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele babone ukuthi amanyathelo afaneleyo njalo eyeneleyo ayenziwa ukuze kudalwe imisebenzi, yabantu bonke beZimbabwe, ikakhulu abesifazana labantu abatsha. 15

Ukutholakala kokudla Isizwe kumele—

(a)

sikhuthaze abantu ukuthi balime njalo bagcine ukudla okweneleyo;

(c)

Sikhuthaze kanye lokuphakamisa ukudla okwakha umzimba okwaneleyo njalo okufaneleyo ngokufundisa uzulu kanye langezinye izindlela ezifaneleyo.

(b) sidinge ukumiswa kwezindawo ezaneleyo zokugcinela ukudla; njalo


Amasiko

(1) Isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele siphakamise njalo silondoloze amasiko lemikhuba ekhuphula isizotha, Ubuntu kanye lokulingana kwabantu beZimbabwe.

(2) Isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye, lezizalwane zonke zeZimbabwe, kumele sizame ukulondoloza lokuvikela ilifa leZimbabwe.

(3) Isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye, kumele bathathe amanyathelo okubona ukuthi isithunzi sezingatsha zomdabu siphiwa inhlonipho esifaneleyo. 17

Ukulingana kobulili

(1) Isizwe kumele Sisekele ukulingana okugcweleyo kobulili emphakathini weZimbabwe, njalo khona kanye sibili—

(a)

isizwe kumele Sisekele ukuphatheka kwabesifazana ngokugcweleyo kuzo zonke izigaba zomphakathi weZimbabwe kugxile ekulinganeni labesilisa;

(b) isizwe kumele sithathe wonke amanyathelo, kugoqela awokwenza imithetho, adingakala ekuboneni ukuthi—

(i) bonke ubulili bumelwe ngokulinganayo kuzo zonke izingatsha lakubameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye; njalo

(ii) abesifazana bayisilinganiso esingaba yingxenye yamalunga ayo yonke imisebenzi leminye evotelwayo lekhethelwayo eyezinhlanganiso zikaHulumende emisiweyo loba engaphansi kweSisekamthetho lesi loba uhlamvu lwePhalamende; njalo

(c)

isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye, kumele sithathe amanyathela asebenzayo okubona ukuthi abesifazana finyelelabayafinyelela izimpahla, ezigoqela umhlabathi, kugxile ekulinganeni labesilisa.

(2) Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathela alusizo okuqondisa ubandlululo ngobulili kanye lokungalingani okwadalwa yimikhuba lezimiso zendulo.

2013

Ukumelwa okulinganayo ezindaweni zelizwe

(1) Isizwe kumele siphakamise ukumelwa okufaneleyo kwezindawo zonke zelizwe leZimbabwe kuzingatsha zonke lakubameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye.

(2) Isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye, kumele sithathe amanyathela asebenzayo okubona ukuthi zonke izindawo ezihlala abantu zifinyelela ngokufaneleyo izimpahla zokuphakamisa intuthuko yazo. 19

Abantwana

(1) Isizwe kumele sisebenzise izimiso lamanyathela okubona ukuthi ezindabeni eziphathelane labantwana, izinto ezisiza bona abantwana zibekwa phambili.

(2) Isizwe kumele sisebenzise izimiso lamanyathela alomqondo, angeneliswa zimpahla esilazo, ekuboneni ukuthi abantwana—

(a)

bayathola ukunakakelwa yimuli loba ngabazali, loba ukunakakelwa okufaneleyo nxa besuswe lapho okuhlala khona imuli;

(b) babelendawo yokuhlala kanye lokudla okwakha umzimba,ukunakakelwa kwempilakahle, kanye lokutholiswa okufaneleyo kwezenhlalo;

(c)

bavikelekile ekuphathweni kubi, ekunganakakelweni loba kwezinye izindlela zobudlwangudlwangu; njalo

(d) bafinyelele imfundo efaneleyo lokuqeqetshiswa.

(3) Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathelo afaneleyo wokwenza imithetho lamanye amanyathelo—

(a)

okuvikela abantwana emikhubeni yemisebenzi ehlukumezayo; njalo

(b) okubona ukuthi abantwana kakudingakali loba abavunyelwa ukuthi basebenze loba benze imisebenzi—

(i) engafanelanga iminyaka yobudala babantwana; loba

(ii) efaka impilo yabantwana engozini, ekuthuthukeni emfundweni, emzimbeni loba kumpilakahle yengqondo loba ezomoya, ekuziphatheni loba kwezenhlalo. 20

Abatsha

(1) Isizwe lezingatsha zonke zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele sithathe amanyathelo alomgqondo kugoqela izinhlelo zokuvumelana, ukuze kubonakale ukuthi abatsha, kusitsho abantu abaleminyaka yokuzalwa ephakathi kwetshumi lanhlanu kusiya kumatshumi amathathu lanhlanu—

(a)

bafinyelele imfundo lokuqeqetshiswa okufaneleyo;

(c)

batholiswe amathuba okuqhatshwa lezinye izindlela zokuthola amandla kwezezimali;

(b) babelamathuba okuhlanganyela lokumelwa kanye lokuphatheka kwezombusazwe, kwezenhlalo, kwezezezimali lakwezinye inhlangothi zempilo;

(d) bathole amathuba okwenza izinto zokuzilibazisa njalo bafinyelele izinto ezidingakalayo ekuzilibaziseni; njalo

(e)

bavikelwe emikhubeni yesintu elimazayo, ekuhlukuluzweni lakuyo yonke imihlobo yobudlwangudlwangu.

(2) Isicaphuno sePhalamende singaba lohlelo lwesizwe lwabatsha olulodwa loba izinhlelo ezidlula lapho.

(3) Amanyathelo lezinhlelo eziqanjwe kusigatshana (1) laku (2) kumele kube ngokupheleleyo okungekho cele linye njalo kube ngokwelizwe lonke. 21

Abantu asebekhulile

(1) Isizwe lezingatsha zonke labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele sithathe amanyathelo alomqondo, kugoqela amanyathelo awokwenziwa kwemithetho, ukuze kutholiswe inhlonipho, usekelo lokuvikelwa kwabantu asebekhulile kanye lokubenza banelise ukuphatheka ezimpilweni zemiphakathi abahlala kuyo.

(2) Isizwe lezingatsha zonke labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele sizame ngokusemandleni ezimpahla esilazo—

(a)

ukukhuthaza abantu asebekhulile ukuthi baphatheke ngokupheleleyo ezindabeni zomphakathi;

(b) ukupha izinto zokusebenzisa, ukudla kanye lokunakakelwa kwezenhlalo kwabantu asebekhulile abaswelayo;

(c)

ukuthuthukisa izinhlelo zokupha abantu asebekhulile ithuba lokuphatheka emisebenzini ehambelana lamandla abo njalo eyeme ekweneliseni kwabo kanye lasezifisweni zabo;

(d) ukukhulisa izinhlanganiso zezenhlalo ezijonge ukuphakamisa uhlonzi lwempilo yabantu asebekhulile. 22

Abantu abakhubazekileyo

(1) Isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele siqakathekise amalungelo abantu abagogekileyo ngokomzimba loba ngokwengqondo, ikakhulu ilungelo labo lokuphathwa ngenhlonipho langesizotha.

(2) Isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele, ngokusemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, sisize abantu abagogekileyo ngokomzimba loba ngokwengqondo ukuthi banelise ukwenza abakwenelisayo ngokugcweleyo kanye lokwehlisa izinto ezibahluphayo.

(3) Ikakhulu, isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele—

(a)

sithuthukise izinhlelo zenhlalakahle yabantu abagogekileyo ngokomzimba langokwengqondo, ikakhulu izinhlelo zemisebenzi ezihambelana lokwenelisa kwabo levumelekileyo kubo loba kubameli babo ababamela emthwethweni;

(b) sinanze zona ngokwazo izindingeko zabantu abalokugogeka kwemihlobo yonke njengeyinye yezinto ezibekwa phambili emacebweni entuthuko;

(c)

sikhuthaze ukusetshenziswa lokuthuthukiswa kwezindlela zokukhulumisana ezifanele abantu abalokugogeka ngokomzimba langokwengqondo; njalo

(d) sikhulise izinhlanganiso zenhlalo ezilenjongo yokukhuphula uhlonzi lwempilo yabantu abalokugogeka kwemihlobo yonke.

(4) Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathela afanelayo okubona ukuthi izakhiwo lezinto ezisizayo ezifinyelelwa nguzulu ziyafinyelelwa njalo ngabantu abalokugogeka.

2013

Abalweli bempi yenkululeko

(1) Isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele baphe inhlonipho efaneleyo, kanye lokunanzwa kubalweli bempi yenkululeko, okutsho ukuthi—

(a)

labo abalwa empini yenkululeko;

(c)

labo ababevalelwe emajele, lababevalelwe loba bevinjelwe ngezizatho zezombusazwe ngesikhathi sempi yenkululeko.

(b) labo abancedisa abalweli empini yenkululeko; njalo

(2) Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathela alomqondo, kugoqela amanyathela owokwenza imithetho, yenhlalakahle lokuphiwa amandla kwezezimali kubalweli bempi yenkululeko. 24

Imisebenzi kanye lokusebenzelana

(1) Isizwe lezingatsha zonke zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele sisebenzise izimiso ezilomqondo kanye lamanyathela, asemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, ukupha abantu bonke ithuba lokuthi basebenze umsebenzi abazikhethele wona ngokukhululeka, ukuze bathole impilo ecolekileyo bona kanye lezimuli zabo.

(2) Isizwe lezingatsha zonke zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele sizame ukutholisa—

(a) ukuqhatshwa okupheleleyo;

(b) ukususwa kwemigoqo evimbelayo ngokungadingakaliyo loba evalela abantu ukuthi basebebenze kanye lokuthi bangene emisebenzini elenzuzo yemali;

(c) ukuqondiswa kwezemisebenzi kanye lokuthuthukiswa wezinhlelo zemisebenzi lezokuqeqetshisela imisebenzi, kugoqela leyabantu abagogekileyo;

(d) ukusetshenziswa kwamanyathela anjengokunakakelwa kwemuli awenza abesifazane bathakazeleli ithuba eliyilo lokusebenza. 25

Ukuvikelwa kwemuli

Isizwe lezingatsha zonke zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele sivekele njalo sikhulise ugatsha lwemuli ikakhulu kumele sizame, ngamandla ezimpahla esilazo, ukusebenzisa amanyathela—

(a)

okupha ukunakela losekelo kubomama, obaba lamanye amalunga emuli abondla abantwana; njalo

(b) okuvikela ubudlwangudlwangu bangemakhaya. 26

Umtshado

Isizwe kumele sithathe wonke amanyathela afaneleyo okubona ukuthi—

(a)

akulakuthathana okwenzakalayo ngaphandle kwemvumo eyenziwe ngokukhululeka langemvumo egcweleyo yalabo abazakuthathana;

(b) kulokulingana kwamalungelo lemilandu yabathathanayo ngesikhathi bethethene kanye lasekwekhukaneni; njalo

(c)

ekwehlukaneni kwabathetheneyo, loba ngokufa loba ngedivosi, kulezimiso ezibekwayo ezokuvikelwa okudingakalayo kwengqe nguwuphi umntwana loba kulabo abehlukeneyo.

Imfundo

(1) Isizwe kumele sithathe wonke amanyathela asebenzayo okuphakamisa—

(a)

imfundo emqoka yamahala eyindingeko engavikekiyo ebantwaneni; kanye le

(b) lemfundo yangaphezulu.

(2) Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathela okubona ukuthi amankazana aphiwa amathuba afana lawabafana okuthola imfundo ezigabeni zonke. 28

Indawo yokuhlala


Ezempilakahle

Isizwe lazo zonke izingatsha labameli bakaHulumende kusigaba sinye ngasinye kumele sithathe amanyathelo awokwenziwa komthetho alomqondo lamanye amanyathelo, asemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, ukwenza wonke umuntu afinyelele indawo yokuhlala efaneleyo.

(1) Isizwe kumele sithathe wonke amanyathelo asebenzayo awokuqiniseka ngokuphiwa kwezindingeko ezimqoka ezifinyelelekayo ezaneleyo zempilakhle iZimbabwe yonke jikelele.

(2) Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathelo afaneleyo, angathathi cele njalo alomqondo okuqiniseka ukuthi akulamuntu oyalelwa ukwelatshwa okudinga isiphangiphangi ingqe kuluphi ugatsha lwezempilakahle.

(3) Isizwe kumele sithathe wonke amanyathela okuvikela asemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, kugoqela imfundiso kanye lezinhlelo zokupha uzulu ulwazi, mayelana lokulwisana lokumemetheka komkhuhlane. 30

Inhlalakahle kazulu


Usizo kwezemithetho


Imidlalo lezindawo zokuzilibazisa


Ukulondolozwa kolwazi lwesintu

Isizwe kumele sithathe wonke amanyathelo asebenzayo, asemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, okupha ukuvikelwa kwezenhlalo, kanye lokunakakelwa kwezenhlalo kulabo abaswelayo.

Isizwe kumele sithathe wonke amanyathela asebenzayo, asemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, ukutholiswa ukumelwa emthethweni emacaleni aphathelane lozulu kanye lawokwephula imithetho kulabo abakudingayo njalo abangenelisiyo ukuthola abameli bomthethweni abazikhethele bona.

Isizwe kumele sithathe wonke amanyathelo asebenzayo okukhuthaza imisebenzi yemidlalo lokuzilibazisa, kugoqela ukutholiswa kwezindawo zemidlalo lezokulibazisa zabantu bonke.

Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathelo awokulondoloza, lokuvikela kanye lokuphakamisa ulwazi lomdabu, kugoqela ulwazi lwezemithi yokwelapha kanye lezinye izinto zempilo yezinyamazana lezihlahla olusezakhamizini lasebantwini.

2013

Ukunanzwa kwemithetho yomhlaba wonke jikelele

Isizwe kumele sibone ukuthi zonke izibopho lezivumelwano zamazwe omhlaba lapho iZimbabwe elilunga khona kumele zihlanganiselwe emthethweni wakuleli ilizwe. ISAHLUKO 3 Ubuzalwane 35 Ukuba yisizalwane seZimbabwe

(1) Umuntu uba yisizalwane seZimbabwe ngokuzalwa, usendo kumbe ukubhalisa.

(2) Zonke izizalwane zeZimbabwe zinikwa amalungelo, izimfanelo kanye lezinkoliso ezifanayo zobuzalwane, njalo ngokufanayo zibotshelwa ukwenza lokuthatha imilandu ekhangelelwe zonke izizalwane.

(3) Zonke izizalwane zeZimbabwe zinikwa amalungelo lezinkoliso ezilandelayo, phezu kwezinye ezizitholiswa ngumthetho—

(a)

ukuvikelwa yiSizwe konke lapho ezikhona;

(c)

ukutholiswa izincwadi zokuzalwa lezinye izithupha ezitholiswa yiSizwe.

(a)

ukwethembeka elizweni labo iZimbabwe;

(c)

ukuhlonipha ifulegi yelizwe leNgoma yesizwe; njalo

(b) ukutholiswa amapasipoti lezinye izincwadi zokuhambisa; njalo

(4) Izizalwane zeZimbabwe zilemilandu elandelayo, phezu kweminye eziyetheswa yiSizwe—

(b) ukunanza iSisekamthetho lokuhlonipha lokho esikumelayo lesikubekileyo;

(d) ngawo wonke amandla azo, ukuvikela iZimbabwe lozibuse wayo. 36

Ukuba yisizalwane ngokuzalwa

(1) Umuntu uba yisizalwane seZimbabwe nxa ezalelwe eZimbabwe njalo, ekuzalweni kwakhe—

(a)

unina kumbe uyise wayeyisizalwane seZimbabwe; kumbe

(b) ulogogo kumbe ukhulu wakhe owayeyisizalwane seZimbabwe ngokuzalwa kumbe ngosendo.

(2) Umuntu ozalelwe phandle kweZimbabwe uba yisizalwane seZimbabwe ngokuzalwa nxa, ekuzalweni kwakhe, unina kumbe uyise wayeyisizalwane seZimbabwe njalo—

(a)

evele engohlala eZimbabwe; kumbe

(b) esebenzela phetsheya kweZimbabwe ngenxa yomsebenzi weSizwe kumbe owenhlanganiso elezingatsha ezigcwele umhlaba.

(3) Umntwana otholakale eZimbabwe eleminyaka, kumbe okhanya eleminyaka engaphansi kwetshumi lanhlanu (15) yokuzalwa, njalo engazakali ukuthi ngowaliphi ilizwe labazali bakhe bengazakali, uthathwa njengesizalwane seZimbabwe ngokuzalwa. 37

Ukuba yisizalwane ngosendo

Ngaphansi kweSigaba 36(2), umuntu ozalelwe phandle kweZimbabwe uba yisizalwane seZimbabwe ngosendo nxa, ekuzalweni kwakhe—

(a)

unina kumbe uyise loba ugogo kumbe ukhulu wakhe wayeyisizalwane seZimbabwe ngokuzalwa kumbe ngosendo; kumbe

(b) unina kumbe uyise wakhe wayeyisizalwane seZimbabwe ngokubhalisa; njalo ukuzalwa kwakhe kubhaliswe eZimbabwe ngemfanelo elandela umthetho wokubhaliswa kwabantwana abazelweyo. 38

Ukuba yisizalwane ngokubhalisa

(1) Wonke umuntu oseleminyaka emihlanu kusiya phambili etshade lesizalwane seZimbabwe, kungelani ukuthi usuku lokwethulwa kweSisekamthetho selufikile kumbe lusazofika, njalo osuthisa izimiso ezibekwe nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, unikwa ilungelo, nxa efake isicelo esibhaliweyo, esokuthi abhaliswe njengesizalwane seZimbabwe.

(2) Umuntu wonke osehlale kweleZimbabwe okwesikhathi eside njalo okusemthethweni okweminyaka efika elitshumi (10) kumbe ukwedlula, kungelani ukuthi usuku lokwethulwa kweSisekamthetho selufikile kumbe lusazofika, njalo osuthisa izimiso ezibekwe nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, unikwa ilungelo, nxa efake isicelo esibhaliweyo, esokuthi abhaliswe njengesizalwane seZimbabwe.

(3) Umntwana ongayisisosizalwane seZimbabwe, kodwa owatholwa ngokusemthethweni njengomntwana ngumuntu oyisizalwane seZimbabwe, kungelani ukuthi usuku lokwethulwa kweSisekamthetho selufikile kumbe lusazofika, njalo osuthisa izimiso ezibekwe nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, unikwa ilungelo, nxa efake isicelo esibhaliweyo, esokuthi abhaliswe njengesizalwane seZimbabwe. 39

Ukwemukwa ubuzalwane

(1) Ukuba yisizalwane seZimbabwe ngokubhalisa kungemukwa nxa—

(a)

lowo muntu wathola lobo buzalwane ngobuqili, ukuqamba amanga kumbe ukufihla iqiniso elithile; kumbe

(b) esikhathini sempi iZimbabwe eyayiphatheke kuyo, lowomuntu wathengiselana lesitha kumbe wasinika ulwazi okungekho emthethweni, kumbe wayesebenzelana lesitha kumbe edlelana laso ngabomo ngendlela eyayizasiza isitha kuleyompi.

(2) Ukuba yisizalwane seZimbabwe ngokuzalwa kungemukwa nxa—

(a)

lobo buzalwane batholakala ngobuqili, ukuqamba amanga kumbe ukufihla iqiniso elithile; kumbe

(b) emuntwini oqanjwe kuSigaba 36(3), sekwazakale ukuthi lowo muntu ngowakuliphi ilizwe kumbe uzalwa ngubani, kube sekuvezakala ukuba lowomuntu uyisizalwane sakwelinye ilizwe.

(3) Ubuzalwane beZimbabwe akumelanga bemukwe ngaphansi kwalesi Sigaba nxa ukwenza lokho kuzabangela ukuthi lowomuntu asale engela lizwe ayisizalwane salo. 40

Ukugcinwa kobuzalwane lanxa umuntu esetshadile kumbe esequme umtshado

Ubuzalwane beZimbabwe abumukwa yikuthathana kumbe ukuchitheka komtshado.

2013

IBhodi ebona ngezobuZalwane kanye lezokuThuthela elizweni

UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele wanelise ukuthi kubunjwe iBhodi ebona ngezobuzalwane kanye lezokuthuthela elizweni emalunga ayo agoqela uMgcinisihlalo lamanye amabili aqhatshwa nguMongameli, umlandu wabo ungowoku—

(a)

ukunika kumbe ukwemuka ubuzalwane ngokubhalisa;

(c)

ukuqhuba eminye imisebenzi abayilawulelwa kumbe abayibotshelwa ukuthi bayenze njengeBhodi nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(b) ukunika abantu abangasizizalwane imvumo yokuhlala lokusebenza eZimbabwe, lokubanika izimiso leziqondiso okumele bazilandele ekuhlaleni lekusebenzini kwabo;


Amandla edale lePhalamende mayelana lezobuZalwane

UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, kukhangelwe izibopho zalesiSigaba, ungaba lakho ukubeka okusemthethweni—

(a)

izindlela umuntu angathola ngazo ubuzalwane beZimbabwe ngokubhalisa;

(c)

izindlela okumele zilandelwe nxa umuntu esemukwa ubuzalwane bakhe beZimbabwe abuthola ngokubhalisa;

(b) ukulandula ubuzalwane beZimbabwe ngokuzithandela;

(d) ukuvuselelwa kobuzalwane beZimbabwe;

(e)

(f) 43

ukwaliswa kobuzalwane bamazwe amabili ebantwini abathola ubuzalwane ngosendo kumbe ngokubhalisa; njalo ukubona nje ukuthi Isahluko lesi siyalandelwa ngemfanelo.

Ukuqhubeka lokuvuselelwa kobuZalwane osebake babakhona

(1) Wonke umuntu obekade eyisizalwane seZimbabwe ngesikhathi esandulela nje ukwethulwa kwalesi Sisekamthetho, uqhubeka eyisizalwane ngemva kwalolosuku.

(2) Wonke umuntu owazalelwa eZimbabwe iSisekamthetho lesi singakethulwa uyisizalwane seZimbabwe ngokuzalwa nxa—

(a)

umzali wakhe oyedwa kumbe bobabili bebeyizizalwane zelizwe elibe lilunga leNhlanganiso yaMazwe eZansi yeAfrika (iSADC) eyabunjwa eWindhoek, isigodlo selizwe leNamibia mhlaka 17 Ncwabakazi 1992; njalo

(b) wayevele ezihlalela eZimbabwe mhla iSisekamthetho sisethulwayo. ISAHLUKO 4 Umqulu Wamalungelo ISAHLUKWANA 1 Ukusetshenziswa Lokuhumutshwa Kukasahluko 4 44

Umlandu wokuhlonipha amalungelo ayinsika lezinkululeko.

Isizwe laye wonke umuntu, kugoqela izinhlanganiso ezithathwa ngumthetho njengabantu, kanye lazo zonke izingatsha zikaHulumende labameli bakaHulumende kuzo zonke izigaba kufuze bahloniphe, bavikele, baphakamise njalo baphelelise amalungelo lezinkululeko eziphawulwe eSahlukweni lesi.

Ukusetshenziswa kukaSahluko 4

(1) ISahluko lesi siphoqa Isizwe leziphathamandla, izitshayamthetho zikaHulumende, lezingatsha zikaHulumende ezigabeni zonke.

(2) ISahluko lesi sibopha abantu bemvelo lezinhlanganiso ezithathwa ngumthetho njengabantu kukhangelwa isimo selungelo kumbe inkululeko esegudwini lomlandu ophawulwa yileyo nkululeko.

(3) Izinhlanganiso ezithathwa ngumthetho njengabantu kanye labantu bemvelo balelungelo emalungelweni lasezinkululekweni eziphawulwe yiSahluko lesi okungangokuthi amalungelo lezinkululeko lezi zingasetshenziswa kubo. 46

Ukuchasiswa kukaSahluko 4

(1) Nxa kuhumutshwa iSahluko lesi, umthethwandaba, ikomithi yabalamuli, inkundla kumbe inhlangano(a)

kufuze inanze ngokupheleleyo amalungelo lezinkululeko eziqukethwe yiSahluko lesi;

(b) kufuze iphakamise imikhuba lezinkolo ezisekela uzulu okhululekileyo olobumgceke, ukwahlulela okulungileyo, isithunzi sobuntu, ukulingana lenkululeko, ikakhulu imikhuba lezinkolo eziphawulwe kusigaba 3;

(c)

(d)

(e)

kufuze inanze umthetho wamazwe wonke lazo zonke izivumelwano lapho iZimbabwe elilunga khona;

kufuze inanze zonke izimiso zeSisekamthetho, ikakhulu imikhuba lezinhloso eziphawulwe kuSahluko 2; njalo ingachwayisisa imithetho yamanye amazwe ehambelana lamalungelo aqukethwe kulesiSahluko;

phezu kokunanzelela ezinye izinto ezihambelana lalokhu ezingasetshenziswa nxa sihumutsha iSisekamthetho.

(2) Nxa kuchasiswa umthetho osuphelelisiwe, lanxa kuthuthukiswa imithetho yemvelo, yonke imithethwandaba, ikomithi yabalamuli, inkundla kumbe inhlangano kufuze iphakamise njalo ziqondiswe ngumqondo kanye lezinhloso ezikuSahluko lesi. 47

Isahluko 4 kasenqabi ubukhona bamanye amalungelo

ISahluko lesi kasenqabi ubukhona bamanye amalungelo lezinye izinkululeko ezingananzelelwa kumbe zivunywe ngumthetho, okungangokuthi ziyavumelana leSisekamthetho. ISAHLUKWANA 2 Amalungelo Ayinsika Lezinkululeko 48

ILungelo lokuPhila

(1) Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lokuphila.

(2) Umthetho ungavumela ukuba isigwebo sentambo setheswe kuphela umuntu ofunyenwe elecala lokubulala ngochuku njalo ekuhlelile lokhu, futhi—

(a)

umthetho kufuze uvumele umthethwandaba ukuthi uzihluzele wodwa ukuba isigwebo sentambo singetheswa loba hatshi;

(b) isigwebo sentambo singaqhutshwa kuphela kulandelwa ukwahlulela komthethwandaba olamakhono;

(c)

2013

isigwebo sentambo akufuzanga setheswe umuntu—

(i) obeleminyaka engaphansi kwengamatshumi amabili lanye yokuzalwa ngesikhathi esenza icala; kumbe

(ii) ongaphezu kweminyaka engamatshumi ayisikhombisa yokuzalwa;

(d) isigwebo sentambo akufuzanga setheswe abesifazana; njalo

(e)

umuntu ogwetshwayo kufuze abe lelungelo lokucela ukuba uMongameli amzwele kumbe axolelwe engatholi isigwebo sentambo.

(3) Umthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ufuze uvikele izimpilo zabantwana abangakazalwa, njalo lowo mthetho ufuze uvumele ukuba isisu singakhitshwa kuphela kulandelwa umthetho omisiweyo. 49

Ilungelo lokukhululeka komuntu

(1) Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lokukhululeka komuntu siqu sakhe, eligoqela ilungelo—

(a)

lokungavalelwa ejele engathonisiswanga; njalo

(b) lokungemukwa ilungelo mahlayana kumbe kungelasizatho esilomsoco.

(2) Akulamuntu ongavalelwa ejele ngenxa yokuthi akenelisanga ukugcwalisa imilandu yesivumelwano. 50

Amalungelo abantu ababotshiweyo labavalelweyo

(1) Ingqe ngubani obotshiweyo—

(a)

Kufuze aziswe icala alibotshelwayo ngesikhathi ebotshwa;

(c)

kufuze aphathwe ngendlela elobuntu lenhlonipho ngenxa yesithunzi sakhe sokubangumuntu;

(b) kufuze avunyelwe, ngokukhawuleza—

(i) esebenzisa izindleko zeSizwe, ukwazisa umkakhe kumbe omunye wakhe, kumbe isihlobo kumbe ummeli kwezomthetho, kumbe ingqe ngubani azikhethela yena; kumbe

(ii) esebenzisa izindleko zakhe, ukuxoxisana nsitha lomeli wezomthetho kanye lodokotela azikhethela yena; njalo kufuze aziswe ngelungelo leli ngokukhawuleza;

(d) kufuze akhululwe kungelakubekwa kwemigoqo ethize kumbe kubekwe imigoqo ezwisisekayo, kusalindelwe ukubekwa icala kumbe ukuthonisiswa, ngaphandle nxa kulezizatho ezimqoka ezisekela ukuthi aqhubeke evalelwe; njalo

(e)

ufuze avunyelwe yena uqobo lwakhe ukulwisa ukubotshwa okungaphandle komthetho phambi komthethwandaba njalo ufuze akhitshwe ngokukhawuleza nxa ebotshwe ngokungaphandle komthetho.

(2) Ingqe ngubani obotshiweyo kumbe ovalelweyo—

(a)

ebotshelwa kumbe evalelelwe ukulethwa phambi komthethwandaba; kumbe

(b) esetheswa icala;

njalo ongakakhutshwa ufuze amiswe phambi komthethwandaba ngokukhawuleza kungakedluli amahola angamatshumi amane lasitshiyagalombili ngemva kokubotshwa kumbe kokuvalelwa, sekusiya ngokuthi yikuphi, kungakhathalakile ukuthi isikhathi lesi siphela kungoMgqibelo, ngeNsonto kumbe kungekhefu.

(3) Ingqe ngubani ongamiswanga phambi komthethwandaba emaholeni angamatshumi amane lasitshiyagalombili abaliswe kusigatshana (2) kufuze akhutshwe ngokukhawuleza ngaphandle nxa kuyikuthi ukuvalelwa kwakhe kwengezwe ngumthethwandaba olamakhono.

(4) Ingqe ngubani obotshelwe kumbe ovalelelwe icala aletheswayo ulelungelo—

(a)

lokuqhubeka ethule;

(c)

ukungabanjwa ngamandla ukuvuma lokwamukela icala; njalo

(b) lokwaziswa ngokukhawulezwa—

(i) ngelungelo lokuqhubeka ethule kanye;

(ii) lemiphumela yokuqhubeka ethule leyokungaqhubeki ethule;

(d) ekumisweni kokuqala phambi komthethwandaba, ulelungelo lokwetheswa icala kumbe elokwazisa ngesizatho sokuqhubeka evalelwe, kumbe elokukhutshwa.

(5) Ingqe ngubani ovalelweyo, kanye lesibotshwa esesigwetshiwe, ulelungelo—

(a)

lokwaziswa ngokukhawuleza isizatho sokuvalelwa kwakhe;

(c)

ukukhulumisana lo, kumbe ukwethekelelwa ngu—

(i) mkakhe kumbe omunye wakhe;

(ii) isihlobo;

(iii) umcebisi wezenkolo azikhethela yena;

(iv) umeli wezomthetho azikhethela yena;

(v) udokotela azikhethela yena; njalo

(vi) kusiya ngemigoqo ebekwa ngabaphatha amajele lezindawo lapho okuvalelwa khona ababotshiweyo, ukwethekelelwa yingqe ngubani azikhethela yena;

(b) esebenzisa izindleko zakhe, ukuxoxisana nsitha lomeli wezomthetho azikhethela yena, njalo lokwaziswa ngelungelo leli ngokuphangisa;

(d) lokuvalelwa emumeni ohambelana lesizotha somuntu, okugoqela ithuba lokwenelisa ukwelula umzimba lokutholiswa, ngendleko zeLizwe, indawo yokuhlala eyaneleyo, izambuzi, ukuhlanzeka komuntu uqobo lwakhe, ukudla okwakha umzimba, imibhalo efaneleyo yokubala kanye lokwelatshwa; njalo

(e)

elokuphikisana lokuvalelwa okungaphandle komthetho phambi komthetho njalo, nxa ukuvalelwa kungekho emthethweni, ufuze akhululwe ngokukhawuleza.

(6) Umuntu wonke ovalelweyo esalinde ukuthonisiswa kwecala aletheswayo njalo engathonisiswanga ngesikhathi esinqunyiweyo ufuze akhutshwe, kungelamigoqo kumbe kube lezimo ezibambekayo ukuze ngemva kokukhutshwa—

(a)

enelise ukubakhona kuthethwa icala lakhe;

(c)

angaphindi enze elinye njalo icala engakathonisiselwa leli alibotshelweyo.

(b) angaphazamisi ubufakazi obuzalethwa ekuthonisisweni kwecala; njalo

(7) Nxa kulezizatho ezibambekayo ezokukholwa ukuthi umuntu uvalelwe ngokungekho emthethweni kumbe nxa kungazakali ukuthi umuntu uvalelwe ngaphi, ingqe ngubani ulakho ukuyadinga isilawulo eMthethwandaba Ophezulu—

(a)

ese-habeascorpus, okutsho isilawulo esiphoqelela ukuba umuntu ovalelweyo akhutshwe, kumbe ukuba amiswe phambi komthethwandaba

ukuze kuhlolisiswe ukufanela kokuvalelwa kwakhe, kumbe esiphoqelela ukuvezwa kwesitokisi lapho avalelwe khona;

(b) ukunika isinqumo sokuthi ukuvalelwa kwesibotshwa akukho emthethweni lokuphoqa ukuthi isibotshwa sikhutshwe ngokukhawuleza; njalo uMthethwandaba Omkhulu ulakho ukunika isilawulo esifaneleyo kusiya ngesehlakalo esikhona.

(8) Ukubotshwa kumbe ukuvalelwa okuphambana lokumiswe kulesisigcawu, kumbe nxa izimiso ezibekwe kulesisigcawu zingananzwa, lokhu kubotshwa/kuvalelwa kuyabe kungekho emthethweni.

(9) Wonke umuntu obotshwe kumbe ovalelwe ngokungekho emthethweni ulelungelo lokufuna inhlawulo kulowo ombophileyo/ omvaleleyo, kodwa umthetho ulakho ukuvikela umenzi lo ekuthini engabi lomlandu ngaphansi kwesahluko lesi—

(a)

umsebenzi womthethwandaba umele umthethwandaba ngokufanela langokuthembeka;

(b) ingqe yisiphi isisebenzi sikaHulumende esimele uHulumende ngokufanela langokuthembeka kungelakungazi kumbe ukunganaki umsebenzi.


llungelo lokuba lesizotha somuntu


Ilungelo lokuvikeleka komuntu

Umuntu wonke ulesizotha semvelo empilweni yakhe yangensitha lasemphakathini, njalo ulelungelo lokuba ilungelo leli lihlonitshwe njalo livikelwe.

Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lobuqotho emzimbeni lasengqondweni, okugoqela ilungelo—

(a) lenkululeko yokungahlukunyeza nsitha lemphakathini;

(b) kulandelwa ezinye izimiso zeSisekamthetho lesi, lokwenza izinqumo mayelana lenzalo;

(c) lokuthi umzimba wakhe ungasetshenziswa ezifundweni zesayensi kumbe ezemithi, kumbe ukukhitshwa kumbe ukusetshenziswa izitho zomzimba wakhe kungelamvumo yakhe. 53

Inkululeko ekuhlukunyezweni, ekujezisweni, ukuphathweni ngochuku kumbe ngendlela eyehlisa isithunzi.

Akulamuntu ofuze ahlukunyezwe emzimbeni kumbe engqondweni, aphathwe ngochuku, aphathwe ngendlela engalasizotha kumbe ajeziswe. 54

Inkululeko ekugqilazweni kumbe ekwenziweni isichaka


Inkululeko ekubanjweni ngamandla ukuthi usebenze.


Ukulinganiswa lokungabandlululwa.

Akulamuntu ongenziwa isichaka kumbe isigqili.

Akulamuntu ongabanjwa ngamandla ukuba enze umsebenzi.

(1) Bonke abantu bayalingana phambi komthetho njalo balelungelo lokuvikelwa ngokulinganayo kanye lokusizwa ngumthetho.

(2) Abesifazana labesilisa balelungelo lokuphathwa ngokulingana, okugoqela ilungelo lokuba lamathuba alinganayo kwezombusazwe, ezomnotho, ezamasiko kanye lakwezenhlalo.

(3) Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lokungaphathwa ngendlela ebandlululayo kukhangelwa ubuzwe, umhlobo, umbala, ulimi, ukuba uzalelwe ngaphi, ukuba udabuka ngaphi kumbe uphuma kuwuphi umphakathi, izinga lomphakathi osuka kuwo, ukholo lwakho, ibandla lakhe lezombusazwe, umbono wakhe, isiko lakhe, ubulili bakhe, ukuthi uthethe/wendile kumbe hatshi, uzithwele, ukhubazekile, umi njani kozomnotho lemphakathini kumbe uzelwe ngabazali behlala bonke kumbe hatshi.

(4) Umuntu kuthiwa uphathwe ngobandlululo kukhangelwa okuphawulwe kusigatshana (3) nxa—

(a)

engaphansi kwesimo esithize, ukungenelisi kumbe ukukhubazeka okungekho kwabanye abantu; kumbe

(b) abanye betholiswe ilungelo kumbe usizo oluthize yena angalitholiswanga.

(5) Ubandlululo olweyeme ezintweni ezibaliswe kusigatshana (3) alulunganga ngaphandle nxa kuphawuliwe ukuthi ubandlululo lolo lulungile, lufanele njalo luvumakala emphakathini okhululekileyo ogxile egcekeni, ekulungeni, esithunzini sobuntu, ekulinganeni lasenkululekweni.

(6) Isizwe kufuze senze imithetho lamanye amanyathela ukuphakamisa ukufezwa kokulingana kanye lokuvikela kumbe ukuphakamisa abantu kumbe amaqembu abantu ababeninelwe khatshana ngobandlululo olungalunganga njalo—

(a)

amanyathela aloluhlobo afuze athathwe ukuze kulungisiswe izehlakalo lapho okulendingeko eqotho khona;

(b) akulanyathela laloluhlobo elizaphathwa sengathi alilunganga nxa kulandelwa isigatshana (3). 57

Ilungelo lokuba lemfihlo

Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lemfihlo, eligoqela ukungabi—

(a)

lomuzi wakhe, impahla kumbe indawo yakhe engenwa ngaphandle kwemvumo yakhe;

(b) ukusetshwa yena uqobo lwakhe, umuzi, indawo kumbe impahla yakhe;

(c)

ukungathathelwa ingcebo yakhe;

(e)

ukungavezwa kwesimo sakhe sempilakahle.

(d) ukungangenelwa kwemfihlo yakhe kuzindlela zokukhulumisana; kumbe


Inkululeko yokubuthana lokuhlanganyela

(1) Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lokuhlanganyela lokubuthana, kanye lelungelo lokuhlanganyela kumbe ukubuthana labanye.

(2) Akulamuntu ongabanjwa ngamandla ukuthi abe yingxenye yombuthano kumbe ukuthi angene emhlanganweni kumbe embuthanweni. 59

Inkululeko yokutshengisela lokunika izikhalazo


Inkululeko yomzwangedwa.

Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lokutshengisela lokunika izikhalazo, kodwa amalungelo la kufuze asetshenziswe ngokuthula.

(1) Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lokukhululeka kunembeza, eligoqela—

(a)

inkululeko yokucabanga, yombono, yokholo kumbe inkolo; njalo

(b) inkululeko yokwenza lokwandisa lokuveza imicabango, imibono, ukholo kumbe inkolo, kungakhathalakile ukuthi kusensitha kumbe hatshi, ukuthi uyedwa kumbe ulabanye abantu.

(2) Akulamuntu ongabanjwa ngamandla ukwenza isifungo esiphambene lokholo kumbe lenkolo yakhe kumbe ukwenza isifungo esiphambene lokholo kumbe lenkolo yakhe.

(3) Abazali labantu abagcina abantwana abancane balelungelo lokunquma, kusiya ngokholo lwabo, umthetho lenkolo yokukhulisa abantwana, nxa kuyikuthi izinqumo zabo azemuki amalungelo atholiswa abantwana yiSisekamthetho, okugoqela amalungelo okufunda, ezempilakahle, awokuvikeleka lawenhlalo.

(4) Ingqe yiwuphu umphakathi wezokholo ulakho ukumisa izakhiwo lezindawo lapho inkolo yawo engafundiswa khona, loba inhlangano ithola usizo lwezemali kuHulumende. 61

Inkululeko yokuveza imibono lenkululeko yemisakazo

(1) Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lokukhuluma akufisayo, okugoqela—

(a)

Inkululeko yokufuna, ukwamukela lokuhambisa imibono leminye imibiko;

(c)

Inkululeko yokufunda ingqe yini.

(b) Inkululeko yokuveza ubuciko ezinkulumeni/ekulobeni lekuchwayisiseni ngezesayensi kanye lobungcitshini bokubumba izinto; njalo

(2) Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lokukhululeka ezindleleni zokuhanjiswa kwemibiko, inkululeko le igoqela ukuvikelwa kwalapho intathelizindaba ezithaphe khona izindaba.

(3) Imisakazo kanye lemisakazo esakaza ebulenjini langamagagasi ilelungelo lokubunjwa, sekusiya ngamanyathela athathwa nguHulumende nxa esipha amacence okusakaza—

(a)

adingakalayo ekulawuleni amagagasi lezinye izindlela zokuhambisa imibiko; njalo

(b) imisakazo izimele yodwa ngoba ayilawulwa nguHulumende kumbe amabandla ezombusazwe kumbe yinhlangano ezikhangelele inzuzo thize.

(4) Zonke izindlela zokuhambisa imibiko ezilawulwa nguHulumende kufuze—

(a)

zikhululeke ukuzihlolela zodwa imibiko equkethweyo ezasakazwa;

(c)

ziphe ithuba ukuba imibono lemicabango etshiyeneyo isakazwe.

(b) zingakhethi hlangothi; njalo

(5) Inkululeko yokuveza imibono lenkululeko yemisakazo ayigoqeli—

(a)

ukuphendla ubudlwangudlwangu;

(c)

ukulinyazwa kwesimilo lesithunzi somuntu; kumbe

(b) ukusekela inzondo kumbe inkulumo ekhulisa inzondo;

(d) ukungenela imfihlo yomuntu ngolunya kungela sizatho.


Ukufinyelela imibiko

(1) Umuntu wonke ohlala kumbe oyisizalwane saseZimbabwe, kugoqelwa inhlanganiso eziphathisa samuntu kunye lemisakazo yeZimbabwe, kulelungelo lokufinyelela imibiko ephethwe yiLizwe kumbe lugatsha kumbe ummeli kaHulumende kuwo wonke amazinga, lapho okudingakala khona ukuthi uHulumende abe lomlandu emphakathini.

(2) Umuntu wonke, kugoqela imisakazo yeZimbabwe, ulelungelo lokufinyelela ingqe yiyiphi imibiko ephethwe ngumuntu, kugoqela uHulumende, nxa imibiko idingakala ekuqhubeni kumbe ekuvikeleni ilungelo.

(3) Umuntu wonke ulelungelo lokuqondisa imibiko, kumbe ukucitsha imibiko engasiqiniso, amanga kumbe imibiko eduhisayo, ephethwe nguHulumende kumbe yiyiphi inhlanganiso kumbe abamele uHulumende iloba kuliphi izinga, engaphathelana lalowo muntu.

(4) Umthetho kumele ubekwe ukuze kube lempumela yalelilungelo, kodwa lokhu kungavimbela ukufinyelelwa kwemibiko ngenxa yokunqinekela umvikela, ukugcinakala kuhle kukazulu loba ukugcinwa kwemfihlo, ngokwesibanga sokuthi ukuvinjelwa lokho kuqondile, kufanele, kuyadingakala njalo kuvumelekile emphakathini okhululekileyo olokulunga isithunzi somuntu, ukulingana, kanye lenkululeko. 63

Ulimi lamasiko

Wonke umuntu ulelungelo—

(a)

lokusebenzisa ulimi azikhethele lona;

(b) ukuphatheka empilweni yamasiko azikhethele wona:

kodwa akulamuntu olandela amalungelo lawa ongenzanjalo ngendlela engavumelani lalesiSahluko. 64

Inkululeko emisebenzini efundelwayo, edinga amakhono leqhatshelwayo

Wonke umuntu ulelungelo lokukhetha kanye lokwenza yiloba yiwuphi umsebenzi ofundelwayo, odinga amakhono loqhatshelwayo. 65

Amalungelo ezemisebenzi

(1) Wonke umuntu ulelungelo lezingqubo lemisebenzi engelangozi efaneleyo njalo ezingelangozi.

(2) Ngaphandle kwamalunga ezombutho lomvikela, wonke umuntu ulelungelo lokubumba kanye lokuhlanganyela kuzinhlanganiso zemisebenzi kanye lezinhlanganiso zezisebenzi loba ezabaqhatshi azikhethele zona.

(3) Ngaphandle kwamalunga ezombutho lomvikela, zonke izisebenzi zilelungelo lokuphatheka ekuhlanganeni labanye ekuthatheni inyathela emisebenzini, kugoqela ukukhalala umsebenzi, ukuzihlalela usemsebenzini, loba ukungenzi umsebenzi kanye lokuthatha amanye amanyathela aqinileyo afana lalawo, kodwa umthetho ungavimbela ukulandelwa kwalelilungelo ukuze imisebenzi edingakala kakhulu iqhubeke isenziwa.

(4) Zonke izisebenzi zilelungelo lokuba semsebenzini olomumo olokulunga, ofaneleyo njalo osuthisayo.

(5) Ngaphandle kwamalunga ezombutho lezomvikela, zonke izisebenzi, abaqhatshi, inhlanganiso zomsebenzi, kanye lezinhlanganiso zezisebenzi lezabaqhatshi zilelungelo loku—

(a)

hlanganyela labanye ekuthengiselaneni;

(c)

lokubumba lokuhlanganyela kumaqembu ezinhlangano lezinhlanganiso ezinjengalezo.

(b) lokuqoqa; kanye

(6) Abesifazana labesilisa balelungelo lokuthola iholo elilinganayo emsebenzini ofanayo.

(7) Izizebenzi ezingabesifazana zilelungelo lokuthola umholo ogcweleyo ngesikhathi zithethe ikhefu lokuyabeletha. 66

Inkululeko yokuhamba lendawo yokuhlala

(1) Zonke izizalwane zeZimbabwe zile—

(a)

lungelo lokungena eZimbabwe;

(c)

lungelo lokubalepassport loba ezinye izincwadi zokuhamba.

(b) lokuvikelwa ekuxotshweni eZimbabwe;

(2) Zonke izizalwane zeZimbabwe laye wonke umuntu oseZimbabwe ngokomthetho ulelungelo loku—

(a)

hamba ngokukhululeka eZimbabwe;

(c)

suka eZimbabwe.

(b) hlala kuloba yiyiphi indawo eseZimbabwe; 67

Amalungelo ezombusazwe

(1) Zonke izizalwane zeZimbabwe zilelungelo—

(a)

olukhululekileyo, olufaneleyo njalo olubuya ngezikhathi ezifaneleyo lwesikhundla esivotelwayo sokuphatha uzulu esimiswe ngokuphawulwe kulesiSisekamthetho loba omunye nje umthetho; kanye

(b) lokukhetha ezombusazwe ngokukhululeka.

(2) Kulandelwa lesiSisekamthetho, zonke izizalwane zeZimbabwe zilelungelo—

(a)

lokubumba, lokuhlanganyela kanye lokuphatheka ezintweni ezenzakala kuqembu lezombusazwe loba kunhlanganiso abazikhethethele zona;

(b) ukuphakamisa/ukukhankaselangokukhululeka langokuthula iqembu lezombusazwe;

(c)

ukuphatheka ezintweni zombusazwe ezenzakalayo ezilokuthula;

(d) ukuphatheka, yena yedwa loba ndawonye labanye, emibuthanweni loba emaqenjini loba ngeyinye indlela, ezintweni ezenzakalayo ezilokuthula ukuqondisa, ukuphikisana loba ukusekela amapholisi kaHulumende loba yiliphi iqembu lezombusazwe loba liphi iqembu.

(3) Kulandelwa leSisekamthetho, zonke izizalwane zeZimbabwe ezileminyaka yokuzalwa engaphezu kwetshumi lasitshiyagalombili zilelungelo—

(a)

lokuvota kulolonke ukhetho lalokho okuphathelane leSisekamthetho lesi;

(b) lokuma ekhethweni lokungena esikhundleni sokuphatha uzulu njalo nxa ekhethiwe, ulelungelo lokuba kulesosikhundla.

(4) Ngezizatho zokuphakamisa idemokrasi yokuba lamaqembu amanengi ezombusazwe, isicaphuno somthetho wePhalamende kumele siphawule ngokuphiwa kwamaqembu ezombusazwe izimali zokusebenzisa. 68

Ilungelo lokuthethwa kwamacala

(1) Wonke umuntu ulelungelo lokuthethwa kwamacala ngengqubo esemthethweni, ephangisayo, esebenza kuhle, efaneleyo, elingeneyo engabandlululiyo njalo elandelwa ngokufaneleyo.

(2) Wonke umuntu ulelungelo, inkululeko, indingeko loba akukhangeleleyo okuyimfanelo osekuphazanyiswe yingqubo yokuthethwa kwamacala ulelungelo lokuphiwa ngokuphangisa langokulotshwe phansi izizatho zaleyo ngqubo.

(3) uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule ngokulandelwa kwamalungelo lawa, njalo kumele—

(a) uphawule ukuhlolisiswa kwengqubo eyenziwa ngumthethwandaba loba, lapho okufanele khona owenziwa ngabameli bemthethwandaba abangelakubandlulula;

(b) uphoqe umlandu esizweni ukuthi kulandelwe amalungelo akusigatshana

(1) laku (2);

(c) 69

ukuphakamisa ukuthethwa kwamacala ngendlela esebenza kuhle.

Ilungelo lokuthonisiswa ngemfanelo

(1) Wonke umuntu owetheswa icala ulelungelo lokuthonisiswa phambi kukazulu ngesikhathi esifaneleyo phambi komthethwandaba ozimele wodwa njalo engalakubandlululwa.

(2) Ngokunquma kwamalungelo kazulu lemilandu, wonke umuntu ulelungelo lokuthonisiswa okufaneleyo, okuphangisayo njalo okwenziwa phambi kukazulu ngesikhathi esifaneleyo phambi komthethwandaba ozimele wodwa njalo ongelabandlululo loba iKomithi yomthetho loba ezinye inhlangano ezimiswe ngumthetho.

(3) Wonke umuntu ulelungelo lokufinyelela imithethwandaba, loba iKomithi yomthetho loba ezinye inhlanganiso ezimiswe ngumthetho ezokuqeda ingxabano.

(4) Wonke umuntu ulelungelo, ngendleko azazihlawulela zona yena ngokwakhe, lokukhetha njalo amelwe ngabameli bomthetho phambi kwengqe yiwuphi umthethwandaba abameli loba isigaba. 70

Amalungelo abantu abetheswa imilandu

(1) Loba nguwuphi umuntu owetheswa icala ulamalungelo alandelayo—

(a)

lokuthathwa njengomuntu ongelacala aze avezwe ukuthi ulecala;

(c)

lokuphiwa isikhathi esaneleyo lezinto zokusebenzisa ukuze alungiselele imvikelo;

(b) lokwaziswa ngokuphangisa ngomlandu, ngokugcweleyo ukuzakweenza anelise ukuwuphendula;

(d) lokukhetha igqwetha njalo, ngezindleko azazihlawulela yena ngokwakhe, amelwe yilelogqwetha;

(e)

(f)

lokumelwa ligqwetha aliphiwe yisizwe njalo ngendleko zeSizwe;

lokwaziswa ngokuphangisa ngamalungelo aphawulwe kuziqendu (d) laku

(e);

(g) lokubakhona nxa ethonisiswa;

(h) lokuletha kanye lokuphikisana lobufakazi;

(i)

(j)

lokuhlala ethule njalo engafakazi loba ukubanjwa ngamandla ukupha ubufakazi obumthengisayo; lokuthi ingqubo zokuthonisiswa zihumutshelwe elimini aluzwayo;

(k) lokungabotshelwa isenzo loba impazamiso ebingasilo cala ngesikhathi isenzakala;

(l)

2013

lokungabotshelwa isenzo loba impazamiso engaseyilo cala;

(m) lokungathonisiselwa icala loba impazamiso owayenzayo eseyaxolelwayo loba engaselacala loba osowayijeziselwayo;

(n) lokugwetshelwa okungaphansi kwesijeziso esibekiweyo nxa isijeziso esibekiweyo secala lelo sesiguquliwe phakathi kwesikhathi sokwenziwa kwecala lesikhathi sokugwetshwa.

(2) Lapho isigatshana lesi sidinga ulwazi ukuthi luphiwe emuntwini—

(a)

ulwazi kumele luphiwe ngolimi oluzwisiswa ngumuntu othonisiswayo;

(b) nxa umuntu lowo engenelisi ukubala loba ukubhala, ingqe yiyiphi incwadi egoqela ulwazi kumele ichasiswe ngendlela ezwisiswa ngumuntu lowo.

(3) Ingqe kukuphi ukuthonisiswa kwecala, ubufakazi obutholakale ngendlela eyephula okuphawulwe kulesiSahluko kumele butshiywe ngaphandle nxa ukuphathwa kobufakazi kuzathatha ukuthonisiswa njengokungafanelanga loba nxa kungaphazamisa ukuphathwa kokulunga loba lokho okufunwa nguzulu.

(4) Ingqe nguwuphi umuntu osethonisiselwe icala ulelungelo, ngokubhadala imali efaneleyo ephawulwe ngumthetho, ukuthi aphiwe obekusenzakala kungqubo ngesikhathi esifaneleyo ngemva kokuba ukwahlulela sokwenziwe ekuthonisiseni.

(5) Ingqe nguwuphi umuntu osethonisisiwe wafunyanwa elecala ulelungelo, ngaphansi kwemigoqo efaneleyo engabekwa ngumthetho—

(a)

lokuthi icala lihlolisiswe ngumthethwandaba omkhulu;

(b) lokulanda umthethwandaba omkhulu ephikisana lokwetheswa umlandu loba isigwebo. 71

Amalungelo aphathelane lempahla

(1) Kulesisigaba—

“inzuzo yepentsheni” kutsho ipentsheni, imali yesibongo loba imali efana laleyo ebhadalwa—

(a) emuntwini evela emalini ehlanganisiweyo;

(b) emuntwini kulandelwa imisebenzi ayenzele umqhatshi;

(c) kulandelwa ukugula komuntu loba ukulimala; loba

(d) emuntwini osefike esikhathini somhlalaphansi ngenxa yeminyaka, ukugula kumbe esinye isizatho; njalo kugoqela ukubhadalwa endaweni yepentsheni, imali eqanjiweyo ebhadalwa ngemva kwesikhathi esithile loba enye imali lokubuyiselwa kwemali eyabhadalelwa ipentsheni, imali eqanjiweyo ebhadalwa ngemva kwesikhathi esithile kumbe i-alawensi. “impahla” kutsho impahla engachasiswa loba ngaluphi uhlobo loba ngaliphi ilungelo loba ukuthinteka empahleni.

(2) Kulandelwa usigaba 72, wonke umuntu ulelungelo, loba kusiphi isigaba seZimbabwe, sokuthola, sokuphatha, okuba ngumnini, ukusebenzisa lokuthuthela komunye, lokunika omunye umuntu okwesikhathi esithize ngokwesivumelwano loba ukuthengisa zonke inhlobo zezimpahla, loba yena ngokwakhe loba ebambane labanye.

(3) Kulandelwa isigaba lesi lesigaba 72, akulamuntu ongabanjwa ngamandla ukwenqatshelwa impahla yakhe ngaphandle kokuthi nxa imumo elandelayo igcwalisiwe—

(a)

nxa ukwenqatshelwa kulandela umthetho kazulu;

(b) nxa ukwenqatshelwa kudingakala ngeyinsinye sezizatho ezilandelayo—

(i) ngokuya komvikelo, ukuvikeleka kukazulu, ukuhleleka kuzulu, ukuziphatha ngokuqondileyo kukazulu, impilakahle kazulu loba ukuhlelwa kwedolobho loba ilizwe; loba

(ii) ukuze kuthuthukiswe loba kusetshenziswe leyompahla loba yiyiphi impahla ngendingeko yokutholisa umphakathi usizo;

(c)

umthetho udinga ukuthi usiphathathamandla ofuna ukuthola impahla—

(i) lokupha isaziso sokufuna ukuthola impahla kuye wonke umuntu ongathintakala loba olelungelo elingaphazamiseka ngokutholakala kwaleyo mpahla;

(ii) lokubhadala inhlawulo efanelayo njalo eyeneleyo yokuthola impahla engakatholi impahla loba ngesikhathi esifaneleyo eseyitholile impahla;

(iii) nxa ukutholakala kwempahla kuphikiswa, lokudinga umthethwandaba owenelisayo engakatholi impahla, loba kungakadluli amalanga angamatshumi amathathu isitholakele impahla, ukuze athole imvumo eveza ukutholakala kwempahla;

(d) umthetho unika amandla emuntwini olempahla etholakeleyo ukuthi alande umthethwandaba owenelisayo ukuze abuyiselwe impahla yakhe ngokuphangisa nxa umthwethwndaba ungavumelani lokutholakala kwaleyompanhla;

(e)

umthetho unika amandla iloba nguwuphi othi impahla ngeyakhe ukuthi alande umthethwandaba ukuze kuphawulwe—

(i) ubukhona, ubunjalo kanye lesisindo sendingeko yakhe ngokuphahathelane lempahla;

(ii) ukuba semthethweni kokunqatshelwa;

(iii) inani lenhlawulo okumele bayithole;

kanye lokulanda umthethwandaba ukuze athole imvumo ephawula ukuhlawulwa ngokuphangisa.

(4) Lapho umuntu elelungelo elilomumo othize mayelana lokubhadala inzuzo yempentsheni, umthetho ophawula ukuqedwa kwelungelo loba ukunciphiswa kwelungelo lelo uyakhangelwa, ngesizatho sesigaba (3), njengomthetho ophawula ukutholakala kwempahla. 72

Amalungelo aphathelane lomhlabathi wokulima lokufuya

(1) Kulesisigaba—

“umhlabathi wokulima” kutsho umhlabathi osetshenziswa loba kuwuphi umsebenzi wokulima, kugoqela:

(a) ukugcina loba ukufuya inkomo, izinyamazane zasendle, inkukhu, izinyamazana loba inyosi; loba

(b) owokudla kwenkomo loba inyamazana zasendle; kodwa kakugoqeli umhlabathi womphakathi loba umhlabathi osemngceleni weziphathamandla zedolobho loba edobheni elincane omiswe ngaphansi komthetho ophathelane lokuhlelwa kwedolobho lelizwe loba njengokuchazwe emthethweni ophathelane lokuhlolwa komhlabathi. “umhlabathi” ugoqela iloba yini entintana lomhlabathi lob ekhula emhlabathini; Okubhaliswa khona imvumo yomhlabathi “Isiqephu somhlabathi wokulima” kutsho isiqephu somhlabathi wokulima obhaliswe njengesiqephu esizimele sodwa.

(2) Lapho umhlabathi wokulima, loba ilungelo loba indingeko emhlabathini onjalo, idingakala ekusetshenzisweni nguzulu, kugoqela—

(a)

indawo yokulima loba eminye imisebenzi;

(b) ukuhlelwa kakutsha komhlabathi, ezeganga, ukulondolozwa kwemvelo loba ukusetshenziswa kwezinyamazana zasendle loba ezinye impahla zemvelo;

(c)

ukuhlaliswa kakutsha kwabantu abaxotshiweyo ngenxa yokusetshenziswa komhlabathi ngokomsebezi okukhulunywe ngawo kusiqendu (a) loba ku

(b); umhlabathi, ilungelo loba indingeko ingatholakala ngokuyimfanelo yiSizwe ngesaziso esilotshwe kuphephandaba liveza umhlabathi, ilungelo loba indingeko mayelana lomhlabathi ilungelo loba indigeko yeSizwe elibeka sobala lokhu okuqukethweyo kusukela ngelanga lokukhutshwa kwesaziso ephepheni.

(3) lapho umhlabathi wokulima, loba ingqe yiliphi ilungelo loba indingeko emhlabathini onjalo utholakale ngemfanelo ukuze wenze umsebenzi okukhulunywe ngawo kusigatshana (2)—

(a)

kulandelwa usigatshana 295(1) lo (2), akulanhlawulo okumele ibhadalwe ekutholakaleni kwawo;

(b) akulamuntu ongalanda umthwethwandaba ukuyaphawulelwa yiloba yiwuphi umbuzo ophathelane lenhlawulo, ngaphandle kwenhlawulo yokulungisisa umhlabathi ungakatholakali, njalo akulamthwethwandaba ongalandela leso sicelo; njalo

(c) ukutholakala komhlabathi akungeke kuphikiswe kulandelwa ukuthi kubelokubandlulula ekwaphulweni kukasigaba 56.

(4) Wonke umhlabathi—

(a)

ophawulwe kuHlelo 7 lweSisekamthetho esidala; kumbe

(b) owawuchazwa ngokulandela usigaba 16B (2) (a)(ii) loba (iii) weSisekamthetho esidala, iSisekamthetho esitsha singakethulwa; siqhubeka sisipha amandla eSizweni, njalo akulanhlawulo engabhadalwa ngokutholakala kwawo ngaphandle kwenhlawulo yokuwuthuthukisa okwayenzakalayo ungakatholakali.

(5) Masinyazane ngesikhathi esifaneleyo ngemva kokuthi umhlabathi usutholakele ngemfanelo kulandelwa usigatshana (2), osesikhundleni onguye olomlandu wokubhaliswa kwemvumo yamhlabathi kumele, kungela saziso esinye, afake imvumo edingakalayo kuncwadi etshengisa umninimhlabathi lokuye okulotshiweyo ukuze kucitshwe ngokufaneleyo incwadi etshengisa umninimhlabathi kanye lokubhalisa ukuthi Isizwe yiso esesingumnikazi womhlabathi lowo.

(6) Isicaphuno somthetho wePhalamende singakuphatha njengecala ukuthi iloba ngubani, ongelamvumo yomthetho, ukuthi abengumnikazi loba ahlale emhlabathini wokulima okukhulunywe ngawo kululesisigaba loba omunye umhlabathi wesizwe.

(7) Kulandelwa ukutholakala komhlabathi wokulima wokuhlalisa kakutsha abantu kulandelwa uhlelo lokwabiwa kutsha komhlabathi, izinto ezilandelayo kumele zikhangelwe njengezinto ezibuya kuqala ngokuqakatheka—

(a)

ngaphansi kombuso wabancindezeli abantu beZimbabwe basuswa emhlabathini wabo lasezimpahleni zabo ngokungelasizatho kungelanhlawulo;

(b) ngalesosizatho abantu bathwala izikhali ukuze bathole njalo umhlabathi wabo kanye lokuzibusa, njalo lokhu kwaphetha ngokutholakala kukaZibuse ngo 1980;

(c)

abantu beZimbabwe kumele batholiswe ukwenelisa ukugcizelela amalungelo abo kanye lokuthola ukuba ngabanikazi bomhlabathi—

(i) umbuso omdala wabancindezeli ulomlandu wokuhlawulela umhlabathi wokulima owathathelwa ukuhlaliswa kutsha kwabantu, ngesikhwama esaneleyo esimiselwe lowo msebenzi; njalo

(ii) nxa umbuso omdala wabancindezeli usehluleka ukuhlawula ngaleso sikhwama, uHulumende weZimbabwe akalamlandu wokubhadalela inhlawulo yomhlabathi wokulima owatholelwa ngemfanelo ukuhlalisa kakutsha abantu.

(8) Lesi sigaba sisetshenziswa kungelakuphathwa kubi komlandu wombuso owadlulayo wabancindezeli ukuthi bahlawulele umhlabathi okukhulunywa ngawo kulesisigaba owatholelwa ukuhlaliswa kutsha kwabantu. 73

Amalungelo aphathelane lemvelo

(1) Wonke umuntu ulelungelo—

(a)

lemvelo engasiyo ngozi kumpilakhle yakhe loba ebomini bakhe;

(b) lokuthi imvelo ivikeleke ukuze ibe lusizo kuzizukulwane ezikhona kanye lezizayo, ngokulandela amanyathelo omthetho lamanye amanyathelo—

(i) ayenqabela ukungcoliswa lokuphazanyiswa kwemvelo

(ii) lokuphakamisa ukulondolozwa;

(iii) lokugcina ukuthuthuka kwemvelo okuqhubekiswayo kanye lokusetshenziswa kwezimpahla zemvelo

(2) Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathela afaneleyo lamanye amanyathela, asemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, ukuthola ukulandelwa ngokwezinga eliphezulu kwamalungelo aphawulwe kulesisahluko. 74

Inkululeko yokungaxotshwa mahlayana endaweni


Ilungelo lemfundo

Akulamuntu ongaxotshwa emzini wakhe, loba adilizelwe umuzi wakhe, kungela silawulosomthethwandaba.

(1) Zonke izizalwane lasebehlala eZimbabwe ngokusemthethweni balelungelo—

(a)

lemfundo emqoka ebhadalelwa yiSizwe, kugoqela imfundo emqoka yabantu abadala; kanye

(b) lemfundo yokuqhubekela phambili, leyo okumele Isizwe, ngamanyathela omthetho lamanye amanyathela siwenze aqhubeke etholakala njalo efinyeleleka.

(2) Wonke umuntu ulelungelo lokumisa lokuqhuba, ngezimali zakhe, izingatsha ezizimele zodwa zemfundo ezilemfundo efaneleyo, kuyikuthi kababandlulili muntu ngendlela engavunyelwa yilesi Sisekamthetho.

(3) Umthetho ungaphawula ngokubhaliswa kwezingatsha zemfundo okukhulunywe ngazo ngaphansi kukasigatshana (2) kanye lokuvalwa kwezingatsha ezinjengalezo ezingaphi imfundo efaneleyo eyabe iphawulwe ekubhalisweni kwazo.

(4) Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathela omthetho lamanye amanyathela, asemandleni empahla esilazo, ukuze kutholakale impumela yokulandelwa kwelungelo.

2013

Ilungelo lokunakekelwa kwempilakahle

(1) Zonke izizalwane lasebahlala eZimbabwe ngokusemthethweni balelungelo lokufinyelela ukuphathiswa ngokunakakelwa kwempilakahle ezimqoka, kugoqela ukuphathiswa ngokunakelwa kwempilakahle ephathelane lenzalo.

(2) Wonke umuntu ophila lomkhuhlane ongelapheki masinyane ulelungelo lokufinyelela ukuphathiswa ngokunakakelwa kwempilakahle.

(3) Akulamuntu ongayalelwa ukwelatshwa ngesiphangiphangi iloba yikuluphi ugatsha olunakakela ngezempilakahle.

(4) Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathela afaneleyo omthetho kanye lamanye amanyathela, asemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, ukuthola ukulandelwa kwalelilungelo ngokuphakemeyo. 77

Ilungelo lokudla lamanzi

Wonke umuntu ulelungelo—

(a)

lamanzi angelangozi, ahlanzekileyo njalo avikelekileyo; kanye

(b) lokudla okwaneleyo:

njalo iSizwe kumele sithathe amanyathela emithetho afaneleyo lamanye amanyathela, asemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, ukuthola ukulandelwa kwalelilungelo ngokuphakemeyo. 78

Amalungelo omtshado

(1) Wonke umuntu osefikise iminyaka yokuzalwa elitshumi lasitshiyagalombili ulelungelo lokusungula imuli.

(2) Akulamuntu ongabanjwa ngamandla ukuthi athathe loba athathwe kungekho entandweni yakhe.

(3) Abantu abalobulili obufananayo kabavunyelwa ukuthi bathathane. ISAHLUKWANA 3 Ukucaciswa Kwamalungelo Athize 79

Ukusetshenziswa kwesahlukwana 3

(1) Isahlukwana lesi sicacisa amalungelo athize lezinkululeko ezithize ukubona ukuthi kuba lokuqiniseka ekusetshenzisweni kwalawo malungelo lalezo zinkululeko emaqenjini abantu abathize.

(2) Isahlukwana lesi akumelanga sithathwe njengokuthi sivimbela ingqe yiliphi ilungelo loba inkululeko ephawulwe kusahlukwana 2. 80

Amalungelo abesifazana

(1) Wonke owesifazana ulesithunzi esigcweleyo somuntu njalo esilingana lesabesilisa njalo lokhu kugoqela amathuba alinganayo kwezombusazwe, kwezezimali lakwezenhlalo.

(2) Abesifazana balamalungelo afanana lawabesilisa mayelana lokugcinwa lokondliwa kwabantwana, kodwa isicaphuno sePhalamende singabeka ukuthi lawo malungelo angasetshenziswa kanjani.

(3) Yonke imithetho, lesintu kanye lamasiko lemikhuba yesintu ephazamisa amalungelo abekwe yilesiSisekamthetho ayacitshwa/kawasebenzi/kuhambelana lokwephulwa kwawo.

Amalungelo abantwana

(1) Wonke umntwana, kusitsho wonke umfana loba inkazana engaphansi kweminyaka yokuzalwa elitshumi lasitshiyagalombili, ulelungelo—

(a)

lokuphathwa ngokulinganayo phambi komthetho, kugoqela ilungelo lokulalelwa;

(b) lokuphiwa ibizo kanye lesibongo;

(c)

nxa engumtwana o—

(i) zalelwe eZimbabwe; loba

(ii) uzelwe ngaphandle kwelizwe leZimbabwe njalo eyisizalwane seZimbabwa ngokosendo:

lokuphiwa incwadi yokuzalwa masinyane;

(d) lokunakakelwa yimuli loba ngabazali, loba ekunakakelweni okufaneleyo nxa ethe wasuswa lapho imuli ehlala khona;

(e)

(f)
