ukuvikelwa ekuhlukuluzweni kwezezimali lakwezemacansini, ekuqhatshweni kwabantwana, kanye lasekuphathweni kubi, ekunganakakweleni loba ingqe kuluphi uhlobo lobudlwangudlwangu; emfundweni, ekutholeni ukunakakelwa kwempilakahle, ekudleni okwakha umzimba lasendaweni yokuhlala;

(g) ukungangeniswa ekufundisweni imisebenzi yamasotsha loba ukuphatheka engxabanweni okulemibhobho khona loba ukulwa;

(h) ukungabanjwa ngamandla ukuphatheka kwezombusazwe; njalo

(i)

ukungavalelwa ngaphandle nxa sokuyiyo yodwa indlela engcono esisele njalo, nxa bevalelwe:

(i) bavalelwe okwesibanga esifitshane kakhulu esifaneleyo;

(ii) kugcinwa eyehlukanisiwe labanye abantu abavalelweyo abaleminyaka ephezu kwetshumi lasitshiyangalombili;

(iii) ukuphathwa ngendlela, lokugcinwa endaweni, evumelana leminyaka yomntwana.

(2) Izindingeko zomntwana ezifaneleyo ziqakathekile kuzo zonke izindaba eziphathelane laye umntwana.

(3) Abantwana balemvumo yokuvikelwa okweneleyo lidale lomthethwandaba, ikakhulu lidale lomthethwandaba elikhulu njengoba liyilo elingumlawuli wabo ophezulu/omkhulu. 82

Amalungelo asebekhulile

Abantu abaleminyaka yokuzalwa engaphezu kwamatshumi ayisikhombisa balelungelo—

(a)

lokuthola ukunakekelwa okufaneleyo kanye losizo ezimulini zabo kanye lakuso isizwe;

(b) lokuthola ukunakakelwa kwempilakahle kanye losizo kuso isizwe; kanye

(c)

lokuthola usekelo lwemali ngendlela yesocial security and welfare;

njalo Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathela okwenza imithetho lamanye amanyathela, asemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, ukuze sithole ukunanzwa ngokupheleleyo kwelungelo leli.

2013

Amalungelo abantu abakhubazekileyo

Isizwe kumele sithathe amanyathela afaneleyo, asemandleni ezimpahla esilazo, okubona ukuthi abantu abalokugogeka bayenza lokho abakwanelisayo ngemizimba yabo langezingqondo zabo ngokugcweleyo, kugoqela amanyathela—

(a)

okubenza banelise ukuzimela;

(c)

awokubavikela kuyo yonke imihlobo yokuhluluzwa leyobudlwangudlwangu;

(e)

ukupha izinto zokusebenzisa eziqakathekileyo ekufundeni kwabo;

(b) okubenza banelise ukuhlala lezimuli zabo njalo baphatheke emisebenzini yezenhlalo, eyokuzenzela loba eyokuzilibazisa;

(d) awokubatholisa ukufinyelela ukwelatshwa ngemithi loba okwengqondo lekusebenzeni;

(f) 84

ukutholisa imfundo lokuqeqetshiswa okubhadalelwa yisizwe lapho abakudinga khona.

Amalungelo abalwa impi yenkululeko

(1) Abalwela impi yenkululeko, kusitsho—

(a)

labo abalwayo empini yenkululeko;

(c)

labo abavalelwa emajele, abavalwayo loba bavinjelwa ngenxa yezizatho zombusazwe ngesikhathi sempi yenkululeko;

(b) labo abancedisa abalweli empini yenkululeko;

balemfanelo yokunanzwa ngomsebenzi abawenzayo ekukhululweni kweZimbabwe, kanye leyokuthola izinto ezifaneleyo ekuphileni kwabo ezinjengepentsheni, kanye lokufinyelela ukunakakelwa okumqoka kwempilakahle.

(2) Isicaphuno somthetho wePhalamende kumele sibeke mayelana labalweli benkululeko izimfanelo ebafanele bazithole ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1). ISAHLUKWANA 4 Ukuphoqelelwa Kwamalungelo Ayinsika Lezinkululeko 85

Ukuphoqelelwa kwamalungelo ayinsika lezinkululeko

(1) Yiloba nguphi umuntu kulaba abalandelayo abathi—

(a)

ingqe nguwuphi umuntu ozifakela yena ngokwakhe isicelo;

(c)

ingqe nguwuphi umuntu ofaka isicelo njengelunga loba owenza lokho ebizweni leqembu, loba lomkhakha othile wabantu;

(b) ingqe nguwuphi umuntu owenza isicelo ebizweni lomunye umuntu ongenelisiyo ukuzenzela yena;

(d) loba nguwuphi umuntu ofaka isicelo esenzela uzulu;

(e)

loba yiyiphi inhlanganiso efaka isicelo ngenhloso yokwenzela amalunga ayo;

ulelungelo lokufaka isicelo edale lomthethwandaba esitsho ukuthi ilungelo eliyinsika loba inkululeko eyinsika ekumqulu ekulesisahluko lephuliwe, loba kungenzakala ukuthi liyephulwe, njalo umthethwandaba ungapha usizo olufaneleyo, olugoqela ukumenyezelwa kwamalungelo kanye lomvunzo wokuhlawulwa.

(2) Ukuthi umuntu wephule umthetho akumvimbeli ukuthi afake isicelo emthethwandaba lokudinga usizo ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1).

(3) Imithetho yemthethwandaba yonke kumele ibeke izindlela okumele zilandelwe nxa kudingakala usizo ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1), njalo leyo mithetho kumele ibone ukuthi—

(a)

ilungelo lokulanda umthethwandaba ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1) liyenziwa ngokugwceleyo;

(b) okufanele kulandelwe mayelana lengqubo, kugoqela ukuqaliswa kwakho kugcinwe kusezingeni eliphansi;

(c)

umthethwandaba, noma ulandela imithetho yezokulunga, kawuvinjelwa zingqubo ezingelamqondo;

(d) umuntu olamakhono athize, ngemvumo yemthethwandaba, angaphathisa njengomngane womthethwandaba.

(4) Ukuswelakala kwemithetho okukhulunywe ngayo kusigatshana (3) akunciphisi ilungelo lokuqalisa izingqubo ngaphansi kukasigatshana (1) kanye lokuthi icala lilawulwe njalo liphawulwe ngumthethwandaba. ISAHLUKWANA 5 Umkhawulo Wamalungelo Ayinsika Lezinkululeko 86

Umkhawulo wamalungelo lezinkululeko

(1) Amalungelo ayinsika lezinkululeko eziqukethwe kulesisahluko kumele asetshenziswe ngokufanelyo kanye langokunanzwa kwamalungelo lezinkululeko zabanye abantu.

(2) Amalungelo ayinsika lezinkululeko eziqukethwe kulesisahluko zingancitshiswa kuphela ngokutsho komthetho osebenza kuloba nguwuphi umuntu njalo kungakadluli ezingeni lokuthi ukuncitshiswa kube kufanele, kulomqondo, kudingakala futhi kwamukelekile emphakathini ovulekileyo, olokulunga, isithunzi sobuntu, ukulingana kanye lenkululeko, kunanzwe zonke izinto eziphathelane lalokho, kugoqela—

(a)

ubunjalo belungelo lelo loba inkululeko yakhona;

(b) injongo yokuncitshiswa, kungabe kuyikuthi kuyadingakala ngokudingakala kwemvikela, ukuvikeleka kukazulu, ukufuna kukazulu, impilakahle kazulu ukuhlelwa kwelizwe kwedolobho loba ngentando kazulu jikelele;

(c) ubunjalo lobukhulu bokuncitshiswa;

(d) indingeko yokubona ukuthi ukutholwa kwamalungelo lezinkululeko yiloba nguwuphi umuntu akuncindezeli amalungelo lezinkululeko zabanye;

(e) ubudlelwano phakathi kokuncitshiswa lomsebenzi wakho, ikakhulu ukuthi ingabe ifaka ukuvimbela okukhulu kumalungelo loba inkululeko yakhona okwedlula okudingakalayo ekufezeni umsebenzi wayo; kanye

(f)

kungabe kukhona yini izindlela zokufeza umsebenzi wakho ezingavimbeli kakhulu.

(3) Akulamthetho onganciphisa amalungelo alandelayo agcinwe kulesisahluko, njalo akulamuntu ongawephula—

(a)

ilungelo lokuphila, ngaphandle kwezingeni eliphawulwe kusigaba 48;

(c)

ilungelo lokungahlukuluzwa loba lokungatshesizwa ngendlela yesihluku, engelabuntu loba eyehlisa isithunzi somuntu;

(b) ilungelo lokuthola isithunzi somuntu;

(d) ilungelo lokungafakwa ebugqilini loba ekugqilazweni;

(e)

(f) 87

ilungelo lokuthethelwa icala ngendlela efaneleyo;

ilungelo lokuthola Iorder yehabeas corpus njengokulotshwe kusigaba 50(7)(a).

Ukuncitshiswa ngesikhathi somumo omanzonzo

(1) Ukwengezelela ekuncitshisweni okuvumeleke kusigaba86, amalungelo ayinsika lezinkululeko eziyinsika eziphawulwe kulesisahluko kungancitshiswa kusiyaphambili ngomthetho olotshiweyo usipha amanyathela okuphatha umumo ovela ngesikhathi sengozi kazulu, kodwa kuphela ngokufika ezingeni elivunyelwe yilesisigaba kanye lohlelo lwesibili.

(2) Umthetho olotshiweyo okukhulunywa ngawo kusigatshana (1) kanye lamanye amanyathela okwenza imithetho athethwe ngaphansi kwalowomthetho, kumele kukhutshwe kuGazette.

(3) Ingqe yikuphi ukuncitshiswa okutshiwo ngumthetho olotshiweyo kusigatshana (1) okungenziwa kulungelo eliyinsika loba inkululeko ephawulwe kulesisahluko akumelanga kube kukhulu kokwedlula lokhu okudingwa yingozi leyo.

(4) Akulamthetho ovumela ukumenyezelwa kwengozi kazulu, njalo akulanyathela lomthetho loba elinye inyathela elingathathwa ngenxa yalesosimemezelo, elinga—

(a)

loba livumele loba liphe amandla okubuyiselwa kwendleko kwe Sizwe loba ugatsha lukaHulumende loba abameli bakahulumende ingqe kusiphi isigaba, loba ingqe nguwuphi umuntu, ngesizatho sesenzo esingekho emthethweni; loba

(b) nciphisa ingqe yiwaphi amalungelo atshiwo kusigaba 86(3), loba liphe amandla loba livumele ingqe yiliphi lalawo malungelo ukuthi liyephulwe. ISAHLUKO 5 Iziphathambuso ISAHLUKWANA 1 88

Igunya Leziphathambuso Igunya leziphathambuso

(1) Amandla eziPhathambuso avelela ebantwini beZimbabwe njalo kumele asetshenziswe maqondana lalesiSisekamthetho.

(2) Amandla eziPhathambuso eZimbabwe aqukethwe nguMongameli onguye owasebenzisa, maqondana lalesiSisekamthetho, ngeKhabhinethi. ISAHLUKWANA 2 Umongameli Labasekeli Bakamongameli 89

UMongameli

UMongameli nguye iNhloko yeSizwe loHulumende njalo enguNdunankulu wamaButho oMvikela.

Imisebenzi kaMongameli

(1) UMongameli kumele ananze, avikele, alandele njalo ahloniphe lesiSisekamthetho njengoMthetho ongaphezu kweminye yonke elizweni njalo kumele abone ukuthi lesiSisekamthetho kanye leminye yonke imithetho iyalandelwa ngokupheleleyo.

(2) UMongameli kumele—

(a)

akhuthaze umanyano lokuthula elizweni ukuze uzulu wonke weZimbabwe akholise njalo abe lenhlalakahle;

(b) ananze njalo ahloniphe izilangazelelo lensika yempi yenkululeko;

(c)

aqinise ukuvikelwa kwamalungelo lezinkululeko eziyinsika kanye lokubusa komthetho; njalo

(d) ahloniphe ukwahlukana kwabantu lezabelo zeZimbabwe. 91

Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu anelise ukuvotelwa njengoMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli

(1) Umuntu uyenelisa ukuvotelwa njengoMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli nxa—

(a)

eyisizalwane seZimbabwe ngokuzalwa kumbe ngosendo;

(c)

engumuntu ovele ehlala kweleZimbabwe; njalo

(b) esefikise iminyaka engamatshumi amane (40) yokuzalwa;

(d) ebhalise njengomuntu ovotayo.

(2) Umuntu akenelisi ukuthi avotelwe esikhundleni sokuba nguMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli nxa kuyikuthi sewake waphatha isikhundla sokuba nguMongameli ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho okwamathemu amabili, alandelanayo loba hatshi, njalo ngaphansi kwalesi sigatshana iminyaka emithathu kusiya phezulu esikhundleni ithathwa njengethemu epheleleyo. 92

Ukuvotelwa kukaMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli

(1) Ukuvotelwa kukaMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli ababili kumele kwenzakale esikhathini esibalulwe kuSigaba 158.

(2) Wonke umuntu oncintisa ukuvotelwa njengoMongameli kumele akhethe abantu ababili abazancintisa ndawonye laye njengabaSekeli bakaMongameli, njalo kumele afake oyedwa walabo babili njengoMsekeli wakhe wakuqala kuthi omunye wakhona abe nguMsekeli wakhe wesibili bancintise benjalo.

(3) UMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli bavotelwa ndawonye ngabantu ababhalise ukuvota eZimbabwe yonke jikelele, njalo indlela okumele ilandelwe ekubavoteleni ibekwe yabalulwa kuMthetho woKhetho.

(4) Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu enelise ukubhalisa ukuze avote lokuthi aphatheke ekhethweni lokudinga uMongameli kanye labaSekeli bakaMongameli kubalulwe kuHlelo 4.

(5) Ukhetho lokudinga uMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli kumele luqhutshwe sikhathi sinye lokhetho-jikelele oluqhutshwa kudingwa abameli bedale lePhalamende, amaKhansili ezifunda kanye lamaKhansilii ezindawo.

2013

Ukuphikisana lokukhetwa kukaMongameli

(1) Ngaphansi kwalesiSigaba, loba ngubani oncintisayo angaba lakho ukuphikisa ubuqotho bemiphumela yokhetho lokudinga uMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli ngokunika isicelo kumbe ngokubhalela kuMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho phakathi kwamalanga ayisikhombisa imiphumela yokhetho iveziwe.

(2) Ukukhethwa koMsekeli kaMongameli kungaphikiswa kuphela nxa kuyikuthi akasuthisi kumbe wayengasuthisi okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu avotelwe kuleso sikhundla.

(3) UMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho kumele uzwe njalo uthethe udaba olulethwe njengesicelo kumbe olubhalelwe kuwo ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1) phakathi kwamalanga alitshumi lane (14) udaba luphanyekiwe, njalo isinqumo soMthethwandaba kasiphikiswa.

(4) Ekuthetheni udaba lwesicelo kumbe olubhalelwe eMthethwandaba ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1), uMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho—

(a)

ungaqamba umnqobi;

(c)

unganika esinye isinqumo osibona siqotho njalo silungile.

(b) ungesula ukhetho, besekuqhutshwa olunye ukhetho phakathi kwamalanga angamatshumi ayisithupha (60) isinqumo sibekiwe; kumbe

(5) Nxa, kusicelo kumbe encwadini ebhalelwe uMthethwandaba ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1)—

(a)

uMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho ungabeka eceleni ukhetho lokudinga uMongameli, ukukhethwa kwabaSekeli bakaMongameli bobabili lakho kuyahle kwesulwe;

(b) uMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho ungabeka eceleni ukukhethwa koMsekeli kaMongameli oyedwa kumbe bobabili, uMongameli kumele aphange akhethe umuntu kumbe abantu abalezimfanelo ezidingakalayo, ngokufaneleyo, ukuze abe kumbe babe ngabaSekeli bakaMongameli. 94

Ukuqalisa umsebenzi kukaMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli

(1) Abantu abakhethelwe ezikhundleni zikaMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli baqalisa umsebenzi ngemva kokuthatha izifungo zikaMongameli lezabaSekeli bakaMongameli phambi kukaMahluleli kumbe omunye uMahluleli olesikhundla esiphezulu kulabanye bonke abayabe bekhona njengokuchazwe kuHlelo 3, njalo lezozofungo kumele bazenze—

(a)

elangeni lesitshiyagalolunye bekhethiwe okusemthethweni; kumbe

(b) nxa kube lokuphikisa ubuqotho bokukhethwa kwabo, phakathi kwamalanga amabili okunanzwa kwabo njengabanqobi nguMthethwandaba.

(2) UMongameli obusayo kumele aqhubeke ebusa uMongameli omutsha aze aqalise umsebenzi njengokuchazwe ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1).

(3) UMsekeli kaMongameli oba nguMongameli ngemva kokufa, ukutshiya umsebenzi kumbe ukususwa esikhundleni kukaMongameli uqalisa umsebenzi nxa esethethe isifungo sokuba nguMongameli phambi kukaMahluleli omkhulu kumbe omunye uMahluleli olesikhundla esiphezulu kulabanye bonke abayabe bekhona njengokuchazwe kuHlelo 3, njalo leso sifungo kumele senziwe ngokuphuthuma, esikhathini esingadluli amahola angamatshumi amane lasitshiyangalombili (48) ngemva kokukhamisa kwesikhundla sikaMongameli.

Isikhathi esibekiweyo sokuba sesikhundleni kukaMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli

(1) Ithemu yokuba sesikhundleni kukaMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli iqalisa mhla ethatha isifungo njalo eqalisa umsebenzi ngaphansi kweSigaba 94(1)(a) kumbe u94(3).

(2) Ithemu yokuba sesikhundleni kukaMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli iyaqhubeka—

(a)

aze atshiye umsebenzi kumbe akhitshwe esikhundleni; kumbe

(b) kuze kube lolunye ukhetho lapho akhethwa khona ngokusemthethweni ukuthi abuse njalo kumbe kukhethwe omunye uMongameli ngokusemthethweni; njalo, ngaphandle nxa kusetshenziswe eminye imigca ekulesi Sisekamthetho, ubude bethemu yokubusa kwabo buyiminyaka emihlanu (5) esikhundleni njalo buhambelana xhathu lempilo yedale lePhalamende. 96

Ukutshiya umsebenzi kukaMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli

(1) Umongameli angatshiya isikhundla sakhe ngokubhalela iSikhulumeli sedale lePhalamende incwadi yokutshiya umsebenzi, yena okumele aphange abikele uzulu ngokufuna ukutshiya umsebenzi kukaMongameli, njalo kumele enze lokhu phakathi kwesikhathi esingedluli amahola angamatshumi amabili lane (24).

(2) UMsekeli kaMongameli angatshiya isikhundla sakhe ngokubhalela uMongameli embikela ngenjongo yakhe, yena okumele aphange abikele uzulu ngokufuna ukutshiya umsebenzi kukaMsekeli kaMongameli, njalo kumele enze lokhu phakathi kwesikhathi esingedluli amahola angamatshumi amabili lane (24). 97

Ukukhitshwa kukaMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli esikhundleni

(1) ISenethi leNdlu yabaMeli, zifuqwa ngesinqumo ezisibophe ndawonye Sisekelwa yingxenye kumbe ukwedlula yabameli bazo, zinganquma ukuthi umbono wokuba uMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli kumele akhitshwe esikhundleni ngenxa—

(a)

yokungaqhubi kuhle umsebenzi;

(c)

ukwephula mahlayana lesi Sisekamthetho;

(b) yokwehluleka kwakhe ukulandela, ukunanza lokuvikela lesi Sisekamthetho

(d) ukungenelisi ukwenza okufunwa yisikhundla sakhe ngenxa yokwehluleka emzimbeni kumbe engqondweni;

kumele uhlolisiswe ngaphansi kwalesi Sigaba.

(2) Ekufakweni kwesinqumo ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1), iKomithi ebona ngeziQondiso zeDale kumele imise iKomithi ebunjwe ndawonye ngamalunga edale leSenethi laweNdlu yabaMeli elamalunga ayisitshiyagalolunye (9) akhombisa ukumelwa kwamabandla ezombusazwe edale lePhalamende, ukuze ihlolisise mayelana lokususwa kukaMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli esikhundleni.

(3) Nxa—

(a)

iKomithi ebunjwe ngaphansi kwesigatshana (2) ikhuthaza ukususwa kukaMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli esikhundleni; njalo

(b) idale leSenethi leNdlu yabaMeli, ngesinqumo esibotshwe ngamalunga afika kumbe edlula ingxenye ezimbili kwezintathu (2/3) zamalunga wonke azo zihlangene, zinganquma ukuthi uMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli kumele asuswe esikhundleni;

uMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli uhle atshiye isikhundla khonaphokhonapho. 98

Ukuvikeleka kukaMongameli eMthethweni

(1) Nxa esaphethe isikhundla, uMongameli akayi kumiswa phambi komthethwandaba wemilandu kumbe owamacala ngenxa yezinto azenzileyo kumbe angazenzanga yena mathupha ezimele yedwa.

(2) Umthethwandaba ungathonisisa umuntu osewake waphatha isikhundla sokuba nguMongameli ngenxa yezinto azenzayo langazenzanga engakabi nguMongameli loba esesenguMongameli.

(3) Ukuqhutshwa kwezinqumo eziphathelane lezikwelede kumbe amacala kaMongameli enziwa engakaqalisi kumbe ephakathi kwethemu yakhe yombuso kuke kufakwe eceleni ngesikhathi esaphethe isikhundla.

(4) Kuzo zonke izingqubo ezethwesa osewake waba nguMongameli ngalokho akwenzayo kumbe angakwenzanga ngesikhathi esaphethe isikhundla sokuba nguMongameli okuphathelane lomsebenzi wakhe, kuyimvikelo kuye nxa engatshengisa obala ukuthi lokho akwenzayo kumbe angakwenzanga wakwenza ngesihle. 99

Imisebenzi yabaSekeli bakaMongameli

AbaSekeli bakaMongameli bancedisa uMongameli ekuqhubeni imisebenzi yakhe baphinde benze eminye imisebenzi abangayinikwa nguMongameli, egoqela ukuhola loba yiluphi ugatsha, uphiko kumbe uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende. 100 Osaphethe isikhundla sikaMongameli

(1) Sonke isikhathi uMongameli engekho elizweni kumbe engenelisi ukuqhuba umsebenzi wakhe ngemfanelo ngenxa yokugula kumbe esinye isizatho, imisebenzi yakhe kumele yenziwe njalo iqhutshwe—

(a)

nguMsekeli wakuqala kaMongameli;

(c)

nxa kungela Msekeli kaMongameli owenelisayo ukwenza leyo misebenzi, nguMphathintambo:

(i) obekelwe isikhathi esinjalo nguMongameli; kumbe

(ii) okhethwe yiKhabhinethi, nxa kungela Mphathintambo obekwe nguMongameli njengokuchazwe ngaphansi kwesiqendwana (i).

(b) nxa uMsekeli wakuqala engenelisi ukuqhuba leyo misebenzi, nguMsekeli wesibili kaMongameli;

(2) Ngaphandle kulandelwa isinqumo esibotshwe linengi lamalunga wonke eKhabhinethi, umuntu oqhuba imisebenzi kaMongameli njengokuchazwe kusigatshana

(1) akumelanga asebenzise amandla kaMongameli—

(a)

okuphumisa ijumo amaButho oMvikela;

(c)

okuqhatsha kumbe ukukhipha esikhundleni uMsekeli kaMongameli, uMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo; kumbe

(b) okudweba izivumelwano zamazwe kumbe ezemithetho yomhlaba wonke jikelele;

(d) okunika loba ukuguqula eminye imisebenzi kaMsekeli kaMongameli, uMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo, kugoqela, esikhundleni soMsekeli kaMongameli kumbe uMphathintambo, ukuholwa koMthetho loba yiwuphi owePhalamende kumbe uGatsha loba uphiko, kumbe ukwesula loba yiphi imisebenzi efanayo. 101 Ukungenwa kwesikhundla sikaMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli nxa angafa, engatshiya umsebenzi kumbe engasenelisi

(1) Nxa uMongameli angafa, angatshiya umsebenzi kumbe eangasuswa esikhundleni—

(a)

uMsekeli wakuqala kaMongameli uyathatha isikhundla njengoMongameli ithemu kaMongameli obekhona ize iphele;

(b) uMsekeli wesibili kaMongameli uyathatha isikhundla njengoMsekeli wakuqala kaMongameli ithemu kaMongameli obekhona ize iphele; njalo

(c)

ekuthatheni isikhundla sikaMongameli, obenguMsekeli wakuqala kaMongameli kumele akhethe umuntu osuthisa okudingakalayo ukuze abe nguMsekeli wesibili kaMongameli ithemu kaMongameli obekhona ize iphele.

(2) Nxa uMsekeli wesibili kaMongameli angafa, angatshiya umsebenzi kumbe angasuswa esikhundleni—

(a)

uMsekeli wesibili kaMongameli uthatha isikhundla njengoMsekeli wakuqala kaMongameli ithemu yokuba sesikhundleni kukaMsekeli wakuqala kaMongameli obekhona ize iphele;

(b) uMongameli kumele angachithi isikhathi akhethe umuntu olezimfanelo ezidingakalayo ukuthi abe nguMsekeli wesibili kaMongameli ithemu yesikhundla sikamSekeli wakuqala kaMongameli obekhona ize iphele. 102 Iholo likaMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli

(1) UMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli babotshelwa ukuthola iholo, imali yezindleko, impentsheni lokunye okubalulwe ngaphansi koMthetho wePhalamande.

(2) Iholo lemali yezindleko ephiwa uMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli kumele ivele kusikhwama sikaHulumende, iConsolidated Revenue Fund.

(3) Umuntu osephumile esikhundleni sokuba nguMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli ubotshelwa ukuthola—

(a)

impentsheni ehambelana leholo likaMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli osesikhundleni; njalo

(b) izimali zendleko lokunye konke okutholiswayo njengokubalulwa nguMthetho wePhalamande. 103 UMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli kanye lasebake baphatha lezozikhundla abazukuphatha ezinye izihlalo kumbe ukuqhatshwa kweminye imisebenzi

UMongameli labaSekeli bakaMongameli, labanye bonke asebake baphatha lezo zikhundla, akumelanga babuye baphathe ezinye izihlalo kuHulumende kumbe baqhatshwe ngomunye umuntu ngesikhathi besakulezozikhundla kumbe bethola impentsheni kuHulumende njengabantu asebake baphatha isikhundla sikaMongameli loba uMsekeli kaMongameli.

ISAHLUKWANA 3 Omphathintambo, Abasekeli Babomphathintambo Lekhabhinethi 104 Ukuqhatshwa kwaboMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo

(1) UMongameli uqhatsha oMphathintambo abuye abalule imisebenzi okumele bayenze, kugoqela ukuholwa kweMithetho yePhalamande kumbe okwaloba yiluphi uGatsha loba uphiko, kodwa uMongameli angazitshiyela umlandu wokuhola uMthetho, uGatsha kumbe uphiko.

(2) UMongameli angaqhatsha abaSekeli baboMphathintambo ukuze bancedise oMphathintambo ekuqhubeni imisebenzi yabo.

(3) OMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo bakhethwa phakathi kwamalunga eSenethi kumbe aweNdlu yabaMeli, kodwa abanye abangedluli abahlanu, abadingelwe ulwazi lamakhono abo, bangaqhatshwa bengasimalunga edale lePhalamende.

(4) Ekuqhatsheni oMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo, uMongameli kumele afake engqondweni umhlubulo welizwe lapho okuvela khona labo bantu kanye lokulinganiswa kobulili.

(5) OMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo abangesimalunga edale lePhalamende bangahlala, kodwa bangavoti, edale leSenethi kumbe eNdlini yabaMeli.

(6) Engakaze aqalise umsebenzi, umuntu oqhatshelwe ukuba nguMphathintambo kumele athathe isifungo esifaneleyo sokuba nguMphathintambo njengokubalulwe kuHlelo 3. 105 IKhabhinethi

(1) KuleKhabhinethi ebalisela uMongameli, njengenhloko yeKhabhinethi, abaSekeli bakaMongameli laboMphathintambo abaqhatshelwe kuleyo Khabhinethi nguMongameli.

(2) Imihlangano yeKhabhinethi iholwa nguMongameli kumbe, nxa engekho, nguMSekeli kaMongameli kumbe, nxa laye engekho, nguMphathintambo okhulunywe ngaye kuSigaba 100(1)(c). 106 Ukuziphatha kwabaSekeli bakaMongameli, oMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo

(1) Wonke uMsekeli kaMongameli, uMphathintambo loMsekeli kaMphathintambo kumele aziphathe ngendlela elandela iSisekamthetho.

(2) AbaSekeli bakaMongameli, oMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo akumelanga, ngesikhathi bephethe lezozikhundla—

(a)

ngendlela ebalulekileyo kumbe engemgceke, baphathe ezinye izihlalo kuHulumende kumbe benze eminye imisebenzi abayiholelayo;

(b) benze loba yini engahambelani lesikhundla sabo, kumbe bazifake esimeni esilengozi yokudonselana phakathi komlandu womsebenzi wabo lezinye izinto eziphathelane laye siqu sakhe; kumbe

(c)

basebenzise isikhundla sabo, kumbe ulwazi abaluphiweyo, ukuthi bazinothise ngudlu bona ngokwabo kumbe omunye umuntu.

(3) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele udwebe umqulu wokuziphatha kwabaSekeli bakaMongameli, oMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo.

107 Umlandu wabaSekeli bakaMongameli, oMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo

(1) Ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho, wonke uMsekeli kaMongameli, uMphathintambo lomeSkeli kaMphathintambo ulomlandu, ndawonye labanye njalo yena qobo lwakhe, wokuveza kuMongameli konke akwenzayo okuphathelane lomsebenzi wakhe.

(2) Wonke uMsekeli kaMongameli, uMphathintambo loMsekeli kaMphathintambo kumele abekhona edale lePhalamende lakumaKhomithi ePhalamende ukuze aziphendulele mayelana lokuphathelane lalokho okungumlandu wakhe ndawonye labanye kumbe yena ngokwakhe. 108 Isikhathi esibekiweyo sokuba sesikhundleni kwaboMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo

(1) Isikhundla sikaMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo siba lesikhala—

(a)

nxa uMongameli engamsusa esikhundleni;

(b) nxa engatshiya umsebenzi ngokubhalela kuMongameli embikela;

(c) ekuqaliseni umsebenzi kukaMongameli omutsha.

(2) Ngaphansi kwesigatshana (4), uMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo owayelilunga ledale lePhalamende ngesikhathi esiba nguMpathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo utshiya isikhundla ngokufanayo nxa engama ukuba lilunga ledale lePhalamende.

(3) Ngaphansi kwesigatshana (4), uMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo owayengesilunga ledale lePhalamende ngesikhathi esiba nguMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo utshiya isikhundla ngokufanayo nxa kungenzakala ukuthi kube lesimo ebesingambangela ukuthi emukwe isihlalo sakhe nga ubelilunga ledale lePhalamende.

(4) Ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho, ekudilizweni kwedale lePhalamende, oMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo baqhubeka besezikhundleni zabo uMongameli omutsha okhethiweyo aze aqalise umsebenzi ngemva kokhetho-jikelele. 109 IVoti yokungabi lethemba kuHulumende

(1) ISenethi leNdlu yabaMeli, ngesinqumo esenziwe ndawonye ngamalunga afika kumbe ukudlula ingxenye ezimbili kwezintathu (2/3) zamalunga azo wonke, zingaba lakho ukunika iVoti yokungabi lethemba kuHulumende.

(2) Injongo yokunquma ukunika iVoti yokungabi lethemba kuHulumende ingaqhutshwa kuphela nxa—

(a)

isiKhulumeli sedale lePhalamende sinikwe umbiko wokwenzenjalo esikhathini esingamalanga ayisikhombisa kumbe ukwedlula; njalo

(b) umbiko waleyonjongo usayinwe yingxenye kumbe ukwedlula eyamalunga wonke eNdlu yabaMeli.

(3) Injongo yokunika iVoti yokungabi lethemba kuHulumende—

(a)

kumele iqhutshwe iziNdlu zombili zedale lePhalamende zihlezi phakathi kwamalanga angamatshumi amabili lanye (21) iSikhulumeli sithole umbiko waleyonjongo; njalo

(b) kumele ivotelwe phakathi kokuhlala kwedale kasikhombisa okulandelanayo kusukela mhla iqhutshwayo; kungenjalo iyathathwa njengokuthi kayiphumelelanga.

(4) Nxa idale lePhalamende lingapasisa iVoti yokwemukwa kwesikhundla kuHulumende, uMongameli kumele, phakathi kwamalanga alitshumi lane (14) ivoti iqhutshiwe—

(a)

asuse bonke oMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo ezikhundleni, ngaphandle nxa behle batshiya umsebenzi ngenxa yesinqumo, abesefaka abanye abantu ezikhundleni zabo; kumbe

(b) adilize idale lePhalamende abesebizela ukhetho-jikelele phakathi kwamalanga angamatshumi ayisitshiyagalolunye (90).

(5) Nxa uMongameli engasenzanga njengokubalulwe kusigatshana (4) phakathi kwamalanga alitshumi lane iVoti yokungabi lethemba kuHulumende iqhutshiwe, idale lePhalamende liyadilizwa. ISAHLUKWANA 4 Imisebenzi Yeziphathamandla 110 Imisebenzi ebhekane loMongameli kanye leKhabhinethi

(1) UMongameli ulamandla awanikwa yilesi Sisekamthethokanye laloba yiwuphi uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumbe eminye imithetho, kugoqela leyo edingakalayo ukuze imisebenzi yeNhloko yeSizwe ifezeke.

(2) Ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho, uMongameli ulomlandu—

(a)

wokuvuma lokusayina iMithetho esavivinywayo;

(c)

wokubizela iNdlu yabaMeli, iSenethi ledale lePhalamende emhlanganweni ongahlelwanga ukuze kuqhutshwe umsebenzi oqakathekileyo;

(b) wokudlulisela umthetho osavivinywayo eMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho ukuze athole umbono lokucetshiswa mayelana lokuba kwawo semthethweni;

(d) wokuqhatsha abantu ezikhundleni ezichazwe ukuba zigcwaliswe ngumuntu oqhatshwe nguMongameli yiSisekamthetho kumbe nguMthetho;

(e)

(f)

wokunqolonga ukhetho ngaphansi kwezimiso zalesi Sisekamthetho;

wokubizela amavoti okudinga imibono kazulu mayelana lendaba ezitshiyeneyo kulandelwa izimiso zomthetho;

(g) wokuphumisa ijumo amabutho omvikela;

(h) wokunika izicoco lemivuzo;

(i)

(j)

wokuqhatsha amanxusa, abameli abalamandla okuthatha izinqumo endaweni kaHulumende labameli beZimbabwe kwamanye amazwe; njalo ukwamukela lokunanza abameli bamanye amazwe kweleZimbabwe;

(3) Ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho, iKhabhinethi ilomlandu—

(a)

wokuqondisa imisebenzi kaHulumende;

(c)

wokuqoqa, ukusungula lokusebenzisa imithetho yelizwe;

(e)

Wokucebisa uMongameli.

(b) wokuqhuba imisebenzi kaHulumende edale lePhalamende;

(d) ukulungisa lokusebenzisa izibopho/zelizwe;

(4) Ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho, uMongameli angaphetha kumbe aqhube imithetho yomhlaba wonke jikelele, izivumelwano lamanye amazwe kanye lezinye izinhlanganiso zomhlaba wonke jikelele.

(5) Isinqumo esenziwa nguMongameli kumele sibe ngesilotshwe phansi nxa sithathwe maqondana lomthetho.

(6) Ekuqhubeni imisebenzi ephathelane lesikhundla sakhe, uMongameli kumele asebenze ecetshiswa yiKhabhinethi, ngaphandle nxa esenza okuphathelane lokuchazwe kusigatshana (2) ophezulu. 111 Impi lokuthula

(1) UMongameli ulamandla okuqalisa impi lokuletha uxolo, njalo kumele abikele iSenethi leNdlu yabaMeli phakathi kwamalanga ayisikhombisa (7) okuhlala kwalawo madale kusukela mhla ethatha leso sinqumo.

(2) ISenethi leNdlu yabaMeli, ngesinqumo ezisenze ndawonye Sisekelwa yingxenye ezimbili kwezintathu (2/3) zamalunga wonke edale lePhalamende, zingabopha ukuthi isinqumo sokuqalisa impi kasigenqulwe.

(3) Lapho osekunqunywe khona lidale lePhalamende ukuthi isinqumo sokuqalisa impi kumele sigenqulwe, uMongameli kumele enze yonke imizamo enelisekayo yokuphuma kuleyompi, enanzelela kakuhle indingeko yokuphepha kwabantu kanye lempahla yeZimbabwe. 112 Amandla okuxolela

(1) UMongameli, ngemva kokubonisana leKhabhinethi, angasebenzisa amandla okuxolela, okutsho ukuthi—

(a)

anganika uxolo emuntwini loba ngubani owethweswa kumbe otholakale elecala lokwephula umthetho loba yiwuphi;

(b) angamisa ukubethwa kwesigwebo maqondana lecala loba yiliphi okwesikhathi esingabekwanga kumbe esithile;

(c)

afake isigwebo esilukhuni okungcono endaweni yaleso esibethiweyo ecaleni loba yiliphi; kumbe

(d) amise okwesikhathi esithile kumbe esule isigwebo sonke kumbe ingxenye yesigwebo secala loba yiliphi kumbe loba yini engabe ithathelwe umuntu njengesijeziso sokwenza icala loba yiliphi;

njalo angafaka iziqondiso eziphathelane lalolo xolo, ukumiswa, ukuguqulwa kumbe ukwesulwa kwesigwebo.

(2) Nxa muntu oyisizalwane seZimbabwe engatholakala elecela kwelinye ilizwe elokwephula umthetho obusa kulelozwe, uMongameli ulakho ukupha isinqumo sokuthi lelocala kalinanzwa njengecala ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho kumbe omunye umthetho obusayo kweleZimbabwe.

(3) Ukunikwa koxolo kumbe ukumiswa kwesigwebo emuntwini otholakale elecala kumbe ukuguqulwa loba ukwesulwa kwesigwebo kumele kwethulwe egwalweni lukaHulumende, iGazette. 113 Izikhathi uzulu ebhekane lomumo omanzonzo

(1) UMongameli ulakho, ngokwethula kugwalo lukaHulumende olweGazette, ukuthi abethe ukuba kukhona isimo esimanzonzo esibhekane lozulu elizweni lonke kumbe emhlubulweni othile weZimbabwe.

(2)Isinqumo sokubetha ukuthi kukhona isimo esimanzonzo esibhekane lozulu siphela Amandla aso ngemva kwensuku ezilitshumi lane kusukela mhla sisethulwayo egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette ngaphandle nxa, kungakedluli leso sikhathi,

leso sinqumo singaqiniswa yingxenye ezimbili kwezintathu (2/3) zamalunga wonke edale lePhalamende ahlanganisela idale leSenethi leNdlu yabaMeli zihlezi ndawonye.

(3) Nxa idale lePhalamende lingadilizwa esikhathini sensuku ezilitshumi lane kubethwe isinqumo sokuthi uzulu ubhekane lesimo esimanzonzo, lesosinqumo siphela Amandla aso ngemva kwensuku ezingamatshumi amabili lanye, kusukela mhla sisethulwayo egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette, ngaphandle nxa phakathi kwaleso sikhathi isinqumo sakhona singaqiniswa linengi lamalunga wonke edale lePhalamende ehlezi ndawonye njengeSenethi leNdlu yabaMeli.

(4) Isinqumo sokubetha ukuthi kukhona isimo esimanzonzo esibhekane lozulu esesiqiniswe ngaphansi kwesigatshana (2) kumbe (3) siba lamandla okwenyanga ezintathu kusukisela mhla sisethulwayo egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette, ngaphandle nxa sike sagenqulwa kumbe saphela amandla aso ngaphansi kwasonalesi Sigaba.

(5) Nxa isinqumo sokubetha ukuthi kukhona isimo esimanzonzo esibhekane lozulu singaqiniswanga ekuhlolisisweni kwaso lidale lePhalamende, kumbe nxa, ngenxa yaloba yisiphi isizatho, singazange sihlaziywe lidale lePhalamende esikhathini esibalulwe kulesi Sigaba, uMongameli kumele, ensukwini eziyisikhombisa, ngokwethula egwaleni lukaHulumende olweGazette, agenqule lesosinqumo.

(6) Nxa, ngesinqumo esipasiswe linengi lamalunga ekuhlaleni ndawonye kwedale leSenethi leNdlu yabaMeli, idale lePhalamende linganquma ukuthi isinqumo esibetha ukuthi kulomumo omanzonzo obhekane lozulu—

(a)

kumele siqhubeke okwesinye isikhathi esingedluli inyanga ezintathu, uMongameli kumele, ngokwethula egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette, engezelele ngalesosikhathi kulesosinqumo;

(b) kumele sigenqulwe kumbe sigoqele indawo encane kulechaziweyo, uMongameli kumele angachithi isikhathi, ngokwethula egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette, agenqule lesosinqumo kumbe anciphise indawo esiyichazayo njengokuvunyelweneyo.

(7) Umthethwandaba weSisekamthetho, nxa ungabhalelwa yiloba ngubani ophathekayo, ungahluza ukuba semthethweni—

(a)

kwesinqumo sokubetha ukuthi kukhona isimo esimanzonzo esibhekane lozulu;

(b) kokwengezelela isikhathi sesinqumo esibetha ukuba khona komumo omanzonzo obhekane lozulu.

(8) Loba yiwuphi umthethwandaba ungahluza ukuba semthethweni komthetho loba yiwuphi obekiweyo, kumbe ezinye izenzo eziqhutshiweyo, kulandela isinqumo esibetha ukuba khona komumo omanzonzo obhekane lozulu. ISAHLUKWANA 5 Ugqwetha-nhloko 114 UGqwetha-Nhloko

(1) KuloGqwetha-Nhloko oqhatshwa nguMongameli.

(2) Umuntu oqhatshwe njengoGqwetha-Nhloko uqalisa umsebenzi ngemva kokuthatha izifungo zokuzinikela lezomsebenzi phambi kukaMongameli, kumbe umuntu ovunyelwe nguMongameli, njengokuchazwe kuHlelo 3.

(3) Umuntu uyenelisa ukuqhatshwa njengoGqwetha-Nhloko nxa elezimfanelo ezimanelisa ukuqhatshwa njengomahluleli woMthethwandaba oMkhulu.

(4) Imisebenzi kaGqwetha-Nhloko igoqela—

(a)

ukuma njengomcebisi omkhulu kaHulumende kwezomthetho;

(c)

ukubhalela uHulumende imithetho;

(e)

ukuqhuba eminye imisebenzi engethulelwa uGqwetha-Nhloko nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende;

(b) ukumela uHulumende ekuqhutshweni kwendaba eziphathelane lokuhlalisana kumbe leSisekamthetho;

(d) ukukhuthaza, ukuvikela lokuphakamisa ukubusa komthetho kanye lokuvikela izifiso zikazulu; njalo

njalo uGqwetha-Nhloko angaqhuba leyo misebenzi yena mathupha kumbe esebenzisa abanye abancane kulaye esikhundleni abasebenzela ngaphansi kwekhwapha leziqondiso ezibekiweyo zikaGqwetha-Nhloko.

(5) UGqwetha-Nhloko—

(a)

angahlala emihlanganweni yeKhabhinethi kodwa engela voti;

(c)

ngemvumo yomthethwandaba ofaneleyo, angahlala njengomngane womthethwandaba ekuqhutshweni kodaba olumayelana lokuhlalisana lapho uHulumende engesingxenye yodaba khona.

(b) angahlala njalo akhulume edale leSenethi leNdlini yabaMeli, kodwa engela voti; njalo

115 Ukususwa esikhundleni kukaGqwetha-Nhloko

UMongameli angasusa uGqwetha-Nhloko esikhundleni ngaloba yisiphi isikhathi. ISAHLUKO 6 Isibumbamthetho ISAHLUKWANA1 Igunya Lesibumbamthetho

116 IsiBumbamthetho

ISibumbamthetho seZimbabwe sigoqela iDale lePhalamende loMongameli abasebenza maqondana lalesi Sahluko. 117 Umhlobo lomkhawulo wamandla esiBumbamthetho

(1) Igunya leSibumbamthetho seZimbabwe livelela ebantwini njalo ligxiliswa kanye lokusetshenziswa maqondana lalesi Sisekamthetho yiSibumbamthetho.

(2) Igunya leSibumbamthetho linika iSibumbamthetho amandla—

(a)

okuguqula lesi Sisekamthetho maqondana leSigaba 328;

(c)

okunikeza amanye amandla okubumba imithetho phezu kwenye inhlanganiso kumbe ibumba maqondana leSigaba 134.

(b) okubumba imithetho eletha ukuthula, ukuhleleka kanye lombuso omuhle eZimbabwe; njalo

ISAHLUKWANA 2 Idale Lephalamende 118 Idale lePhalamende

IDale lePhalamende ligoqela idale leSenethi leleNdlu yabaMeli.

119 Umlandu wedale lePhalamende

(1) IDale lePhalamende kumele livikele lesi Sisekamthetho njalo likhuthaze umbuso oqotho kweleZimbabwe.

(2) IDale lePhalamende lilamandla okuba lesiqiniselo sokuthi izimiso zalesi Sisekamthetho ziyalandelwa, kanye lokuthi iSizwe lazo zonke izinhlanganiso labameli bakaHulumende emabangeni wonke baziphatha ngendlela esemthethweni njalo elonda izifiso zelizwe.

(3) Ngaphansi kwesigatshana (2), zonke izinhlanganiso labameli beSizwe emabangeni wonke kumele baziphendulele eDale lePhalamende. ISAHLUKWANA 3 Idale Lesenethi 120 Amalunga abumba iDale leSenethi

(1) IDale leSenethi ligoqela amalunga angamatshumi ayisitshiyagalombili, kusithi-(a)

abayisithupha bakhona bakhethwa esabelweni sinye ngasinye esiyingxenye yokuqunywa okwenziwe iZimbabwe, kulandelwa indlela yokumelwa okulinganiselayo okumiswe kusigatshana (2);

(b) abalitshumi lasithupha bakhona yizinduna, ezimbili zakhona kungezikhethwe yinhlangano yezabelo eyezinduna ezivela ezabelweni ezimbili, kungabalwa izabelo ezisemadolobheni amakhulu, eziyingxenye yokuqunywa okwenziwe iZimbabwe;

(c)

uMongameli loMsekeli kaMongameli weKhansili yeZinduna eyeLizwe; njalo

(d) ababili bakhona bakhethwa ngendlela ephawulwe eMthethweni wezoKhetho ukuze bamele abantu abalobugoga.

(2) Ukhetho lokudinga amaSenetha kumele luqhutshwe kulandelwa uMthetho wezoKhetho, wona okumele ube lesiqiniselo sokuthi amaSenetha aqanjwe kuSigaba

(1)(a) akhethwe ngendlela elinganisela ukumelwa okufaneleyo kwamabandla ezombusazwe—

(a)

kukhangelwa inani lamavoti atholwe ngoncintisayo munye ngamunye esabelweni sinye ngasinye kuKhetho-jikelele olokudinga abaMeli beDale lePhalamende; njalo

(b) lapho amabizo abesilisa labesifazane abhalwa khona eluhlwini elandelana etshintshana njalonjalo, kusithi uluhlu lunye ngalunye luqalise ngokubhalwa ibizo lomuntu wesifazane.

121 Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu enelise kumbe engenelisi ukuvotelwa njengelunga ledale leSenethi

(1) Umuntu uyenelisa ukuvotelwa esikhundleni sobuSenetha esiqanjwe kuSigaba 120(a) kumbe (d) nxa—

(a)

ebhalise njengomvoti; njalo

(b) esefikise iminyaka engamatshumi amane kusiya phezulu eyokuzalwa;

ngaphandle nxa engafanelanga ngaphansi kweSigaba (4) kumbe (5).

(2) Umuntu uyenelisa ukukhethwa njengoSenetha-Nduna oqanjwe kuSigaba 120(1)(b) nxa—

(a)

eyinduna; njalo

(b) ebhalise njengomvoti;

ngaphandle nxa engafanelanga ngaphansi kwesigatshana (4) kumbe (5).

(3) Umuntu uyenelisa ukuvotelwa njengoSenetha oqanjwe kuSigaba 120(1)(d) nxa engumuntu olobugoga njengokuchazwe eMthethweni wezoKhetho, ngaphandle nxa engafanelanga ngaphansi kwesigatshana (4) kumbe (5).

(4) Umuntu akafanelanga ukuncintisa kumavoti okudinga uSenetha nxa—

(a)

engafanelanga ngaphansi koHlelo lwesine ukubhalisa njengomvoti;

(b) eminyakeni emihlanu eyandulela ukhetho, etshiye isihlalo edale leSenethi kumbe eNdlini yabaMeli kulandelwa uSigaba 129 (1)(i) ngenxa yokuthi uke watholakala elecala.

(5) Umuntu akafanelanga ukuncintisa kumavoti okudinga uSenetha ekhethweni lwesihlalo esisodwa nxa engumMeli weDale lePhalamende. 122 UMongameli wedale leSenethi

(1) Ekuhlaleni kwalo kwakuqala kulandela uKhetho-jikelele njalo kungakenziwa okunye konke, iDale leSenethi kumele likhethe isiphathamandla esihola izinto esaziwa ngokuthi nguMongameli weDale leSenethi.

(2) Kungavele kube lesikhexe esikhundleni sikaMongameli weDale leSenethi, kuyamele iDale leSenethi likhethe umuntu wokugcwalisa leso sikhexe masinyazanyana nje.

(3) Umuntu uyanelisa ukuvotelwa esikhundleni sikaMongameli weDale leSenethi nxa engu, kumbe eseke waba nguSenetha, kumbe efanele ukuvotelwa ukuthi angene eDale leSenethi.

(4) Ukhetho lokudinga umuntu ozaphatha isikhundla sikaMongameli weDale leSenethi kumele luqhutshwe nguNobhala weDale lePhalamende eholwa yiKomithi ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe, ivoti yomuntu ibe yimfihlo yakhe kulandelwa iziLawulo ezimiyo, njalo imiphumela yakhona kumele imenyezelwe khonaphokhonapho.

(5) Engakaqalisi umsebenzi wakhe, uMongameli weDale leSenethi kumele athathe, phambi kukaNdunankulu waboMahluli kumbe omlandelayo emandleni ongabe ekhona, isifungo sokwethembeka lesokuqala umsebenzi njengokuchazwe eHlelweni lwesithathu.

(6) USenetha osekhethwe ukuba nguMongameli weDale leSenethi uhle atshiye isikhundla sokuba nguSenetha, njalo isikhexe asitshiyileyo kumele sigcwaliswe kulandelwa uMthetho wezoKhetho.

(7) UMongameli weDale leSenethi angatshiya isikhundla sakhe ngokuma phambi kweDale leSenethi elibikela ngenjongo yakhe eyokutshiya umsebenzi, kumbe nxa iDale lingahlali ngalesosikhathi, ngokunikeza uNobhala weDale lePhalamende incwadi elaleso saziso.

(8) UMongameli weDale leSenethi kumele aphume esikhundleni—

(a)

mhla iDale leSenethi liqalayo ukuhlala ngemva koKhetho-jikelele;

(c)

ekubeni lilunga leDale lePhalamende kumbe uSomlomo;

(e)

nxa kungaba khona umumo omphoqayo ukuba atshiye isikhundla, nxa kuyikuthi ube enguSenetha; kumbe

(b) ekungeneni kwakhe omunye umsebenzi ophathelane lozulu kumbe ekuqhatshweni kwakhe ngomunye umuntu;

(d) ekubeni nguMsekeli kaMongameli, kumbe uMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo;

(f)

nxa kungathathwa isinqumo sokuthi asuswe esikhundleni ngamalunga ayisilinganiso sengxenye ezimbili kwezintathu eDale leSenethi.

123 UMsekeli kaMongameli wedale leSenethi

(1) Ngokukhulu ukuphangisa okwanelisekayo ngemva kokukhetha uMongameli weDale leSenethi kulandela uKhetho-jikelele, iDale leSenethi kumele likhethe uSenetha oyedwa ukuthi abe nguMsekeli kaMongameli weDale leSenethi.

(2) Sonke isikhathi kusiba lesikhexe esikhundleni soMsekeli kaMongameli weDale lePhalamende, iDale leSenethi kumele, masinyazanyana nje, likhethe umuntu wokugcwalisa leso sikhundla.

(3) Ukhetho lokudinga uMsekeli kaMongameli weDale leSenethi kumele luqhutshwe nguNobhala weDale lePhalamende eholwa yiKomithi ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe, ivoti yomuntu ibe yimfihlo yakhe kulandelwa iziLawulo ezimiyo, njalo imiphumela yakhona kumele imenyezelwe khonapho-khonapho.

(4) Engakaqalisi umsebenzi wakhe, uMsekeli kaMongameli weDale leSenethi kumele athathe, phambi kukaNdunankulu waboMahluli kumbe omlandelayo emandleni ongabe ekhona, isifungo sokwethembeka lesokuqala umsebenzi njengokuchazwe eHlelweni lwesithathu.

(5) UMsekeli kaMongameli weDale leSenethi ulakho ukutshiya umsebenzi ngokuzimela yena mathupha phambi kweDale leSenethi esitsho injongo yakhe, kumbe nxa iSenethi ingahlali ngaleso sikhathi, nokubhala incwadi ayinike uMongameli weDale leSenethi kumbe, nxa uMongameli weDale leSenethi engekho, ayinike uNobhala weDale lePhalamende.

(6) UMsekeli kaMongameli weDale leSenethi kumele aphume esikhundleni—

(a)

nxa engasenguye Senetha;

(c)

nxa engaba nguMsekeli kaMongameli, uMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo;

(b) nxa engaba nguSomlomo;

(d) nxa kungathathwa isinqumo sokuthi asuswe esikhundleni ngamalunga ayisilinganiso sengxenye ezimbili kwezintathu eDale leSenethi.

ISAHLUKWANA 4 Indlu Yabameli 124 Amalunga abumba iNdlu Yabameli

(1) INdlu yabaMeli igoqela—

(a)

amalunga angamakhulu amabili letshumi elilodwa akhethwe ngevoti eyimfihlo ezifundeni zokhetho zonke ezingamakhulu amabili letshumi elilodwa eziqunyiweyo eZimbabwe; njalo

(b) okwempilo yamaDale amabili ePhalamende kusukela mhla lesi Sisekamthetho siqala ukusebenza, amalunga esifazane angamatshumi ayisithupha phezulu, abayisithupha bevela kusabelo sinye ngasinye seZimbabwe njengokuqunya kwayo, bekhethwa ngendlela yokumelwa kwamabandla ehlukeneyo ezombusazwe kusiya ngenani lamavoti atholwe ngabancintisa ekhethweni bemele amabandla atshiyeneyo kuKhethojikelele lokudinga abameli bezifunda zokhetho kulezo zabelo.

(2) Ukhetho lokudinga amaLunga eNdlu yabaMeli kumele luqhutshwe kulandelwa uMthetho wezoKhetho.

(3) Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu anelise ukubhalisa njengomvoti kanye lokuvota kuKhetho lokudinga amaLunga eNdlu yabaMeli kuphawulwe eHlelweni lweSine. 125 Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu enelise ukuvotelwa eNdlini Yabameli lokungenza angafaneli ukuvotelwa

(1) Umuntu uyenelisa ukuvotelwa njengeLunga leNdlu yabaMeli nxa—

(a)

ebhalise njengomvoti; njalo

(b) nxa esefikise iminyaka engamatshumi amabili lanye yokuzalwa;

ngaphandle nxa engafanelanga ngaphansi kwesigatshana (2) kumbe (3).

(2) Umuntu akafanelanga ukuvotelwa njengeLunga leNdlu yabaMeli nxa—

(a)

engafanelanga ngaphansi koHlelo lweSine ukubhalisa njengomvoti; kumbe;

(b) eminyakeni emihlanu eyandulela ukhetho, ube ephume esihlalweni seDale leSenethi kumbe eleNdlu yabaMeli ngendlela ebalulwe kuSigaba 129(1)

(i) ngenxa yokuba eke watholakala elecala emthethwandaba.

(3) Umuntu akafanelanga ukuvotelwa ekhethweni lokudinga umMeli oyedwa eNdlini yabaMelei nxa elilunga leDale lePhalamende. 126 USomlomo weNdlu Yabameli

(1) Ekuhlaleni kwalo kwakuqala kulandela uKhetho-jikelele njalo kungakenziwa okunye konke, iDale leNdlu yabaMeli kumele likhethe isiphathamandla esihola izinto esaziwa ngokuthi nguSomlomo.

(2) Kungavele kube lesikhexe esikhundleni sikaSomlomo, kuyamele iDale leNdlu yabaMeli likhethe umuntu wokugcwalisa leso sikhexe masinyazanyana nje.

(3) Umuntu uyanelisa ukuvotelwa njengoSomlomo nxa engu kumbe eseke waba liLunga leNdlu yabaMeli kumbe efanele ukuvotelwa ukuthi angene eNdlini yabaMeli.

(4) Ukhetho lokudinga umuntu ozaphatha isikhundla sikaSomlomo kumele luqhutshwe nguNobhala weDale lePhalamende eholwa yiKomithi ebona ngezoKhetho

eZimbabwe, ivoti yomuntu ibe yimfihlo yakhe kulandelwa iziLawulo ezimiyo, njalo imiphumela yakhona kumele imenyezelwe khonapho-khonapho.

(5) Engakaqalisi umsebenzi wakhe, uSomlomo kumele athathe, phambi kukaNdunankulu waboMahluli kumbe omlandelayo emandleni ongabe ekhona, isifungo sokwethembeka lesokuqala umsebenzi njengokuchazwe eHlelweni lweSithathu.

(6) ILunga leNdlu yabaMeli eselikhethwe ukuba nguSomlomo akaseyilo Lunga leNdlu yabaMeli, njalo isikhexe asesitshiyile esihlalweni kumele sigcwaliswe kulandelwa uMthetho wezoKhetho.

(7) USomlomo ulakho ukutshiya umsebenzi ngokuzimela yena mathupha phambi kweNdlu yabaMeli esitsho injongo yakhe, kumbe nxa iNdlu yabaMeli ingahlali ngaleso sikhathi, ngokubhala incwadi ayinike uNobhala weDale lePhalamende.

(8) USomlomo kumele atshiye isikhundla sakhe—

(a)

Mhla iNdlu yabaMeli ihlalayo okwakuqala ngemva kokhetho-jikelele;

(c)

ekubeni kwakhe liLunga leNdlu yabaMeli kumbe uMongameli weDale leSenethi;

(b) ekwamukeleni kwakhe omunye umsebenzi ophathelane lozulu kumbe ekuqhatshweni kwakhe ngomunye umuntu;

(d) ekubeni kwakhe nguMsekeli kaMongameli, uMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo;

(e)

(f)

nxa kungaba khona umumo omphoqayo ukuba atshiye isikhundla, nxa kuyikuthi ube eliLunga leNdlu yabaMeli; kumbe

nxa kungathathwa isinqumo sokuthi asuswe esikhundleni ngamalunga ayisilinganiso sengxenye ezimbili kwezintathu eNdlini yabaMeli.

127 UMsekeli kaSomlomo weNdlu Yabameli

(1) Ngokukhulu ukuphangisa okwanelisekayo kulandele uKhetho-jikelele, iNdlu yabaMeli kumele ikhethe oyedwa wamalunga ayo ukuthi abe nguMsekeli kaSomlomo.

(2) Sonke isikhathi kusiba lesikhexe esikhundleni soMsekeli kaSomlomo, iNdlunyabaMeli kumele, masinyazanyana nje, ikhethe umuntu wokugcwalisa lesosikhundla.

(3) Ukhetho lokudinga umuntu ozaphatha isikhundla sioMsekeli kaSomlomo kumele luqhutshwe nguNobhala weDale lePhalamende eholwa yiKomithi ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe, ivoti yomuntu ibe yimfihlo yakhe kulandelwa iziLawulo ezimiyo, njalo imiphumela yakhona kumele imenyezelwe khonapho-khonapho.

(4) Engakaqalisi umsebenzi wakhe, uMsekeli kaSomlomo kumele athathe, phambi kukaNdunankulu waboMahluli kumbe omlandelayo emandleni ongabe ekhona, isifungo sokwethembeka lesokuqala umsebenzi njengokuchazwe eHlelweni lweSithathu.

(5) UMsekeli kaSomlomo ulakho ukutshiya umsebenzi ngokuzimela yena mathupha phambi kweNdlu yabaMeli esitsho injongo yakhe, kumbe nxa iNdlu yabaMeli ingahlali ngaleso sikhathi, ngokubhala incwadi ayinike uSomlomo kumbe, nxa uSomlomo engekho, uNobhala weDale lePhalamende.

(6) uMsekeli kaSomlomo kumele atshiye isikhundla sakhe—

(a)

ekumeni kwakhe ukuba liLunga leNdlu yabaMeli;

(b) ekubeni kwakhe nguMongameli weDale leSenethi;

(c)

ekubeni kwakhe nguMsekeli kaMongameli, uMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo; kumbe

(d) nxa kungathathwa isinqumo sokuthi asuswe esikhundleni ngamalunga ayisilinganiso sengxenye ezimbili kwezintathu eNdlini yabaMeli. ISAHLUKWANA 5 Isikhathi Esibekiweyo Sokuphatha Isikhundla Kwamalunga Edale Lephalamende 128 Isifungo seLunga leDale lePhalamende

(1) ILunga leDale liPhalamende lingakathathi isihlalo salo eDale lePhalamende, kumele lithathe isifungo seLunga leDale lePhalamende njengokuphawulwe eHlelweni lweSithathu.

(2) Isifungo esiqanjwe kusigatshana (1) kumele sithathelwe phambi kuka nobhala weDale lePhalamende. 129 Isikhathi esibekiweyo sokuhlala esikhundleni kweLunga ledale lePhalamende

(1) Isihlalo seLunga leDale lePhalamende siba lesikhexe—

(a)

ekudilizweni kweDale lePhalamende;

(c)

ekubeni nguMongameli kumbe uMsekeli kaMongameli kweLunga;

(e)

nxa iLunga lingasafanelanga ukubhalisa njengomvoti;

(b) ekutshiyeni kweLunga isihlalo salo ngokubhala incwadi ebikela uMongameli weSenethi kumbe uSomlomo, njengokufaneleyo;

(d) ekubeni nguMongameli weDale leSenethi kumbe uSomlomo kumbe ilunga lenye iNdlu;

(f)

nxa iLunga lingaswelakala eNdlini lapho elililunga khona okwamalanga angamatshumi amabili lanye alandelanayo okungela mvumo kaSomlomo kumbe uMongameli weSenethi, kusiya ngendlu elikiyo, lapho iNdlu ihlanganayo khona, njalo leyoNdlu ibisivotela ngenani lamavoti ayisilinganiso sengxenye yamalunga wonke aleyoNdlu ukuthi lesosihlalo kasibe lesikhexe;

(g) nxa ilunga lingemukela umsebenzi ophathelane lozulu kumbe njengelunga lenhlanganiso ephathelane lomthetho, inhlanganiso eholwa nguHulumende, ikhansili yesabelo kumbe eyedolobho elikhulu kumbe uhulumende wesabelo kumbe ukuqhatshwa njengesisebenzi senhlanganiso ephathelane lomthetho, ikhansili yesabelo kumbe eyedolobho elikhulu kumbe uhulumende wesabelo;

(h) nxa iLunga beliyisisebenzi sikazulu kumbe ilunga loba isisebenzi senhlanganiso ephathelane lomthetho, inhlanganiso eholwa nguHulumende, ikhansili yesabelo kumbe eyedolobho elikhulu kumbe uhulumende wesabelo mhla ebekwa njengeLunga leDale lePhalamende, abesesehluleka ukutshiya lesosikhundla, ukuba lilunga kumbe lowomsebenzi ensukwini ezingamatshumi amathathu kusukela lelo langa;

(i)

nxa ilunga lingatholakala lilecala:

(i) eZimbabwe eligoqela ukwephula ithemba, ukungathembakali kumbe ubudlwangudlwangu;

(ii) phandle kweZimbabwe eligoqela ukuziphatha ngendlela eyokuthi

nga lenzakale eZimbabwe, bekuyabe kulicala elengame ukwephula ithemba, ukungathembakali kumbe ubudlwangudlwangu;

njalo ligwetshelwe ukuhlala ejele okwezinyanga eziyisithupha kusiya phezulu kungela thuba lokubhadala ifayindi esikhundleni sokuhlala ejele kumbe elokuphiwa esinye isijeziso esingasikuhlala ejele, ngaphandle nxa iLunga lingakhalaza icala lalo beselisesulwa kumbe isigwebo sokuhlala ejele besesisehliswa ngaphansi kwenyanga eziyisithupha kumbe isijeziso esingasikuhlala ejele sesithatha isikhundla sesigwebo sakuqala;

(j)

nxa iLunga lingathathwa njengomuntu osehluleke ukukhokha izikwelede zakhe ngomthetho osebenza eZimbabwe njalo engakalungisiswa leso simo kumbe ukukhululwa, kumbe nxa iLunga lenze isivumelwano labantu elilezikwelede labo ngaphansi komthetho ofanayo esingachithwanga kumbe ukubekwa eceleni;

(k) nxa iLunga lingasayingxenye yebandla lombusazwe elikade likilo ngesikhathi livotelwa ukungena ePhalamende, njalo ibandla lakhona beselibhalela uSomlomo kumbe uMongameli weSenethi, kusiya ngokuthi ulilunga layiphi iNdlu, liphawula ukuthi leloLunga kaliseyingxenye yalelobandla;

(l)

nxa iLunga, ebelingela bandla lezombusazwe eliyingxenye yalo ngesikhathi livotelwa ukungena ePhalamende, lingaba lilunga lebandla lezombusazwe;

(m) nxa iLunga lingatholakala ukuthi lilomkhuhlane wengqondo kumbe inkinga yokucabanga ngaphansi komthetho ongabe usebenza eZimbabwe; kumbe;

(n) nxa iLunga lingatholakala lilecala ngaphansi koMthetho wezoKhetho njalo labethwa nguMthethwandaba oPhezulu njengomuntu ongasafanelanga ukubhalisa njengomvoti kumbe ukuvota kuloba yiluphi ukhetho.

(2) ILunga eliqanjwe kusigatshana (1)(i) eseliqansise isikhalazo mayelana lesigwebo salo lingaqhubeka lisenza imisebenzi yalo njengeLunga njalo lithola iholo lalo, udaba lwesikhalazo salo luze luphele, ngaphandle nxa umthethwandaba ungabopha ukuthi kumele livalelwe entolongweni kusamelelwe umphumela wodaba lwesikhalazo sakhe.

(3) ILunga leDale lePhalamende eliba nguMgcinisihlalo wekhansili yesabelo liyatshiya isihlalo ebelisiphethe lingakaqalisi umsebenzi njengoMgcinisihlalo. ISAHLUKWANA 6 Amandla Ezibumbamthetho Lamanye Athile 130 Amandla lemisebenzi yedale leSenethi kanye leNdlu Yabameli

(1) Ngaphandle kwezimiso ezibekwe eHlelweni lwesiHlanu, ekusebenziseni igunya lawo lesibumbamthetho, womabili amaDale – eleSenethi kanye leNdlu yabaMeli, alamandla okukhanda, ukulungisa, ukuhlaziya kumbe ukujikela phandle loba yiwuphi umthetho.

(2) Phezu kwemisebenzi yawo ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho, amaDale athi eleSenethi leleNdlu yabaMeli angaba lakho ukwenza eminye imisebenzi ethulelwe kumbe ethweswe phezu kwawo ngaloba yiwuphi umthetho.

131 IMithetho eyethulwa liDale lePhalamende lokuqhutshwa kwayo ukuze igcwaliseke

(1) Igunya leDale lePhalamende elokulungisa umthetho lisetshenziswa ngokupasisa iMithetho emiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende nguMthetho osaKhandwayo—

(a)

osewethulwe njalo wapasiswa eziNdlini zombili zeDale lePhalamende; njalo

(b) Osuvunywe njalo wasayinwa nguMongameli; kulandelwa izimiso zalesi Sisekamthetho.

(3) Amabala okupasisa iMithetho emiswe yiPhalamende ngathi “Opasiswe yiPhalamende loMongameli welizwe leZimbabwe”, kumbe amanye atsho okufanayo.

(4) Indlela okumele ilandelwe yiNdlu yabaMeli kanye leDale leSenethi maqondana loKhandwa kweMithetho iphawulwe eHlelweni lwesiHlanu.

(5) Ngemva kokuba uMthetho osaKhandwayo usupasisiwe eziNdlini zombili kulandelwa izimiso ezikuHlelo lwesiHlanu, uMongameli weDale leSenethi kumbe uSomlomo, kusiya ngeNdlu yakhona, kumele aphuthume—

(a)

abangele ukuthi wethulwe kuMongameli ukuze awuvume njalo awusayine, ndawonye lencwadi yobufakazi edingakalayo ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho ukuthi iphelekezele lowoMthetho oKhandwayo; njalo

(b) akhuphe isaziso kuzulu esitsho ilanga lapho lowoMthetho osaKhandwa wasiwa kuMongameli.

(6) Nxa uMthetho ungasiwa kuMongameli ukuze awuvume kanye lokuwusayina, kumele, phakathi kwamalanga angamatshumi amabili lanye—

(a)

awuvume abesewusayina, abuye abangele ukuthi ukhutshwe eGwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette ngokukhulu ukuphangisa; kumbe

(b) nxa ewubona usephula izimiso zeSisekamthetho kumbe ebe leminye imibono engahambelani lawo, abuyisele lowoMthetho osaKhandwa eDale lePhalamende udlula ezandleni zikaNobhala weDale lePhalamende, ndawonye lezizatho ezilotshwe zaphelela ukuthi kungani ethandabuza ukwemukela lowoMthetho osaKhandwa kanye lesicelo sokuthi lowoMthetho osaKhandwa uke uhlolisiswe.

(7) Lapho kube loMthetho osaKhandwayo obuyiselwe eDale lePhalamende ngezimiso zesigatshana (6)(b), uSomlomo kumele, masinyazanyana nje, abizele ukuthi iNdlu yabameli ihlale, yona okumele—

(a)

ikhangelisise njalo uMthetho osakhandwayo loyana ikhangela lezizatho zikaMongameli zokuthandabuza; kumbe;

(b) iwupasise, unjalo kumbe isike yawulungisisa, ngesilinganiso sokubili kungxenye ezintathu zenani lamaLunga wonke aweNdlu yabaMeli;

njalo loba kukuwuphi umphumela kuleyo yomibili uSomlomo kumele abangele uMthetho lo osakhandwayo ukuthi uqhutshelwe uMongameli masinyane ukuze awuvume njalo awusayine, njalo akhiphe isaziso kuzulu esitsho ilanga lapho lowomthetho oKhandwayo uhanjiswe khona kuMongameli.

(8) Nxa uMthetho osaKhandwayo ohanjiswe kuMongameli kulandelwa izimiso zesigatshana (7) ukhangele ngokupheleleyo zonke izizatho zikaMongameli zokuthandabuza ukuwuvuma, uMongameli kumele awuvume lowoMthetho okhandwayo njalo awusayine phakathi kwamalanga angamatshumi amabili lanye abesebangela

ukuthi ukhutshwe egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette ngokuphangisa, kodwa nxa uMongameli elokhu elezizatho zokuthandabuza mayelana lalowoMthetho oKhandwayo, kumele, phakathi kwasonaleso sikhathi—

(a)

awuvume njalo awusayine lowoMthetho okhandwayo, kungelani lezizatho zakhe zokuthandabuza; kumbe

(b) Adlulisele uMthetho wakhona oKhandwayo phezulu eMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho ukuze athole ukucetshiswa ngokuhambelana kwawo lezimiso zeSisekamthetho.

(9) Nxa uMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho, ngemva kokudluliselwa uMthetho osaKhandwayo njengokuqanjwe ngaphansi kwesigatshana (8), ungacebisa ukuthi lowoMthetho uhambelana lezimiso zeSisekamthetho, uMongameli kumele awuvume njalo awusayine khonokho-khonokho nje abuye awubangele ukuthi ukhutshwe egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette masinyazanyana nje.

(10) Nxa uMthetho oKhandwayo ungasiwa kuMongameli ukuze awuvume njalo awusayine ungaphelekezelwanga yincwadi yobufakazi njengokudingwa yizimiso zalesi Sisekamthetho, uMongameli akumelanga awuvume lowoMthetho loba ukuwusayina incwadi yobufakazi leyo ize ibe khona, kodwa kumele abangele ukuthi uNobhala weDale lePhalamende ahle aziswe, khonapho-khonapho ngencwadi elotshiweyo, ukuthi incwadi yobufakazi kayithunyezelwanga ndawonye loMthetho oKhandwayo. 132 Ukuqalisa ukusebenza kweMithetho eyethulwa liDale lePhalamende

UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende uqala ukusebenza ekuqaliseni kwelanga okhutshwe ngalo egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette, kumbe ekuqaliseni kwelinye ilanga elingabe liqonywe yilowo Mthetho kumbe omunye omisiweyo. 133 Ukubhalwa kweMithetho eyethulwa lidale lePhalamende

(1) Nxa uMongameli esevume njalo wasayina uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, uNobhala weDale lePhalamende kumele adlulise ugwalo lwawo olumsulwa, olusayinwe nguMongameli lwafakwa lophawu lukazulu olweZmbabwe ukutshengisa ukuthi ngolweqiniso, ukuthi luyebhaliswa emawofisini kaMbhali woMthethwandaba oPhezulu, njalo lologwalo luyibufakazi obupheleleyo bezimiso zalowoMthetho ngaphandle nxa uMthetho wakhona ungaguqulwa ngaphansi koMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende oqanjwe kusigatshana (2).

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungaphawula ukuthi umthetho obunjiweyo, kumbe ingxenye yawo, ukhutshwe ngendlela eguquliweyo njalo ungamisa ukuthi—

(a)

ekukhutshweni kwawo, inguquko yona leyo yiyo yodwa eyisimo soqotho somthetho oqukethwe kiso;

(b) ugwalo olulaleyo nguquko kumele luhanjiswe emawofisini kaMbhali woMthethwandaba oPhezulu; njalo

(c)

Ugwalo olutshiywe emawofisini kaMbhali woMthethwandaba oPhezulu yilo oluyibufakazi obupheleleyo bezimiso zonke zemithetho equkethwe kilo.

(3) Ukuma koMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumbe ukuguqulwa kogwalo eliwuquketheyo akuyi ngokubhaliswa kwawo kumbe ukufakwa ngaphansi kwalesiSigaba.

134 Umthetho osekelayo

IDale lePhalamende lilakho, emthethweni omiswe yiPhalamende, ukunikeza amandla okubumba imigomo ebalulwe njalo elezinjongo ezichazwe kulowoMthetho, kodwa—

(a)

amandla-nsika eDale lePhalamende awokubumba imithetho akumelanga anikezwe komunye;

(b) imigomo akumelanga inciphise kumbe yephule amalungelo kanye lezinkululeko ezibalulwe eMqulwini wamaLungelo;

(c)

imigomo kumele ihambelane loMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende eyilungiselwe wona;

(d) lowoMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele ubalule umkhawulo wamandla, uhlobo kanye lokugoqelwayo emigomeni engaba lakho ukwenziwa, lezimiso kanye lokunye okuphathelane lalowo mgomo;

(e)

(f)

imigomo kayilawo amandla omthetho ngaphandle nxa ikhutshwe egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette;

imigomo kumele ilethwe phambi kweNdlu yabaMeli kulandelwa izimiso zeZilawulo zayo ezimiyo ibisihanjiswa kuKomithi ebona ngezeMithetho eDale lePhalamende ukuthi iyecutshungulwa. ISAHLUKWANA 7 Ukuqhutshwa Komsebenzi Edale Lephalamende

135 Inhloko yedale lePhalamende

(1) USomlomo nguye oyinhloko yeDale lePhalamende kodwa kumele aqhube imisebenzi yakhe ngendlela yokuthi uholwa yiZilawulo ezimiyo.

(2) Eholwa yiziLawulo ezimiyo, uMongameli weDale leSenethi nguye umlandeli weNhloko yeDale lePhalamende njalo uthatha isikhundla seNhloko nxa uSomlomo engenelisi ngenxa yezizatho ezitshiyeneyo. 136 Abantu abakhokhela umsebenzi edale lePhalamende ngu—

(1)Umuntu okhokhelayo sonke isikhathi iDale leSenethi lihlezi kumele kube

(a)

Mongameli weDale leSenethi kumbe, nxa engekho, uMsekeli kaMongameli weDale leSenethi; kumbe

(b) Nxa uMongameli loMsekeli kaMongameli weDale leSenethi bengekho, uSenetha owakhethelwa leyonjongo liDale leSenethi, kodwa lowo Senetha akumelanga abe enguMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo.

(2) Umuntu okhokhelayo sonke isikhathi iDale leNdlu yabaMeli kuhlezi kumele kube ngu—

(a)

Somlomo kumbe, nxa engekho, uMsekeli kaSomlomo; kumbe

(b) Nxa uSomlomo loMsekeli kaSomlomo bengekho, iLunga leNdlu yabaMeli elakhethelwa leyonjongo yiNdlu yabaMeli, kodwa leloLunga akumelanga libe linguMphathintambo kumbe uMsekeli kaMphathintambo.

(3) USomlomo, kumbe yena nxa engekho, uMongameli weDale leSenethi, kumele akhokhele ingqubo zonke ezigoqela amaDale womabili eNdlu yabaMeli leleSenethi ehlezi ndawonye.

137 Inani lamalunga okumele abe khona ukuze umsebenzi uqhutshwe eDale lePhalamende

AmaDale eSenethi leNdlu yabaMeli kumele aphawule inani lamaLunga okumele abe khona ngasikhathi sinye ukuze umsebenzi uqhutshwe. 138 Ukuvota kanye lelungelo lokuzwakala eDale lePhalamende

(1) Ngaphandle kwalapho okutshiwo okunye yilesi Sisekamthetho—

(a)

yonke imibuzo eqonywe ukuthi yenzelwe izinqumo eziNdlini zombili zeDale lePhalamende kumele ivunyelwe linengi lamavoti amaLunga aleyoNdlu akhona njalo avotayo ngaleso sikhathi.

(b) umuntu okhokhelayo eziNdlini zombili zeDale lePhalamende akalayo ivoti ekuhumutsheni lasekufakeni izinqumo mayelana laloba yiluphi udaba olulethwe eNdlini;

(c)

nxa amavoti eziNdlini zombili zedale lePhalamende elingene ngapha langapha edabeni oluthile, kutsho ukuthi isiphakamiso leso sesilahlekile.

(2) AbaSekeli bakaMongameli balelungelo lokuhlala lokukhuluma, kodwa hatshi ukuvota, ezindlini zombili zeDale lePhalamende.

(3) OMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo balelungelo lokuhlala lokukhuluma eziNdlini zombili zeDale lePhalamende, kodwa kabala Lungelo lokuvota eNdlini abangasiLunga layo.

(4) Amalunga eKomithi ebona ngezoMthetho eDale lePhalamende lenye IKomithi yePhalamende esebenzelana ndawonye layo alelungelo lokuhlala lokukhuluma ezindlini zombili zeDale lePhalamende ngenjongo yokuthi angenise loba ukuphikisa udaba lwaleyoKomithi olungabe lusendlini, kodwa kabalalo ilungelo lokuvota endlini abangasi Lunga layo.

(5) Nxa kube leLunga leDale lePhalamende, ngaphandle kwaboMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMpathintambo, elingenise umthetho osakhandwayo eNdlini lapho aliLunga khona, leyoNdlu yasipasisa lowoMthetho, iLunga lelo kumbe, nxa lingekho, elinye loba yiliphi iLunga laleyoNdlu, lilelungelo lokuyahlala lokukhuluma kwenye iNdlu leya ngenjongo yokuqhubezela uMthetho wakhona osakhandwayo kwenye leyoNdlu, kodwa kalila Lungelo lokuvota khona. 139 Izilawulo Ezimiyo

(1) Ingqubo zeDale leSenethi lezeNdlu yabaMeli zilawulwa yiziqondiso ezaziwa ngokuthi yiZilawulo ezimiyo, zona ezilungiswa yiziNdlu zitshiyene kumbe zihlangene kusiya ngezicebiso zeKomithi yeMigomo leZilawulo ezimiyo zePhalamende.

(2) Izilawulo ezimiyo zingaphawula—

(a)

ukupasiswa kweMithetho eKhandwayo;

(c)

indlela okumele amandla, amalungelo kanye lokuvikeleka emthethweni kweziNdlu kusetshenziswe lokuphakanyiswa ngayo;

(b) ukuqoqwa kanye lemisebenzi yamaKomithi lokubalula imisebenzi yawo;

(d) ukubuzwa kwaboMphathintambo labaSekeli baboMphathintambo ngamaLunga eDale lePhalamende;

(e)

(f)

indlela okumele amaLunga eDale lePhalamende aziphathe ngayo;

ukusetshenziswa kwelungelo likazulu elokuletha izikhalazo eDale lePhalamende; njalo

(g) iziqondiso nje kanye lokuqhutshwa komsebenzi lezingqubo okuhlelekileyo eziNdlini laphakathi kweziNdlu.

(3) Izindlela lokuqhutshwa kwemisebenzi yeDale lePhalamende lamaKomithi ayo, njengokuphawulwe yiziLawulo ezimiyo, kumele kukhuthaze ukuba mgceke, kukhuthaze ukuphatheka kwamalunga amabandla wonke ezombusazwe aseDale lePhalamende kanye lozulu, njalo kumele kube qotho kulunge futhi.

(4) Yonke iKomithi esungulwe yiziLawulo ezimiyo kumbe ngaphansi kwazo kumele itshengise, ngokusemandleni wonke, ukumelwa kwamabandla ezombusazwe lobulili eDale lePhalamende kumbe eNdlini efanele ukuholwa yilezo ziLawulo ezimiyo. 140 Izethulo lemibiko kaMongameli kudale lePhalamende

(1) UMongemeli, ngaloba yisiphi isikhathi, angaba lakho ukwethula umbiko endlini zombili zeDale lePhalamende, zitshiyene kumbe zihlezi ndawonye.

(2) UMongamli angaba lakho ukuthumela imibiko ezindlini zombili zeDale lePhalamende, njalo wonke umbiko onjalo kumele ubalwe nguMsekeli kaMongameli kumbe uMphathintambo kumbe ngumuntu okhokhela ingqubo zeNdlu yakhona ebhalelweyo.

(3) UMongameli angayahlala eDale lePhalamende ukuze aphendule imibuzo ngodaba oluthile njengokuyabe kumiswe eziLawulweni ezimiyo.

(4) UMongameli, kanye ngomnyaka kumbe ukwedlula, kumele ethule umbiko ezindlini zombili zeDale lePhalamende zihlezi ndawonye mayelana lomumo welizwe, njalo uSomlomo loMongameli weDale leSenethi kumele benze amalungiselelo afaneleyo okuthi iDale lePhalamende lemukele umbiko onjalo. 141 Ukufinyelela lokuphatheka kukazulu emisebenzini yedale lePhalamende IDale lePhalamende kumele—

(a)

lanelisise ukuphatheka kukazulu enhlelweni zalo zokubumba imithetho lakwezinye ezehlukeneyo njalo, ezinhlelweni zamaKomithi alo;

(b) libe lesiqiniselo sokuthi inhlangothi zonke eziphathekayo ziyakhulunyiswa ukuthi zinike imibono mayelana leMithetho efisa ukukhandwa liDale lePhalamende, ngaphandle nxa ukubonisana lokho kungafanelanga kumbe kungafezeki; njalo

(c)

liqhube imisebenzi yalo ngendlela ekhanyayo lokuhlala imihlangano yalo, kanye leyamaKomithi alo, egcekeni, loba nje imigoqo ingafakwa—

(i) eyokugcina ukuhleleka kuhle kwengqubo zePhalamende;

(ii) eyokulawula ukufinyelela kukazulu, kugoqela onozindaba, eDale lePhalamende lakumaKomithi alo;

(iii) eyokuvalela uzulu, kugoqela onozindaba, phandle kwemihlangano ehlalwa ngamaKomithi; njalo

(iv) eyokuvumela ukusetshwa kwabantu njalo, lapho kufanele, ukwaliswa ukungena kumbe ukuxotshwa kwabantu eDale lePhalamende;

kodwa leyo migoqo kumele ibe qotho, ibe lengqondo njalo ichazeke ukufanela kwayo emphakathini olombuso oqotho oyakhelwe phezu kokubaluleka, ukulinganiswa emehlweni omthetho, isithunzi somuntu, ukulingana kanye lenkululeko.

142 Ukuba semthethweni kwengqubo zeDale lePhalamende

(1) Isikhexe emalungeni eDale leSenethi leleNdlu yabaMeli, kumbe ukumiswa umsebenzi kweLunga leDale lePhalamende, akulimisi iDale leSenethi kumbe iNdlu yabaMeli ekuqhubeni imisebenzi yalo.

(2) Ukuthi kube khona umuntu obe kungafanelanga ukuba khona ohlale njalo wavota eDale leSenethi kumbe eNdlini yabaMeli kumbe omane waphatheka ekuqhubekeni kwengqubo yeDale leSenethi kumbe iNdlu yabaMeli akutsho ukuthi imiphumela yaleyongqubo ayiseyi kunanzwa. ISAHLUKWANA 8 Isikhathi Esibekiweyo, Ukudilizwa Lokuhlalwa Kwedale Lephalamende 143 Isikhathi esibekiweyo lokudilizwa kweDale lePhalamende

(1) IDale lePhalamende likhethelwe ukuhlala okwesikhathi esifika iminyaka emihlanu kusukela mhla okhethwe ukuba nguMongameli ethatha isifungo njalo eqalisa umsebenzi ngezimiso zesigaba 94(1)(a), njalo iDale lePhalamende liba ngeselidiliziweyo phakathi kwamabili mhla kusisa kusiba lusuku lwakuqala lokuvota kuKhetho-jikelele olulandelayo olumenyezelwa ngaphansi kweSigaba 144.

(2) UMongameli kumele, ngesimemezelo, adilize iDale lePhalamende nxa iDale leSenethi leNdlu yabaMeli, ehlezi etshiyene, ngamavoti afika kumbe ukwedlula isilinganiso sengxenye ezimbili kwezintathu zenani lamalunga wonke alezozindlu, esepasise isinqumo sokuthi liDilizwe.

(3) UMongameli, ngesimemezelo, angadiliza iDale lePhalamende nxa iDale lePhalamende selale okungelangqondo ukupasisa uMthetho woKwabiwa kwemali oqanjwe kuSigaba 305.

(4) Isinqumo sokudiliza iDale lePhalamende ngezimiso zesigatshana (3), ekubhalelweni liLunga loba liphi eleDale lePhalamende, singabekwa eceleni ukuze sicutshungulwe nguMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho.

(5) Incwadi yokucela ukuthi kucutshungulwe isinqumo sokudiliza iDale lePhalamende kumele isiwe eMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho kusesephakathi kwamalanga ayisikhombisa leso sinqumo sikhutshiwe, njalo—

(a)

uMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho kumele unike impendulo yaleyo ncwadi phakathi kwamalanga alitshumi lane ilethiwe; njalo

(b) kusamelelwe impendulo yoMthethwandaba kuleyo ncwadi, isinqumo sokudiliza iDale lePhalamende siyabekwa eceleni. 144 Ukhetho-jikelele ngemva kokudilizwa kweDale lePhalamende

(1) Nxa iDale lePhalamende lingazange kelipasise isinqumo sokulidiliza ekuqaleni ngaphansi kweSigaba 143(2), uMongameli kumele, ngesimemezelo, abizele uKhetho-jikelele afake lamalanga lapho oluzaqhutshwa khona esikhathini esibekwe kulandelwa iSigaba 158.

(2) Lapho—

(a)

iDale lePhalamende selipasise ukudilizwa kwalo ngezimiso zeSigaba 143(2);

(b) uMongameli esedilize iDale lePhalamende ngaphansi kwezimiso zeSigaba 143(3);

(c)

uMongameli esedilize iDale lePhalamende kulandele iVoti yokungathembi ngezimiso zeSigaba 109(4); kumbe

(d) iDale lePhalamende selingelidiliziweyo kulandele iVoti yokungathembi ngezimiso zeSigaba 109(5);

uMongameli kumele, ngesimemezelo, abizele uKhetho-jikelele abesefaka lamalanga lapho luzaqhutshwa khona esikhathini esingadluli amalanga angamatshumi ayisitshiyagalolunye iDale lePhalamende lipasise lezozinqumo kumbe uMongameli adilize iDale lePhalamende kumbe iDale lePhalamende libe ngelidiliziweyo, kusiya ngodaba lwakhona.

(3) Amalanga oKhetho-jikelele olubizelwe ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1) kumbe (2) kumele afakwe nguMongameli ngemva kokubonisana leKomithi ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe. 145 Ukuhlala kwakuqala kweDale lePhalamende kulandela uKhethojikelele

(1) Ukuhlala kwakuqala kweDale lePhalamende kulandele uKhetho-jikelele kumele kwenziwe ngesikhathi langosuku olumiswe nguMongameli, kodwa lolosuku akumelanga lubuye sekudlule amalanga angamatshumi amathathu okhethwe ukuba nguMongameli eseqalise umsebenzi ngaphansi kweSigaba 94.

(2) Kuze kufike isikhathi sokuthi sekukhethwe uMongameli weDale leSenethi kumbe uSomlomo, kusiya ngeNdlu, umhlangano wakuqala weNdlu yeDale lePhalamende kumele ukhokhelwe nguNobhala weDale lePhalamende. 146 Izikhathi zokuhlala lezokuthatha ikhefu

INdlu inye ngayinye yeDale lePhalamende iyaziholela ukuthi imihlangano yayo izokube iqalisa sikhathi bani njalo ithatha isikhathi esingakanani, kungasabalwa ukuhlala kwayo kwakuqala, lezikhathi zekhefu, kodwa—

(a)

uMongameli anganqolonga iDale lePhalamende ngaloba yisiphi isikhathi ukuze kuqhutshwe umsebenzi oqakathekileyo;

(b) akumelanga kudlule isikhathi esingaba ngamalanga alikhulu lamatshumi ayisitshiyagalombili iNdlu ingakahlali njalonje. 147 Ukugoqwa kweMithetho ebisakhandwa, iziphakamiso, izikhalazo lezinye izingqubo ekudilizweni kwedale lePhalamende

Ekudilizweni kwedale lePhalamende, zonke izingqubo ebezingakaqedisiswa kufika leso sikhathi layonke imithetho ebe isakhandwa, iziphakamiso, izikhalazo leminye imisebenzi iyahle igoqwe kuphele kunjalo. ISAHLUKWANA 9 Okunye Okuphathelane Ledale Lephalamende 148 Izimfanelo lokuvikeleka emthethweni kweDale lePhalamende

(1) UMongameli weDale leSenethi, uSomlomo kanye lamaLunga eDale lePhalamende alenkululeko yokukhuluma loba yini eDale lePhalamende lakuwonke amaKomithi amiswe yiPhalamende njalo, loba nje kumele alandele zonke izimiso lezilawulo zeNdlu abayimeleyo, abayi kubotshelwa ngumthetho wemilandu kumbe owamacala, ukuvalelwa kumbe ukuhlawuliselwa izinto abazikhulume, abazikhiphe kumbe abazilethe eDale lePhalamende kumbe enye yamaKomithi alo.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungaba lakho—

(a)

ukunika amanye amalungelo, ukuvikeleka emthethweni lamandla eDale lePhalamende lamaLunga kanye leziphathamandla zalo;

(b) ukuchaza ukuziphatha okuthathwa njengokudelela iDale lePhalamende, kungabe kwenziwe ngamaLunga eDale lePhalamende loba abanye abantu; njalo

(c)

ukunika ilungelo lokuphendula, ngomlomo kaSomlomo kumbe uMongameli weDale leSenethi, kusiya ngeNdlu, ebantwini abalinyazwe ngaphandle kwemfanelo yilokho okutshiwo ngabo eDale lePhalamende;

kodwa akula Mthetho walolo hlobo onganika iDale lePhalamende kumbe amaLunga alo leziphathamandla imvumo yokwethwesa isijeziso esifana lesigwebo samacala, ngaphandle kwefayindi, emuntwini owephule ilungelo kumbe odelele iDale lePhalamende. 149 Ilungelo lokukhalazela idale lePhalamende

(1) Sonke isizalwane lomuntu ohlala eZimbabwe okwempilo yakhe yonke ulelungelo lokubhalela iDale lePhalamende isikhalazo sokuthi libhekisise udaba oluthile olusemandleni alo, kugoqela ukumiswa komthetho, ukuguqulwa kanye lokwesulwa komthetho.

(2) Indlela okumele izikhalazo zinikezwe ngayo edale lePhalamende, lamanyathelo okumele athathwe liDale lePhalamende ekunikweni isikhalazo, kumele kubekwe obala eZilawulweni ezimiyo. 150 Lapho okungenelwa khona idale lePhalamende

IDale lePhalamende lingahlala kwezinye izindawo ezingesi sigodlo esijayelekileyo seDale lePhalamende, kodwa lokhu kumele kwenzelwe kuphela izifiso zikazulu, ukuvikeleka kanye lokufinyeleleka ngcono. 151 IKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende

(1) IDale lePhalamende kumele liqoqe iKomithi ezakwaziwa ngokuthi yiKomithi yeMigomo leZilawulo ezimiyo ngenjongo—

(a)

yokuhola ukuphathwa kwemisebenzi yeDale lePhalamende;

(c)

yokukhangelisisa lokunquma ezindabeni zonke eziphathelane leDale lePhalamende; njalo

(b) yokulungisa iziLawulo ezimiyo;

(d) yokwenza yonke eminye imisebenzi engethulelwa kumbe ukwethweswa phezu kwaleyo Komithi yilesi Sisekamthetho kumbe yiziLawulo ezimiyo kumbe omunye umthetho.

(2) IKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo zePhalamende kumele zigoqele uSomlomo loMongameli weDale leSenethi lamaLunga alandelayo eDale lePhalamende—

(a)

uMsekeli kaSomlomo;

(c)

uMphathintambo obona ngezeMali labanye oMphathintambo abakhethwe nguMongameli;

(b) uMsekeli kaMongameli weDale leSenethi;

(d) uMkhokheli weMisebenzi kaHulumende eziNdlini zombili;

(e)

(f)

uMkhokheli weBandla eliphikisayo ezindlini zombili;

oMchilonduna bamabandla wonke ezombusazwe amelweyo eziNdlini zombili;

(g) uMongameli weKhansili yeziNduna elizweni;

(h) amaLunga amabili angesiboMphathintambo kumbe abaSekeli baboMphathintambo, oyedwa wabo unguSenetha okhethelwe kuliKomithi nguMongameli weDale leSenethi lomunye oliLunga leNdlu yabaMeli okhethwe nguSomlomo; njalo

(i)

amaLunga ayisitshiyagalombili angesiboMphathintambo kumbe abaSekeli baboMphathintambo, amane akhona ekhethwe liDale leSenethi abanye abane bekhethwe yiNdlu yabaMeli.

(3) AmaLunga kumele akhethwe ukuba abe kuliKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo zePhalamende masinyazanyana ngemva kokuqaliswa komhlangano wakuqala weDale lePhalamende inye ngayinye, njalo kumele bakhethwe ngendlela yokuthi iKomithi yakhona izaveza ngamandla wonke anelisekayo ukumelwa okukhona ngamabandla ezombusazwe langobulili obukhona eziNdlini zombili zeDale lePhalamende zihlanganisiwe.

(4) IKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende iqhatshelwa ukusebenza okwempilo yePhalamende inye ngayinye.

(5) IKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende ithungamelwa nguSomlomo kumbe, nxa engekho, nguMongameli weDale leSenethi.

(6) Okumele kulandelwe yile iKomithi yeMigomo leZilawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende kumele kuphawulwe ezilawulweni ezimiyo.

(7) Kungavele kube lesikhexe kuKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo zePhalamende, ilunga kumele likhethwe kumbe liqhatshwe, kusiya ngesikhexe sakhona, ngokukhulu ukuphangisa ukuze kugcwaliswe lesosikhexe. 152 IKomithi yezoMthetho eDale lePhalamende

(1) Ngokuphangisa okusemandleni eDale ngemva kokuqaliswa kwesikhathi sokuhla kweDale lePhalamende, iKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende kumele ibumbe iKomithi ezakwaziwa ngokuthi yiKomithi yezeMithetho eDale lePhalamende, egoqela amaLunga amathathu kusiya phezulu angasiwo Mphathintambo kumbe abaSekeli baboMphathintambo.

(2) Inengi lamaLunga eKomithi yezeMithetho eDale lePhalamende kumele kube ngelifanele ukusebenza eZimbabwe njengamagqwetha ngaphandle nxa kungela maLunga aneleyo agcwalisa lezozidingo ukuba afakwe kuleyoKomithi.

(3) IKomithi yezeMithetho eDale lePhalamende kumele ihlolisise—

(a)

wonke uMthetho oKhandwayo, ngaphandle koMthetho weSisekamthetho esikhandwayo, ungakavotelwa okokucina eDale leSenethi kumbe endlini yabaMeli;

(b) wonke uMthetho okhandwayo osuke waguqulwa ngemva kokuhlolisiswa yileyo Komithi, uMthetho wakhona oakhandwayo ungakabuyiselwa ukuyavotelwa okokucina eDale leSenethi leNdlini yabaMeli;

(c)

yonke imigomo ekhutshwe egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette;

(d) wonke uMthetho osakhandwayo olethwe kuliKomithi nguMsekeli kaMongameli kumbe uMphathintambo; njalo

(e)

wonke umgomo osabunjwayo olethwe kuliKomithi yinhlanganiso enikwe amandla okulungisa lowomgomo; njalo kumele itsho kuDale lePhalamende kumbe kuMsekeli kaMongameli, uMphathintambo loba inhlanganiso, kutshiyana njalonjalo, nxa ibona sengathi isimiso esithile eMthethweni okhandwayo, umgomo kumbe okunye okunjalo okusaphephethwa uyephula kumbe, nxa ungapasiswa, ungaba lakho ukwephula isimiso esithile seSisekamthetho.

(4) Ngemva kokuhlolisisa umgomo kumbe umgomo osabunjwayo iKomithi yezeMithetho eDale lePhalamende kumele itsho kuDale lePhalamende kumbe kuMsekeli kaMongameli, uMphathintambo loba inhlanganiso ephathekayo, nxa ibona sengathi isimiso esithile kulowomthetho singaphezulu kwamandla, kumbe ekupasisweni kwawo, ungaba ngaphezu kwamandla oMthetho ofaneleyo omiswe yiPhalamende.

(5) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumbe iziLawulo ezimileyo zingethulela amanye amandla phezu kweKomithi yezeMithetho ePhalamende. 153 Iholo likaMongameli wedale leSenethi, uSomlomo kanye lamaLunga eDale lePhalamende

(1) Iholo likaSomlomo loMongameli weDale leSenethi—

(a) kumele liphawulwe eMthethweni omiswe yiPhalamende njalo lithathwa eSikhwameni esiQoqiweyo seLizwe;

(b) akumelanga lehliswe besaphethe izikhundla zabo; njalo

(c) kumele baqhubeke beliphiwa loba iDale selidiliziwe baze baphume ezikhundleni zonezo.

(2) Iholo elinikwa amaLunga eDale lePhalamende kumele liphawulwe ngaphansi koMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende. 154 UNobhala wedale lePhalamende lezinye izisebenzi

(1) IKomithi yeMigomo leZilawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende, ngemvumo yeNdlu yabaMeli, kumele iqhatshe isiphathamandla esizaziwa ngokuthi nguNobhala weDale lePhalamende olumlandu, eholwa yiziLawulo ezimiyo kanye languSomlomo, wokuphatha imisebenzi yansukuzonke yeDale lePhalamende.

(2) UNobhala weDale lePhalamende uqhatshelwa ukuhlala esikhundleni okwesikhathi esifika iminyaka eyisithupha, njalo angelulelwa isikhathi sakhe kanye ngeminye iminyaka efanayo.

(3) UNobhala weDale lePhalamende kumele aphume esikhundleni sakhe—

nxa, ekucetshisweni yiKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende, inani eledlula ingxenye yamaLunga wonke eNdlu yabaMeli enganquma ukuthi uNobhala lowo akasuswe;

(b) nxa uNobhala engadingakala ukuba asuke esihlalweni sakhe, nxa kuyikuthi ube eliLunga leDale lePhalamende; kumbe

(c) loba sekutheni, ngemva kokuhlala kwakhe esikhundleni okweminyaka elitshumi lambili.

(a)

(4) IKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo zePhalamende kumele iqhatshe esinye isisebenzi esinjalo sedale lePhalamende ebona sengathi siyadingakala.

(5) UNobhala weDale lePhalamende lezinye izisebenzi zeDale lePhalamende—

(a) ziqhatshwa ngezimiso ezivunyelwa isikhathi lesikhathi yikomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo; njalo

(b) ziyizisebenzi eziphathelane lozulu kodwa ezingesingxenye yezisebenzi zikazulu.

ISAHLUKO 7 Ukhetho ISAHLUKWANA 1 Izindlela Zokuqhutshwa Kokhetho 155 Izimiso zendlela zokuqhuba ukhetho

(1) UKhetho, lona okumele luqhutshwe ngezikhathi ezibekiweyo, lamavoti okudinga imibono kazulu, okumele aqhutshwe lakulesi Sisekamthetho kumele—

(a)

aqhutshwe ngokuthula, lokukhululeka njalo ngobuqotho;

(c)

enziwe esimeni esinika wonke umuntu oseleminyaka efaneleyo ilungelo lokuvota lokulinganiswa kwamathuba okuvota; njalo

(b) aqhutshwe ngebhalothi esithekileyo;

(d) angabi lodlakela kanye lokunye ukuziphatha okungafanelananga okuqondane lokhetho.
