(2) ISizwe kumele sithathe wonke amanyathelo, okugoqela ukufaka imithetho, ebona ukuthi izimiso ezichazwe kusigatshana (1) ziyalandelwa, njalo empeleni, kumele(a)

sibone ukuthi zonke izizalwane ezifanele ukuvota, okutsho ukuthi izizalwane ezichazwe kuHlelo 4, zibhalisiwe ukuthi zanelise ukuvota;

(b) sibone ukuthi sonke isizalwane esifanele ukuvota ekhethweni loba kumavoti okudinga umbono kazulu silethuba lokuvota, njalo kumele sincedise abagogekileyo kumbe abalezinye izindingeko ukuthi labo benelise ukuvota;

(c)

sibone ukuthi amabandla wonke ezombusazwe kanye lamalunga wonke ancintisayo ekhethweni kumbe aphathekayo kumavoti okudinga umbono kazulu ayenelisa ukufinyelela zonke izinto kumbe ulwazi olufunakalayo ukuze baphatheke ngokupheleleyo;

(d) sinike wonke amabandla ezombusazwe kanye lamalunga ancintisayo ekhethweni kumbe aphathekayo kumavoti okudinga umbono kazulu amathuba aqotho njalo alinganayo okufinyelela imisakazo yemitshina kagetsi kumbe eyamaphepha, ekaHulumende lezimele yodwa; njalo

(e)

Sibone ukuthi zonke izingxabano eziphathelane lokhetho zilungisiswe ngesikhathi esifaneleyo.

156 Ukuqhutshwa kokhetho lamavoti okudinga umbono kazulu

Kulo lonke ukhetho lamavoti okudinga umbono kazulu, iKomishini ebona ngezokuqhutshwa kokhetho eZimbabwe, iZimbabwe Electoral Commission, kumele ibone ukuthi—

(a)

loba yiphi indlela yokuvota esetshenzisiweyo, ilula, iqondile, iyahlaziyeka, ivikelekile njalo imgceke;

(b) njalo imiphumela yokhetho kumbe amavoti okudinga umbono kazulu ikhutshwa ngokuphangisa ekupheleni kokuvota;

(c)

izindlela leziqondiso ezifaneleyo zibekiwe—

(i) ukuze kwenqatshelwe udlakela oluphathelane lokhetho kanye lokunye ukuziphatha okungafanelanga okuqondane lokhetho; njalo

(ii) Ibone ukuthi yonke impahla ephathelane lokhetho igcinakele.

157 Umthetho wezokhetho

(1) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele ubalule indlela yokuqhuba ngayo ukhetho lamavoti okudinga umbono kazulu okumele aqhutshwe ngendlela ebalulwe kulesi Sisekamthetho, ikakhulu okulandelayo—

(a)

Ukuqunywa kwezigaba lamawadi kulandelwa izimiso zikaSigaba 161;

(c)

ugwalo lwendlela yokuziphatha ngayo kwamabandla ezombusazwe, amalunga ancintisayo labanye abantu abaphatheka ekhethweni kumbe kumavoti okudinga umbono kazulu;

(b) ukubhaliswa kwabavotayo lokudingakalayo ekubhaliseni kuluhlu oluthile lwamabizo abantu abavotayo;

(d) indlela ebeka izilinganiso ezimele zilandelwe ekukhetheni abantu maqondana lenani lezihlalo zedale leSenethi njengokubalulwe kuSigaba 120(1)(a) lezihlalo ezigcinelwe abesifazane eNdlini yabaMeli njengokubalulwe kuSigaba 124(1)(b), kanye lezindlela okumele zilandelwe ekugcwaliseni izikhexe kulezo zihlalo, okuzikhexe zimele zigcwaliswe ngabantu—

(i) abavela kubandla linye lezombusazwe njengalabo akade bevele bephethe lezozihlalo; njalo

(ii) ababulili bunye njengabantu akade bevele bephethe lezo zihlalo;

(e)

(f)

ukukhethwa kwabantu abalobugoga njengokuchazwe ngaphansi kweSigaba 120(1)(d); ukuqhutshwa kwenketho zamaKhansili ezifunda kanye lamaKhansili ezindawo;

(g) ukuphikisana lemiphumela yokhetho.

(2) Indlela yokumela ngezilinganiso ezichazwe kusigatshana (1)(d) kumele ibone ukuthi kube lokumelwa kwabesifazane ngokulinganayo kumalunga eSenethi njengokubalulwe kuSigaba 120(1)(a).

(3) Umthetho wezoKhetho kumele ubalule ukuthi ukuqanjwa kwamalunga azancintisa ekhethweni loba yiluphi kumele kwenziwe sekudlule insuku ezilitshumi lane loba ukwedlula ngemva kokukhitshwa kombiko omemezela lolokhetho. Amavoti kumele aqhutshwe sekudlule insuku ezingamatshumi amathathu kusiya phezulu ngemva kokuqanjwa kwabancintisi.

(4) Akula nguquko ezakwenziwa eMthethweni wezoKhetho, kumbe komunye umthetho ophathelane lawo, ngaphandle nxa iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe ike yaboniswa ngaleyo nguquko njalo imibono yayo ithethwe layo ekufakeni leyonguquko.

(5) Ngemva kokumemezelwa kokhetho, yonke inguquko enziwe eMthethweni wezoKhetho kumbe kweminye imithetho ephathelane lokhetho kayizukusebenza ekhethweni lona lolo olumenyezelweyo. ISAHLUKWANA 2 Ukubekwa Kwezikhathi Zokhetho 158 Ukubekwa kwezikhathi zokhetho

(1) Ukhetho-jikelele kumele lwenziwe ukuze kuvotwe phakathi kwesikhathi esingedluli—

(a)

insuku ezingamatshumi amathathu andulela ukuphela kwethemu yeminyaka emihlanu echazwe kuSigaba 143;

(b) lapho idale lePhalamende selinqume khona ukuthi lidilizwe ngaphansi kwezimiso zeSigaba 143(2), insuku ezingamatshumi ayisitshiyagalolunye ngemva kokupasiswa kwaleso sinqumo; kumbe

(c)

lapho idale lePhalamande selidilizwe ngaphansi kwezimiso zeSigaba 109(4) kumbe (5) kulandela iVoti yokuswela ithemba kuHulumende, insuku ezingamatshumi ayisitshiyagalolunye kulandela lokho kudilizwa.

(2) Ukhetho-jikelele olwamaKhansili ezindawo kumele lwenziwe sikhathi sinye lokhetho-jikelele lokudinga uMongameli labameli bedale lePhalamende.

(3) Ukhetho lokugcwalisa izikhexe edale lePhalamende lakumaKhansili ezindawo kumele luqhutshwe kungakedluli insuku ezingamatshumi ayisitshiyagalolunye lezo zikhexe zibe khona ngaphandle nxa izikhexe zakhona zidaleke sekusele izinyanga eziyisitshiyagalolunye ukuthi ukhetho-jikelele luqhutshwe, lapho-ke izikhexe zingaqhubeka zingagcwaliswanga kuze kufike isikhathi sokhetho-jikelele. 159 Ukugcwaliswa kwezikhundla ezisele zingela bantu ngamavoti

Sonke isikhathi kungaba lesikhexe esiseleyo esikhundleni esivotelwayo esidalwe ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho, singasabali isikhundla esichazwe ngaphansi kweSigaba 158, inhlanganiso elomlandu wokuqoqa ukhetho lwalolohlobo kumele ibangele ukuqhutshwa kokhetho insuku ezingamatshumi ayisitshiyagalolunye zingakedluli ukuze lesosikhexe sigcwaliswe. ISAHLUKWANA 3 Ukuqunywa Kwemingcele Yokhetho 160 Inani lezifunda lamawadi

(1) Ngenjongo yokukhetha abameli edale lePhalamende, iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele iqume ilizwe leZimbabwe libe lezifunda zokudinga abameli ezifika amakhulu amabili letshumi.

(2) Ngenjongo yokudinga abameli kumaKhansili ezindawo, iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele iqume izindawo zamaKhansili zibe ngamawadi alingana lenani labameli okumele babe khona kukhansili yendawo inye ngayinye. 161 Ukuqunywa kwemingcele yokhetho

(1) Kanye ngemva kweminyaka elitshumi inye ngayinye, ngosuku kumbe phakathi kwesikhathi esibekwe yiKomishini esimaduzane nje lesikhathi okuqhutshwe ngaso uhlelo lokubala abantu elizweni, iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele iqhube uhlelo lokuquma imingcele yokhetho phakathi kweZimbabwe.

(2) Nxa ukuqunywa kwemingcele yokhetho kuphele sekusele isikhathi esingaphansi kwezinyanga eziyisithupha ukuthi usuku lokuvota lufike kukhethojikelele, leyomingcele equnyiweyo kayizukusetshenziswa kulolokhetho, njalo imingcele ezasetshenziswa khonapho yileyo ekade ivele ikhona andubana kuqunywe imingcele emitsha.

(3) Imingcele yezifunda zokhetho kumele, ngakho konke okusemandleni, iqunywe ngendlela ezakwanelisa ukuthi isifunda sinye ngasinye sibe lenani labantu ababhalisa ukuvota elingene lakwezinye zonke izifunda ezitholakala eZimbabwe ngesikhathi kuqunywa leyo mingcele.

(4) Imingcele yamawadi kumele, ngakho konke okusemandleni, iqunywe ngendlela ezakwanelisa ukuthi iwadi inye ngayinye ibe lenani labantu ababhalisa ukuvota elilingene lakwamanye wonke amawadi atholakala kuleyondawo ngesikhathi kuqunywa leyo mingcele.

(5) Ekuqumeni—

(a)

imingcele yamawadi, iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele ibone ukuthi akula wadi equnyelwe izindawo ezimbili kusiya phezulu zamakhansili;

(b) imingcele yezifunda zokhetho, iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele ibone ukuthi akula wadi equnyelwe izifunda ezimbili kusiya phezulu.

(6) Ekuqumeni iZimbabwe isiba ngamawadi lezifunda zokhetho, iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele, ngendawo ngendawo, ikhangele—

(a)

isimo sayo;

(c)

ukusabalala kwabantu ababhalise ukuvota abahlala kuleyo ndawo;

(e)

ekuqumeni indawo eseyake yaqunywa kuqala, imingcele yokhetho evele ikhona; njalo

(b) izindlela zokukhulumisana ezikuleyo ndawo;

(d) umphakathi ongabe ubalulekile phakathi kwabantu ababhalise ukuvota;

(f)

inani labantu abakhona;

njalo ukuze konke lokhu kuphumelele, iKomishini ingabeka eceleni isimiso sokuthi izifunda zokhetho lamawadi kumele abe lamanani abantu ababhalise ukuvota alinganayo, kodwa akula sifunda sokhetho kumbe iwadi okumele ibe lenani eliyisilinganiso samatshumi amabili ekhulwini labantu ababhalise ukuvota abanengi kumbe abalutshwana kulezinye izifunda kumbe amawadi agqagqeleneyo.

(7) Ngemva kokuquma amawadi lezifunda zokhetho, iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele inike uMongameli incwadi yakuqala equkethe—

(a)

uluhlu lwamawadi lezifunda zokhetho, kunye lamabizo azo zonke lengcazelo yemingcele yazo;

(b) imephu kumbe amamephu atshengisa amawadi lezifunda zokhetho; njalo

(c)

olunye ulwazi kumbe imininingwane iKomishini ebona angani iqakathekile; njalo uMongameli kumele abone ukuthi incwadi yakuqala le equkethe ulwazi ngokuqunywa kwemingcele yezindawo zokhetho ibekwe phambi kwedale lePhalamende phakathi kwezinsuku eziyisikhombisa.

(8) Phakathi kwezinsuku ezilitshumi lane incwadi yakuqala ebalula ukuqunywa kwemingcele yezindawo zokhetho ibekiwe edale lePhalamende—

(a)

uMongameli angabuyisela leyoncwadi kuKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe ukuthi icubungulisise udaba oluthile ngayo;

(b) enye yeziNdlu zabameli inganquma ukuthi leyoncwadi ibuyiselwe kuKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe ukuze icubungulisise udaba oluthile ngayo, njalo kungenzeka lokhu uMongameli-ke sekumele ayibuyisele leyoncwadi kuKomishini ukuze icubungule udaba lwakhona.

(9) Nxa incwadi yakuqala ebalula ukuqunywa kwemingcele yezindawo zokhetho ingabuyiselwa njengokuchazwe ngaphansi kwesigatshana (8), iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele icubungulisise udaba olulethiweyo, kodwa isinqumo seKomishini ngalo kasiyi kuphikiswa.

(10) Ngokuphangisa okusemandleni ayo, ngemva kokulandela izimiso zezigatshana (8) lo (9), iKomishini ebona ngezokhetho eZimbabwe kumele inikeze uMongameli incwadi yokucina ebalula imingcele yezindawo zokhetho.

(11) Kungakedluli izinsuku ezilitshumi lane enikezwe incwadi yokucina yeKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe, uMongameli kumele ethule umbiko kugwalo lukaHulumende olweGazette ebetha amabizo kanye lemingcele yamawadi lezifunda zokhetho njengokucine kuchazwa yiKomishini.

(12) Nxa kukhona ukungahambelani-thize phakathi kwengcazelo yewadi kumbe isifunda sokhetho lemephu kumbe amamephu adwetshwe yiKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe, ingcazelo yiyo ebusayo. ISAHLUKO 8 Izilawulamthetho Lemithethwandaba ISAHLUKWANA 1 Ukusebenza Komthethwandaba 162 Igunya leZilawulamthetho

Igunyalezilawulamthetho livela ebantwini beZimbabwe njalo liphiwe imithethwandaba, egoqela—

(a)

uMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho;

(c)

uMthethwandaba Ophezulu;

(e)

uMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa Kwamandla;

(b) uMthethwandaba Omkhulu;

(d) uMthwethwandaba wezokuSebenzelana;

(f)

uMthethwandaba kaMantshi;

(g) imithethwandaba yemithetho yoMdabu;

(h) eminye imithethwandaba emiswe nguMthetho loba ngaphansi komthetho wePhalamende. 163 lzilawulamthetho

(1) Ugatsha lwezilawulamthetho lweZimbabwe lugoqela—

(a)

Indunankulu yabeHluleli, uMsekeli wendunankulu yaBehluleli labanye aBehluli bomthethwandaba weSisekamthetho;

(b) aBehluleli boMthethwandaba Omkhulu;

(c)

uMongameli waBehluleli bomthethwandaba Ophezulu;

(e)

uMongameli wabeHluli womthethwandaba obona ngokuqgutshwa kwezinto;

(d) uMongameli wabeHluleli boMthethwandaba wokusebenzelana labanye aBehluleli balowo mthethwandaba;

(f)

abantu abaphethe imithethwandaba kamantshi, imithethwandaba yomthetho womdabu leminye imithethwandaba emiswe nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende loba omiswe ngaphansi kwawo.

(2) Indunankulu yabeHluli uyinhloko yezilawulamthetho njalo ulawula uMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho kanye loMthethwandaba Omkhulu.

(3) UMongameli wabeHluleli woMthethwandaba waPhezulu uphethe lowo mthethwandaba.

(4) UMongameli wabeHluleli womthethwandaba obona ngokusebenzelana ulawula lowo mthethwandaba.

(5) UMongameli wabeHluleli woMthethwandaba obona ngokuqhutshwa kwezinto ulawula lowo mthethwandaba. 164 Ukuzimela kogatsha lweziLawulamthetho

(1) Imithethwandaba izimele yodwa njalo ilomlandu kuphela kulesi Sisekamthetho kanye lomthetho, okumele iwusebenzise ngendlela engathathi uhlangothi, engachithi isikhathi njalo engela kwesaba, inkethabantu loba ukuthatha icele.

(2) Ukuzimela, ukungathathi hlangothi kanye lokusebenza kuhle kwemithethwandaba kuyisiqokoqela ekubuseni komthetho lombuso oledemokrasi, njalo ngalokho ke—

(a)

isizwe loba ugatsha loba inhlanganiso kaHulumende loba kusiphi isigaba, loba nguwuphi umuntu, akungeke kuphazamise ukusebenza kwemithethwandaba;

(b) isizwe, ngezindlela zokwenziwa kwemithetho lamanye amanyathela, kumele sisize njalo sivikele imithethwandaba ukuze kube lesiqiniselo sokuzimela kwayo, ukungathathi hlangothi, isithunzi ukufinyeleleka lokusebenza ngemfanelo kanye lokuba lesiqiniselo sokuthi imithethwandaba iyalandela izimiso ezimiswe kusigatshana 165.

(3) Isilawulo loba isinqumo somthethwandaba sibopha iSizwe labo bonke abantu kanye lezingatsha zikaHulumende labameli lapho esisebenza khona, njalo kumele kulalelwe yibo.

(4) Akulalutho olukulesi Sigaba okumele luthathwe njengokuthi lwenqabela uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ekupheni imisebenzi eminye engayisiyo yokwahlulela elungeni lezilawulamthetho, kusiyangokuthi ukwenziwa kwalowo msebenzi akuphazamisi ukuzimela kwalesosisebenzi silawulamthetho ekwenzeni imisebenzi yaso yokwahlulela njalo akuphazamisi ukuzimela kwezilawulamthetho jikelele. 165 Izimiso ezihola izilawulamthetho

(1) Ekusebenziseni amandla ezilawulamthetho, amalunga ezilawulamthetho kumele aholwe yizimiso ezilandelayo—

(a)

ukwahlulela okulungileyo kumele kwenziwe kubobonke, kungelani lesimo somuntu;

(b) ukwahlulela okulungileyo akumelanga kuphuziswe, ngakho ke amalunga ezilawulamthetho kumele enze imisebenzi ayo yemithetho ngendlela efaneleyo njalo ngokuphangisa okufaneleyo;

(c)

Umsebenzi wemithethwandaba umkhulu ekuvikeleni amalungelo abantu lezinkululeko kanye lokubusa komthetho.

(2) Amalunga ezilawulamthetho, ngalinye ngalinye lanxa endawonye, kumele ahloniphe izikhundla zawo njengenhlanganiso kazulu njalo kumele azame ngamandla wonke ukuphakamisa ukuzimela kwawo ukuze agcine ukuzethemba phambi kukazulu emsebenzini wezokulawula imithetho.

(3) Nxa lisenza isinqumo esimayelana lokulawula umthetho, ilunga lezemithetho

kumele lisenze ngokukhululeka njalo kungela kungenela loba ukudonselwa ekwenzeni okuthize.

(4) Amalunga ezilawulamthetho awamelanga—

(a)

angene ezintweni/ezenzweni ezenzakalayo ezombusazwe;

(c)

acelele izimali loba ahambise izimali kuloba yiyiphi inhlanganiso yezombusazwe;

(b) abelesikhundla loba abe ngamalunga aloba yiliphi iqembu lezombusazwe;

(d) ahlanganyele emihlanganweni yezombusazwe.

(5) Amalunga ogatsha lwezemithetho akumelanga acele loba avume ukwamukela loba yisiphi isipho, okuphiwayo, imali yokwebolekwa loba ukusizwa okungaba lendonsela ekuqhubeni ezemithetho loba kuveze ukungalungi kwezokulawulwa kwemithetho.

(6) Amalunga ezilawulamthetho kumele aqakathekise imisebenzi yawo yezemithetho okwedlula yonke eminye imisebenzi, njalo akumelanga angene kuloba yiziphi izinto ezenzakalayo ezingenela loba eziphazamisa imisebenzi yabo yokulawula imithetho.

(7) Amalunga ezilawulamthetho kumele athathe amanyathela afaneleyo ukuze agcine njalo aphakamise ulwazi lwawo lwemisebenzi, amakhono kanye lonhlozi lomuntu, njalo ikakhulu kumele azigcine elolwazi lokwenzakalayo emthethweni welizwe lalowo ophathelane lamanye amazwe. 166 UMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho

(1) Umthethwandaba wesiSisekamthetho ngumthethwandaba ongaphezulu ogcina izindaba eziphathelane lomthetho zilotshwe phansi, njalo ugoqela—

(a)

iNdunankulu yabeHluleli kanye loMsekeli weNdunankulu yabeHluleli;

(b) abehluleli abanye abahlanu boMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho.

(2) Nxa imisebenzi kaMahluli omele isikhundla sikaMahluli idingakala emthethwandaba obona ngeSisekamthetho okwesikhathi esifitshane, iNdunankulu yaboMahluli ingaqhatsha uMahluli loba lowo owake wabanguMahluli ukuthi asebenze ngengoMahluli womthethwandaba weSisekamthetho okwalesosikhathi.

(3) Amacala aphambi koMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho:

(a)

mayelana lokucatshangelwayo ukwephulwa kwelungelo labantu eliyinsika loba inkululeko ephawulwe kusahluko 4, loba mayelana lokukhethwa kukaMongameli loba uMsekeli kaMongameli, kumele kulalelwe ngaBehluleli bonke beMthethwandaba;

(b) ngaphandle kwamacala okukhulunywe ngawo kusiqendu (a), kumele alalelwe ngaBehluleli abangaqalisela kwabathathu abeMthethwandaba.

(4) Abehluleli loba abake babangaBehluleli abaqhatshwe ukuthi baphathe ngokuphawulwe kusigatshana (2) bengaqhubeka behlala njengaboMahluli bomthethwandaba obona ngeSisekamthetho ngemva kokuba isikhathi ebebesiqhatshelwe sesiphelile, kusenzelwa ekuqhutshweni kwezingqubo ezazilethwe kubo ngesikhathi basaphethe isihlalo. 167 Umkhawulo wamandla omthethwandaba weSisekamthetho

(1) UMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho—

(a)

nguMthethwandaba oPhezulu kuleminye kuzo zonke izindaba

zeSisekamthetho, njalo izinqumo zawo kulezo zindaba zibopha yonke eminye imithethwandaba;

(b) unquma kuphela izindaba eziphathelane leSisekamthetho kanye lezindaba eziphathelane lezinqumo ezindabeni zeSisekamthetho, ikakhulu amareference lezincwadi zezicelo ezingaphansi kwesigaba 131(8)(b) lakusiqendu 9(2) koHlelo lwesihlanu; njalo

(c)

wenza isinqumo sokucina sokuphawula ukuthi udaba lungoluphathelane leSisekamthetho na kumbe indaba iphathelane lesinqumo esimayelana lendaba ephathelane leSisekamthetho.

(2) Kulandelwa lesiSisekamthetho, ngumthethwandaba weSisekamthetho wodwa onga—

(a)

cebisa ngokuqonda ngokusemthethweni kwaloba yikuphi ukwenziwa komthetho okuyabe kuqanjiwe, kodwa ungenza ngokunjalo kuphela nxa ukwenziwa komthetho lowo kukhulunywe ngakho ngokuphawula kwalesi Sisekamthetho;

(b) ulalele njalo ulawule izingxabano ezimayelana lokukhethelwa esikhundleni sikaMongameli;

(c)

ulalele njalo ulawule izingxabano ezimayelana lokuthi umuntu ulakho yini konke okudingakalayo okumenza afanele ukuphatha isikhundla sikaMsekeli kaMongameli loba hatshi;

(d) ulawule ukuthi iPhalemende loba uMongameli wehlulekile umlandu ophathelane leSisekamthetho loba hatshi.

(3) UMthethwandaba obona ngeSisekamthetho wenza isinqumo sokucina sokuthi uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende loba ukuqhuba kukaMongameli loba okwePhalamende kusemthethweni yini, njalo kumele sitsho ngesimiso esiphambene lomthetho esenziwe ngomunye umthethwandaba Isimiso leso singakalandelwa.

(4) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungaphawula ngokusetshenziswa kwamandla yiSisekamthetho njalo ngalesosizatho ungakhuluma ngamandla okwenza imithetho yeMthethwandaba.

(5) Nxa kuyikuthi kusenzuzweni yokulunga njalo kungelani lemvumo ephiwa nguMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho, imithetho yoMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho kumele ivumele umuntu—

(a)

ukuletha indaba ephathelane leSisekamthetho khona kanye eMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho;

(b) ukufaka isicelo khona kanye eMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho esivela kuloba yiwuphi omunye umthethwandaba;

(c)

ukuba khona njengomngane womthethwandaba.

168 Umthethwandaba Ophezulu

(1) UMthethwandaba Ophezulu ngumthethwandaba ongaphezulu ogcina izindaba eziphathelane lomthetho zilotshwe phansi, njalo ugoqela—

(a)

iNdunankulu yabeHluleli kanye loMsekeli weNdunankulu yabeHluleli;

(c)

labanye aBehluleli bokwengezelela abaqhatshwe ngaphansi kukasigatshana (2).

(b) abanye abeHluleli boMthethwandaba Ophezulu abangekho ngaphansi kwababili; kanye

(2) iNdunankulu yabeHluleli ingaqhatsha uMahluli woMthethwandaba

waPhezulu, loba owake wabanguMahluli ukuthi asebenze njengomahluli woMthethwandaba Ophezulu okwesikhathi leso.

(3) ABehluleli loba abake babangaBahluleli abaqhatshwe ukuthi baphathe ngokuphawulwe kusigatshana (2) bengaqhubeka behlala njengabeHluleli bomthethwandaba weSisekamthetho ngemva kokuba isikhathi ebebesiqhatshelwe sesiphelile, kusenzelwa ekuqhutshweni kwezingqubo ezazilethwe kubo ngesikhathi basaphethe isihlalo. 169 Umkhawulo wamandla Omthethwandaba Ophezulu

(1) UMthethwandaba Ophezulu umgumthethwandaba wokucina wokubeka isikhalazo eZimbabwe, Ngaphandle kwezindabeni lapho uMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho olomkhawulo wamandla khona.

(2) Kulandelwa isigatshana (1), uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungatsho amanye amandla awokwengeza kuMthethwandaba Ophezulu.

(3) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungaphawula ngokusetshenziswa kwamandla nguMthethwandaba Ophezulu njalo ngalesosizatho ungatsho ngamandla okwenza imithetho yeMthethwandaba.

(4) Imithetho yoMthethwandaba ingethwesa uMbhali woMthethwandaba Ophezulu iloba yiwaphi amandla oMthethwandaba lamandla phezu kwemilandu kazulu—

(a)

ukwenza izimiso emacaleni angaphikiswayo, ngaphandle kwezilawulo ezithinta ngezokugcinwa loba ukuba sesikhundleni somzali ekondliweni kwabantwana;

(b) ukunquma izindaba zekusunguleni kugoqela izicelo zokuqondiswa, kodwa hatshi izindaba eziphazamisa ukukhululeka kwaloba nguwuphi umuntu; kodwa imithetho kumele iphe umuntu ophazanyiswe yisimiso loba yisinqumo salowo obona ngezokuqhutshwa koMthwethwandaba ilungelo lokuthi sihlolisiswe nguMahluleli woMthethwandaba Ophezulu ongasivuma loba asilungisise loba asibeke eceleni kumbe aphe esinye nje isimiso loba isinqumo asibona sifanele. 170 Umthethwandaba Omkhulu

UMthethwandaba Omkhulu nguMthethwandaba omkhulukazi owaziwayo njalo ugoqela—

(a)

iNdunankulu yaboMahluli, Umsekeli weNdunankulu yaboMahluli kanye loMongameli wabehluleli woMthethwandaba omkhulu; kanye

(b) labanye abehluleli abanjalo boMthethwandaba Omkhulu abangaqhatshwa ngokuqhubeka kwesikhathi. 171 Amandla oMthethwandaba Omkhulu

(1) UMthethwandaba Omkhulu—

(a)

ulomkhawulo wamandla ayiwo kanye phezu kwemilandu lamacala wonke eZimbabwe yonke;

(b) ulamandla okuqondisa imithethwandaba yaboMantshi kanye leminye imithethwandaba engaphansi kwayo kanye lokuhlolisisa izinqumo zabo;

(c)

unganquma izindaba eziphathelane leSisekamthetho ngaphandle kwalezo ezinganqunywa kuphela nguMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho;

(d) ulomkhawulo wamandla aphathelane lokwenziwa kwezikhalazo njengokuyabe kuphiwe kuwo nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungaphawula ngokusetshenziswa komkhawulo wamandla nguMthethwandaba Omkhulu njalo ngaleso sizatho ungapha amandla okwenza imithetho yoMthethwandaba.

(3) UMthetho ungaphawula ukuthi uMthethwandaba Ophezulu wehlukaniswe ubesezigabeni ezikhethekileyo, kodwa isigaba ngasinye ngasinye kumele senelise ukusebenzisa umkhawulo wamandla oMthethwandaba OPhezulu jikelele loba kuyiphi indaba elethwe phambi kwawo.

(4) Imithetho yemithethwandaba ingaphawula amandla kumilandu kulowo obona ngezokuphathwa kwezingqubo owoMthethwandaba Omkhulu—

(a)

ukwenza imiyalo emacaleni engaphikisiswayo, ngaphandle kwemiyalo ethinta ngokuphathelane lokondliwa lokugcinwa kwabantwana ngumuntu othize;

(b) ukunquma izindaba ezekuqaleni, kugoqela izicelo zokuqondiswa, kodwa hatshi izindaba ezithinta ukukhululeka kwaloba nguwuphi umuntu; Kodwa imithetho kumele iphe iloba nguwuphi umuntu othintwe yimiyalo loba isinqumo salowo obona ngezokuphathwa kwezingqubo ilungelo lokuthi imiyalo ihlolisiswe nguMahluleli woMthethwandaba Ophezulu, ozayivuma ukuthi iqondile, ayilungisise loba ayibeke eceleni loba aphe eyinye nje imiyalo loba isinqumo acabanga ukuthi sifanele.

172 UMthwethwandaba wezeMisebenzi

(1) UMthethwandaba wezeMisebenzi nguMthwethandaba ogcina izindaba eziphathelane lomthetho zilotshwe phansi njalo ugoqela—

(a)

uMahluleli omkhulu; njalo

(b) labanye aBehluleli abanjalo boMthethwandaba obona ngezemisebenzi abangaqhubeka beqhatshwa ngokuqhubeka kwesikhathi. [Isigatshana lesi sitshintshwe yiSigaba 3 soMthetho 10 ka2017]

(2) UMthethwandaba wezeMisebenzi ulamandla phezu kwezindaba zokusebenzelana lokuqhatshwa njengokungabe kuphiwe kuwo nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(3) UMthethwandaba wePhalamende ungaphawula ngokusetshenziswa kwamandla nguMthethwandaba wezeMisebenzi njalo ngokwaleso sizatho ungapha amandla okwenza imithetho yoMthethwandaba. 173 UMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa kwaMandla

(1) UMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa kwaMandla ngumthethwandaba omkhulu njalo ugoqela—

(a)

uMahluleli omkhulu; njalo

(b) labanye oMahlulelei abanjengalabo boMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa kwaMandla abangaqhubeka bekhethwa ngokuqhubeka kwesikhathi. [Isigatshana lesi sitshintshwe yiSigaba 4 soMthetho 10 ka2017]

(2) UMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa kwaMandla ulamandla ezindabeni zokuphathwa kwamandla njengalokhu okuyabe kuphiwe kuwo nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(3) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungaphawula ngokusetshensizwa komkhawulo wamandla nguMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa kwaMandla njalo ngalesosizatho ungapha amandla okwenza imithetho yomthethwandaba.

174 Eminye imithethwandaba lamadale

(1) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungaphawula ngokumiswa, ukubunjwa kanye lomkhawulo wamandla e—

(a)

mithethwandaba kaMantshi, okunquma ngamilandu lamacala;

(c)

eminye imithethwandaba engaphansi koMthethwandaba Ophezulu;

(b) mithethwandaba yomthetho womdabu olamandla agxile ekusetshenzisweni komthetho womdabu;

(d) amadale okulungisiswa lokungenela izindaba kanye leminye imihlobo yokuqeda ingxabano.

(2) Kulandelwa isigaba 188(4) seSisekamthetho, imiholo, izimbadalo kanye lezinye inzuzo zaBehluleli boMthethwandaba wezeMisebenzi laboMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa kwaMandla abaphethe isikhundla kumbe abamele ophethe isikhundla ngelanga lo uMthetho uqalisa ukusebenza aziyikwehliswa. [Isigatshana lesi sitshintshwe yiSigaba 5 soMthetho 10 ka2017]

175 Amandla emithethwandaba ezindabeni zeSisekamthetho

(1) Lapho umthethwandaba usenza imiyalo mayelana lokungabi seSisekelweni kwaloba yiwuphi umthetho loba yiphi ingqubo kaMongameli loba iPhalamende, imiyalo ayilamandla ngaphandle nxa isivunyelwe nguMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho.

(2) Umthethwandaba owenza umyalo wokungabi seSisekelweni okukhulunywe ngakho ku sigatshana (1) ungapha ukuvimbela okwesikhathi sonaleso loba okunye ukukhululeka elungeni, loba ungaphuzisa izingqubo, kumelelwe isinqumo soMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho mayelana lesisindo somthetho loba ukuqhuba lokho.

(3) Iloba nguwuphi umuntu odinga ngokupheleleyo ukukhalaza engakhalaza, loba abhalele khona kanye eMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho ukuze avume loba aphikisane lomyalo owenziwe nguMthethwandaba ophathelane lokuba lesisindo kodaba emthethweni weSisekamthetho kulandelwa usigatshana (1).

(4) Nxa indaba ephathelane leSisekamthetho ivuka iloba kuziphi izingqubo, phambi koMthethwandaba, umuntu onguye olawulayo kulowo Mthethwandaba nxa kuceliwe ngomunye wabayingxenye yezingqubo, kumele adlulisele indaba leyo eMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho ngaphandle kokuthi nxa ebona ukuthi isicelo leso asiqinisekanga kumbe singesiphazamisayo.

(5) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende loba imithetho yoMthethwandaba ungaphawula ngokudluliselwa eMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho ngokomyalo ophathelane lokungekho eSisekelweni okwenziwe ngokuqondisa kwesigatshana (1) nguMthethwandaba ongayiyisiwo uMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho.

(6) Nxa kuqunywa indaba ephathelane leSisekamthetho esemkhawulweni wamandla awo, uMthethwandaba unga—

(a)

ngaphawula usitsho ukuthi iloba yiwuphi umthetho loba ukuqhuba okungavumelani leSisekamthetho akusebenzi kusiya ngokungahambelani kwakho lomthetho;

(b) ukwenza isinqumo esilungileyo njalo esingathathi hlangothi, kugoqela esinciphisa amandla esinqumo esake sabekwa ngaphambili kokubeka sobala lokho okungasebenziyo kanye lesinqumo esimisa ukubekwa sobala kokungasebenzi lokho okwesikhathi esithize ukuze kuphe isikhathi sokuthi isiphathamandla esilolwazi siqondise lelo phutha.

176 Amandla aqukethwengu Mthethwandaba weSisekamthetho, UMthethwandaba oMkhulu loMthethwandaba oPhezulu

UMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho, uMthethwandaba Ophezulu kanye loMthethwandaba Omkhulu ulamandla ohlezi ulawo okuvikela kanye lokulawula ingqubo yawo kanye lokuthuthukisa umthetho osetshenziswa ngabantu bonke loba umthetho ovamileyo, kunanzelelwa izidingo zokulunga kanye lokuphawulwe kulesi Sisekamthetho. ISAHLUKWANA 2 Ukuqhatshwa Kwezilawulamthetho Lesikhathi Esibekiweyo Sokuphatha Kwazo Izikhundla 177 Izindingeko ekuqhatshweni kwaBehluleli boMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho

(1) Umuntu uyabe elezindingeko ezifaneleyo ekuqhatshweni njengoMahluleli weMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho nxa eyisizalwane seZimbabwe, eleminyaka yokuzala engamatshumi amane kusiya phezulu njalo elolwazi olubanzi ngommthetho weSisekamthetho njalo, phezu kwalokhu, kumele abelezindengeko ezilandelayo—

(a)

wake waba ngumahluleli womthethwandaba olomkhawulo wamandla angancitshiswanga ezindabeni zamacala nje kanye lamacala okwephula imithetho elizweni lapho umthetho olandelwayo kakhulu uyiRomanDutch loba owesingisi, njalo lapho isingisi esiphathwa njengolimi oluqakathekileyo lokusebenzisa ezindaweni ezitshiyeneyo;

(b) owayelezindingeko ezifaneleyo ezamvumela ukusebenza njengegqwetha okweminyaka elitshumi lambili kusiya phezulu, loba ukusebenza lokhu kwakuqhubeka ngokulandelana kweminyaka loba hatshi,

(i) eZimbabwe;

(ii) elizweni lapho umthetho olandelwayo kakhulu uyiRoman-Dutch loba owesingisi, njalo lapho isingisi esiphathwa njengolimi oluqakathekileyo lokusebenzisa ezindaweni ezitshiyeneyo; njalo elokhu elazo izindingeko ezimvumela ukuthi asebenze.

(2) Ukuze umuntu aqhatshwe njengoMahluleli woMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho kumele abengumuntu ofaneleyo ngempela ukuphatha isikhundla sokubanguMahluleli. 178 Izindingeko ekuqhatshweni ukuba nguMahluleli woMthethwandaba Ophezulu

(1) Umuntu uyabe elezindingeko ezifaneleyo—

(a)

wake waba ngumahluli womthethwandaba olomkhawulo wamandla ongancitshiswanga ezindabeni zamacala nje kanye lamacala okwephula imithetho elizweni lapho umthetho olandelwayo kakhulu uyiRomanDutch loba owesingisi, njalo lapho isingisi esiphathwa njengolimi oluqakathekileyo lokusebenzisa ezindaweni ezitshiyeneyo;

(b) owayelezindingeko ezifaneleyo ezamvumela ukusebenza njengegqwetha okweminyaka elitshumi kusiya phezulu, loba ukusebenza lokhu kwakuqhubeka ngokulandelana kweminyaka loba hatshi:

(i) eZimbabwe; loba

(ii) elizweni lapho umthetho olandelwayo kakhulu uyiRoman-Dutch

loba owesingisi, njalo lapho isingisi esiphathwa njengolimi oluqakathekileyo lokusebenzisa ezindaweni ezitshiyeneyo; njalo elokhu elazo izindingeko ezimvumela ukuthi asebenze.

(2) Ukuze umuntu aqhatshwe njengoMahluleli woMthethwandaba Ophezulu kumele abengumuntu ofaneleyo ngempela ukuphatha isikhundla sokubanguMahluleli. 179 Okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu anelise ukuqhatshwa njengoMahluleli woMthethwandaba Omkhulu, uMthethwandaba wezemisebenzi lomthethwandaba wezokuphathwa kwamandla

(1) Umuntu uyabe elakho okudingakalayo ekuqhatshweni njengomahluleli woMthethwandaba Ophezulu, uMthethwandaba Wezemisebenzi loMthethwandaba Wezokuphatha nxa eleminyaka engamatshumi amane kusiya phezulu yobudala, njalo—

(a)

wake waba ngumahluli womthethwandaba olomkhawulo wamandla ongancitshiswanga ezindabeni zamacala nje kanye lamacala okwephula imithetho elizweni lapho umthetho olandelwayo kakhulu uyiRomanDutch loba owesingisi, njalo lapho isingisi esiphathwa njengolimi oluqakathekileyo lokusebenzisa ezindaweni ezitshiyeneyo;

(b) owayelezindingeko ezifaneleyo ezamvumela ukusebenza njengegqwetha okweminyaka eyisikhombisa kusiya phezulu, loba ukusebenza lokhu kwakuqhubeka ngokulandelana kweminyaka loba hatshi:

(i) eZimbabwe;

(ii) elizweni lapho umthetho olandelwayo kakhulu uyiRoman-Dutch loba owesingisi, njalo lapho isingisi esiphathwa njengolimi oluqakathekileyo lokusebenzisa ezindaweni ezitshiyeneyo;

(iii) nxa eyisizalwane seZimbabwe, elizweni lapho umthetho osetshenziswa ngabantu bonke ungoweSingisi njalo isilungu silulimi olusemthethweni lokusetshenziswa.

(2) Ukuze umuntu aqhatshwe njengoMahluli woMthethwandaba oMkhulu kumele abengumuntu ofaneleyo ngempela ukuphatha isikhundla sokubanguMahluli. 180 Ukuqhatshwa kwaBehluleli

(1) UNdunankulu waBehluleli, uMsekeli kaNdunankulu waBehluleli kanye loMongameli waBehluleli eMthethwandaba oPhezulu labanye bonke abehluleli baqatshwa nguMongameli weLizwe maqondana lalesi isigaba.

(2) UNdunankulu waBehluleli, uMsekeli kaNdunankulu waBehluleli kanye loMongameli waBehluleli eMthethwandaba oPhezulu bazaqatshwa nguMongameli weLizwe ngemva kokubonisana leKhomishini yeMisebenzi YezeMithetho.

(3) Nxa ukuqhatshwa kukaNdunankulu waBehluleli, uMsekeli kaNdunankulu waBehluleli kanye loMongameli waBehluleli eMthethwandaba oPhezulu kungahambelani lenkuthazo loba yiphi ephiwe yiKomishini yeMisebenzi Yezemithetho njengokubalulwa kusigatshana (2), uMongameli uzobangela ukuthi iDale leSenethi libikelwe ngalokhu ngokuphangisa; Nxa kuyikuthi, ukuze kungabi lokuthandabuza, kuyaziswa ukuthi isinqumo sikaMongameli ngalokho kuqatshwa asiyikutshintshwa.

(4) Noma nini, nxa kudingakala ukuthi kuqatshwe uMahluleli, ongesuye UNdunankulu waBehluleli, uMsekeli kaNdunankulu waBehluleli Kanye loMongameli waBehluleli eMthethwandaba oPhezulu, iKomishini yeMisebenzi yeZemithetho kumele—

(a)

imemezele lesosikhundla; njalo

(c)

yenze ingxoxo phambi kukaZulu lalabo abafuna umsebenzi lowo; njalo

(e)

inike uMongameli lololuhlu; kuthi, kulandelwa isigatshana (5), uMongameli kumele aqhatshe oyedwa wabakhethiweyo ukuze aphathe leso sikhundla.

(b) inxuse uMongameli kanye loZulu ukuthi bakhethe amabizo; njalo

(d) iqoqe uluhlu lwabantu abathathu abakhethiweyo njengabantu abafanele lowo msebenzi; njalo

(5) Nxa uMongameli engananzelela ukuthi akula muntu ofanele umsebenzi eluhlwini aluphiwe njengokubalulwe kuSigatshana (4)(e), kumele enze ukuba iKomishini yemisebenzi yezeMithetho imnikeze olunye uluhlu lwabantu abathathu abafanele lowo msebenzi, uMongameli abeseqhatsha oyedwa wabo ukuze aphathe lesosikhundla.

(6) UMongameli kumele abangele ukuthi kukhitshwe umbiko owazisa ngokuqhatshwa konke okwenzakalayo okuphathelane lalesi sigaba egwalweni lweGazette.

(7) Izikhundla zikaMahluleli omkhulu woMthethwandaba wezeMisebenzi loMehluleli omkhulu woMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa kwaMandla kumele ziphathwe ngomunye umahluleli kumbe omunye ngaphezulu kumbe umahluleli osaphethe isikhundla, kusiya ngesimo esikhona, emthethwandaba ofaneleyo, njalo baqhatshwa nguNdunankulu waBehluleli ngemva kokubonisana leKomishini yemisebenzi yezeMithetho. [Isigatshana lesi sitshintshwe yiSigaba 6 soMthetho 10 ka2017]


Ukuqhatshwa kwabasamele izilawulamthetho

(1) Nxa isikhundla seNdunankulu yaBahluleli singelamuntu loba nxa ophethe leso sikhundla engenelisi ukwenza imisebenzi yalesosikhundla, uMsekeli weNdunankulu yaBehluleli uyaphatha isikhundla sakhe, kodwa nxa izikhundla zombili zingelabantu loba abanikazi balezo zikhundla bobabili bengenelisi ukwenza imisebenzi yazo, umahluleli onguye omkhulu kulabanye esikhundleni oweSisekamthetho uyasebenza njengeNdunankulu yaBehluleli.

(2) Nxa isikhundla sika—

(a)

Mongameli waBehluleli womthethwandaba Ophezulu;

(c)

uMehluleli omkhulu woMthethwandaba wezokuPhathwa kwaMandla;

(b) uMehluleli omkhulu woMthethwandaba weziMisebenzi; kumbe

singelamuntu loba nxa umnikazi wesikhundla engenelisi ukwenza imisebenzi yalesosikhundla, uMahluleli omkhulu onguye omkhulu kulabanye esikhundleni woMthethwandaba lowo uyasebenza njengo Mongameli waBehluleli. [Isigatshana lesi sitshintshwe yiSigaba 7 soMthetho 10 ka2017]

(3) Nxa imisebenzi yomunye uMahluleli weMthethwandaba Omkhulu, uMthethwandaba Wemisebenzi loba uMthethwandaba Wezokuphathwa Kwamandla idingakala okwesikhathi esifitshane uMongameli, esebenza ngokucetshiswa yiKomishini yemisebenzi yeZilawulamthetho, engaqhatsha umuntu owake waba nguMahluleli ukuthi aphathe leso sikhundla okwesikhathi esingadluli izinyanga ezilitshumi lambili, leso sikhathi esesikhundleni singengezelelwa njalo okwesikhathi esizinyanga ezilitshumi lambili.

(4) Abantu abaqhatshelwe ukuphatha izikhundla ngaphansi kukasigatshana (3) bangaqhubeka behlala njengaBehluleli ngemva kokuba isikhathi abasiqhatshelweyo

sesiphelile, kusenzelwa ukuthi baphathe izingqubo ezazisungulwe phambi kwabo ngesikhathi besaphethe leso sikhundla. 182 Ukuqhatshwa kwaboMantshi lamanye amalunga ezilawulamthetho

UMthetho wePhalemende kumele uphawule ngokuqhatshwa kwaboMantshi kanye labanye abasezikhundleni zezilawulamthetho abangayisibo aBehluleli, kodwa—

(a)

omantshi kumele baqhatswe yiKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho;

(c)

konke ukuqhatsha okunjengalokhu kumele kwenziwe mgceke njalo kungelakwesaba, ukukhetha, ukuthatha uhlangothi loba ukubandlulula.

(b) izisebenzi ezikugatsha lwezilawulamthetho ezingasibo oMantshi loba abehluleli kumele ziqhatshwe ngemvumo yeKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho;

183 Abasezikhundleni zezilawulamthetho abafanelanga baqhatshelwe imithethwandaba edlula owodwa

Ngaphandle nxa kuphawulwe kulesi Sisekamthetho, umuntu akumelanga aqhatshelwe isikhundla sezilawulamthetho emithethawndaba edlula owodwa. 184 Ukuqhatshwa kwezilawulamthetho kumele kukhombise isimo somphakathi

Ukuqhatshwa kwezilawulamthetho kumele kukhombise ukwehlukana kwabantu kanye lokubakhona kobulili obutshiyeneyo eZimbabwe. 185 Isifungo sokuqhatshwa

(1) Anduba iNdunankulu yaBehluleli loba uMsekeli weNdunankulu yaBehluleli angene esikhundleni, kumele enze isifungo sezilawulamthetho ngendlela ephawulwe kuhlelo lwesithathu, phambi kukaMongameli loba phambi komuntu ophiwe imvumo nguMongameli.

(2) Anduba uMehluleli, ongayisuye iNdunankulu yaBehluleli loba uMsekeli weNdunankulu angene esikhundleni, kumele enze isifungo seZilawulamthetho ngendlela ephawulwe kuhlelo lwesithathu phambi kweNdunankulu loba uMehluleli omkhulu kulabanye esikhundleni oyabe ekhona,

(3) IMithetho yePhalamende okuyiyo okuqhatshwa ngayo oMantshi lamanye amalunga eZilawulamthetho, angayisibo aBehluleli kumele itsho isifungo okumele senziwe yilawo malunga ezilawulamthetho. 186 Isikhathi esibekiweyo sokuhlala esikhundleni kwaBehluleli

(1) Abehluleli boMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho baqhatshelwa ukusebenza okwebanga elingengezelelwayo elingedluli iminyaka elitshumi lanhlanu, kodwa—

(a)

kumele batshiye umsebenzi isikhathi leso singakafiki nxa sebefikise iminyaka yobudala engamatshumi ayisikhombisa;

(b) ngemva kokuba sebeqedile ibanga abaliqhatshelweyo, bangaqhatshwa njengaBehluleli boMthethwandaba Ophezulu loba uMthethwandaba Omkhulu, nxa kuyikuthi bafanele.

(2) ABehluleli boMthethwandaba Ophezulu, aboMthethwandaba Omkhulu labanye aBehluleli baphatha isikhundla kusukela ngelanga lokungena kwabo esikhundleni baze bafike iminyaka yobudala engamatshumi ayisikhombisa, lapho okumele batshiye khona umsebenzi.

(3) Umuntu angaqhatshwa njengoMahluleli weMthethwandaba Ophezulu, uMthethwandaba Omkhulu leminye imithethwandaba okwesikhathi esithize esibekiweyo, kodwa nxa umuntu eseqhatshiwe ngokunjalo, ongayisuye oqhatshelwe ukuthatha isikhundla somunye, uyama ukuba nguMahluleli ekufikiseni kwakhe iminyaka yobudala engamatshumi ayisikhombisa lanxa isikhathi ayesiqhatshelwe singakapheli.

(4) Lanxa UMahluleli esetshiye umsebenzi loba esefikise iminyaka yobudala engamatshumi ayisikhombisa loba, malungana loMahluli woMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho loba uMahluleli okukhulunywe ngaye kusigatshana (3), esefikise ukuphela kwesikhathi ayesiqhatshelwe sokuba sesikhundleni, angaqhubeka ehlala njengoMahluli kusenzelwa ukuthi kuphathwe izingqubo ezaziqaliswe phambi kwakhe esasenguMahluleli.

(5) UMahluleli angatshiya isikhundla sakhe loba ngasiphi isikhathi ngokubhala isaziso salokho esiyakuMongameli esizahanjiswa yiKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho.

(6) Isikhundla sikaMahluleli akumelanga simiswe ngesikhathi esibekelwe ukuqhatshwa kwakhe. 187 Ukususwa kwaBehluleli esikhundleni

(1) UMahluleli angasuswa esikhundleni kuphela ngezizatho—

(a)

zokwehluleka ukuqhuba imisebenzi ephathelane lesikhundla ngesizatho sokufiphala engqondweni kumbe emzimbeni;

(b) zokwehluleka umsebenzi okwedlulisa amalawulo; loba

(c)

zokungaziphathi ngemfanelo okwedlulisa amalawulo;

njalo uMahluleli kangeke asuswe esikhundleni ngaphandle nxa kulandelwa okuphawulwe kulesiSigaba.

(2) Nxa uMongameli ebona ukuthi indaba yokukhipha iNdunankulu yaBehluleli esikhundleni kumele ichwayisiswe, uMongameli kumele aqhatshe iqula laBehluleli ukuthi lihlolisise leyo ndaba.

(3) Nxa iKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho icebisa uMongameli ebona ukuthi indaba yokukhipha iloba nguwuphi uMahluleli esikhundleni kumele ichwayisiswe, uMongameli kumele aqhatshe iqula laBehluleli ukuthi lihlolisise leyo ndaba.

(4) Idale laBehluleli eliqhatshwe ngaphansi kwalesisigaba kumele libe lamalunga angekho ngaphansi kwamathathu aqhatshwe nguMongameli, abathi—

(a)

oyedwa kumele abengumuntu o—

(i) wake wasebenaza njengoMahluleli woMthethwandaba Ophezulu loba woMthethwandaba Omkhulu eZimbabwe;

(ii) phethe loba owake waphatha isikhundla njengoMahluleli womthethwandaba olomkhawulo wamandla ongancitshiswanga eindabeni zamacala elizweni lapho umthetho osetshenziswa ngumuntu wonke uyiRoman-Dutch loba isingisi, njalo lapho isilungu esiphathwa khona njengolimi lokusetshenziswa ezindaweni ezinengi;

(b) oyedwa kumele akhethwe eluhlwini lwamagqwetha amathathu aleminyaka eyisikhombisa esebenza loba edlula lelonani akhethwe yinhlanganiso, emiswe ngaphansi koMthetho wePhalamende, abamele amagqwetha eZimbabwe.

(5) Inhlanganiso okukhulunywa ngayo kusigatshana (4)(b) kumele ilungise uluhlu okukhulunywe ngalo kulesosigatshana nxa uMongameli ekudinga lokho.

(6) UMongameli kumele abeke omunye wamalunga edale labehluleli eliqhatshwe ngaphansi kwalesisigaba ukuthi abengumgcinisihlalo waleloqula.

(7) Idale laBehluleli eliqhatshwe ngaphansi kwesigatshana (2) loba (3) kumele lihlolisise ngendaba yokususwa kukaMahluleli esikhundleni njalo selikwenzile lokho kumele libike elikutholileyo kuMongameli njalo lisekele ukuthi uMahluleli engasuswa esikhundleni yini loba hatshi.

(8) UMongameli kumele enze okuhambelana lokusekelwe liqula laBehluleli kulandelwa usigatshana (7).

(9) Iqula eliqhatshwe ngaphansi kwalesisigaba lilamalungelo afanayo kanye lamandla njengemaKhomishina ngaphansi koMthetho wamaKhomoshini (Commissions of Inquiry act) [isahluko 10:07] loba omunye umthetho obekwa endaweni yalowo.

(10) Nxa indaba yokususa uMahluleli esikhundleni ihanjiswe edale laBehluleli ngaphansi kwalesisahluko, uMahluleli angamiswa esikhundleni kuze kuthi uMongameli, ngokusekela kwequla laBehluleli, asuse ukumiswa loba asuse uMahluleli esikhundleni.

(11) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungapha amandlla iKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho loba idale labehluleli eliqhatshwe ngaphansi kwalesisigaba ukuthi lidinge ukuthi iloba nguwuphi uMahluleli alethe incwadi yokuhlolwa kwakhe okwenziwe liqula labezempilakahle elimiselwe lowo msebenzi, ukuze kube lesiqiniselo ngempilakahle yakhe emzimbeni lasengqondweni. 188 Izimiso zokusebenza kanye lokuqhatshwa kwamalunga aBehluleli

(1) ABehluleli balelungelo emiholweni, izimbhadalo kanye lezinye inzuzo ezibekwa ngezikhathi ezitshiyeneyo ngabeKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho ngemvumo kaMongameli oyipha ngemva kokukhululumisana loMphathintambo obona ngezokulunga njalo langokuvuma kukaMphathintambo obona ngezezimali.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule ngezimiso zomsebenzi wezisebenzi zabasesikhundleni zezilawulamthetho abangayisibo aBehluleli njalo kumele aqiniseke ukuthi ukuphakanyiswa kwabo emsebenzini, ukutshintshelwa ukuya kweyinye indawo, kanye lokukhitshwa emsebenzini, kanye lamanye amanyathela okukhuza/okuqondisa abawathathelwayo, kwenziwa—

(a)

ngemvumo yeKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho; njalo

(b) ngendlela engathathi hlangothi njalo emgceke njalo kungelakwesaba, ukukhetha loba ukubandlulula.

(3) Imiholo, ama alawensi lezinye inzuzo zamalunga aBehluleli angumlandu wesikhwama sezimali ezihlanganisiweyo.

(4) Imiholo, lezimali zezindleko kanye lezinye inzuzo zamalunga ezilawulamthetho akumelanga zehliswe ngesikhathi lapho bephethe loba besaphethe isikhundla. ISAHLUKWANA 3 Ikomishini Yemisebenzi Yezilawulamthetho 189 Ukumiswa kanye lokubunjwa kweKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho

(1) KuleKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho egoqela—

(a)

iNdunankulu yabeHluleli;

(c)

uMongameli wabeHluleli woMthethwandaba Omkhulu;

(e)

uGqwethanhloko;

2013

(b) uMsekeli weNdunankulu yaBehluleli;

(d) uMahluleli oyedwa okhethwe ngaBehluleli boMthethwandaba obona ngeSisekamthetho, uMthethwandaba Ophezulu, uMthethwandaba Omkhulu, uMthethwandaba Wezemisebenzi kanye loMthethwandaba obona ngokuqhutshwa kwezingqubo ngemfanelo;

(f)

iNdunankulu yaboMantshi;

(g) uMgcinisihlalo weKomishini yeMisebenzi kaHulumende;

(h) abameli bomthetho abathathu abaleminyaka eqala kweyisikhombisa besemsebenzini abakhethwe yinhlanganiso, equkethwe ngaphansi komthetho wePhalamende, emele abameli bomthetho eZimbabwe;

(i)

(j)

isikhwicamfundo esisodwa loba umbalisi weYunivesithi omkhulu ofundisa ngezomthetho okhethwe yinhlanganiso emelele ubunengi bababalisi bezomthetho emayunivesithi eZimbabwe loba, lapho inhlanganiso enjalo ingekho;

umuntu oyedwa oseleminyaka ephezu kweyisikhombisa esebenza eZimbabwe njengonozimali loba umhlolizimali, njalo okhethwe yinhlanganiso, equkethwe ngaphansi koMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, omelele abantu labo;

(k) umuntu oyedwa oleminyaka ephezu kweyisikhombisa esebenza esesikhundleni esiphathelane lokuholwa kwezisebenzi.

(2) Indunankulu yaBehluleli loba, lapho ingekho, uMsekeli weNdunankulu yaboMahluli uyathungamela emihlanganweni yeKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho, njalo nxa bonke bengekho iloba kuwuphi umhlangano amalunga ayabe ekhona lapho ayakhetha elinye ilunga lawo ukuthi lilawule umhlangano.

(3) Amalunga eKomishini yemisebenzi yezilawulamithetho okukhulunywe ngayo kuziqendu (d), (h), (i), (j) lakusigatshana (1) baqhatshelwa esikhundleni okwesigaba esisodwa esiqhubeka okweminyaka eyisithupha njalo esingeke sengezwa nxa sesiphelile. 190 Imisebenzi yeKomishini yemisebenzi yezilawulamthetho

(1) IKomishini yemisebenzi yezilawulamthetho ingacebisa uHulumende loba kukuyiphi indaba ephathelane lezilawulamthetho loba ukuphathwa kokulunga, njalo uHulumende kumele aphathe ukucetshiswa lokho ngemfanelo.

(2) IKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho kumele iphakamise njalo ifuqe ukukhululeka kanye lokwenelisa ukuvuma umlandu kwaBehluleli kanye lokuphathwa kakuhle njalo okumgceke kwemisebenzi yezokwahlulela okulungileyo kweleZimbabwe, njalo ilamandla wonke adingakalayo alumsebenzi.

(3) IKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho, ngemvumo yoMphathintambo obona ngezokwahlulela okulungileyo, ingenza izimiso zaloba yiwuphi umsebenzi okuphawulwe kulesisigaba.

(4) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungapha kuKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho mayelana lokuqhatshwa izindlela zokuqondiswa lezimiso zomsebenzi wabantu abaqhatshwe eMthethwandaba weSisekamthetho, uMthethwandaba Ophezulu, uMthethwandaba Omkhulu uMthethwandaba Wezemisebenzi, uMthethwandaba Wezokuqhutshwa kwezingqubo leminye iMithethwandaba.

191 Ukufanela lokubamgceke kwezingqubo zeKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho

IKomishini yemisebenzi yeziLawulamthetho kumele iqhube imisebenzi yayo ngendlela elungileyo, efaneleyo njalo emgceke. ISAHLUKWANA 4 Okunye 192 UMthetho ozalawulwa

UMthetho ozalawulwa yimithethwandaba yeZimbabwe ngumthetho oqalise ukusebenza kusukela ngelanga omiswe ngalo, lokuguqulwa owenziwe khona ngokuhamba kwesikhathi. 193 Umkhawulo wamandla emithethwandaba emacaleni okwephula imithetho

Kuphela imithethwandaba elandelayo yiyo engasebenzisa loba iphiwe imandla emacaleni okwephula imithetho—

(a) uMthethwandaba obona ngeSisekamthetho, uMthethwandaba Ophezulu, uMthethwandaba Omkhulu kanye loMthethwandaba kaMantshi;

(b) umthethwandaba loba idale lezilawulamthetho eliphatha amacala angaphansi komthetho wokuqondisa, kuze kufike ebangeni lokuthi umkhawulo wamandla uyadingakala ekwenzeni ukuthi ukuqondiswa kwenzakale kulabo abaqondiswayo. ISAHLUKO 9 Izimiso Zokuphathwa Lokukhokhelwa Kwemisebenzi Ephathelane Lozulu 194 Okuqakathekiswayo kanye lezimiso ezihola ukuphathwa kwemisebenzi ephathelane lozulu

(1) Abaphathi bemisebenzi ephathelane lozulu emabangeni wonke kaHulumende, kugoqela izinhlanganiso kanye labameli beSizwe, kunye lezinhlanganiso ezingaphansi kukaHulumende lamanye amaqembu aqhuba imisebenzi ephathelane lozulu, kumele baholwe yizimiso kanye lokuqakathekiswa ngaphansi kombuso oqotho njengokuqukethwe kulesi Sisekamthetho. Lokhu kugoqela izimiso ezilandelayo—

(a)

izinga eliphakemeyo lokuziphatha ngendlela elungileyo emisebenzini kumele likhuthazwe njalo lilandelwe kokuphela;

(b) kumele kukhuthazwe ukusetshenziswa kwezingcebo ngendlela efaneleyo njalo engela butshapha;

(c)

abaphathi bemisebenzi ephathelane lozulu kumele babe lombono ojongise intuthuko;

(d) imisebenzi kumele iqhutshwe ngendlela engathathi hlangothi, elungileyo, elinganisa abantu njalo engela nkethabetshabi;

(e) izidingo zikazulu kumele zigcwalisiswe ngesikhathi esikhanyayo, njalo uzulu kumele akhuthazwe ukuthi aphatheke ngokupheleleyo ekulungisweni kwezimiso ezitshiyatshiyeneyo;

(f)

abaphathi bemisebenzi ephathelane lozulu kumele banelise ukunika izimpendulo edale lePhalamende lakuzulu mayelana lemisebenzi yabo;

(g) izinhlanganiso kanye labameli bakaHulumende kumabanga wonke kumele basebenzelane ngokudlelana okuhle;

(h) ukuqhutshwa kwemisebenzi ngendlela ebalulekileyo kumele kuqiniswe ngokunika uzulu ulwazi olufinyelelekayo njalo oluqondileyo ngesikhathi esifaneleyo;

(i)

kumele kudalwe njalo kukhuthazwe imikhuba emihle yokuhola izisebenzi lokuthuthukisa imisebenzi ukuze abantu basebenzise amakhono abo ngokupheleleyo;

(j) ukuqhatshwa kwabaphathi bemisebenzi ephathelane lozulu kumele kwenziwe ngendlela egoqela ngokulingeneyo abantu abavela kunhlangothi zonke zeZimbabwe ngokwehlukana kwazo;

(k) izingqubo zokuqhatsha izisebenzi, ukuzifundisa kanye lokuziphakamisa kumele zilandele ukufanela, ukwanelisa, iziphokophelo, ukwahlulela okulungileyo kanye lokulinganiswa kwabesilisa labesifazana, ndawonye lokufakwa kwabalobugoga ezikhundleni ezifanayo; njalo iSizwe kumele sithathe amanyathelo, okugoqela amanyathelo omthetho, ukuze sikhuthaze lezi zinto eziqakathekiswayo lezimiso zonke.

(2) Ukuqhatshelwa ezikhundleni ezikumabanga wonke kaHulumende, kugoqela izinhlanganiso zikaHulumende kanye lezinhlanganiso ezingaphansi kukaHulumende lamanye amaqembu asebenzela uzulu, kumele kwenziwe ngokufanela komuntu ukuphatha isikhundla kungakakhangelwa okunye konke. 195 Amabhizimusi angaphansi kweSizwe

(1) Phezu kokulandela imigomo enikwe ngaphansi kweSigaba 194(1), amankampani lamanye amabhizimusi kaHulumende kumbe aholwa ngokupheleleyo nguHulumende kumele aqhube imisebenzi yawo ngendlela elondoloza ukuphumelela kwamabhizimusi njalo alandele iziqondiso ezilungileyo zokuqhutshwa kuhle kwemisebenzi.

(2) Amankampani lamanye amabhizimusi abalulwe kuSigatshana (1) kumele amise izindlela zokuthola ingcebo ezibalulekileyo, ezimgceke njalo ezivumela ukuncintisana kwabatholisa lezongcebo. 196 Imilandu yezisebenzi zikazulu lezimiso zokukhokhela

(1) Amandla anikwa isisebenzi sikazulu aqukethe ithemba likazulu njalo kumele asetshenziswe ngendlela—

(a) ehambelana lezinjongo langezinhloso zalesi Sisekamthetho;

(b) ekhombisa inhlonipho kuzulu lokuzinikela ekumsebenzeleni, hatshi ukumbusa; njalo

(c)

ekhuthaza ukuthi uzulu athembe umsebenzi owenziwa yisisebenzi sikazulu.

(2) Izisebenzi zikazulu kumele ziziphathe, emphakathini lasensitha, ngendlela enqabela ukuphikisana phakathi kwezifiso zazo lezinqineko zomsebenzi wazo, njalo zingaze zaziphatha ngendlela eyehlisa isithunzi somsebenzi wazo.

(3) Izisebenzi zikazulu eziphethe izikhundla kumele zilandele imigomo elandelayo yokukhokhela—

(a) ubuqotho lokungabi lenkethabetshabi ekwenzeni izinqumo;

(b) ukuthembeka ekuqhubeni imisebenzi kazulu;

(c)

ukuba lezimpendulo kuzulu mayelana lezinqumo kanye lamanyathelo athethweyo; njalo

(d) ukuziphatha kuhle kanye lokuzinikela ekusebenzeleni abantu.

197 Izikhathi ezibekiweyo zokuphatha izikhundla kwezinkokheli zezinhlanganiso ezingaphansi kukaHulumende

UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende unganciphisa izikhathi ezibekiweyo ezokuphatha isikhundla kweziphathamandla lezinhloko zenhlanganiso eziholwa nguHulumende lezinye izinhlanganiso zikaHulumende ezikwezokuthengiselana lezisebenzelana lozulu kumbe eziholwa ngokupheleleyo nguHulumende. 198 Imithetho yokuphoqelela iSahluko 9

UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele unikeze amanyathelo okuphoqelela izibopho zalesiSahluko, kugoqela amanyathelo—

(a)

adinga ukuthi izisebenzi zikazulu zihlale ziveza impahla zazo;

(c)

abalisa amazinga okubuswa kuhle kwezinhlanganiso okumele ananzwe yizinhlanganiso eziholwa nguHulumende langezinye izinhlanganiso zokuhweba ezingaphansi kumbe eziholwa ngokupheleleyo nguHulumende;

(b) abeka imithetho yezindlela zokuziphatha ngazo;

(d) avumela ukujeziswa kwabantu abephula izinqumo ezimiswe kulesiSahluko kumbe ezeminye imithetho ebalula izindlela zokuziphatha ngazo. ISAHLUKO 10 Izisebenzi Zikahulumende 199 Ukusebenzela uHulumende

(1) Kulogatsha olulodwa lwezisebenzi zikaHulumende oluyilo olukhangelane lokuphatha iZimbabwe.

(2) Ugatsha lwezisebenzi zikaHulumende lulabantu abaqhatshwe yiSizwe abangasibo—

(a)

malunga awezokuvikela ilizwe lamanye amaqembu ezokuvikela ilizwe angamiswa;

(b) behluleli, omantshi labanye ababona ngokuhamba kwezomthwethwandaba ezimiswe nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende;

(c)

malunga amaKomishini amiswe yiSisekamthetholesi;

(e)

yiloba ngubani olesikhundla esibhalwe kulesiSisekamthetho kumbe uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, ukuthi kabi yingxenye yabasebenzela uHulumende.

(d) zisebenzi zePhalamende; njalo

(3) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule ngokuhlelwa, isimo, ukuphathwa, isimiso kanye lokuqondiswa kulandelwa usigaba 203, izimiso zokusebenza kwamalunga ezisebenzi zikaHulumende. 200 Ukuziphatha kwezisebenzi zikaHulumende

(1) Amalunga ezisebenzi zikaHulumende kumele enze njengokukuSisekamthetho lesi lasemthethweni.

(2) Akula sisebenzi sika Hulumende okumele silandele umlayo ongekho emthethweni.

(3) Ilunga lezisebenzi zikaHulumende akumelanga lithi lisenza umsebenzi walo—

(a)

lisebenze ngendlela elobandlululo;

(c)

livalele intando esemthethweni yaloba yiliphi ibandla lezombusazwe; kumbe

(b) likhulise intando lodumo lwebandla lezombusazwe;

(d) lephule amalungelo ayinsika lenkululeko yaloba nguwuphi umuntu.

(4) Amalunga ezisebenzi zikaHulumende akumelanga abelezikhundla emabandleni ezombusazwe.

(5) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule ukuze kuqiniseke ukuthi imisebenzi kaHulumende ayithathi hlangothi kwezombusazwe. 201 UMphathintambo okhangelane lezisebenzi zikaHulumende

UMongameli kumele akhethe uMphathintambo ozakhangelana lezisebenzi zikaHulumende. 202 Ukumiswa lokubunjwa kweKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende

(1) Kule Komishini yeMisebenzi kaHulumende equkethe—

(a)

umgcinisihlalo loMsekeli kamgcinisihlalo; njalo

(b) amanye amalunga angekho ngaphansi kwamabili loba phezulu kwamahlanu; akhethwe nguMongameli.

(2) Amalunga eKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende kumele akhethwe ngolwazi lwawo loba ubungcitshi ekuholeni lasekuphatheni kanye lekwenzeni umsebenzi. 203 Umsebenzi weKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende

(1) IKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende isebenza okulandelayo—

(a)

ukukhetha abantu abafanele njalo abalolwazi olufaneleyo lokuba sezikhundleni zemisebenzi kaHulumende;

(b) ilandela usigaba 65(5), ukuqinisa lokulungisa umumo womsebenzi kugoqela umholo imali ephiwayo lezinye inzuzo zamalunga asebenzela uHulumende;

(c)

ukulawula kanye lokuba lamandla okuqondisa kumalunga asebenzela uHulumende;

(d) ukuphenya izikhalazo lokubuya losizo kuzikhalazo ezamalunga asebenzela uHulumende mayelana lokwenziwa loba okungenziwa yilabo abasezikhundleni;

(e)

(f)

ukuthatha amanyathelo awokuqiniseka ukuthi umsebenzi uqhutshwa ngemfanelo kanye lempilakahle yezisebenzi zikaHulumende jikelele;

ukuqiniseka ukuthi amalunga ezisebenzi zikaHulumende enza umsebenzi wawo ngokufaneleyo okungathathi hlangothi;

(g) ukucebisa uMongameli loMphathintambo ngaloba yiyiphi indaba ephathelane lemisebenzi kaHulumende;

(h) ukuphakamisa kuzo zonke izisebenzi zikaHulumende okumqoka kanye lezimiso okuphawulwe kulesi Sisekamthetho; njalo

(i)

ukwenza laloba kuyini okulawulelwa iKomishini yiSisekamthetholoba uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) IKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende, ngemvumo kaMphathintambo okhangelane lemisebenzi kaHulumende ingabeka izimiso ngaloba kuyini okuphawulwe kusigatshana (1).

(3) iKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende kumele yenze umsebenzi wayo ngendlela ehambelana leziqondiso ezilotshiweyo engaziphiwa nguMphathintambo okhangelane lemisebenzi kaHulumende.

(4) Ekulungiseni imiholo, imali yezinye indleko lezinye inzuzo zamalunga asebenzela uHulumende, iKomishini yamalunga emisebenzi kaHulumende kumele isebenze ngokuvumela kukaMongameli okuphiwe ekuvumeni kukaMphathintambo wezemali emuva kokuba esexoxisane loMphathintambo okhangelane lezisebenzi zikaHulumende. 204 Amanxusa elizwe bekwamanye amazwe labanye abakhulu abamela iZimbabwe

UMongameli angakhetha abantu abangaba ngabameli belizwe kwamanye amazwe kumbe abanye abameli abakhulu abamela iZimbabwe kwamanye amazwe kumbe bayemela kuzinhlanganiso eziphathelane lamazwe omhlaba njalo angasusa ngaloba yisiphi isikhathi abantu labo emisebenzini yabo. 205 ONobhala Abakhulu

(1) ONobhala abakhulu bakhethwa nguMongameli emuva kokuxoxisana leKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende.

(2) Isikhathi sikaNobhala omkhulu ehofisini siyiminyaka emihlanu njalo siyaphindeka kuphela nxa elolwazi olufaneleyo esebenza ngokufanele kulempumela ebonakalayo. ISAHLUKO 11 Umvikela ISAHLUKWANA 1

Izindingeko Jikelele 206 Ukuvikelwa kwelizwe

(1) Inhloso yokuvikelwa kwelizwe leZimbabwe kumele iveze isibopho sabantu baseZimbabwe esokuphila ngokulingana, bekhululekile, belokuthula, lokubambana, bengela kwesaba njalo belokuphumelela.

(2) Ukuvikelwa kwelizwe laseZimbabwe kumele kulondolozwe yiso lesi Sisekamthetho lomthetho.

(3) Ukuvikelwa kwelizwe kumele kubanjwe ngokuhlonipha ikakhulu—

(a)

Isiqu samalungelo lokukhululeka lokuqakatheka kokubusa ngentando yabantu okuqakathekiswa yiSisekamthetho; njalo

(b) ukubusa komthetho.

207 Imisebenzi yabavikela ilizwe

(1) Imisebenzi yabavikela ilizwe lase Zimbabwe igoqela—

(a)

Amabutho okuvikela isizwe;

(b) AmaPholisa;

(c)

Abacuphi;

(e)

Laloba yibaphi abomvikela abamiswe nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(d) AbeZintolongo lokuQondiswa Kwezibotshwa;

(2) Abavikela ilizwe babuswa yilo umthetho weSisekamthetho, uMongameli, lekhabinethi kaHulumende, njalo bahlolwa yiPhalamende.

(3) Emalungeni asebenza ukuvikela ilizwe kumele kukhombise imihlobo etshiyeneyo yabantu beZimbabwe. 208 Ukuziphatha kwabavikela ilizwe

(1) Amalunga avikela ilizwe kumele aziphathe ngendlela ehambelana leSisekamthetho kanye lomthetho.

(2) Akumelanga abavikela ilizwe loba omunye wamalunga abo bathi besenza umsebenzi wabo—

(a)

bawenze ngendlela elobandlululo;

(c)

bavalele izifiso ezisemthethweni zebandla lombusazwe, loba umgomo; loba

(b) baqhubekise izifiso zaloba yiliphi ibandla lombusazwe;

(d)

bephule amalungelo lezinkululeko eziyinsika zaloba nguwuphi umuntu.

(3) Amalunga abavikela ilizwe akumelanga abengabahambela phambili loba abalezikhundla emabandleni ezombusazwe loba enhlanganisweni yezombusazwe.

(4) Abasebenza ukuvikela ilizwe akumelanga baqhatshwe loba baphathise kwezinye izingatsha zomphakathi, ngaphandle kwesikhathi sekulomonakalo kaZulu. 209 Ikhansili yokuvikelwa kwelizwe

(1) Kukhona ikhansili yokuvikelwa kwelizwe eloMongameli welizwe, engumgcinisihlalo, loMsekeli kaMongameli labaPhathintambo abanjalo lamalunga ezokuvikela ilizwe labanye abangabe bephawulwe eMthethweni wePhalamende.

(2) Umsebenzi wenhlanganiso yokuvikelwa kwelizwe yiku—

(a)

thuthukisa izimiso zelizwe zokuvikela ilizwe laseZimbabwe;

(c)

ukwenza laloba yiyiphi eminye imisebenzi engabe iphawuliwe eMthethweni omiswe yiPhalamende.

(b) ukwazisa lokucebisa uMongameli ngezindaba ezimayelana lokuvikeleka kwelizwe; njalo

(3) Ababusi bezokuvikelwa kwelizwe kumele babikele inhlangano yokuvikelwa kwelizwe ngomumo wokuvikeleka eZimbabwe njengalokhu okungadingwa ngokufaneleyo yinhlangano. 210 Indlela ezimeleyo yezikhalazo

UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphe indlela esebenzayo njalo ezimeleyo eyokuthola lokuchwayisisa izikhalazo ezivela kumalunga kazulu mayelana lokuziphatha okungalunganga kwabasebenza ukuvikela ilizwe, lokulungisa umonakalo odalwe yilokhu kungaziphathi.

ISAHLUKWANA 2 Amabutho Omvikela 211 Amabutho omvikela

(1) Amabutho omvikela eZimbabwe agoqela amasotsha, amabutho asemoyeni leminye imisebenzi engamiswa ngaphansi koMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) Amabutho omvikela yiwo wodwa amabutho empi avunyelwayo eZimbabwe ngokusemthethweni.

(3) Amabutho omvikela kumele ahloniphe amalungelo ayinsika lezinkululeko zabantu bonke, angabi lokukhetha abantu, abe lesimilo sobuzwe, athande ilizwe lawo, abe zingcitshi njalo azehlise emphakathini njengoba kubekwa yiSisekamthetho lesi.

(4) Amabutho omvikela kumele ahlale egcinwe elibutho lempini elilokuziphatha ngemfanelo.

(5) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule ngokuhleleka, isimo, ukuphathwa, umthetho, ukuziphatha okufaneleyo lokukhuphula lokwehliswa esikhundleni kwezinduna lamanye amalunga, kulandelwa usigaba 218, izimiso zokusebenza kwamalunga amabutho okuvikela ilizwe. 212 Umsebenzi wamabutho omvikela Umsebenzi wamabutho omvikela yikuvikela iZimbabwe, abantu bayo, ukuphepha kwesizwe lesizotha somhlaba waso, lokugcina iSisekamthetho lesi. 213 Ukuthunyelwa kwamabutho omvikela

(1) Kulandelwa iSisekamthetho, nguMongameli yedwa njengendunankulu yamabutho omvikela, olamandla—

(a)

okuvumela ukuthumela amabutho omvikela loba;

(b) okubeka isinqumo sokusebenza kwamabutho omvikela.

(2) Ngemvumo kaMongameli, amabutho omvikela angathunywa phakathi eZimbabwe—

(a)

evikela iZimbabwe;

(c)

encedisa amapholisa lezinye inhlanganiso zabantu nxa kungaba lengozi evele ingalindelwanga kumbe isehlakalo esibi.

(b) encedisa amapholisa ekugcineni ukuthula emphakathini; loba

(3) Ngokulawula kukaMongameli, amabutho avikela ilizwe angathunyelwa ngaphandle kwelizwe leZimbabwe—

(a)

ezinhlelweni zokugcina ukuthula bengaphansi kwenhlanganiso yamazwe amanyeneyo loba eyinye inhlanganiso yamazwe omhlaba loba umkhono lapha iZimbabwe elilunga khona;

(b) ukuvikela umhlaba welinye ilizwe langaphandle;

(c)

ukugcwalisisa isivumelwano esiphathelane lamazwe omhlaba; loba

(d) ukuvikela iZimbabwe lokuvikela okungokwayo.

(4) Ngenxezu ezimbili kwezintathu ezamalunga ePhalamende kuhlangene iDale leSenethi le Ndlu yabaMeli, iPhalamende ingavumelana ukuthi Amabutho omvikela angaphandle kweZimbabwe aphendukiswe.

(5) Lapha iPhalamende isivumile kubana Amabutho omvikela athunywe phandle kwelizwe aphendukiswe, uMongameli kumele athathe amanyathelo wonke afaneleyo, ukubona ukubana Amabutho omvikela aphendukiswe, lokhu akwenze ecabangela ukuphepha kwezisebenzi lezikhali zaseZimbabwe. 214 Umlandu wezombusazwe ngokuthunyelwa kwamabutho ezomvikela Nxa amabutho omvikela engathunyelwa—

(a)

phakathi kweZimbabwe ukuze ancedise ekuletheni ukuthula emphakathini; loba

(b) ngaphandle kweZimbabwe;

uMongameli kumele enze ukuthi iPhalamende ibikelwe masinyane njalo ngokugcweleyo njalo, izizatho zokuthunyelwa kwamabutho—

(i) lapho ethunyelwa kweleZimbabwe, kumele kuvezwe lapha abathunyelwa khona;

(ii) lapho ethunyelwa ngaphandle kwelizwe, kumele kuvezwe ilizwe lapha athunyelwa khona. 215 UMphathintambo okhangelane lamabutho omvikela

UMongameli kumele akhethe uMphathintambo okhangelane lamabutho omvikela.

216 Ukuholwa kwamabutho omvikela

(1) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungaphawula ukuthi—

(a)

amabutho omvikela kufanele aholwe yiNdunayamabutho eyodwa; loba

(b) ugatsha lunye ngalunye lwamabutho omvikela, loba amabili kumbe amanengi angaba ngaphansi kweyinye iNduna.

(2) INduna zonke zamabutho omvikela lezinye iNduna zengatsha zamabutho omvikela zikhethwa nguMongameli ngemuva kokukhulumisana loMphathintambo okhangelane lezamabutho omvikela.

(3) Izinduna zamabutho omvikela lezinye induna zengatsha zamabutho omvikela ziqhatshwa ukuba ziphathe isikhundla okweminyaka engadluli emihlanu, njalo umuntu akumelanga asebenze kuloba yisiphi salezo zikhundla okwamahlandla adlula amabili.

(4) Umuntu oseke waba yiNduna yengatsha zamabutho omvikela, angaqhatshwa njengeNduna yamabutho omvikela, kodwa umuntu oseke waba yiNduna yamabutho omvikela kalakho ukuqatshwa njengeNduna yengatsha zamabutho omvikela kumbe ukuba yiNduna eyaloba yiluphi ugatsha lokuvikela ilizwe.

(5) Zonke iziNduna zamabutho omvikela lazo zonke iziNduna zengatsha zamabutho avikela ilizwe kumele zilawule njengokulotshwe kuziqondiso zemigomo eziphiwe nguMphathintambo okhangelane lamabutho omvikela esebenza ngaphansi kwamandla kaMongameli. 217 Ukumiswa lokubunjwa kweKomishini yengatsha zamabutho omvikela

(1) Kukhona iKomishini yengatsha zamabutho omvikela elomgcinisihlalo okumele abe ngumgcinisihlalo weKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende labanye abangabangaphezulu kwababili loba phansi kwabayisithupha abaqhatshwe nguMongameli.

(2) Amalunga eKomishini yengatsha zamabutho omvikela kumele akhethwe ngenxa yolwazi lobungcitshi babo ekulawuleni, ekuphatheni lasezindabeni zempi, ulwazi lwabo lomsebenzi loba ukubonakala bengabantu abafaneleyo ukuqhatshwa njalo—

(a)

ingxenye yabo kumele ibe ngabantu abangayisiwo malunga omvikela njalo abangazange babe ngamalunga Amabutho omvikela;

(b) omunye wabo kumele abe ngumuntu owake waba ngumphathi Wamabutho omvikela okwesikhathi esibekiweyo esisodwa loba ukwedlula, esifika iminyaka emihlanu kusiya phezulu. 218 Umsebenzi weKomishini yogatsha lwamabutho omvikela

(1) IKomishini yamabutho omvikela ilemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukuqhatsha abantu abafaneleyo njalo abalolwazi ukuba baphathe izikhundla phakathi kwamabutho omvikela;

(b) ukulungisa lokuhambisa ngomthetho isimo semisebenzi, okuhlanganisela imiholo, imali ephiwayo lezinye inzuzo zamalunga amabutho omvikela;

(c)

ukubona ngezenhlalakahle lokuqhutshwa kuhle komsebenzi wamabutho omvikela lokugcinwa besezingeni elikhulu lokwenza umsebenzi ngokupheleleyo;

(d) ukuqiniseka ukuba amalunga amabutho omvikela alandela isigaba 208;

(e)

(f)

ukugcina ukuthula lokuzwisisana phakathi kwamabutho omvikela lomphakathi;

ukucebisa uMongameli loMphathintambo mayelana yiloba yini okuhlangene laMabutho omvikela; njalo

(g) ukusebenzisa yiloba yiyiphi indlela ecetshiswe loba ebekwe kuKomishini yiSisekamthetho lesi loba nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(3) IKomishini yengatsha zaMabutho omvikela, iphiwe imvumo nguMphathintambo okhangelane lemabutho omvikela ingabeka imithetho engabe ikhangelane laloba yikuphi okulandiswe kusigatshana (1).

(4) Ekulungiseni imiholo, imali ephiwayo lezinye inzuzo ezamalunga Amabutho omvikela, iKomishini yamalunga Amabutho omvikela kumele isebenze ngemvumo kaMongameli exwayiswe nguMphathintambo wezemali emuva kokuba esekhulumisane loMphathintambo waMabutho omvikela. ISAHLUKWANA 3 Ugatsha Lwamapholisa 219 Ugatsha lwamapholisa lomsebenzi walo

(1) Kulogatsha lwamamaPholisa olukhangelane lokhu—

(a)

Hlolisisa, ukuchwayisisa lokuvimbela ubugebengu;

(c)

ukuvikela lokuphephisa izimpilo lempahla zabantu;

(e)

ukusekela umthetho weSisekamthetho lokuqinisa umthetho kungela kwesaba loba ukukhetha.

(b) ukugcina ukuphepha ngaphakathi kwelizwe laseZimbabwe;

(d) ukuqinisa umthetho lokuhleleka; njalo

(2) Ingatsha zamaPholisa kumele ziqhube umsebenzi wazo zincedisana—

(a)

laloba yiluphi ugatsha lwabacuphi olubekwe ngokusemthethweni;

(c)

lezinhlanganiso zamazwe omhlaba lawomkhono ezidalelwe ukuqeda ubugebengu;

(b) yiloba ngubani ongabe ebekwe ngokusemthethweni ukubana ahlole, achwayisise loba enqabele amacala asezingeni elithile; njalo

(3) Ingatsha zamapholisa kumele zingabi lobandlululo, zibe lesimilo selizwe, zithande ilizwe lazo, zibezingcitshi njalo zizehlise emandleni omphakathi njengokumiswe yiSisekamthetho.

(4) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphe inhlanganiso le ngokuhlelwa, isimo, ukuphathwa, umthetho, ukuziphatha okuqinileyo lokukhuphula lokwehliswa esikhundleni kwezinduna lamanye amalunga, kulandelwa isigaba 223, izimiso zokusebenza kwamalunga amaPholisa 220 UMphathintambo wogatsha lwamapholisa

UMongameli kumele aqhatshe uMphathintambo okhangelane lengatsha zamaPholisa. 221 INdunankulu yamaPholisa

(1) Ingatsha zamaPholisa zingaphansi kombuso weNdunankulu yamaPholisa eqhatshwa nguMongameli emuva kokubana exoxisane loMphathintambo okhangelane lamaPholisa.

(2) INduna yamapholisa iqhatshwa ukusebenza okwesibanga seminyaka emihlanu engaphindwa kanye.

(3) Umuntu oseke wasebenza eyiNdunankulu yamapholisa ngeke aqhatshelwe ukukhokhela loba yiluphi ugatsha lwemisebenzi yokuvikela ilizwe.

(4) INdunankulu yamaPholisa ilakho ukulawula ngamandla ayo njengokuvuma kombhalo wesivumelwano esiphiwe nguMphathintambo okhangelane lamaPholisa esebenza ngaphansi kwamandla kaMongameli. 222 Ukumiswa lokubunjwa kweKomishini yemiSebenzi yamaPholisa

(1) Kukhona iKomishini yemiSebenzi yamaPholisa elomgcinisihlalo okumele abe ngumgcinisihlalo woHlangothi lweziSebenzi zikaHulumende labanye abangabangaphezulu kwababili lobaphansi kwabayisithupha abakhethwe nguMongameli.

(2) Amalunga eKomishini yemiSebenzi yamaPholisa kumele akhethwe ngenxa yolwazi lobungcitshi babo ekulawuleni lasekuphatheni, ulwazi lwabo lomsebenzi loba ukubonakala bengabantu abafaneleyo njalo—

(a)

ingxenye yabo kumele ibe ngabantu abangazange babe ngamalunga omsebenzi wamaPholisa;

(b) omunye wabo kumele abe ngumuntu owake waba lesikhundla esiphezulu emisebenzini yamaPholisa okwesikhathi esibekiweyo esisodwa loba ukwedlula, esifika iminyaka emihlanu kusiya phezulu. 223 Umsebenzi weKomishini yemiSebenzi yamaPholisa

(1) IKomishini yemiSebenzi yamaPholisa ilemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukuqhatsha abantu abafaneleyo njalo abalolwazi ukuba baphathe izikhundla phakathi kwengatsha zemisebenzi yamaPholisa;

(b) ukulungisa lokuhambisa ngomthetho isimo semisebenzi, okuhlanganisela imholo, imali ephiwayo lezinye inzuzo zamalunga engatsha zamaPholisa;

(c)

ukubona ngezenhlalakahle lokuqhutshwa kuhle komsebenzi wezingatsha zamaPholisa lokuhlala besezingeni elikhulu elokwenza umsebenzi ngokupheleleyo;

(d) ukuqiniseka ukubana amalunga awengatsha zamaPholisa alandela isigaba 208;

(e)

(f)

ukugcina ukuthula lokuzwisisana phakathi kwamaPholisa lomphakathi;

ukuxwayisa uMongameli loMphathintambo mayelana laloba kuyini okuphathelane lamapholisa; njalo

(g) ukwenza yiloba yiwuphi omunye umsebenzi owethulwe kumbe owethweswe phezu kwaleyo Komishini yiSisekamthetho lesi loba nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) IKomishini yemiSebenzi yamaPholisa, ngemvumo eyiphiwe nguMphathintambo okhangelane lengatsha zamaPholisa ingabeka imithetho engabe ikhangelane laloba yikuphi okulandiswe kusigatshana (1).

(3) Ekulungiseni imiholo, imali ephiwayo lezinye inzuzo ezamalunga engatsha zamaPholisa, iKomishini yemiSebenzi yamaPholisa kumele isebenze ngokuvunyelwa nguMongameli exwayiswe nguMphathintambo wezemali emuva kokuba esekhulumisane loMphathintambo okhangelane lengatsha zamaPholisa. ISAHLUKWANA 4 Imisebenzi Yabacuphi 224 Ukumiswa kwengatsha zemisebenzi yabaCuphi

(1) Yiloba yiluphi ugatsha lwabacuphi olungasilo lwamabutho omvikela loba amaPholisa, kumele lumiswe ngaphansi komthetho loba ngaphansi komyalo loba ukuqondisa kukaMongameli loba ikhabhinethi.

(2) Ingatsha zemisebenzi yabaCuphi kumele zingabi lenkethabantu, zibe lesimilo selizwe, zithande ilizwe lazo, zibezingcitshi njalo zizehlise emphakathini njengokumiswe yiSisekamthetho. 225 UMphathintambo okhangelane lengatsha zemisebenzi yabaCuphi elizweni

UMongameli kumele aqhatshe uMphathintambo okhangelane lengqe yiluphi ugatsha lwemisebenzi yabacuphi elizweni. 226 Ukubuswa lokulawulwa kwezingatsha zemisebenzi yabaCuphi elizweni

(1) Ugatsha lwabaCuphi kufanele luholwe kumbe lulawulwe nguMqondisi Omkhulu wemisebenzi yabaCuphi okumele aqhatshwe nguMongameli ukuze asebenze okwesikhathi esibekiweyo seminyaka emihlanu esingaba lakho ukuphindwa kanye.

(2) UMqondisi Omkhulu wabaCuphi kumele asebenzise amandla akhe njengokubekwe eziqondisweni zemigomo jikelele elotshiweyo ephiwe nguMphathintambo obona ngezogatsha lwemisebenzi yabaCuphi elizweni esebenza ngaphansi kwamandla kaMongameli.

(3) Umuntu oseke wasebenza njengoMqondisi Omkhulu wemisebenzi yabaCuphi kalakho ukuphinde aqhatshwe njengomlawuli waloba yiluphi olunye ugatsha lokuvikela ilizwe. ISAHLUKWANA 5 Abezintolongo Lokuqondiswa Kwezibotshwa 227 Ugatsha lweZintolongo lokuqondiswa kwezibotshwa lomsebenzi walo loku—

(1) Kulogatsha lweZintolongo lokuqondiswa kwezibotshwa olukhangelane

(a)

(c)

vikelwa komphakathi ezigebengweni ngokuvalela lokubisela amalungelweni laba abagwetshiweyo, labavalelweyo ngokusemthethweni, lokuthi bayamukeleke emphakathini; loku phathwa kwezinto zeZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa.

(2) Ugatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondisa iziBotshwa kumele lungabi lenkethabantu, lube lesimilo selizwe, luthande ilizwe lalo, lubeyingcitshi njalo luzehlise emphakathini njengokumiswe yiSisekamthetho.

(3) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphe inhlanganiso le isimo, ukuphathwa, umthetho, ukuziphatha okuqinileyo lokukhuphula lokwehlisa esikhundleni izinduna lamanye amalunga, bazinikele kusahluko 231, izimiselo zokusebenza kwamalunga awezinkantolo lokuqondisa izibotshwa. 228 UMphathintambo okhangelane leZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa

Umongameli kumele akhethe uMphathintambo okhangelane logatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa. 229 UNdunankulu wogatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondisa iziBotshwa

(1) Ugatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa lungaphansi kwenkokheli kaNdunankulu wogatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa okhethwe nguMongameli emuva kokuthi exwayisane loMphathintambo okhangelane lezentolongweni lokuqondiswa kwezibotshwa.

(2) UNdunankulu weZintolongo lokuQondisa iziBotshwa ukhethwa ukusebenza okwesikhathi esibekiweyo seminyaka emihlanu engaphindwa kanye.

(3) Umuntu oseke wasebenza njengoNdunankulu weZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa angeke aqhatshwa ukuthi alawule olunye ugatsha lokuvikela ilizwe.

(4) UNdunankulu weZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa ulakho ukulawula ngamandla akhe njengokubekwe eziqondisweni zemigomo jikelele elotshiweyo ephiwe nguMphathintambo obona ngezeZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa esebenza ngaphansi kwamandla kaMongameli. 230 Ukumiswa lokubunjwa kweKomishini yogatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa

(1) Kule Komishini yogatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa elomgcinisihlalo okumele abe ngumgcinisihlalo weKomishini yezisebenzi zikaHulumende labanye abangabangaphezulu kwababili loba phansi kwabayisithupha abakhethwe nguMongameli.

(2) Amalunga aweKomishini yeZintolongo lokuQondisa iziBotshwa kumele akhethwe ngenxa yolwazi lobungcitshi babo ekulawuleni lasekuphatheni, ezokuvikela ilizwe, ulwazi lwabo lomsebenzi loba ukubonakala bengabantu abafaneleyo njalo—

(a)

ingxenye yabo kumele ibe ngabantu abangazange babe ngamalunga abasebenza egatsheni lweZintolongo lokuQondisa iziBotshwa;

(b) omunye wabo kumele abe waphatha isikhundla esikhulu kugatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa okweminyaka emihlanu kusiya phezulu. 231 Umsebenzi weKomishini yogatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa

(1) IKomishini yogatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa isebenza okulandelayo—

(a)

ukuqhatsha abantu abafaneleyo njalo belolwazi ukuba baphathe izikhundla phakathi kogatsha lweZintolongo lokuQondiswa kweZibotshwa;

(b) ukulungisa lokuhambisa ngomthetho isimo semisebenzi, okuhlanganisela imiholo, imali ephiwayo lezinye inzuzo zamalunga engatsha zeNtolongo lokuQondiswa kweziBotshwa;

(c)

ukubona ngezenhlalakahle lokuqhutshwa kuhle komsebenzi kugatsha lweziNtolongo lokuQondisa iziBotshwa njalo lokuhlala besezingeni elikhulu elokwenza umsebenzi ngokupheleleyo;

(d) ukuqiniseka ukubana amalunga awengatsha zeziNtolongo lokuQondiswa kweziBotshwa alandela isahluko 208;

(e)

(f)

ukugcina ukuthula lokuzwisisana phakathi kwabeziNtolongo lokuQondiswa kweziBotshwa lomphakathi;

ukuxwayisa uMongameli loMphathintambo mayelana yiloba kuyini okuhlangene leziNtolongo lokuQondiswa kweziBotshwa; njalo

(g) ukuqhuba loba yiwuphi omunye umsebenzi owethulwe kumbe owethweswe kuKomishini yilesi Sisekamthetho loba uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) IKomishini yogatsha, lwezeNtolongo lokuQondiswa kweziBotshwa nxa iphiwe imvumo nguMphathintambo okhangelane lengatsha zentolongo lokuqondiswa kwezioitshwa ingabeka imithetho engabe ikhangelane laloba yikuphi okulandiswe kusiqetshana.

(3) Ekulungiseni imiholo, imali yezindleko lezinye inzuzo ezamalunga engatsha zabezintolongo lokuqondiswa kwezibotshwa, iKomishini yengatsha zezintolongo lokuqondiswa kwezibotshwa kumele kisebenze ngokutshelwa nguMongameli exwayiswe nguMphathintambo wezemali emuva kokuba esekhulumisane loMphathintambo okhangelane lengatsha zeziNtolongo lokuQondiswa kweziBotshwa ISAHLUKO 12 Amakomishini Azimele Wodwa Asekela Umbuso Kazulu ISAHLUKWANA 1 Izimiso-jikelele 232 AmaKomishini Azimele Wodwa

AmaKomishini alandelayo yiwo Azimele Wodwa—

(a)

iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe;

(c)

iKomishini ebona ngezoBulili eZimbabwe;

(e)

iKomishini ebona ngezokuThula lokuKhumisana Umlotha eZimbabwe.

(b) iKomishini ebona ngezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe;

(d) iKomishini ebona ngezokuKhutshwa kweMibiko eZimbabwe; 233 Izinhloso zamaKomishini Azimele Wodwa

AmaKomishini Azimele Wodwa alezinhloso-jikelele ezilandelayo ngaphezu kwalezo eziwanikwayo inye ngayinye—

(a)

ukusekela lokugxilisa amalungelo oluntu lombuso oqotho;

(c)

ukukhuthaza ukuqakanyiswa kweSisekamthetho ezintweni zonke;

(e)

ukulondoloza ukunanzwa kwamagugu lezimiso zeSizwe lazonke izinhlanganiso labameli bakaHulumende, kanye lezinhlanganiso eziholwa nguHulumende; njalo

(b) ukuvikela uzibuse lezilangazelelo zikazulu;

(d) ukukhuthaza ukuba mgceke lokunika izimpendulo ezisuthisayo ezinhlanganisweni ezisebenzelana lozulu;

(f)

ukuba lesiqiniselo sokuthi konke okungalunganga emehlweni omthetho kuyalungisiswa.

234 Izisebenzi zamaKomishini Azimele Wodwa

AmaKomishini Azimele Wodwa alamandla okuqhatsha izisebenzi njalo, elandela izimiso zomthetho, okulawula izimo zawo zomsebenzi. 235 Inkululeko yamaKomishini

(1) AmaKomishini Azimele Wodwa—

(a)

akhululekile njalo akalakho ukuqondiswa loba ukulawulwa nguloba ngubani;

(b) kumele alandele izimiso zalesi Sisekamthetho;

(c)

kumele aqhube imisebenzi yawo engela kwesaba, ukusekela uhlangothi kumbe ukubandlulula;

loba nje ebika kuDale lePhalamende.

(2) ISizwe kanye lezinhlanganiso labameli bakaHulumende emabangeni wonke, kusetshenziswa indlela zomthetho lamanye amanyathelo, kumele bancedise amaKomishini azimele wodwa babuye bavikele inkululeko, ukungeyami cele, isithunzi kanye lokuphumelela kwawo.

(3) Akula muntu okumele aphazamise ukusebenza kwamaKomishini Azimele Wodwa. 236 Amalunga amaKomishini Azimele Wodwa kumele angabi lebandla lombusazwe alisekelayo

(1) Amalunga amaKomishini Azimele Wodwa akumelanga, ekuqhubeni kwawo imisebenzi—

(a)

aziphathe ngendlela eyamele ekusekeleni uhlangothi oluthile;

(b) enze izinto ezikhuphula izilangazelelo zebandla loba uhlangothi oluthile;

(c)

abandlulule izifiso ezisemthethweni zebandla kumbe uhlangothi oluthile lwezombusazwe; kumbe

(d) Ephule amalungelo ayinsika kumbe inkululeko kaloba ngubani.

(2) Abantu abangamalunga ebandla kumbe inhlanganiso yezombusazwe ngesikhathi beqhatshwelwa kuKomishini ezimele yodwa kumele badele ukuba lilunga kwabo masinyazanyana nje njalo, loba sekutheni, kusesephakathi kwezinsuku ezingamatshumi amathathu beqhatshiwe.

(3) Nxa ilunga leKomishini ezimele yodwa—

(a) lingaba lilunga lebandla kumbe inhlanganiso yezombusazwe; kumbe

(b) selike laba lilunga lebandla kumbe inhlanganiso yezombusazwe ngesikhathi liqhatshelwa kuKomishini, beselisehluleka ukudela ukuba lilunga kwalo phakathi kwezinsuku ezingamatshumi amathathu liqhatshiwe;

khonapho-khonapho nje lihle lime ukuba lilunga laleyo Komishini.

237 Ukuqhatshwa lokususwa esikhundleni kwamalunga amaKomishini azimele wodwa

(1) Ngenjongo yokukhetha abantu bokuqhatshela kumaKomishini azimele wodwa, iKomithi yeMigomo leZilawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende kumele—

(a)

imemezele isikhundla sakhona;

(c)
