iqhube izingxoxo ezihleliweyo emehlweni kazulu labantu abafisa ukuqhatshwa;

(b) inxuse uzulu ukuba anikeze amabizo angakhethwa;

(d) idwebe uluhlu lwenani elifaneleyo labantu abadinga ukuthi baqhatshwe; njalo

(e)

Inike lolo luhlu kuMongameli.

(2) Ilunga leKomishini ezimele yodwa lingasuswa esikhundleni kuphela nxa ilunga lakhona—

(a) lingenelisi ukuqhuba imisebenzi yalo ngenxa yokufiphala engqondweni kumbe emzimbeni;

(b) litshengise ukwehluleka okwedlulisa amalawulo;

(c)

litholakale lilecala lokungaziphathi ngemfanelo okwedlulisa amalawulo; kumbe

(d) lingasalungelanga ukuqhatshwa kuleyoKomishini.

(3) Indlela elandelwayo nxa kususwa omahluli esikhundleni yiyo elandelwayo ekususeni ilunga esikhundleni seKomishini ezimele yodwa. ISAHLUKWANA 2 Ikomishini Ebona Ngezokhetho eZimbabwe 238 Ukubunjwa lokuqoqwa kweKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe

(1) KuleKomishini ezakwaziwa ngokuthi yiKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe egoqela—

(a) umgcinisihlalo oqhatshwa nguMongameli ngemva kokubonisana leKomishini ebona ngezemiSebenzi yeziLawulamthetho kanye leKomithi yeMigomo leZilawulo ezimiyo zePhalamende; njalo

(b) amanye amalunga ayisitshiyagalombili aqhatshwe nguMongameli eluhlwini lwabantu abangekho phansi kwabalitshumi lambili abaqonywe yiKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende.

(2) Umgcinisihlalo weKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele abe ngumahluli kumbe umuntu osewake waba ngumahluli kumbe umuntu olezimfanelo zokuthi angaqhatshwa njengomahluli.

(3) Nxa ukuqhatshwa kukamgcinisihlalo kuKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kungahambelani lezicebiso zeKomishini ebona ngezeMisebenzi yeZilawulamthetho, uMongameli kumele abangele ukuthi iKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende yaziswe ngokukhulu ukuphangisa.

(4) Amalunga eKhomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele abe yizizalwane zeZimbabwe njalo akhethelwe isithunzi lolwazi lwawo kanye lokwanelisa kwawo ukuqhuba imisebenzi ephathelane lozulu kumbe inhlangothi ezizimele zodwa.

(5) Amalunga eKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe aqhatshelwa ukuthi asebenze okwesikhathi esibekiweyo eseminyaka eyisithupha elandelanayo njalo alakho ukwelulelwa kanye ukuthi asebenze okwesinye isikhathi esifanayo njalo, kodwa akula muntu okumele aqhatshwe kumbe asebenze kule iKomishini ngemva kokuba eke waba lilunga okwesikhathi esibekiweyo esisodwa kusiya phezulu esifika iminyaka elitshumi lambili, kungelani ukuthi ibe ingelandelanayo na leyo minyaka loba hatshi. 239 Imisebenzi yeKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe

IKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe ilemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukulungiselela, ukuqhuba lokuhola—

(i) ukhetho lokudinga uMongameli lolokudinga abaMeli bedale lePhalamende;

(ii) ukhetho lokudinga abameli emakhansilini ezabelo lawemadolobheni amakhulu kanye lenhlangano zombuso kuboHulumende bezabelo;

(iii) ukhetho lokudinga amalunga eKhansili yeziNduna eyelizwe ebunjwe ngaphansi kweSigaba 285; njalo

(iv) ukhetho lokudinga umbono kazulu;

lokuba lesiqiniseko sokuthi ukhetho lunye ngalunye, kugoqela olokudinga umbono kazulu, luqhutshwe kuhle, ngendlela ekhululekileyo, eqotho, ekhanyayo njalo elandela ibala lomthetho;

(b) ukuhola ukhetho lukaMongameli weDale leSenethi kanye loSomlomo, njalo lokuba lesiqiniseko sokuthi ukhetho lunye ngalunye khonapho luqhutshwe kuhle kulandelwa ibala lomthetho;

(c)

ukubhalisa abantu abafisa ukuvota;

(e)

ukuba lesiqiniseko sokuthi uluhlu lwabantu ababhalise ukuvota lerejista yabavoti igcinwe kuhle yanakwa ngendlela;

(d) ukulungisa uluhlu lwabantu ababhalise ukuvota kanye lerejista yabavoti;

(f)

ukuquma izifunda zokuvotela, amawadi leminye imingcele ephathelane lamavoti;

(g) ukulungisa, ukukhipha lokusabalalisa amaphepha okuvotisa, ukuvumela isimo samabhokisi okuvotela lokuwathenga, lokudinga kanye lokusebenzisa izindawo zokuvotela;

(h) ukuqhuba lokuhola imfundiso yabavoti;

(i)

ukubhalisa abahloli bokhetho, kugoqela olokudinga umbono kazulu;

(j)

ukunika iziqondiso ebantwini abaqhatshwe yiSizwe kumbe nguhulumende wesabelo ngenjongo yokuba lesiqiniseko sokuthi ukhetho, kugoqela olokudinga umbono kazulu, luqhutshwe kuhle, ngendlela ekhululekileyo, eqotho, elungileyo njalo ebalulekileyo; njalo

(k) ukwamukela lokuhlaziya izikhalazo ezivela kuzulu lokuthatha amanyathelo ewabona efanele maqondana lezikhalazo ezilethiweyo. 240 Ukungafaneli ukukhethwa kuKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe

Phezu kwabantu abaqanjwe kuSigaba 320(3), abantu abalandelayo kabafanelanga ukuqhatshelwa kuKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe—

(a)

izisebenzi ezisebenzelana lozulu, ngaphandle kwabomahluli;

(c)

Amalunga lezisebenzi zezinhlanganiso ezimiswe ngumthetho kanye leziholwa nguHulumende.

(b) izisebenzi zamakhansili ezabelo lamadolobho amakhulu kanye lezabohulumende bezabelo; njalo

241 IKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele yazise mayelana lokhetho, kugoqela olokudinga umbono kazulu

Phezu kombiko okumele iwukhiphe njengokuchazwe kuSigaba 323, iKomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kumele, masinyazana nje, njalo ngendlela yoMphathintambo ofaneleyo, ikhiphe umbiko eyawunikeza eDale lePhalamende mayelana lokuqhutshwa kokhetho lunye ngalunye, kugoqela olokudinga umbono kazulu. ISAHLUKWANA 3 Ikomishini Ebona Ngezamalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe 242 Ukubunjwa lokuqoqwa kweKomishini ebona ngezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe

(1) Kulekomishini ezakwaziwa ngokuthi yiKomishini ebona ngezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe egoqela—

(a)

umgcinisihlalo oqhatshwa nguMongameli ngemva kokubonisana leKomishini ebona ngezemiSebenzi yeziLawulamthetho kanye leKomithi yeMigomo leZilawulo ezimiyo zePhalamende; njalo

(b) amanye amalunga ayisitshiyagalombili aqhatshwe nguMongameli eluhlwini lwabantu abangekho phansi kwabalitshumi lambili abaqonywe yiKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo zePhalamende.

(2) Umgcinisihlalo weKomishini ebona ngezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe kumele abe ngumuntu oseleminyaka eyisikhombisa ethole izimfanelo zokusebenza njengegqwetha kweleZimbabwe.

(3) Nxa ukuqhatshwa kukamgcinisihlalo kuKomishini ebona ngezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe kungahambelani lokucetshiswe yiKomishini ebona ngezeMisebenzi yeZilawulamthetho, uMongameli kumele abangele ukuthi iKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende yaziswe ngokukhulu ukuphangisa.

(4) Amalunga eKomishini ebona ngezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe kumele akhethelwe isithunzi lolwazi lwawo kanye lokuzwisia langokujwayela kwawo ukukhankasela amalungelo oluntu.

243 Imisebenzi yeKomishini ebona ngezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe

(1) IKomishini ebona ngezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe ilemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukukhuthaza ulwazi lokuhlonitshwa kwamalungelo oluntu kanye lezinkululeko emabangeni wonke omphakathi;

(b) ukukhuthaza ukuvikelwa, ukuthuthukiswa kanye lokutholiswa kwamalungelo oluntu lezinkululeko;

(c)

ukuqapha, ukuhluza kanye lokuba lesiqiniseko sokuthi amalungelo oluntu kanye lezinkululeko kuyananzwa ngempela;

(d) ukwamukela lokuhlaziya izikhalazo ezivela kuzulu lokuthatha amanyathelo ewabona efanele maqondana lezikhalazo ezilethiweyo;

(e) ukuvikela uzulu ekusetshenziselweni amandla ngendlela engesiyo lekungaqhutshelwa kuhle imisebenzi yiSizwe lezinhlanganiso ezisebenzelana lozulu kanye lezisebenzi zalezo nhlanganiso;

(f)

ukuchwayisisa ukuziphatha kwenhlanganiso kumbe umuntu, lapho okwetheswa khona umlandu wokuthi kulamalungelo oluntu lezinkululeko ezibalulwe emqhulwini wamalungelo esezephulwe yileyo nhlanganiso kumbe umuntu;

(g) ukubona ukuthi kube khona ukulungisiswa kodaba lwakhona, kugoqela ukuthonisiswa kwalabo abaphambanisileyo, lapho kube khona ukwephulwa kwamalungelo oluntu kumbe izinkululeko;

(h) ukulawula ukuthi uNdunankulu wamaPholisa achwayisise izindaba lapho okusolelwa khona ukuthi kwephulwe amalungelo oluntu lezinkululeko ngendlela engekho emthethweni, lokuthi azise iKomishini ngemiphumela yokuchwayisisa kwakhona;

(i)

(j)

ukucebisa iDale lePhalamende ngamanyathelo asebenzayo okukhuthaza amalungelo oluntu lezinkululeko;

ukuqhuba izindingisiso ezintweni eziphathelane lamalungelo oluntu lezinkululeko kanye lokulinganiswa kwabantu kwezenhlalo; njalo

(k) ukwethekelela lokuyahlola—

(i) amajele, izintolongo, izinkamba zeziphepheli lezinye izindawo ezifanayo; njalo

(ii) izindawo lapho okuvalelwe khona abantu abalezifo zengqondo kumbe abalenkinga ekucabangeni;

ukuze kutholakale ukuthi izindawo abagcinwa kizo zimi njani, lokucebisa uMphathintambo obona ngezokuphathwa komthetho maqondana lalezondawo ukuthi kuyini okumele kwenziwe mayelana lesimo sazo.

(2) UNdunankulu wamaPholisa kumele alandele isilawulo asiphiwe yiKomishini yezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe ngezimiso zesigatshana (1)(h). 244 Imibiko eya kumbe evela kuKomishini yezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe

(1) IKomishini yezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe inganqolonga ukuthi umuntu, inhlanganiso kumbe ummeli, kungaba ngoweSizwe kumbe oqhatshwe yiSizwe loba abanye—

(a)

azobikela iKomishini ngamanyathelo abawathetheyo ukuze baphumelelise amalungelo oluntu lezinkululeko ezibethwe eMqulwini waMalungelo; njalo

(b) ukunika iKomishini ulwazi eludingayo ukuze ilungise ugwalo lwemibiko okumele iluhambise enhlanganisweni yomkhono kumbe eyomhlaba wonke jikelele ngaphansi kwaloba yiwuphi umthetho womhlaba ohola ezamalungelo oluntu, izibopho lezivumelwano ezangenwa yeZimbabwe.

(2) Phezu kogwalo lwemibiko okumele ilukhiphe ngaphansi kwezimiso zeSigaba 323, iKomishini yezaMalungelo Oluntu eZimbabwe, isebenza loMphathintambo ofaneleyo, kumele ihambise imibiko eDale lePhalamende mayelana lezindaba ezimqoka eziphathelane lamalungelo oluntu lezinkululeko iKomishini ibona angazathi zifanele ukulethwa phambi kwePhalamende. ISAHLUKWANA 4 Ikomishini Ebona Ngezobulili eZimbabwe 245 Ukubunjwa lokuqoqwa kweKomishini ebona ngezoBulili eZimbabwe

(1) KuleKomishini ezakwaziwa ngokuthi yiKomishini ebona ngezoBulili eZimbabwe egoqela—

(a)

umgcinisihlalo oqhatshwa nguMongameli ngemva kokubonisana leKomithi yeMigomo leZilawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende; njalo

(b) amanye amalunga ayisitshiyagalombili, okuthi—

(i) abayisikhombisa bakhona baqhatshwe nguMongameli eluhlwini lwabantu abangekho phansi kwabalitshumi lambili abaqonywe yiKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende, njalo

(ii) oyedwa wakhona ukhethwe phakathi kweKhansili yeziNduna zelizwe, eqhatshwe nguMongameli.

(2) Amalunga eKomishini ebona ngezoBulili eZimbabwe kumele akhethelwe isithunzi lolwazi lwawo kanye lokuzwisia kwawo izindaba eziphathelane lezokuhlalisana, ezemikhuba lamasiko, ezomkhonomi kanye lezombusazwe, njalo ubulili kumele bumelwe ngokulingeneyo kuliKomishini. 246 Imisebenzi yeKomishini ebona ngezoBulili eZimbabwe

IKomishini ebona ngezoBulili eZimbabwe ilemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukuqapha izindaba eziphathelane lokulinganiswa kobulili njengokubekwe kulesi Sisekamthetho;

(b) ukuchwayisisa ngalapho okungabe kube khona ukwephulwa kwamalungelo aphathelane lezobulili;

(c)

ukwamukela lokuhlaziya izikhalazo ezivela kuzulu lokuthatha amanyathelo iKomishini ebona angazani yiwo afaneleyo maqondana lalezo zikhalazo;

(d) ukuqhuba izindingisiso mayelana lezinto eziphathelane lobulili lokulinganiswa kwezokuhlalisa, lokucebisa ngenguquko okumele ibe khona emithethweni lakumikhuba edala ubandlululo oluphathelane lobulili;

(e)

(f)

ukucebisa izinhlanganiso ezisebenzelana lozulu kanye lezizimele zodwa ngamanyathelo okumele athathwe ukuze kube lesiqinisekiso sokulinganiswa kobulili; ukucebisa ngemizamo yokukhuthaza izinhlelo zesenzangakhona eziphumelelisa ukulinganiswa kobulili;

(g) ukucebisa lapho okudinga khona ukuthonisiswa amacala aphathelane lokwephulwa kwamalungelo alokokwenza lobulili;

(h) ukudinga izindlela zokulungisisa lapho okube khona lokwephulwa kwamalungelo aphathelane lezobulili;

(i)

ukwenza konke okudingakalayo ukuze kube lokulinganiswa kobulili.

247 Imibiko ebhalwa yiKomishini ebona ngezoBulili eZimbabwe

Phezu kogwalo lwemibiko okumele ilukhiphe ngaphansi kwezimiso zeSigaba 323, iKomishini ebona ngezoBulili eZimbabwe, isebenzaa uloMphathintambo ofaneleyo, kumele ihambise imibiko eDale lePhalamende mayelana lezindaba ezimqoka eziphathelane loBulili iKomishini ebona angazathi zifanele ukulethwa phambi kweDale lePhalamende. ISAHLUKWANA 5 IKomishini Ebona Ngezokukhitshwa kweMibiko eZimbabwe 248 Ukubunjwa lokuqoqwa kweKomishini ebona ngezokuKhitshwa kwemiBiko eZimbabwe

(1) KuleKomishini ezakwaziwa ngokuthi yiKomishini ebona ngezokuKhitshwa kwemiBiko eZimbabwe egoqela—

(a)

umgcinisihlalo oqhatshwa nguMongameli ngemva kokubonisana leKomishini ebona ngezemiSebenzi yeziLawulamthetho kanye leKomithi yeMigomo leZilawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende; njalo

(b) amanye amalunga ayisitshiyagalombili aqhatshwe nguMongameli eluhlwini lwabantu abangekho phansi kwabalitshumi lambili abaqonywe yiKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende.

(2) Amalunga eKomishini ebona ngezokuKhitshwa kwemiBiko eZimbabwe kumele akhethelwe isithunzi lolwazi lwawo kanye lokuzwisisa kwawo izindaba eziphathelane lamalungelo oluntu kanye lokumele kwenzakale ezindabeni zokukhitshwa kwemibiko. 249 Imisebenzi yeKomishini ebona ngezokukhitshwa kweMibiko eZimbabwe

(1) iKomishini ebona ngezokuKhitshwa kwemiBiko eZimbabwe ilemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukuphakamisa, ukukhuthaza lokuthuthukisa inkululeko yezindlu zemibiko;

(b) ukukhuthaza lokuphoqelela imisebenzi emihle lezimiso zokuhle ezindlini zemibiko;

(c) ukuqapha ukukhitshwa kwemibiko kubhekwe izilangazelelo zikazulu njalo, ikakhulu, ukuba lesiqinisekiso sobuqotho lokwahluka kwemibono emela umphakathi weZimbabwe kabanzi;

(d) ukukhuthaza ukusungulwa kwemigomo yokuziphatha kwabantu abaqhatshwe kwezemibiko njalo, lapho engekhoyo khona, ukuyisungula lokuyiphoqelela;

(e)

ukwemukela lokuhlaziya izikhalazo zikazulu njalo, lapho okufanele khona, ukuthatha amanyathelo okubopha inthathelizindaba labanye abantu abaqhatshwe kwezemibiko kumbe ukusakaza abatholakale belecala lokwephula umthetho kumbe umgomo wokuziphatha oqondane labo;

(f)

ukuba lesiqiniseko sokuthi abantu beZimbabwe bafinyelela imibiko ngendlela eqotho njalo ebanzi;

(g) ukukhuthaza ukusetshenziswa lokuthuthukiswa kwezindimi zonke ezinanzwa okusemthethweni njengendimi zeZimbabwe;

(h) ukukhuthaza ukusetshenziswa kolwazi lwemitshina yesimanjemanje kwezemibiko lasekusabalaliseni ulwazi;

(i)

(j)

ukukhuthaza ukuncintisana okuqotho lokwehlukana kwezemibiko; njalo ukuqhuba izindingisiso ngendaba eziphathelane lenkululeko yamaphephandaba leyokuzitsho, njalo siseselapho, ukukhuthaza inguquko emthethweni.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungethulela amandla phezu kweKomishini ebona ngezemibiko eZimbabwe—

(a)

awokuchwayisisa lokudingisisa—

(i) ngokuziphatha kumbe izehlakalo ezikhanya zifaka inkululeko yezemibiko engozini;

(ii) ukuziphatha kwabonozindaba; njalo

(b) ukuthatha kumbe ukucebisa ukuthi kuthathwe amanyathelo okujezisa onozindaba abatholakala belecala lokwephula umthetho kumbe imigomo yokuziphatha eqondane labo.

(3) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende unganika izimiso ezimayelana lokulawulwa kwabonozindaba. 250 Imibiko ekhitshwa yiKomishini yezokuKhitshwa kwemiBiko eZimbabwe

Phezu kogwalo lwemibiko okumele ilukhiphe ngaphansi kwezimiso zeSigaba 323, iKomishini yezokuKhitshwa kwemiBiko eZimbabwe, isebenza uloMphathintambo ofaneleyo, kumele ihambise imibiko eDale lePhalamende mayelana lezindaba ezimqoka eziphathelane lamalungelo oluntu lezinkululeko iKomishini ebona angazathi zifanele ukulethwa phambi kwePhalamende. ISAHLUKWANA 6 Ikomishini Ebona Ngezokuthula Lokukhumisana Umlotha eZimbabwe 251 Ukubunjwa lokuqoqwa kweKomishini ebona ngezokuThula lokuKhumisana Umlotha eZimbabwe

(1) Okwesikhathi esifika iminyaka elitshimi lesi Sisekamthetho sesiqalise ukusebenza, kuleKomishini ezakwaziwa ngokuthi yiKomishini ebona ngezokuthula lokukhumisana umlotha elizweni egoqela—

(a)

umgcinisihlalo oqhatshwa nguMongameli ngemva kokubonisana leKomishini ebona ngezemiSebenzi yeziLawulamthetho kanye leKomithi yeMigomo leZilawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende; njalo

(b) amanye amalunga ayisitshiyagalombili aqhatshwe nguMongameli eluhlwini lwabantu abangekho phansi kwabalitshumi lambili abaqonywe yiKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo yePhalamende.

(2) Umgcinisihlalo weKomishini ebona ngezokuthula lokukhumisana umlotha elizweni kumele abe ngumuntu oseleminyaka eyisikhombisa ethole izimfanelo zokusebenza njengegqwetha kweleZimbabwe.

(3) Nxa ukuqhatshwa kukamgcinisihlalo kuKomishini ebona ngezokuThula lokuKhumisana Umlotha eZimbabwe kungahambelani lokucetshiswe yiKomishini

ebona ngezeMisebenzi yeZilawulamthetho, uMongameli kumele abangele ukuthi iKomithi yeMigomo leziLawulo ezimiyo zePhalamende yaziswe ngokukhulu ukuphangisa.

(4) Amalunga eKomishini ebona ngezokuthula lokukhumisana umlotha elizweni kumele akhethelwe isithunzi lolwazi lwawo kanye lokuzwisia langokujwayela kwawo ukuxoxisana, ukukhumisana umlotha, ukuvikela lokuhola ukulwisana, ukukhumisana umlotha ngemva kokulwisana kanye lokwakha ukuthula. 252 Imisebenzi yeKomishini ebona ngezokuthula lokukhumisana umlotha elizweni

IKomishini ebona ngezokuthula lokukhumisana umlotha elizweni ilemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukuba lesiqinisekiso sokulinganiswa emehlweni omthetho, ukupholiswa kwamanxeba lokukhumisana umlotha ekudluleni kokulwisana;

(b) ukusungula lokungenisa izinhlelo ezikhuthaza uxolo elizweni, ukubambana lokudlelana kanye lokuqedwa kokudonselana ngendlela ecwebiliyo kweleZimbabwe;

(c)

ukuletha ukukhumisana umlotha elizweni ngokukhuthaza ukuthi abantu bakhulume iqiniso mayelana lokwenzakala emuva njalo lokuncedisa ukuthi kube lokulungisiswa kanye lokutholiswa kokulinganiswa emehlweni omthetho;

(d) ukusungula izindlela lezinhlanganiso elizweni ukuze kube khona ukukhulumisana phakathi kwamabandla ezombusazwe, umphakathi, izinhlanganiso lamanye amaqembu ukuze kwenqatshelwe ukulwisana lokudonselana okungaba khona kusasa;

(e)

ukusungula izinhlelo zokuqinisekisa ukuthi abantu abathwaliswe luklubhu, bakhahlanyezwa njalo batshengiswa eminye imihlobo yodlame bayathola usizo lokubelapha lokubasekela;

(f) ukwemukela lokuhlaziya izikhalazo zikazulu njalo, lapho okufanele khona, ukuthatha amanyathelo maqondana lalezo zikhalazo ebona angazathi yiwo afaneleyo;

(g) ukusungula izindlela zokuphangisa ukubona izindawo ezingaba lakho ukuqhamuka ukulwisana lokudonselana, kanye lokuthatha amanyathelo afaneleyo okuvikela lokho;

(h) ukwenza loba yini edingakalayo ukuze kwenqatshelwe ukulwisana kukhuthazwe ukuthula;

(i)

(j)

ukukhumisa umlotha lokuletha ukuxoxisana mayelana lokudonselana emphakathini, ezinhlanganisweni, emaqenjini lasebantwini; njalo ukucebisa ngemithetho okumele ikhandwe ukuze kube lesiqiniseko sokuthi kuba khona uncedo, kugoqela olwamaphepha, oluphiwa abantu abathintwe yikulwisana, imikhuhlane ebhahileyo kumbe ezinye izehlakalo.

253 Imibiko ekhitshwa yiKomishini ebona ngezokuThula lokuKhumisana Umlotha eZimbabwe

Phezu kogwalo lwemibiko okumele ilukhiphe ngaphansi kwezimiso zeSigaba 323, iKomishini ebona ngezokuThula lokuKhumisana Umlotha eZimbabwe, isebenza uloMphathintambo ofaneleyo, kumele ihambise imibiko eDale lePhalamende mayelana lezindaba ezimqoka eziphathelane lamalungelo oluntu lezinkululeko iKomishini ebona angazathi zifanele ukulethwa phambi kwePhalamende.

ISAHLUKO 13 Izinhlanganiso Zokulwisa Ubugwelegwele Lamacala ISAHLUKWANA 1 IKomishini yeZimbabwe Elwisa Ubugwelegwele 254 Ukuma lokubunjwa kweKomishini yeZimbabwe elwisa ubugwelegwele

(1) KuleKomishini eyazakala ngokuthi yiKomishini eLwisa ubugwelegweyeZimbabwe egoqela—

(a)

umgcinisihlalo okhethwa nguMongameli ngemva kokukhulumisana lekhomithi ebona ngemithetho emisiweyo kanye lezibopho; njalo

(b) lamalunga amanye ayisitshiyagalombili akhethwa nguMongameli eluhlwini lwabantu abangekho ngaphansi kwabalitshumi lambili abakhethwa yiKomithi yeMithetho eMisiweyo kanye leZibopho.

(2) Amalunga eKomishini eLwisana lobuGwelegwele yeZimbabwe kufuze akhethelwe ukwethembeka kwawo kunye lolwazi abalalo oluphathelane lomsebenzi wobuphathi kumbe ukutshutshisa kumbe ukuchwayisisa amacala kumbe ukufanela kwabo ukuqhatshwa, njalo—

(a)

omunye wabo kumele abelamakhono okubaligqwetha eZimbabwe, njalo abe wathola amakhono la eminyakeni eyisikhombisa edlulileyo;

(b) omunye wabo kumele abelamakhono okusebenza njengonozimali emisebenzini kazulu kumbe ukuhlola ngokuqhutshwa kwezimali emisebenzini kaZulu kweleZimbabwe, njalo abe wathola amakhono la eminyakeni eyisikhombisa edlulileyo; njalo

(c)

omunye wabo kumele abe ngumuntu osesebenze ukuchwayisisa amacala okweminyaka elitshumi.

255 Imisebenzi yeKomishini yeZimbabwe eLwisa ubuGwelegwele

(1) IKomishini yeZimbabwe eLwisa ubugwelegwele ilemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukuchwayisisa lokuveza amacala obugwelegwele emawofisini kaHulumende lasenhlanganisweni ezizimele zodwa;

(b) ukulwisa ubugwelegwele, ubusela, ukuphanjulwa kwezimali ngokungekho emthethwe, inkohlakalo lokungaziphathi ngemfanelo emawofisini kaHulumende lawenhlanganiso ezizimele zodwa;

(c)

ukukhuthaza ukwethembeka, ukuziphatha kahle kokwezezimali lobumgceke emawofisini kazulu lawenhlanganiso ezizimeleyo;

(d) ukwamukela lokuhluza izikhalazo zomphakathi lokuthatha amanyathelo afaneleyo kukhangelwe izikhalazo ezethuliweyo;

(e)

(f)

ukulayeza uMtshutshisi-Jikelele wamaPholisa ukuthi achwayisise amacala abantu abacatshangelwa ubugwelegwele lokubikela iKomishini izimpumela zesichwayisiso sakhona; ukudlulisa izindaba kuMtshutshisi weSizwe onguMaqhuzu ukuze zitshutshiswe;

(g) ukudinga usizo emalungeni oGatsha lwamaPholisa kunye lezingatsha zeSizwe ezichwayisisayo; njalo

(h) ukwenza inkuthazo kuHulumende labanye abantu mayelana lamanyathela angathathwa ukuphakamisa isithunzi ukwethembeka njalo kuvikelwe ukuziphatha okungafanelanga kuzingatsha zikazulu lakulezo ezizimele zodwa.

(2) USikomishi omKhulu wamaPholisa kufuze alandele inqe yisiphi isilayezo asiphiwa yiKomishini yeZimbabwe eLwisa ubugwelegwele ngaphansi kwesigatshana

(1)(e).

(3) UHulumende kufuze aqinise, esebenzisa umthetho lezinye izindlela, ukuthi iKomishini yeZimbabwe eLwisa ubugwelegwelilamandla okuqondisa ukubotshwa lokutshutshiswa kwabantu abacatshangelwa ngokulengqondo ubugwelegwele, inkohlakalo kanye lokunye ukungaziphathi kahle okusemandleni omkhawulo weKomishini. 256 Ukusetshenziswa kwezinye izimiso zikaSahluko 12 kuKomishini yeZimbabwe eLwisa ubugwelegwele

Izigaba 234, 235, 236 lo237 ziyasebenza kuKomishini yeZimbabwe eLwisa ubugwelegwele kunye lakumalunga ayo sengathi iyiKomishini ezimeleyo emiswe nguSahluko 12. 257 Imibiko yeKomishini yeZimbabwe eLwisa Ubugwelegwele

Phezu kombiko okufuze iwethule kulandelwa isigcawu 323, iKomishini yeZimbabwe eLwisa ubugwelegwele ilakho, isebenza loMphathintambo kaHulumende, ukwethula umbiko ePhalamende ophathelane lezindaba ezithile ezokutshaphazwa kwewofisi kaHulumende kunye leyezinhlanganiso ezizimeleyo, embonweni weKomishini, okufuze yaziswe inkundla yePhalamende. ISAHLUKWANA 2 Ithimba Labalawuli Besizwe Bokutshutshisa 258 Ukumiswa lemisebenzi yeQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa

Kukhona iQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa elilomlandu wokusungula lokuqhuba umsebenzi wokutshutshisa amacala limele iSizwe lokufeza eminye imisebenzi edingakalayo kumbe ephathelane lokuthonisiswa kwaloluhlobo. 259 UMtshutshisi-Jikelele lezinye iziphathamandla

(1) Kukhona uMtshutshisi-Jikelele oyinhloko yeQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa.

(2) Iwofisi kaMtshutshisi omKhulu yiwofisi kazulu loba ingasi ngxenye yeziSebenzi zikaHulumende.

(3) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele ukhethwa nguMongameli ngokuxwayiswa yiKomishini ebona ngokusebenza kwaBahluleli kulandelwa indlela yokukhethwa kwabahluleli.

(4) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele kumele kube ngumuntu olobungcitshi lamakhono okuthi akhethelwe umsebenzi wokuba ngumahluleli eMthethwandaba omKhulu.

(5) Isibanga sokuqhuba umsebenzi kaMtshutshisi omkhulu ngeseminyaka eyisithupha njalo ulakho ukuqhubeka okweminye iminyaka eyisithupha njalo.

(6) Engakaqali lumsebenzi, uMtshutshisi-Jikelele kufanele enze, phambi kukaMongameli kumbe phambi komuntu ophiwe amandla nguMongameli, isifungo salo umsebenzi elandela ukumiswa kwendlela yokufunga ephawulwe kuhlelo lwesithathu.

(7) Izimiso zomthetho eziphathelane lokususwa kukamahluleli esikhundleni ziyasebenza njalo ekususweni kukaMtshutshisi omKhulu esikhundleni.

(8) Okuphathelane lokuqhutshwa komsebenzi kaMtshutshisi omKhulu, okugoqela iholo lakhe, kufuze kubalulwe nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, kodwa iholo lakhe akumelanga lehliswe nxa uMtshutshisi-Jikelele esasesikhundleni.

(9) Iholo likaMshutshisi omKhulu livela esikhwameni sikaHulumende okungena khona imithelo.

(10) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uvumele ukukhethwa kwenkundla ezaqhatsha abantu bokusiza uMtshutshisi-Jikelele ekufezeni imisebenzi, njalo kumele iphawule—

(a)

ubungcitshi lamakhono alaba bantu;

(c)

lokuthi ekufezeni imisebenzi yabo laba bantu kumele bazimele bengathathi inhlangothi njalo kumele baholwe ngumthetho baqondiswe njalo balawulwe nguMtshutshisi-omKhulu;

(b) mayelana ngokusebenza, ukuziphatha kwalaba bantu;

(d) ngesimo lokuhlelwa kweQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa; njalo

(e)

ukusebenza kahle lokuhlaliseka kweQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa.

(11) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele angaphoqa uSikomitshi omKhulu wamaPholisa ukuthi achwayisise lokuthi ambikele ingqe yini, embonweni kaMtshutshisi omKhulu, ephathelane lecala kumbe icala elicatshangelwayo, njalo uSikomitshi-omKhulu wamaPholisa kumele alandele indlela leyo. 260 Ukuzimela kukaMtshutshisi-Jikelele

(1) Kulandelwa iSisekamthetho lesi, uMtshutshisi-Jikelele—

(a)

uzimele yedwa futhi kaqondiswa njalo kalawulwa muntu; futhi

(b) kufuze afeze umsebenzi wakhe engela valo, nkethabetshani, buqili lakuthanda muntu.

(2) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele kufuze abumbe njalo azise uzulu indlela azanquma ngayo ukusungula lokuqhuba umsebenzi wokucwayisisa amacala. 261 Ukuziphatha kweziphathamandla zeQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa

(1) UMtshutshisi-Jikelele leziphathamandla zeQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa kufuze baziphathe ngendlela ephawulwa yiSisekamthetho.

(2) Akula siphathamandla seQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa, ekufezeni umsebenzi waso—

(a)

esingaziphatha ngendlela eveza ukuba singesebandla lezombusazwe liphi;

(c)

onganyathezela izifiso zebandla thile ngokuphambene lomthetho;

(b) esingaqhuba izifiso lezizatho zebandla thile lezombusazwe;

(d) ongephula izinkululeko eziyinsika lamalungelo ayinsika aloba nguwuphi umuntu.

(3) Iziphathamandla zeQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa akumelanga zibengamalunga aphatheka ezikhundleni zezinhlanganiso kumbe ezamabandla ezombusazwe.

(4) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ulakho njalo ukuphawula ukungabilenkethabatshani kweziphathamandla zeQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa.

262 UMtshutshisi-Jikelele uzakwethula kanye ngomnyaka umbiko edale lePhalamende

UMtshutshisi-Jikelele kumele ethule umbiko ePhalamende, ngoMphathintambo kaHulumende, Kanye ngomnyaka mayelana ngengqubo langemisebenzi yeQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa, umbiko lo owomnyaka odlulileyo kufanele wethulwe kungakadluli inyanga eziyisithupha umnyaka olandelayo uqalisile. 263 Amanye amandla okutshutshisa

Umthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule amandla okutshutshisa ebantwini abanye abangasiQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa, kodwa amandla la abengumgoqo kumbe aphikisana lamandla eQula labaLawuli bokuTshutshisa. ISAHLUKO 14 Uhulumende Wezabelo Lezifunda ISENDLALELO Kukanti kuyalangazeleleka ukuthi kube khona isiqiniseko:

(a) sokulondolozwa komanyano elizweni leZimbabwe lokwenqatshelwa kwemihlobo yonke yokungabambani lokuqhekezeka;

(b) sokuphatheka ngendlela eqotho kwezizalwane zonke lomphakathi wonke weZimbabwe kwezombuso; njalo

(c)

sokwabiwa ngendlela elinganisela umuntu wonke kwezingcebo zelizwe kanye lokuphatheka kwezigaba zonke ekuhleleni izinhloso zentuthuko ezindaweni zabo;

kumele kube khona ukudluliselwa kwamandla lemilandu emabangeni aphansi ombuso kweleZimbabwe. ISAHLUKWANA 1 Isiqalo 264 Ukwabiwa kwamandla kaHulumende kanye lemilandu yombuso

(1) Ngaso sonke isikhathi nxa kusaneliseka, amandla lemilandu yombuso kumele idluliselwe phansi emakhansilini ezabelo lawamadolobho kanye lawezifunda anelisayo ukufeza leyomilandu kuhle kuhle langendlela esebenzayo.

(2) Izinhloso zokudlulisela phansi amandla lemilandu yombuso usiya emakhansilini ezabelo lawamadolobho kanye lawezifunda yikuze—

(a)

kunikwe amandla okubuswa kwezabelo ebantwini njalo kwengezwe ukuphatheka kwabo ekusetshenzisweni kwamandla eSizwe ekubetheni izinqumo eziphathelane labo;

(b) kukhuthazwe umbuso oqotho, osebenzayo, okhanyayo, onika izimpendulo ezisuthisayo njalo oqondakalayo eZimbabwe yonke jikelele;

(c)

kulondolozwe njalo kondliwe ukuthula, umanyano elizweni lokungehlukaniseki kwesizwe seZimbabwe;

(d) kunanzwe ilungelo lomphakathi elokuziholela izinto zawo lokuqhelisa intuthuko yawo;

(e)

kuqinisekaliswe ukwabiwa okulinganiselayo kwezingcebo zezifunda lezelizwe; njalo

(f)

kudluliswe imilandu lezingcebo ezivela kuHulumende welizwe kusenzelwa ukuthi kusungulwe isikhwama esiqinileyo semali yekhansili yesabelo leyedolobho kanye leyesifunda sinye ngasinye.

265 Imigomo jikelele yokubuswa kwezabelo lezifunda

(1) Amakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho kanye lamakhansili ezifunda kumele, ezindaweni azibusayo—

(a)

abe lesiqiniseko sokuthi kulombuso omuhle ngokukusebenza oqotho, ngendlela ekhanyayo, enika impendulo ezisuthisayo njalo ehambelana lezimiso zenhlanganiso zalolohlobo;

(b) enze kuphela imisebenzi ayethulelwa yilesi Sisekamthetho kumbe ngoMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende;

(c)

aqhube imisebenzi yawo ngendlela engafinyeleli endaweni, emsebenzini kumbe esizotheni selinye ibanga lombuso;

(d) asebenzelane lamanye, ikakhulu ngoku—

(i) bikelana mayelana, kanye lokubonisana, ngezindaba ababhekane lazo ezohlobo lunye;

(ii) abambanise lokuhlela kuhle ukuqhutshwa kwemisebenzi yawo;

(e)

(f)

alondoloze ukuthula, ukumanyana kwelizwe lokungaxhezuleki kwesizwe seZimbabwe; avikele inhlalakahle kazulu; njalo

(g) abe lesiqiniseko sokuthi kuba lokumelwa okuqotho njalo okulinganiselayo kwabantu abasezindaweni awazibusayo.

(2) Wonke amalunga amakhansili ezifunda kumele akhethwe ngabantu ababhalise ukuvota ezindaweni lezo ezimele zibuswe ngalawo makhansili.

(3) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamande kumele unike amanyathelo lezindlela ezifaneleyo ezivumela ukuhleleka kokusebenzelena phakathi kukaHulumende, amakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho kanye lamakhansili ezifunda. 266 Ukuziphatha kwezisebenzi zikaHulumende wezabelo lezifunda

(1) Izisebenzi zamakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho kanye lawezifunda kumele ziziphathe ngendlela ephawulwa yilesi Sisekamthetho ndawonye lomthetho.

(2) Akula sisebenzi sekhansili yesabelo leyedolobho kumbe eyesifunda esingaba lakho ukuthi, ekuqhubeni umsebenzi waso—

(a)

senze izinto ezikhomba icele esilisekelayo;

(c)

sibandlulule izilangazelelo zamanye amabandla kumbe imibono yezombusazwe; kumbe

(b) siqakamise izifiso zebandla kumbe umbono othile wezombusazwe;

(d) sephule amalungelo amqoka kumbe izinkulukeko zomunye umuntu.

(3) Izisebenzi zamakhansili ezabelo lamadolobho kanye lamakhansili ezifunda akumelanga ziphathe izikhundla emabandleni ezombusazwe.

(4) Umthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele wanelise isiqiniseko sokuthi izisebenzi zamakhansili ezabelo lamadolobho kanye lamakhansili ezifunda kazeyameli eceleni elithile kwezombusazwe.

ISAHLUKWANA 2 Izabelo Lamakhansili Ezabelo Lawamadolobho Amakhulu 267 Izabelo lezifunda zeZimbabwe

(1) IZimbabwe iqunyelwe ezabelweni ezithi—

(a)

Isabelo sedolobho lakoBulawayo;

(c)

Isabelo seManicaland;

(e)

Isabelo seMashonaland East;

(b) Isabelo sedolobho laseHarare;

(d) Isabelo seMashonaland Central;

(f)

Isabelo seMashonaland West;

(g) Isabelo seMasvingo;

(h) Isabelo seMatabeleland North;

(i)

(j)

Isabelo seMatabeleland South; Isabelo seMidlands;

ezimingcelo yazo idwetshwa nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) Umthetho omiswe yiPhalamende—

(a)

kumele wenze kwaneliseke ukuqamula izabelo zisiba yizifunda; njalo

(b) ungavumela ukuguqulwa kwemingcele yezabelo lezifunda; ngemva kokubonisana leKhomishini ebona ngezoKhetho eZimbabwe kanye labantu abasezabelweni lezifundeni ezitshiwoyo. 268 Amakhansili ezabelo

(1) kulekhansili yesabelo esabelweni sinye ngasinye, ngaphandle kwezabelo ezingamadolobho amakhulu, egoqela—

(a)

umgcinisihlalo wekhansili, okhethwe ngezimiso ezichazwe kuSigaba 272;

(b) amaSenetha akhethwe esabelweni sonaleso;

(c)

izinduna ezimbili ezingamaSenetha akhethwe esabelweni soneso ngezimiso ezichazwe kuSigaba 120(1)(b);

(d) umongameli loMsekeli kamongameli weKhansili yeziNduna eLizweni, lapho indawo zabo zitholakala esabelweni soneso;

(e)

(f)

wonke amalunga eNdlu yabaMeli okuzifunda zabo zokhetho zitholakala esabelweni soneso; amalunga esifazane aweNdlu yabaMeli abakhethwe ngezimiso ezichazwe kuSigaba 124(1)(b) esabelweni soneso;

(g) oMeya laboMgcinisihlalo, kungakhathalekile ukuthi babizwa bethiweni, bamakhansili wonke awemadolobheni lawezifunda ezisemaphandleni esabelweni soneso; njalo

(h) abantu abalitshumi abakhethwe ngohlelo lokumela olulinganiselayo oluchazwe kusigatshana (3).

(2) Umuntu ulungele ukukhethelwa ekhansilini yesabelo ngezimiso zesigatshana

(1)(h) nxa elungele ukukhethwa njengeLunga leNdlu yabaMeli.

(3) Ukhetho lwamakhansili ezabelo kumele luqhutshwe maqondana loMthetho wezoKhetho, wona okumele ube lesiqiniseko sokuthi abantu abaqanjwe kusigatshana

(1)(h) bakhethwe ngaphansi koluhlu lwamabandla olulinganiselayo—

(a)

lusiya ngokuthi amavoti atholwe lilunga lebandla linye ngalinye ebelincintisa ekhethweni lwamaLunga eNdlu yabaMeli ami njani;

(b) okuthi khona abancintisi besilisa labesifazane babhalwe amabizo abo elandelana njalonjalo, uluhlu lunye ngalunye luqalisa ngebizo lowesifazane.

(4) Isihlalo selunga lekhansili yesabelo eliqanjwe—

(a)

kusiqendu (b), (c), (e) kumbe (f) wesigatshana (1) sisala singaselamuntu nxa ilunga lakhona lingatshiya isihlalo salo eDale lePhalamende;

(b) kusiqendu (g) wesigatshana (1) sisala singasela muntu nxa ilunga lakhona lingasengumeya kumbe umgcinisihlalo wekhansili esabelweni sakhona;

(c)

kusiqendu (h) wesigatshana (1) sisala singasela muntu ezehlakalweni ezichazwe kuSigaba 129, angani ilunga lakhona belililunga leNdlu yabaMeli.

269 Amakhansili amadolobho amakhulu

(1) Kusabelo sedolobho elikhulu linye ngalinye kulekhansili yedolobho egoqela—

(a)

koBulawayo, umeya weDolobho lakoBulawayo, ongumgcinisihlalo weKhansili yeDolobho lakoBulawayo;

(b) eHarare:

(i) umeya weDolobho laseHarare, ongumgcinishlalo weKhansili yeDolobho laseHarare; njalo

(ii) umeya kumbe umgcinisihlalo wekhansili yedolobho elilandelayo ngobukhulu kulesosabelo, yena ongumlandeli kamgcinisihlalo weKhansili yeDolobho laseHarare;

(c)

wonke amalunga eNdlu yabaMeli abazifunda zabo zokhetho zitholakala esabelweni sedolobho lonalelo;

(d) abaMeli besifazane eNdlini yabaMeli abakhethwe maqondana loSigaba 124 (1)(b) esabelweni sedolobho soneso;

(e)

(f)

amaSenetha akhethwe esabelweni sedolobho soneso; njalo

omeya labalandeli babomeya kanye labomgcinisihlalo labalandeli babomgcinisihlalo, kungakhathalekile ukuthi babizwa bathiweni, abamakhansili wonke angaphansi kwesabelo soneso.

(2) Isihlalo selunga lekhansili eliqanjwe—

(a)

kusiqendu (a) kumbe (b) wesigatshana (1) sisala singasela muntu nxa ilunga lakhona lingasengumeya, umlandeli kameya kumbe umgcinisihlalo, loba yikuphi kwakhona;

(b) kusiqendu (c), (d) kumbe (e) wesigatshana (1) sisala singasela muntu nxa ilunga lakhona lingatshiya isihlalo salo seDale lePhalamende;

(c)

kusiqendu (f) wesigatshana (1) sisala singasela muntu nxa ilunga lakhona lingasengumeya, umlandeli kameya, umgcinisihlalo kumbe umlandeli kamgcinisihlalo, loba yikuphi kwakhona, owekhansili yesifunda esabelweni soneso.

270 Imisebenzi yamakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho amakhulu

(1) Ikhansili yesabelo kumbe eyedolobho elikhulu ilomlandu wokuthuthukisa impilo lomkhonomi wesabelo sayo, kugoqela—

(a)

ukuhlela lokuqalisa imisebenzi yokuthuthukisa impilo lomkhonomi wesabelo sayo;

(b) ukuqoqa lokuqalisa izinhlelo zikaHulumende esabelweni sayo;

(c)

ukuhlela lokuqalisa amanyathelo okulondoloza, ukuthuthukisa lokulawula ingcebo zemvelo esabelweni sayo;

(d) ukugqugquzelela ukwethekelelwa kwezindawo ezisesabelweni sayo, lokuthuthukisa izikhungo eziphathelane lalokho;

(e)

(f)

ukuqapha lokulinganisa ukusetshenziswa esabelweni sayo; njalo

ukuqhuba loba yiphi eminye imisebenzi, kugoqela imisebenzi ephathelane lomthetho, engayethulelwa kumbe ukuyethweswa nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) Umthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele ubalule ukubunjwa, isimo kanye lezisebenzi zamakhansili amadolobho amakhulu, langendlela okumele aqhube ngayo imisebenzi yawo.

(3) Amalunga amakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho amakhulu kumele anike izimpendulo ezisuthisayo, ndawonye kumbe ezimele wodwa, kubahlali bezabelo lakuHulumende mayelana lokuphatha kwawo imisebenzi. 271 Amakhomithi amakhansili ezabelo

Ukuze aqhube kuhle imisebenzi yawo, amakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho amakhulu angabumba amakhomithi kodwa iKomithi inye ngayinye ebunjiweyo kumele ikhokhelwe lilunga eliqanjwe kuSigaba 268(1)(h) kumbe 269(1)(h), kusiya ngokuthi yikuphi. 272 Omgcinisihlalo bamakhansili ezabelo

(1) Ekuhlaleni kwayo okwakuqala kulandele ukhetho-jikelele lunye ngalunye, ikhansili yesabelo kumele ikhethe umgcinisihlalo eluhlwini lwabantu ababili kusiya phezulu lwabantu abalungele lowomsebenzi abaqonywe—

(a)

libandla lombusazwe eliyilo elithole inani elikhulu ukwedlula amanye wonke elezihlalo zeNdlini yabaMeli kulesosabelo; kumbe

(b) nxa kungela bandla elifana lalelo eliqanjwe kusiqendu (a) ibandla eliyilo elithole inani elikhulu ukwedlula amanye wonke kumavoti aphume kulolo khetho-jikelele lwamaLunga endlu yabaMeli.

(2) Umuntu ulungele ukukhethwa njengomgcinisihlalo wekhansili yesabelo nxa elungele ukuvotelwa njengeLunga leSenethi.

(3) Isikhundla sikamgcinisihlalo wekhansili yesabelo yisikhundla sokusebenza lomphakathi kodwa kasisingxenye yezisebenzi zikazulu.

(4) Engakaqalisi umsebenzi wakhe, umgcinisihlalo wekhansili yesabelo kumele athathe, phambi kukanobhala wekhansili yesabelo, isifungo sokwethembeka lesokuqalisa umsebenzi njengokuchazwe kuHlelo lwesithathu.

(5) Umgcinisihlalo wekhansili yesabelo angatshiya umsebenzi ngokumemezela siqu sakhe injongo yakhe yokutshiya umsebenzi phambi kwekhansili yesabelo.

(6) Umgcinisihlalo wekhansili yesabelo kumele atshiye isikhundla—

(a)

mhla ikhansili yesabelo iqalayo ukuhlangana kulandela ukhetho-jikelele.

(c)

nxa isinqumo sokumsusa esikhundleni singapasiswa ngesilinganiso sengxenye ezimbili kusiya phezulu kwezintathu samalunga wonke ekhansili yesabelo; kumbe

(b) nxa engasalungelanga ukuba lilunga lekhansili yesabelo;

(d) nxa engakhitshwa esikhundleni yinkundla eqanjwe kusigatshana (7).

(7) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uvumele ukubunjwa kwenkundla ezaqhuba umsebenzi wokususa omgcinisihlalo bamakhansili ezabelo ezikhundleni, kodwa ukususwa lokho kumele kwenziwe kuphela ngenxa—

(a)

yokwehluleka ukuqhuba imisebenzi ephathelane lesikhundla ngesizatho sokufiphala engqondweni kumbe emzimbeni;

(b) yokwehluleka umsebenzi okwedlulisa amalawulo;

(c)

yokungaziphathi ngemfanelo okwedlulisa amalawulo;

(e)

Yokwephula umthetho ngamabomo, kugoqela imithetho yekhansili.

(d) yokutholakala elecala eliphathelane lokungathembeki, ubugwelegwele kumbe ukusebenzisa kubi amandla esikhundla; kumbe

(8) Umgcinisihlalo wekhansili yesabelo katshiyi isikhundla ngaphandle kwezimiso zalesi Sigaba. 273 Izimiso-jikelele eziphathelane lamakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho amakhulu

(1) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele unike izimiso, ezihambelana lalesi Sahluko, ezokubunjwa kanye lemisebenzi yamakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho amakhulu njalo, ikakhulu, zibalule—

(a)

okumele kulandelwe ngamakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho amakhulu;

(c)

izimo zomsebenzi wamalunga amakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho amakhulu; njalo

(b) imisebenzi yabomgcinisihlalo bamakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho amakhulu;

(d) izimo zomsebenzi lokuxotshwa umsebenzi kwezisebenzi zamakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho amakhulu.

(2) Umthetho wezoKhetho kumele unike izimiso, ezilandela lesi Sahluko, ezibetha ukugcwaliswa kwezikhundla ezihlalweni esezisele zingela bantu kumalunga amakhansili ezabelo aqanjwe kuSigaba 268(1)(f) lasezikhundleni zabomgcinisihlalo bamakhansili ezabelo, okuzikhundla kumele zigcwaliswe—

(a)

ngabantu abavela kubandla lombusazwe linye lalelo ebelimelwe yilabo akade bephethe lezozikhundla kumbe izihlalo; njalo

(b) singasabali izikhundla zabomgcinisihlalo, ngumuntu obulili bunye lalowo okade ephethe leso sikhundla ekuqaleni. ISAHLUKWANA 3 UHulumende Wezabelo 274 Uhulumende wezabelo ezisemadolobheni

(1) KulaboHulumende bezabelo abamela njalo abahola izinqineko zabantu bemadolobheni iZimbabwe yonke jikelele.

(2) OHulumende bezabelo zemadolobheni baholwa ngamakhansili abunjwe ngamakhansila akhethwe ngabantu ababhalise ukuvota ezindaweni zonezo ezemadolobheni njalo aholwa ngomeya kanye labomgcinisihlalo abakhethiweyo, kungakhathalekile ukuthi babizwa bathiweni.

(3) Imihlobo yaboHulumende bezabelo ehlukeneyo ingabunjwa emadolobheni atshiyeneyo, njalo amadolobho amabili kusiya phezulu atshiyeneyo angaba lakho ukulumbaniswa afakwe ngaphansi kombuso kaHulumende oyedwa wezabelo.

(4) Ukufanela ukukhethwa lendlela zokuqhutshwa kokhetho lwabantu abaqanjwe kusigatshana (2) kumele kubalulwe eMthethweni wezoKhetho.

(5) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungethulela amandla apheleleyo emahlombe kameya kumbe umgcinisihlalo wekhansili yedolobho. 275 UHulumende wezabelo emaphandleni

(1) KulaboHulumende bezabelo abamela njalo abahola izinqineko zabantu bemaphandleni iZimbabwe yonke jikelele.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele unike izimiso ezivumela—

(a) ukubunjwa kwaboHulumende bezabelo emaphandleni;

(b) ukukhethwa, ngabantu ababhalise ukuvota emaphandleni akhona, kwamakhansili okuhola izinqineko zaboHulumende bezabelo abaqanjwe kusiqendu (a);

(c) ukukhethwa kwabomgcinisihlalo, kungakhathalekile ukuthi babizwa bathiweni, ukuthi bakhokhele lawo makhansili aqanjwe kusiqendu (b); njalo

(d) ukubalulwa kwezimfanelo okumele amalunga amakhansili aqanjwe kusiqendu (b) abe lazo.

(3) imihlobo ehlukeneyo yaboHulumende bezabelo emaphandleni ingabunjwa emaphandleni atshiyeneyo, njalo izindawo ezimbili kusiya phezulu zingaba lakho ukulumbaniswa besezifakwa ngaphansi kombuso kaHulumende oyedwa wezabelo. 276 Imisebenzi yaboHulumende bezabelo

(1) Ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho laloba yiwuphi omunye uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, uHulumende wesabelo ulelungelo lokubusa, ngokuthanda kwakhe, izinqineko zabantu abasendaweni ayibunjelweyo, njalo ulamandla wonke awadingayo ukuze enze lokho.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ungethulela imisebenzi phezu kwaboHulumende bezabelo, egoqela—

(a)

amandla okukhanda imithetho yekhansili, iziqondiso kanye lemithetho yokuholwa kuhle kwezindawo abazibunjelweyo;

(b) amandla okuthelisa izindleko lemithelo kanye lokuqogelela izimali ezanela ukuthi bafeze izinhloso lemilandu yabo. 277 Ukuvotelwa kwaboHulumende bezabelo

(1) Amavoti amakhansila aboHulumende bezabelo kumele aqhutshwe—

(a)

nxa kulukhetho-jikelele lokudinga omeya lamakhansila, sikhathi sinye lokhetho-jikelele lokudinga amaLunga eNdlu yabaMeli loMongameli;

(b) nxa kulukhetho, olungesikhetho-jikelele, ukuze kugcwaliswe isikhundla kumbe izikhundla esezisele zingelamuntu ngokuphangisa okwanelisekayo kulandela ukuphuma kwabantu ezikhundleni.

(2) Ukhetho lokudinga omeya labomgcinisihlalo baboHulumende bezabelo, ngaphandle kwabomeya labomgcinisihlalo abethulelwe amandla apheleleyo ngaphansi kweSigaba 274(5), kumele luqhutshwe ekuhlaleni kwakuqala kwamakhansili akhona kulandela ukhetho-jikelele.

(3) Ngaphandle kulandelwa izimiso ezichazwe kusigatshana (2) kumbe uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, omeya, abagcinisihlalo lamakhansila aboHulumende bezabelo baqalisa umsebenzi esukwini lwesitshiyagalolunye kulandela ukumenyezelwa kwemiphumela yokhetho-jikelele lapho okwakhethwa khona lawo makhansila. 278 Isikhathi esibekiweyo sokuphatha isikhundla kwamalunga aboHulumende bezabelo

(1) Isihlalo sikameya, umgcinisihlalo kumbe ukhansila kaHulumende wesabelo sisala singasela muntu nxa kungenzakala isehlakalo esichazwe kuSigaba 129, angazathi ube eliLunga leDale lePhalamende, besekusithi lapho kukhulunywa ngoSomlomo kumbe uMongameli weDale leSenethi kuSigaba 129(1)(k) kuthathwe njengokuthi kukhulunywa ngoMphathintambo obona ngezikaHulumende wezabelo.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele unike izimiso ezivumela ukubunjwa kwenkundla ezimele yodwa ukuthi yetshathe umsebenzi wokususa omeya, omgcinisihlalo lamakhansila esikhundleni, kodwa lokho kususwa kumele kwenzakale kuphela ngenxa—

(a)

yokwehluleka ukuqhuba imisebenzi ephathelane lesikhundla ngesizatho sokufiphala engqondweni kumbe emzimbeni;

(b) yokwehluleka umsebenzi okwedlulisa amalawulo;

(c)

yokungaziphathi ngemfanelo okwedlulisa amalawulo;

(e)

yokwephula umthetho ngamabomo, kugoqela imithetho yekhansili.

(d) yokutholakala elecala eliphathelane lokungathembeki, ubugwelegwele kumbe ukusebenzisa kubi amandla esikhundla; kumbe

(3) Umeya, umgcinisihlalo kumbe ukhansila kaHulumende wesabelo akatshiyi isikhundla sakhe ngaphandle kulandelwa izimiso zalesi Sigaba. 279 Okumele kulandelwe ngoHulumende bezabelo

UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele unike izimiso ezibalula okumele kulandelwe ngamakhansili aboHulumende bezabelo. ISAHLUKO 15 Inkokheli Zomdabu

280 Ubukhokheli bomdabu

(1) Ugatsha, isikhundla kanye lomsebenzi wenkokheli zomdabu ezingaphansi komthetho wesintu kuyananzwa.

(2) Inkokheli yomdabu ilomlandu wokwenza imisebenzi yamasiko, yesintu eyeNduna, umphathi loba umphathi wesigaba, sokukusiya ngalokho okuyabe kukhona, emphakathini wakhe. 281 Izimiso okumele zilandelwe zinkokheli zomdabu

(1) Izinkokheli zomdabu kumele—

(a)

zisebenze ngokuvumelana lalesi Sisekamthetho kanye lemithetho yeZimbabwe;

(b) zilandele amasiko aphathelane lobukhokheli bomdabu njalo zenze imisebenzi yazo ngokwemisebenzi eyiyona ugatsha lobukhokheli bomdabu onanzwa ngayo yilesi Sisekamthetho;

(c)

ziphathe abantu bonke abasezigabeni zabo ngokulingeneyo okufaneleyo.

(a)

zibe ngamalunga aloba yiliphi ibandla lombusazwe loba zihlanganyele loba ngayiphi indlela kwezombusazwe ezithatha uhlangothi oluthize;

(2) Inkokheli zomdabu akumelanga—

(b) zenze ngendlela engathathi hlangothi;

(c)

kumbeziqhubekise intando yaloba yiliphi ibandla lombusazwe;

(d) zephule amalungelo ayinsika kanye lezinkululeko eziyinsika zaloba nguwuphi umuntu. 282 Imisebenzi yenkokheli zomdabu

(1) Inkokheli zomdabu zilemisebenzi elandelayo ezindaweni ezisemikhawulweni yamandla azo—

(a)

ukuphakamisa kanye lokuqhubekisela phambili okuqakathiswa emasikweni emiphakathi yabo njalo, ikakhulu ukuphakamisa okuqakathekiswa emulini okulesisindo;

(b) ukuthatha amanyathela okugcina amasiko, imikhuba, imbali kanye lelifa lemiphakathi yazo, kugoqela izindawo ezihlonitshwayo;

(c)

ukuqhubekisela phambili intuthuko;

(e)

ukulungisisa ingxabano phakathi kwabantu emiphakathini yabo ngokuvumelana lomthetho womdabu;

(d) Ngokuvumelana loMthetho wePhalamende, ukuphatha umhlaba womphakathi kanye lokuvikela imvelo;

(f)

Ukwenza eminye nje imisebenzi eziyiphiweyo loba ephanyekwe kuzo nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) Ngaphandle kokuphawulwe eMthethweni omiswe yiPhalamende, inkokheli zomdabu zilamandla, umkhawulo wamandla kanye lokulawula phezu komhlaba womphakathi loba ezinye izindawo abaziqhatshelweyo, njalo laphezu kwabantu abakulowomhlaba womphakathi loba izindawo.

(3) Ekuqhubeni umsebenzi wazo, inkokheli zomdabu azikho ngaphansi kokuqondiswa loba ukulawula kwaloba nguwuphi umuntu loba amandla, ngaphandle kwalokho okungabe kuphawulwe eMthethweni omiswe yiPhalamende.

(4) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule ngesimiso sokuziphatha kwenkokheli zomdabu. 283 Ukuqhatshwa lokususwa esikhundleni kwenkokheli zomdabu

UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule ngokulandelayo ngokuvumelana lamasiko ayiwo aqhutshwayo, imikhuba, isintu kanye lemikhuba yemiphakathi eqondane lalokho—

(a)

ukuqhatshwa, ukumiswa emsebenzini, lokudluliselwa kwesikhundla phambili kanye lokususwa esikhundleni kwenkokheli zomdabu;

(b) ukudalwa lokuvuselelwa kobuduna; kanye

(c)

ukulungisiswa kwezingxabano eziphathelane lokuqhatshwa, ukumiswa emsebenzini, lokudluliselwa kwesikhundla phambili kanye lokususwa esikhundleni kwenkokheli zomdabu;

kodwa—

(i) ukuqhatshwa, ukumiswa emsebenzini, kweziNduna kumele kwenziwe nguMongameli ngokuxwayisa kwedale lesabelo lezinduna isebenza leKhansili yelizwe yeziNduna kanye loMphathintambo obona ngenkokheli zomdabu njalo ngokuvumelana lemikhuba eqhutshwayo lamasiko aleyomiphakathi;

(ii) Izingxabano eziphathelane lokuqhatshwa, ukumiswa umsebenzi lokususwa esikhundleni kwenkokheli zomdabu kumele zilungisiswe nguMongameli ngokuxwayisa kwedale lesabelo leziNduna esebenza loMphathintambo obona ngenkokheli zomdabu;

(iii) uMthetho kumele uphe amanyathela okuqiniseka ukuthi zonke izindaba lezi ziphathwa ngokufaneleyo njalo okungelakunanza okuphathelane lombusazwe;

(iv) uMthetho kumele uphe amanyathela okuvikela isithunzi sezingatsha zomdabu kanye lokuzimela kwawo wodwa okungelakungenela kwezombusazwe.

284 Umholo kanye lenzuzo zenkokheli zomdabu

(1) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule ngomholo lenzuzo zenkokheli zomdabu kumiswe ngokuvumela kukaMongameli okuphiwe ngokuxwayisa kukaMphathintambo obona ngenkokheli zomdabu.

(2) Umholo wenkokheli yomdabu kumele ubekwe njalo ubhadalwe uthathwa esikhwameni semali yelizwe.

(3) Umholo wenkokheli yomdabu akumelanga wehliswe ngesikhathi esaphethe isikhundla loba esasesikhundleni leso. 285 IKhansili yelizwe lamadale ezabelo eziNduna

(1) KuleKhansili yeLizwe eyeziNduna ebunjwa ngokuvumelana loMthetho wePhalamende, yokumela zonke iziNduna eZimbabwe.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele umisele isabelo sinye ngasinye, ngaphandle kwezabelo zemadolobheni, idale lesabelo leziNduna eligoqela iziNduna kuleso sabelo.

(3) Kusiya ngokuqhuba okuvumayo iziNduna esabelweni ngasinye kumele zimelwe ngokufaneleyo eKhansilini yeLizwe eyeziNduna.

(4) Ukukhethwa kukaMongameli, uMsekeli kaMongameli lamalunga eKhansili yeLizwe eyeziNduna kumele kwenziwe yiKomishini yokhetho yeZimbabwe iZimbabwe Electoral Commission.

(5) Idale lesabelo leziNduna kumele likhethe, ngokuvumelana loMthetho woKhetho, iziNduna zokumela isabelo edale eliphezulu lePhalamende kulandelwa isigaba 120(1)(b).

(6) UMongameli kanye loMsekeli kaMongameli weKhansili yelizwe eyeziNduna bakhethelwa esikhundleni okwesibanga esiyiminyaka emihlanu njalo bafanele ukukhethwa njalo okwesibanga esisodwa esinjengaleso, kodwa umuntu osesebenze okwezibanga ezimbili njengoMsekeli kaMongameli ufanele ukukhethwa njengoMongameli.

(7) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule—

(a)

ngokukhethwa kweziNduna zikhethelwa eKhansilini yeLizwe eyeziNduna, kanye langalokhu okudingakalayo ukuze umuntu akhethwe loba okungadingakaliyo;

(b) isifungo sesikhundla okumele senziwe ngamalunga eKhansili yeLizwe eyeziNduna kanye lamadale ezabelo aweziNduna;

(c)

isikhathi sokuba sesikhundleni kwamalunga eKhansili yeLizwe eyeziNduna;

(d) umholo, ipentsheni lezinye inzuzo zikaMongameli, uMsekeli kaMongameli kanye lamalunga eKhansili yeLizwe eyeziNduna;

(e)

(f)

indlela yokuqhuba okumele ilandelwe emihlanganweni yeKhansili yeLizwe eyeziNduna kanye lemadale ezabelo eziNduna; ukumiswa kwaboNobhala beKhansili yeLizwe eyeziNduna lamadale eziNduna.

286 Imisebenzi yeKhansili yeLizwe lamadale ezabelo eziNduna

(1) IKhansili yeLizwe eyeziNduna kanye ledale lesabelo leziNduna esabelweni salo silemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukuvikela, ukuphakamisa kanye lokuthuthukisa amasiko eZimbabwe lemikhuba yesintu;

(b) ukumela imibono yenkokheli zomdabu kanye lokuqhubekisela phambili isithunzi lesimo sezingatsha zomdabu;

(c)

ukuvikela, ukuphakamisa kanye lokuqhuba intando yenkokheli zomdabu;

(e)

ukuchaza kanye lokumisa kokulandelwawa kokuziphatha ngenhlonipho kwenkokheli zomdabu kanye lokuthuthukisa amandla azo ekukhokheleni;

(d) okunakana ngokumelwa kanye langezikhalazo ezilethwe kuyo zinkokheli zomdabu;

(f)

ukwenza ukulungisiswa kwezingxabano phakathi kwenkokheli zomdabu njalo lengxabano eziphathelane lazo;

(g) ukwenza eminye nje imisebenzi engayiphiwa loba iphanyekwe kuzo nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uqiniseke ukuthi—

(a) ikhansili yeLizwe eyeziNduna kanye lawo wonke amadale ezabelo eziNduna ayenelisa ukuqhuba imisebenzi yawo ezimele wodwa njalo kakuhle;

(b) abantu abaqhatshwe yiKhansili yeLizwe eyeziNduna kanye lamadale ezabelo eziNduna ayaqhuba imisebenzi yawo ngonanzelelo njalo ngokungela kukhetha. 287 IKomithi ebona ngokuziphatha ngobuntu kanye langokwethembeka

UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uphawule ngokumiswa, ukuba ngamalunga kanye lokumele kulandelwe yiKomithi ebona ngokuziphatha ngobuntu langokwethembeka eyeziNduna, ukuthi zenze imisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukuthuthukisa kanye lokusebenzisa ukwethembeka kanye lokuqhuba ngokuhlonipha kuzinkokheli zomdabu;

(b) ukuqeda izingxabano phakathi kwenkokheli zomdabu;

(c)

ukuphatha izikhalazo ezimayelana lenkokheli zomdabu.

ISAHLUKO 16 Umhlabathi Wokulima Lokufuya 288 Ukuchasiswa kweSahluko 16 Kulesi Sahluko—

Ukuthi “umhlabathi wokulima lokufuya” kutsho okubethwe nguSigaba 72. 289 Izimiso eziqondisa imigomo mayelana lomhlabathi

Ukuze kulungisiswe umumo ongaqondanga njalo ongalunganga wokuba lomhlabathi, owadalwa ngumbuso wocindezelo, njalo ukuze kulethwe inguquko yokwabiwa kutsha komhlabathi lokufinyelelwa okulingeneyo kwengcebo zelizwe zemvelo yizizalwane zonke zeZimbabwe, imigomo ephathelane lezomhlabathi wokulima kumele iqondiswe yizimiso ezilandelayo—

(a)

umhlabathi uyingcebo yemvelo ekhawulayo ebumba ingxenye yegugu likazulu weleZimbabwe;

(b) ngaphansi kweSigaba 72, wonke umuntu oyisizalwane seZimbabwe ulelungelo lokuthola, ukubamba, ukuhlala, ukusebenzisa, ukudlulisela komunye, ukubambisa, ukuhlalisa loba ukuthengisa umhlabathi kungelani lomhlobo kumbe umbala wakhe;

(c)

ukuqanyulwa lokwabiwa komhlabathi kumele kuqhutshwe ngendlela efaneleyo njalo elinganisayo, eqakathekisa ukulinganiswa kobulili kanye lokwenhlangothi zomphakathi lokwehlukana kwazo;

(d) ukwabiwa komhlabathi kumele kukhuthaze ukwengezwa kwempumelelo kanye lokuthelwa kwemali ngabantu beZimbabwe emhlabathini wokulima

(e)

(f)

ukusetshenziswa komhlabathi wokulima kumele kuthuthukise ukutholakala kokudla okwaneleyo, impilakahle lokudla okwakha umzimba, kubuye kudale imisebenzi ngasikhathi sinye kuvikela lokulondolozela izizukulwane ezizayo imvelo;

akula muntu okumele athathelwe mahlayana nje ilungelo lakhe lokusebenzisa lokuhlala emhlabathini wokulima.

290 Ukuqhubeka kwamalungelo eSizwe emhlabathini wokulima lokufuya

(1) Wonke umhlabathi wokulima—

(a)

oqanjwe ngaphansi kohlelo 7 lweSisekamthetho esidlulileyo; kumbe

(b) oqanjwe, lesi Sisekamthetho singakaqalisi ukusebenza, ngaphansi kweSigaba 16B(2)(a)(ii) kumbe (iii) weSisekamthetho esidlulileyo; uqhubeka ungoweSizwe.

(2) Konke ukungahambelani ezintweni eziqukethwe—

(a)

esazisweni esiqanjwe kuHlelo 7 lweSisekamthetho esidlulileyo; kumbe

(b) esazisweni esiphathelane lomhlabathi wokulima esikhutshwe maqondana leSigaba 16B(2)(a)(ii) kumbe (iii) kuSisekamthetho esidlulileyo; njalo incwadi etshengisa ubumnini ekhonjwa yilokho okungahambelaniyo, kumbe ejonge ukukhonjwa yikho, layo yonke enye impazamo engabe imunyethwe yisaziso esinjalo, kayithinti ngitsho ukusebenza kwesigatshana (1) kumbe ukuqeda Amandla eSizwe phezu komhlabathi wokulima otshiwoyo ngaphansi kwaleso sigatshana.

291 Ukuqhubeka kwamalungelo abahlali bomhlabathi wokulima

Ngaphansi kwalesi Sisekamthetho, wonke umuntu okade esebenzisa loba ehlala, kumbe okade elelungelo lokusebenzisa loba ukuhlala emhlabathini wokulima ngaphansi kwesivumelwano esilotshiweyo kumbe esolunye uhlobo leSizwe, lesi Sisekamthetho singakaqalisi ukusebenza, uqhubeka elelungelo lokusebenzisa kumbe ukuhlala kulowo mhlabathi ngemva kokuba lesi Sisekamthetho siqalise ukusebenza, njengokubalulwe kuleso sivumelwano sakuqala. 292 Ukuvikeleka kwemvumo yokuhlala kwalabo abasemhlabathini wokulima

ISizwe kumele sithathe amanyathelo afeneleyo, agoqela ukubeka imithetho, ukuze kuvikelwe imvumo ephiwe wonke umuntu olomhlabathi kumbe ohlala emhlabathini wokulima. 293 Ukudluliselwa komhlabathi yiSizwe komunye umuntu

(1) ISizwe singaba lakho ukudlulisela umhlabathi wokulima ongaphansi kwaso komunye umuntu ngokumnikeza incwadi yemvumo loba omunye umhlobo welungelo lokuhlala, kodwa lokho kudlulisela kumele kuqhutshwe maqondana lezimiso ezibalulwe kuSigaba 289.

(2) ISizwe akumelanga sidlulisele emuntwini oyedwa kanye lasebantwaneni bakhe ingqephu zomhlabathi wokulima ezedlula eyodwa.

(3) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele ubeke phansi izindlela okumele zilandelwe ekudluliselweni lasekwabelweni komhlabthi wokulima yiSizwe, njalo wonke umthetho ofanayo kumele ulandele ngqo izimiso ezibalulwe kuSigaba 289. 294 Ukudluliselwa komhlabathi wokulima komunye ngabaniniwo kumbe ngabantu abahlala khona

Ngaphansi kwemigca ethile ephoqelelwa ngumthetho, umnini kumbe umuntu ohlala emhlabathini wokulima ulelungelo lokudlulisela komunye, ukubambisa, ukuhlalisa omunye loba ukuthengisa ilungelo lakhe phezu kwalowo mhlabathi wokulima. 295 Ukuhlawulelwa komhlabathi wokulima osewake wathathwa yiSizwe

(1) Iloba nguwuphi umuntu ongowalapha eZimbabwe owake wathathelwa umhlabathi wakhe wokulima yiSizwe lesi Sisekamthetho singakaqalisi ukusebenza ulelungelo lokuhlawulwa yiSizwe maqondana lalowo mhlabathi kanye lendleko zokunye konke obesekwenziwe ukuthuthukisa lowo mhlabathi ngesikhathi iSizwe siwuthatha.

(2) Wonke umuntu owathathelwa umhlabathi wakhe wokulima yiSizwe lesi Sisekamthetho singakaqalisi ukusebenza, omalungelo akhe okuba lempahla ngaleso sikhathi ayethenjiswa njalo evikelwa yisivumelwano phakathi kukaHulumende weZimbabwe lohulumende wakwelinye ilizwe, ulelungelo lokuhlawulwa yiSizwe maqondana lalowo mhlabathi kanye lezindleko zokunye konke obesekwenziwe ukuthuthukisa lowo mhlabathi njengokubethwe kuleso sivumelwano.

(3) Wonke umuntu, ngaphandle kwalowo oqanjwe kusigatshana (1) kumbe (2), owathathelwa umhlabathi wakhe wokulima yiSizwe lesi Sisekamthetho singakaqalisi ukusebenza, ulelungelo lokuhlawulwa yiSizwe maqondana lezindleko zokunye konke obesekwenziwe ukuthuthukisa lowo mhlabathi kuphela ngesikhathi iSizwe siwuthatha.

(4) Inhlawulo ekhokhelwayo ngaphansi kwezigatshana (1), (2) lo (3) kumele ihlolisiswe njalo ikhutshwe maqondana loMthetho wedale lePhalamende.

296 Ukubunjwa lamalunga okumele angene kuKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe

(1) KuleKomishini ezakwaziwa njengeKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe, emalunga ayo agoqela—

(a)

umgcinisihlalo loMsekeli kamgcinisihlalo;

(b) lamalunga angekho ngaphansi kwamabili njalo engedluli isikhombisa; abekwa nguMongameli.

(2) Amalunga eKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe kumele—

(a)

akhethwe ngenxa yesithunzi sawo kanye lobungcwethi, lolwazi kanye lokuzwisisa kakuhle ukusebenza ngendlela eyiyo efunakalayo sibili kwezokuhola lokuphatha umhlaba; njalo

(b) akhombise ukwahlukana okukhona ebantwini beZimbabwe, ikakhulu sikhangele izindawo lapho abavela khona kanye lokulinganiswa kobulili.

(3) ISigaba 237 yiso esisetshenziswayo ekususeni amalunga eKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe esikhundleni, sengathi layo iyiKomishini ezimele yodwa njengokuchazwe kuSahluko 12. 297 Imisebenzi yeKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe

(1) IKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe ilemisebenzi elandelayo—

(a)

ukuba lesiqiniseko sokuthi konke okwenzakalayo kulakho ukuba lempendulo, ukwahlulela okulungileyo kanye lokukhanya ekuphathweni komhlabathi wokulima ongaphansi kweSizwe;

(b) ukuqhuba izinhlolisiso zokusetshenziswa komhlabathi ezikhathini ezithile ezibekiweyo;

(c)

ukunika uHulumende izeluleko maqondana—

(i) lokuthathwa komhlabathi wabantu abazimeleyo ukuze ube ngokazulu;

(ii) lokufinyeleleka okulingeneyo lokubanjwa kanye lokuhlalwa komhlabathi wokulima, ikakhulu— A. ukwesulwa kwemihlobo yonke yobandlululo olungalunganga, ikakhulu ubandlululo olungenxa yobulili; B. ukuphoqelelwa komthetho loba yiwuphi obeka umkhawulo wenani lomhlabathi wokulima ongaba lakho ukubanjwa ngumuntu kumbe imuli eyodwa;

(iii) lokusetshenziswa komhlabathi kanye lobukhulu bomhlabathi wokulima oqunyelwa abantu;

(iv) lokwenza ukuthi kube lula ukuthola kanye lokudlulisela komunye amalungelo aqondane lomhlabathi;

(v) lemihlobo etshiyeneyo yokuhlaliswa emhlabathini;

(vi) ukuhlawulwa okufaneleyo okukhokhelwa ngaphansi kwaloba yiwuphi umthetho ofaneleyo, komhlabathi wokulima kanye lezindleko zokunye konke okwenziwe ukuthuthukisa umhlabathi othethwe nguHulumende;

(vii) lokwabiwa kanye lokudluliselwa komhlabathi wokulima yiSizwe komunye umuntu;

(d) Ukuchwayisisa kanye lokucubungula izikhalazo lembango engaba khona mayelana lokuholwa, ukuphathwa kanye lokwabiwa komhlabathi wokulima.

(2) IKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe, ngemvumo kaMphathintambo obona ngezomhlabathi, ilakho ukulungisa iziqondiso eziqondane lokubalulwe kusigatshana (1).

(3) IKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe kumele iqhube imisebenzi yayo maqondana lemigomo jikelele ebhaliweyo enganikwa yona nguMphathintambo obona ngezomhlabathi.

(4) Ekuqhubeni imisebenzi yayo, iKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe kumele yelulekwe yizimiso ezibalulwe kuSigaba 289.

(5) ISizwe kanye lezinhlanganiso labameli bakaHulumende emabangeni wonke, ngemithetho yePhalamende langamanye amanyathelo, kumele sincedise iKomishini yezoMhlabathi ukuqhubeni imisebenzi yayo, njalo kumele sivikele ukuzimela kwayo, ukungabi lenkethabetshabi, ukuba lesithunzi lokuphumelela emisebenzini yayo.

(6) UHulumende kumele avumele ngokwaneleyo langokufaneleyo, ngemithetho kumbe ezinye izindlela ezifaneleyo, ukuze abe lesiqiniseko sokuthi—

(a)

iKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe iyanelisa ukuqhuba imisebenzi yayo kuhle izimele; njalo

(b) abantu abaqhatshwe yiKomishini yezoMhlabathi yeZimbabwe baqhuba imisebenzi yabo kuhlekuhle, ngendlela elungileyo njalo engela nkethabetshabi. ISAHLUKO 17 Ezemali ISAHLUKWANA 1 Ukuphathwa Kwezimali 298 Izimiso zokuphathwa kwezimali kazulu

(1) Izimiso ezilandelayo kumele zikhokhele zonke izinhlangothi eziphathelane lezimali zikazulu eZimbabwe—

(a)

kumele kube lokuba mgceke kanye lokutholakala kwezimpendulo endabeni eziphathelane lezimali;

(b) izimali zikazulu kumele ziqondiswe ekuthuthukiseni ilizwe njalo, ikakhulu—

(i) umthwalo wemithelo kumele wabelwane ngendlela elungileyo;

(ii) izimali ezibuthaniswe elizweni kumele zigabelwane ngokulingeneyo phakathi kukaHulumende lamabanga ehlukeneyo kahulumende wezabelo leziqinti; njalo

(iii) izimali zonke ezisetshenziswayo kumele ziqondiswe ekuthuthukiseni iZimbabwe, njalo amaqembu abantu lezindawo ezikhangelelwa phansi kumele zinanzwe kuhle ziphiwe ingxenye ebalulekileyo;

(c)

imithwalo lenzuzo ezphuma ekusetshenzisweni kwezingcebo kumele kwabelwane ngokulingeneyo Phakathi kwabantu abaphila lamuhla lezizukulwane ezizalandela;

(d) izimali zikazulu kumele zisetshenziswe ngendlela emgceke, elobuhlakani, engela butshapha njalo esebenzayo;

(e)

ukuphathwa kwezimali kumele kube lengqondo, njalo imibiko ephathelane lemali kazulu kumele ibe sobala; njalo

(f)

ukwebolekwa kwezimali zikazulu layo yonke imicimbicimbi yokusetshenziswa kwezimali okuphathelane lisikwelede selizwe kumele kwenziwe ngendlela ekhanyayo njalo enqinekele okuthakazelelwa nguzulu weZimbabwe.

(2) Akula mthelo okumele uhlawuliswe ngaphandle kwemvumo yalesi Sisekamthetho kumbe eyomthetho omiswe yiPhalamende. 299 Amandla ePhalamende okucubungula ukuhanjiswa kwezimali ezingenayo leziphumayo zeSizwe

(1) IPhalamende kumele ihlole njalo ibe yiyo ebona ukuthi izimali zonke ezisetshenziswa yiSizwe lamaKomishini wonke kanye lezinhlanganiso labameli bakaHulumende emabangeni wonke, kugoqela izinhlanganiso ezimiswe ngumthetho, izinhlanganiso eziholwa nguHulumende, amakhansili ezabelo lawamadolobho kanye laweziqinti kuqhutshwe kuhle, ukuze ibe lesiqiniseko sokuthi—

(a)

zonke izimali ezingenileyo ziyazakala ukuthi ziye ngaphi;

(c)

imigoqo yonke lezimiso ezimayelana lokwabiwa kwemali zilandelwe kuhle.

(b) zonke izimali ezisebenzileyo zisetshenziswe ngemfanelo; njalo

(2) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele unikeze idale lePhalamende izindlela zokuhlola lokuba yilo elibona ngokusetshenziswa kwezimali okuchazwe kusigatshana (1). 300 Umkhawulo wezikwelede zeSizwe, isikwelede sikazulu leziqiniselo zeSizwe

(1) Umthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uqume imikhawulo—

(a)

yezikwelede zeSizwe;

(c)

yezikwelede lemilandu enikwa iziqiniselo zokubhadalwa yiSizwe; njalo leyo mikhawulo akumelanga idluliswe ngaphandle kwemvumo yeNdlu yabaMeli.

(b) yesikwelede sikazulu; njalo

(2) Umthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele unike imigoqo ehola ukunikwa kweziqiniselo zemali yokweboleka.

(3) Ensukwini ezingamatshumi ayisithupha ngemva kokuba uHulumende esengene esivumelanweni sokweboleka imali kumbe esokunika isiqiniselo, uMphathintambo obona ngezemali kumele abangele ukuba lezo zivumelwano zikhutshwe egwalweni lukaHulumende olweGazette.

(4) UMphathintambo obona ngezemali kumele—

(a)

kabili kusiya phambili ngomnyaka, abikele iPhalamende ngokuqhuba—

(i) kwezimali ezibolekwe yiSizwe; njalo

(ii) kwezimali ezinikelwe isiqiniselo yiSizwe;

(b) ngasikhathi sinye, lapho kubalulwa izimali ezingenileyo leziphumileyo zelizwe eNdlini yabaMeli njengokuchazwe kuSigaba 305, abeke obala edale lePhalamende umbiko ocacileyo owesikwelede sikazulu eZimbabwe.

301 Ukwabiwa kwezimali phakathi kukaHulumende wezabelo lamabanga ehlukeneyo kaHulumende wezigaba

(1) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamande kumele uvumele—

(a)

ukwabiwa okulingeneyo kwezimali zokusungula imisebenzi ephiwa amakhansili ezabelo lamadolobho kanye lawezigaba yiSizwe; njalo

(b) ukwabiwa kwezinye izimali ezifundeni lakumakhansili eziqinti, kanye lokunika imigoqo ephathelane lokwabiwa kwalezo zimali.

(2) UMthetho ochazwe kusigatshana (1) kumele unanzelele, phakathi kwezinye izinto—

(a)

izifiso zelizwe;

(c)

izindingeko lezifiso zikaHulumende, zona ezikhokhelwa ngendlela yezinhloso;

(b) imigca okumele ifakwe mayelana lesikwelede selizwe leminye imilandu yelizwe;

(d) indingeko yokutholisa izindingakalo ezimqoka zempilo, okugoqela izindawo zokufundela lezokuthola impilakahle, amanzi, imigwaqo, ozambuzi kanye lamagetsi ezindaweni ezithathelwa phansi;

(e)

(f)

amandla amakhansili ezabelo, awamadolobho lawezigaba okuhola lokuhambisa kuhle izindaba zemali;

indingeko yokuletha intuthuko lokunye okufunakalayo emakhansilini ezabelo, awamadolobho lawezigaba;

(g) ukutshiyana komumo womkhonomi ngezabelo ngezabelo.

(3) Imali eyisilinganiso esingayi ngaphansi kwehlanu ekhulwini semali yonke engenayo elizweni ngomnyaka owodwa kumele yabelwe ezifundeni leziqintini njengengxenye yabo yalowomnyaka. ISAHLUKWANA 2 Isikhwama Esiqoqiweyo Selizwe 302 Isikhwama esiqoqiweyo selizwe

Kulesikhwama esiqoqiweyo selizwe lapho okumele kufakwe khona zonke izimbadalo, imithelo lemali yokweboleka kanye lezinye zonke izimali ezingena kuhulumende, kungakhathalekile ukuthi ivela ngaphi, ngaphandle kokuba uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende—

(a)

udinga kumbe uvumela ukuthi zibhadalwe kwesinye isikhwama esidalelwe injongo ethile; kumbe

(b) uvumela inhlanganiso eyamukele lezozimali ukuthi izigcine, zonke kumbe ingxenye yazo, ukuze yanelise ukukhokhela izindleko zayo. 303 Ukuthathwa kwemali esikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe

(1) Akula mali okumele ithathwe esikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe ngaphandle nxa ingeyokukhokhela izindleko ezivunyelwe yilesi Sisekamthetho kumbe nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(2) Imali ethethwe esikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe kumele isetshenziswe ukubhadala umuntu onguye ofanele ukuthola leyo mbadalo kuphela.

(3) Umthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele ubeke indlela okumele—

(a)

imali ithathwe ngayo esikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe lakwesinye isikhwama semali kazulu; njalo

(b) imali esesikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe lakwezinye izikhwama igcinwe lokuhlanyelwa ngayo. 304 Izimbadalo ezihlawuliswa isikhwama esiqoqiweyo selizwe

(1) Zonke izindleko zezikwelede okumele zibhadalwe yiSizwe kumele zethweswe isikhwama esiqoqiweyo selizwe.

(2) Izindleko lezimbadalo ezidalwa yikuqoqa lokuhola isikhwama esiqoqiweyo selizwe ziba yimbadalo yakuqala ebhekane laleso sikhwama.

(3) Ngaphansi kwesigatshana (1)—

Ukuthi “izindleko zezikwelede” kugoqela isandiso, izinhlawulo zemali ebekelwe isikwelede, ukukhokha kumbe ukuphungula isikwelede lazo zonke ezinye izindleko eziphathelane lokuboleka imali ukuze isikhwama esiqoqiweyo selizwe sigcinakale, kanye lokubhadalwa kunye lokuhlengwa kwezikwelede okudalwe yilowo mboleko. ISAHLUKWANA 3 Ukuvunyelwa Kokusetshenziswa Kwemali Yesikhwama Esiqoqiweyo Selizwe 305 Ukwabiwa kwemali yesikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe

(1) Mnyaka wonke uMphathintambo obona ngezemali kumele ethulele iNdlu yabaMeli umbiko oqukethe amanani ezimali ezingaba lakho ukungena lokuphuma esikhwameni sikaHulumende emnyakeni olandelayo.

(2) Amanani awemali engaba lakho ukungena lokuphuma esikhwameni kumele ethulelwe iNdlu yabaMeli njengokuchazwe kusigatshana (1) ngelanga lapho iNdlu ihlala khona kungakafiki kumbe ukwedlula insuku ezingamatshumi amathathu umnyaka wemali uqalisile, kodwa nxa iPhalamende idiliziwe njalo kungeneliseki ukuthi lowo manani ethulelwe iNdlu ngaleso sikhathi, sekuyamele ethulelwe iNdlu ensukwini ezingamatshumi amathathu iNdlu ihlangane okwakuqala ngemva kokudilizwa.

(3) Amanani ezimali ezingaba lakho ukungena lokuphuma esikhwameni kumele ehlukaniselwe okunye lokunye kokulandelayo—

(a)

iKomishini inye ngayinye ebunjwe yilesi Sisekamthetho;

(c)

iNhlanganiso yabaTshutshisi yeLizwe;

(b) iwofisi kaMhlolizimali jikelele;

(d) iNkundla yeziNduna; njalo

(e) enye inhlanganiso ekhonjwe nguMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(4) Nxa iNdlu yabaMeli isivume lawomanani okusetshenziswa kwezimali kumnyaka wemali, ngaphandle kwezindleko ezihlawuliswa isikhwama esiqoqiweyo selizwe sona ngokwaso yilesi Sisekamthetho kumbe ngumthetho omiswe yiPhalamende, uMphathintambo obona ngezemali kumele abangele ukuthi kungeniswe Umthetho osavivinywa ozakwaziwa ngokuthi nguMthetho wokwabiwa kwezimali, wona ozangeniswa eNdlini yabaMeli, njalo lowoMthetho kumele—

(a)

wanelise ukuthi kube lemali eyaphuma esikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe isiyafeza zonke izindleko ezivunyelweyo; njalo

(b) wahlukanise izimali njengokwethulwe kumanani aqanjiweyo, ibekwa ezikhwameni zezinhloko zengatsha ezehlukeneyo ukuthi ziyisebenzise njengokuvunyelweyo.

(5) Nxa kuyikuthi imali eyabelwe injonjgo ethile ngaphansi koMthetho wokwabiwa kwemali ingeneli kumbe nxa kungaba khona okunye okumele ikusebenze okungazange kwabelwe imali, uMphathintambo obona ngezemali kumele abangele ukuthi kube lamanye amanani okwengezelela imali edingakalayo azakwethulelwa iNdlu yabaMeli, njalo nxa iNdlu yabaMeli ingavumela lawo manani, uMphathintambo obona ngezemali kumele abangele ukuthi kungeniswe omunye uMthetho wokwabiwa kwemali eNdlini phezu kowakuqala, ozavumela ukuthi leyo mali edingakalayo itholiswe yisikhwama esiqoqiweyo selizwe. 306 Imvumo yokusebenzisa imali phambilini kokuyabelwa

(1) Umthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ulakho ukunika uMongameli imvumo yokuthi avumeze ukuba kuthathwe imali esikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe ukuze ikhokhele izindleko ebe zingakhangelelwanga kumbe okumkhawulo wazo ubungakhangelelwanga njalo kungekho mali ebe ibekelwe lokho ngaphansi komthetho, kodwa—

(a)

uMthetho akumelanga uvumele ukuthathwa kwemali edlulisa isilinganiso esisodwa lengxenye ekhulwini semali yonke eyahlukanisiweyo eMthethweni wakuqala wokwaba izimali ocinele ukwenziwa;

(b) yonke imali ethethwe ngaphansi kwalowoMthetho kumele ifakwe ngaphansi kwamanani okwengeza izimali ezidingakalayo azakwethulwa ngokukhulu ukuphangisa phambi kwendlu yabaMeli njalo, nxa iNdlu ingawavuma lawo manani, sekumele imali yakhona ikhutshwe esikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe ngomunye uMthetho wokwabiwa kwemali phezu kowakuqala.

(2) Nxa kuyikuthi uMthetho wokwabiwa kwemali owomnyaka othile wemali kawukaqalisi ukusebenza ngesikhathi umnyaka wemali uqalisa, uMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende ulakho ukunika uMongameli imvumo yokuvumeza ukuthathwa kwemali esikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe ukuze kukhokhelwe izindleko zokuqhuba imisebenzi kaHulumende yezinyanga ezine zakuqala kulowo mnyaka wemali, kodwa—

(a)

luMthetho akumelanga uvumeze ukuthathwa kwemali edlula ingxenye eyodwa kwezintathu eyemali yonke egoqela amanani ezimali ezikhangelelwe ukusebenza awomnyaka wemali odlulileyo;

(b) yonke imali ethethwe ngaphansi kwaluMthetho kumele ifakwe eMthethweni wokwabiwa kwemali owalowo mnyaka wemali, ngaphansi kwezikhwama ezitshiyeneyo ezenhloko zengatsha okumele zisebenzise leyomali.

(3) Nxa idale lePhalamende lingadilizwa kungakenziwa amalungiselelo apheleleyo okunika izimali ezanela ukuqhutshwa kwemisebenzi kaHulumende, uMthetho obekwe yiPhalamende ungaba lakho ukunika uMongameli imvumo yokuvumela ukuthathwa kwemali esikhwameni esiqoqiweyo selizwe ukuze kukhokhelwe izindleko ezidingakalayo ekuqhutshweni kwaleyo misebenzi kuze kube zinyanga ezintathu iNdlu yabaMeli ihlangane okokuqala ngemva kokudilizwa kweDale, kodwa yonke imali ethethwe ngaphansi kwalowoMthetho kumele ifakwe eMthethweni wokwabiwa kwezimali ngaphansi kwezikhwama ezitshiyeneyo zezinhloko zengatsha okumele zisebenzise leyo mali. 307 Ukusetshenziswa kwemali ngaphandle kwemvumo

(1) Nxa kungatholakala ukuthi sekusetshenziswe imali enengi kulaleyo ebe ibekelwe injongo ethile ekade iyabelwe ngaphansi kwalesi Sahlukwana, kumbe kuyikuthi imali isisetshenziswe ukufeza enye injongo ebe ivele ingabekelwanga mali

ngezimiso zalesi Sahlukwana, uMphathintambo obona ngezemali kumele angenise uMthetho osakhandwayo eNdlini yabaMeli ojonge ukudinga isibusiso ngaleyo mali esetshenziswe ngaphandle kwemvumo.

(2) UMthetho oqanjwe kusigatshana (1) kumele ungeniswe eNdlini yabameli ngokukhulu ukuphangisa njalo, loba sekutheni, kungakedluli amalanga angamatshumi ayisithupha ngemva kokutholakala kolwazi mayelana lomkhawulo waleyomali esetshenziswe ngaphandle kwemvumo. ISAHLUKWANA 4 Ukugcinakala Kwezimali Lezimpahla Zikazulu 308 Imisebenzi yabagcini bezimali lezimahla zikazulu

(1) Kulesi Sigaba—

ukuthi “izimali zikazulu” kugoqela loba yiphi imali yeSizwe loba ebanjwe yiSizwe kumbe enye inhlanganiso loba ugatsha olumela uHulumende, sibalisela phakathi amabanga ombuso kaHulumende ezabelweni leziqintini, izinhlanganiso ezimiswe okusemthethweni kanye lezinhlanganiso eziholwa nguHulumende; ukuthi “impahla kazulu” kutsho loba yiphi impahla yeSizwe loba ebanjwe yiSizwe kumbe enye inhlanganiso loba ugatsha olumela uHulumende, sibalisela phakathi amabanga ombuso kaHulumende ezabelweni leziqintini, izinhlanganiso ezimiswe okusemthethweni kanye lezinhlanganiso eziholwa nguHulumende.

(2) Kungumlandu kaloba ngubani osesikhundleni sokukusebenzisa izimali zikazulu ukuthi azilondoloze lezo mali njalo abe lesiqiniselo sokuthi zisetshenziswe ezintweni ezivunyelwe ngumthetho njalo kulandelwa izintengo ezivumelekileyo emthethweni.

(3) Kungumlandu womuntu wonke ophethe izimpahla zikazulu ukuthi azigcine kuhle lezo mpahla njalo abe lesiqiniselo sokuthi kayilahleki, kayibhidliki, kayilimali, kayenziswa okungamelanga ikwenze kumbe ukusetshenziswa okungekho emfanelweni.

(4) UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uvumele ukubonakala masinyazanyana kokwephulwa kwezimiso ezikuzigatshana (2) lo (3) lokuqondiswa kanye lokujeziswa kwabantu abenze lobo bukliwi njalo, nxa kusenzeka, ukubuyiselwa kwalezo zimali kumbe lezo mpahla ezitshaphaziweyo. ISAHLUKWANA 5 U-oditha-jikelele 309 UMhlolizimali-Jikelele lemisebenzi yakhe

(1) Kumele kube loMhlolizimali-Jikelele, owofisi yakhe yiwofisi kazulu kodwa ingesi ngxenye yezisebenzi zikazulu.

(2) Imisebenzi kaMhlolizimali-Jikelele yile-(a)

ukuhlola amabhuku ezimali, ukuhanjiswa kwezimali lokuholwa kwezimali ezingatsheni zonke, kuzinhlanganiso lakubameli bonke bakaHulumende;

(b) ngemva kokunikwa isicelo nguHulumende, ukuqhuba inhlelo ezimqoka zokuhlola amabhuku ezimali kuloba yiphi inhlanganiso emiswe okusemthethweni kumbe eholwa nguHulumende;

(c)

ukulawula ukuthi kuthathwe amanyathelo okulungisisa okungabe kungamanga kuhle ekuholweni lekuvikelweni kwezimali lezimpahla zikazulu; njalo

(d) Ukuqhuba eminye imisebenzi engabekwa kumbe ukwethweswa phezu kwakhe nguMthetho kumbe ngaphansi koMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende.

(3) Izisebenzi ezimela inhlanganiso kuzulu kumele zilandele iziqondiso ezizinikwa nguMhlolizimali-Jikelele maqondana lesigatshana (2)(c). 310 Ukuqhatshwa kukaMhlolizimali-Jikelele

(1) UMhlolizimali-Jikelele uqhatshwa nguMongamele elemvumo yeDale lePhalamende.

(2) UMhlolizimali-Jikelele kumele abe yisizalwane seZimbabwe okhethelwe isithunzi sakhe, njalo kumele abe ngumuntu oseleminyaka elitshumi kusiya phezulu epasele ukuba ngumhlolizimali.

(3) Isikhathi esibekiweyo sokuphatha isikhundla sikaMhlolizimali-Jikelele yisikhathi esingedluli iminyaka eyisithupha, njalo umuntu akumelanga aqhatshwe njengoMhlolizimali-Jikelele nxa sewake waphatha lesosikhundla okwesikhathi esibekiweyo esisodwa kusiya phezulu esifika iminyaka elitshumi lambili, kungakhathalekile ukuthi leso sikhathi usifikise ngeminyaka elandelanayo loba hatshi.

(4) Engakangeni esikhundleni, uMhlolizimali-Jikelele kumele athathe, phambi kukaMongameli kumbe omunye umuntu onikwe imvumo nguMongameli, isifungo sokwethembeka lesokuqalisa umsebenzi njengokubalulwe eHlelweni lwesithathu, 311 UkuzimelakukaMhlolizimali-Jikelele

Ekuqhubeni umsebenzi wakhe, uMhlolizimali-Jikelele uzimele njalo ungaphansi komthetho kuphela qha! 312 Iholo likaMhlolizimali-Jikelele

(1). UMthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uvumele ukuthi iholo lezinye inzuzo zikaMhlolizimali-Jikelele zibekwe ngemvumo kaMongameli exhwayiswa nguMphathintambo obona ngezemali.

(2) Iholo likaMhlolizimali-Jikelele kumele lihlawulwe livela esikhwameni esioqiweyo selizwe njalo akumelanga lehliswe phakathi kwesikhathi esibekiweyo sokuphatha kwakhe isikhundla. 313 Ukususwa kukaMhlolizimali-Jikelele esikhundleni

(1) UMhlolizimali-jikelele angasuswa esikhundleni kuphela ngenxa(a)

yokwehluleka ukuqhuba imisebenzi yakhe ngesizatho sokufiphala engqondweni kumbe emzimbeni;

(b) yokwehluleka umsebenzi okwedlulisa amalawulo

(c)

yokungaziphathi ngemfanelo okudlulisa amalawulo.

(2) Nxa uMphathintambo obona ngezemali, evumelana leKomithi yePhalamende ebona ngezezimali zikazulu, engabikela uMongameli ukuthi udaba lokususa uMhlolizimali-Jikele esikhundleni kumele luchwayisiswe, uMongameli kumele abumbe inkundla ezakhangelisisa lolodaba.

(3) Inkundla ebunjwe ngaphansi kwesigatshana (2) kumele igoqele amalunga amathathu kusiya phezulu aqhutshwe nguMongameli, kuthi(a)

oyedwa walawo malunga abe ngumuntu oseke wasebenza njengomahluli; njalo

(b) omunye kumbe abanye bakhona babe ngabakhethwe equleni labantu abathathu abakhethwe libumba kumbe inhlanganiso esungulwe ngomthetho ukuze imele abahlolizimali zikazulu kweleZimbabwe.

(4) Ibumba kumbe inhlanganiso eqanjwe kusigatshana (3)(b) kumele ikhethe iqula eliqanjwe kulesosigatshana nxa linikwe lesosicelo nguMongameli.

(5) Inkundla ekhethwe ngaphansi kwesigatshana (2) kumele icingisise mayelana lodaba lokususa uMhlolizimali-Jikelele esikhundleni njalo, ngemva kwalokho, kumele libikele uMongameli ngelikutholileyo ibuye ilaye ukuthi uMhlolizimali-Jikelele kumele asuswe esikhundleni na loba hatshi, njalo nxa linkundla ingathi kasuswe, uMongameli kumele–anika isilawulo ngaphansi kophawu lukazulu, asuse uMholizimali-Jikelele esikhundleni.

(6) Inkundla eqhatshwe ngaphansi kwesigatshana (2) ilamalungelo kanye lamandla afana lawaboskomitshi ngaphansi koMthetho wamaKomishini Okuchwayisisa [Isahluko 10:7], kumbe omunye uMthetho ongathatha isikhundla sawo. 314 Izisebenzi zikaMhlolizimali-Jikelele

Umthetho omiswe yiPhalamende kumele uvumele ukuthi kubunjwe iBhodi ezaqhatsha abantu bokuncedisa uMhlolizimali-Jikelele ekuqhubeni imisebenzi yakhe, ubuye ubethe njalo—

(a)

imfundo okumele labo bantu babe layo;

(c)

inkululeko, ukungabi lenkethabetshabi kanye lesithunzi salabo bantu; njalo

(b) isimo somsebenzi wabo, ukwenza kanye lokuziphatha kwabo emsebenzini;
